We present GenVer, a generative verifier that scores triples using both their serialized form and generated natural language representations (NLRs). A triple-text alignment procedure selects training pairs for a sequence-to-sequence generator; a BERT-based classifier then combines features from each triple and its generated NLR. On four FactFB variants, constructed from FB15k using perturbed negative triples, GenVer obtains the highest classification accuracy among the evaluated baselines, ranging from 88.56% to 91.52%. The experiments suggest that generated representations can help triple classification, while the case analysis also exposes semantic drift and high-confidence errors.

</details>

*SK Deeraj,Yash Ingle,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器翻译模型在特定语种组合和文本领域中的表现仍有提升空间；同时，高质量平行语料的整理与消费级显卡上的可复现微调方法，有助于降低领域适配和实际部署的门槛。

Method: 从开放图书语料库提取约四万五千组原始句对，经编码校验、长度过滤、语种识别、去重和质量评分，筛得约两万五千组高质量样本。使用混合精度、梯度累积和梯度检查点，在消费级显卡上微调六亿参数模型，并以自动化指标评估德英文学与技术文本翻译效果；随后将模型接入既有开源文档翻译系统，以支持保留格式的文档翻译。

Result: 相较基础模型，微调模型的双语评估替补指标提高三点四分，字符级指标提高二点六分，翻译编辑率降低三点二分，且差异具有统计显著性。系统可处理便携式文档和办公文档，并保留原有格式。论文同时提供数据准备、训练及评估代码，模型也已公开发布。

Conclusion: 研究表明，经过严格筛选的领域相关语料与资源高效的微调流程，能够显著改善德英翻译，并可将效果落地至保留格式的文档翻译应用。公开数据处理和训练工具提升了成果的可复现性与实用价值。

Abstract: This paper describes the curation of a high-quality parallel corpus from OPUS Books and the fine-tuning of Meta's NLLB-200 models for German--English translation. We detail a dataset extraction pipeline that filters 45k raw OPUS sentence pairs to 25k high-quality examples through encoding validation, length filtering, language identification, deduplication, and quality scoring. We present a reproducible fine-tuning methodology optimized for consumer GPUs using mixed precision, gradient accumulation, and gradient checkpointing. Our fine-tuned NLLB-600M model achieves statistically significant improvements (+3.4 BLEU, +2.6 chrF++, -3.2 TER) over the base model on literary and technical German-English translation. We demonstrate practical deployment by integrating the fine-tuned model into LinguaHaru, an existing open-source document translation system, achieving format-preserving translation across PDF and Office documents. All dataset preparation scripts, training code, and evaluation tools are provided for reproducibility. The fine-tuned model is available at HuggingFace.

</details>


### [38] [Decodability Is Not Validity: Evaluating Nuisance Sensitivity in Language Model Concept Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07805)
*Ayal Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 概念探针即使预测准确度相同，也可能以不同程度混入相关干扰属性；因此，概念测量不能只看解码准确度，还须检验其对干扰属性的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从语言模型隐藏状态中预测某个概念的探针，可能同时编码目标概念与相关的干扰属性。仅凭预测准确度，无法判断探针是否真正测量了目标概念，因而需要评估其对干扰因素的依赖。

Method: 研究在四类目标—干扰属性场景、两个大型语言模型及多种概念提取器中比较探针。除在留出数据上的预测表现外，研究还检验：在目标固定时，探针分数与干扰属性的关联；以及通过旨在只改变干扰因素的文本编辑，观察分数如何变化。编辑结果由两个语言模型评审，并与安慰剂编辑进行比较。研究也考察了训练数据相关性及利用带干扰属性标签的开发集筛选提取器的效果。

Result: 在训练数据中目标与干扰属性相关时，即使提取器的留出集区分能力相当，其干扰敏感度在三个场景中仍相差数倍；预测能力与干扰敏感度的排序关联还会随场景改变方向。影响主要取决于探针的训练数据，而非干扰属性在自然文本中与目标的关联强弱：第一人称表达虽与自然评论中的情感无关，却至少与否定表达一样容易被探针吸收。去除训练数据中的相关性后，这种系统性影响消失。使用带干扰属性标签的开发诊断进行筛选，可在默认选择较敏感的场景中降低反事实干扰敏感度，代价是准确度略有下降。

Conclusion: 概念探针的预测准确度不能可靠代表其测量是否忠实于目标概念。评估概念测量时，应直接检查探针对干扰属性的依赖，并采用干扰感知的验证与筛选，而不能只依据解码表现。

Abstract: Two concept probes can be equally accurate at predicting a concept from a language model's hidden states while their scores contain different mixtures of the target concept and a correlated nuisance attribute. In four target--nuisance settings (two aspect sentiments in CEBaB; occupation and gender in BiasBios; Amazon review sentiment with negation, and with first-person framing), two LLMs, and a population of concept extractors, we compare decodability with discriminant evidence: the score's dependence on the nuisance at fixed target, and its response to input edits intended to change only the nuisance, filtered by two LLM judges and compared against placebo edits. Under correlated training data, extractors that are statistically equivalent in held-out AUROC differ up to several-fold in nuisance sensitivity in three of the four settings, and decodability is not a reliable proxy for it: their rank correlation changes sign across settings. The effect follows the probe's training data, not the nuisance's natural association with the target: first-person framing, unrelated to sentiment in natural reviews, is absorbed at least as much as negation, which co-occurs with negative sentiment, and decorrelated training removes the systematic effect. Selecting extractors with nuisance-aware development diagnostics, which need nuisance labels but no counterfactuals, lowers counterfactual nuisance sensitivity wherever the default choice is sensitive, at a small accuracy cost. Concept measurements should be validated, not just decoded.

</details>


### [39] [Different Corpora, Different Skills? A Paired Study of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07812)
*Edoardo Vaira,Georgios Chavales,Pierre Nugues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在小规模语言模型预训练中，语料选择会塑造不同的能力侧重，而非产生单一、普遍的优劣排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型常受训练数据预算限制，因此预训练语料可能与模型规模、训练方案同样重要。仅用单一指标比较语料，可能无法反映它们对不同能力的影响。

Method: 研究在一千万词和三千七百万词两种数据预算下，对比《大英百科全书》一九一一版与二〇二六年婴儿语言模型严格预训练语料。研究分别训练教师模型、经蒸馏的五千八百万参数学生模型，以及未蒸馏学生模型；各语料使用相同的模型系列、训练方案和评测流程，并通过控制实验考察训练暴露量、诊断上下文长度及教师模型集成的影响。

Result: 两种语料呈现不同的能力画像，而非统一的质量排名。婴儿语言模型语料在语言可接受性基准上持续更强；《大英百科全书》语料则常在合成的实体追踪任务上表现更好。这一差异见于教师与学生模型及两种数据预算，其中蒸馏学生模型的跨随机种子结果最稳定。控制实验表明，核心差异在匹配训练暴露量、延长诊断上下文后仍存在，直接集成教师模型也无法复现一千万词条件下《大英百科全书》学生模型的实体追踪表现。不过，实体追踪成绩的幅度仍会受到训练动态影响。

Conclusion: 在本文考察的语料范围内，预训练数据会使小模型形成不同的相对能力侧重。因此，比较预训练语料时应采用多项基准评测，避免依据单一任务或总分作出片面判断。

Abstract: Small language models are often trained under tight data budgets, where the choice of pretraining corpus may matter as much as model size or training recipe. We compare two contrasting corpora at matched 10-million- and 37-million-word budgets: the 1911 Encyclopaedia Britannica and the BabyLM 2026 strict pretraining corpus. For each corpus, we train teacher models, distilled 58-million-parameter Baby-Llama students, and no-distillation student ablations, using the same model families, training recipes, and evaluation protocol across corpora. Across BLiMP, BLiMP Supplement, EWoK, Entity Tracking, and COMPS, the corpora produce different skill profiles rather than a single quality ranking. BabyLM 2026 is consistently stronger on linguistic acceptability benchmarks, while Britannica is often stronger on Entity Tracking, a synthetic state-tracking task. The contrast appears across teacher and student models and at both budgets, with the cleanest cross-seed replication in the distilled student comparison. Targeted controls show that the central contrast persists after matching training exposure and at a longer diagnostic context; direct teacher ensembling also does not reproduce the 10M Britannica student's Entity Tracking behavior. Entity Tracking magnitudes nevertheless remain sensitive to training dynamics. For this corpus pair, corpus choice can therefore produce different relative skill profiles at small scale, motivating multi-benchmark evaluation when comparing pretraining data.

</details>


### [40] [Axis-LoRA: Probing and Adapting Embedding Models for Personal-Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07820)
*Dohoon Kim,Junho Park,Hyunwoong Bae,Seyoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按查询动态融合时间、地点、人物和事件信息的适配方法，在多种模型与基准上提升个人记忆检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人经历类问题的检索需要准确区分时间、地点、人物和事件，但现有流程依赖冻结的稠密嵌入模型，这些模型对四类信息的编码不足。更换记忆中的日期或地点几乎不会改变嵌入表示，单纯在冻结嵌入上训练投影层也无法补回缺失信号。

Method: 研究发现，四类信息在模型内部对应不同的注意力头模式。据此提出轴向低秩适配方法：在嵌入模型内部设置四个分别对应时间、地点、人物和事件的低秩适配分支，并通过针对每个查询的门控机制组合各分支。训练使用小规模的轴向反事实数据集，仅调整基础模型参数中的一小部分。

Result: 在三种标准嵌入模型上，针对长期记忆评测、自动化记忆基准和洛科莫等基准进行评估。该方法在四项基准上的平均前十召回率提升了1.28至1.56个百分点。

Conclusion: 个人记忆检索的关键属性可能无法通过冻结嵌入后的简单投影恢复，而需要在嵌入模型内部针对不同信息轴进行适配。实验结果表明，按轴设置轻量适配分支并按查询动态融合，是一种参数开销较小且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly asked questions about a user's personal experiences, retrieval must distinguish memories by time, place, person, and event. Current personal-memory pipelines rely on frozen dense embedding models to embed memories for retrieval, but we find that these models do not capture the four axes well. Swapping a memory's date or place barely shifts its embedding, and a trained projection on top of the frozen embedding does not recover the missing signal. The problem lives inside the model, where each axis is encoded by a distinct attention-head pattern. Guided by these findings, we propose Axis-LoRA, a per-axis adapter that places four LoRA branches inside the embedding model and combines them via a per-query gate. Across three standard embedding models evaluated on LongMemEval, ATM-Bench, and LoCoMo, Axis-LoRA improves 4-benchmark mean R@10 by $+1.28$ to $+1.56$~pp while training only a small fraction of the base parameters on a small axis-counterfactual training set. Code is available at https://github.com/dohoonkim-ai/axis-lora.

</details>


### [41] [Towards Foundation Decision Models: Drex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07854)
*Nischay Dhankhar,Alisher Amantay,Zhanibek Datbayev,Darkhan Akhmetov,Yash Shah,Nurmukhamed Ubaidullayev,Raman Saparkhan,Dos Baha,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出德雷克斯系列开放式决策模型，可直接对给定选项输出概率分布，无需逐词生成；其最大版本在多项评测中表现突出，并以显著更低的延迟完成推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式推理等自回归方法虽然准确率较高，但在实际部署中可能产生不一致答案，还需要解析生成文本，并带来额外成本与延迟。决策模型针对这一问题，直接对用户提供的选项进行判断，更适合稳定、高效的生产应用。

Method: 作者发布四种规模的德雷克斯模型，参数规模分别为八十亿、九十亿、三百五十亿（每次激活三十亿）和二百七十亿。输入由文档、记录或智能体上下文等状态，以及针对该状态的选择题、是非题或有序评分题构成，也可包含图像。训练采用监督微调与基于调整后反馈的强化学习，并为最大版本加入视觉训练；消融实验还考察了反馈强化学习和选项顺序一致性损失的作用。

Result: 在决策指数公开测试集上，最大版本取得六十七点五二分，高于对比模型的六十点七零分。在决策基准第四版上，其表现与两种商业模型统计持平，较另一领先模型低一点六分；在决定基准上，它是决策模型中准确率最高的。典型请求的响应时间为一百八十三毫秒，显著快于对比商业模型的约两秒和三点五秒；选项重新排序也不会明显影响输出稳定性。实验还表明，基于反馈的强化学习可提升准确率，一致性损失可增强对选项顺序变化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 德雷克斯展示了专用决策模型在准确率、稳定性和响应速度之间取得良好平衡的潜力。其最大版本在多项基准中达到有竞争力或领先的表现，同时具备低延迟优势；四种模型均以开放源码形式发布。

Abstract: Autoregressive approaches with large language models, such as long chain-of-thought reasoning, have become the default for new AI applications because they reach higher accuracy on a wide range of tasks. In production, however, they give inconsistent answers, require parsing of outputs, and add cost and latency. Decision models address this: given a question and a set of options supplied by the user, they return a probability distribution over the options without autoregressive decoding. We present Drex, a family of four open decision models with 8B (DLM), 9B (v1.5), 35B with 3B active (v2.0), and 27B (Max) parameters. Each request consists of a state (a document, record, or agent context, optionally with images) and questions about it, each asking for a choice among options, a yes/no judgment, or a score on an ordered scale. Training combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning from adjusted feedback (RLAF), plus vision tuning for Max. Ablations show that RLAF improves accuracy and that consistency losses improve robustness to option order. On Decision Index 0.4.1, Max sets a new state of the art among the decision models we compare (67.52 on the public suite vs. 60.70 for Jev 1.14.0). On Decision Bench v4, it is statistically tied with d3-turbo and GPT-6 Sol and trails Claude Opus 5.5 by 1.6 points, and it has the highest accuracy among decision models on DecideBench. Max answers a typical request in 183 ms, against about 2.0 s for GPT-6 Sol and 3.5 s for Claude Opus 5.5, and its outputs are stable under option reordering. All four models are open source: https://github.com/nace-ai/drex-decision-models

</details>


### [42] [Do Recommenders Need to Think in Words? Latent Reasoning for Efficient LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07866)
*Hongyang Su,Beibei Kong,Lei Cheng,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该方法将推荐时的逐步文本推理转为训练阶段的隐状态监督，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的推荐系统常在每次预测前生成思维链文本，以激活模型的推理能力，但这会额外消耗数十至数百个词元，增加推理成本。论文探讨能否仅通过训练阶段的监督保留推理带来的准确率提升，而不在部署时生成文本或执行额外的隐式推理步骤。

Method: 论文提出一种生成式推荐框架，训练时使用一个含二百五十万个参数的连续思维模块，在语言模型的隐状态上迭代执行若干步潜在推理。软词元选择用于缓解隐状态与输入嵌入之间约十四倍的范数差异。逐步推理损失则通过模型预测头监督每个中间状态，使其预测目标语义编号的首个词元。训练完成后，部署时可移除连续思维模块，直接使用语言模型进行预测。

Result: 在三个亚马逊数据集上，该方法相较于经过思维链蒸馏的基线，前十命中率相对提升百分之四至百分之五；在批量大小为一时，推理延迟降低至基线的约十八分之一。实验还显示，逐步训练监督在三个数据集上都能稳定提升准确率。

Conclusion: 推荐模型的推理能力可以通过训练阶段对中间状态的逐步监督来获得，而不必在每次推荐时生成推理文本或保留额外模块。该方案将计算开销从每次推理转移到一次性训练，有望兼顾推荐准确率与部署效率。

Abstract: LLM-based recommender systems increasingly generate chain-of-thought (CoT) text before each item prediction to activate the LLM's reasoning capacity, costing tens to hundreds of extra tokens per recommendation at inference. We ask whether inference-time reasoning artifacts (textual or latent) can be replaced by training-time supervision alone, without loss of accuracy. We propose LatentRec, a framework for LLM-based generative recommendation that replaces inference-time reasoning artifacts with training-time per-step head supervision. LatentRec has three components. A 2.5M-parameter Continuous Thought Module iterates K latent reasoning steps over the LLM's hidden state during training. Soft Token Selection bridges the ~14x norm gap between hidden states and input embeddings. A Per-step Reasoning Loss (PRL) supervises every intermediate state through the LLM's prediction head against the first token of the target Semantic ID. The trained LLM retains this accuracy without the module at inference, so the module can be discarded at deployment. On three Amazon benchmarks, LatentRec improves HR@10 by 4.0-5.0% (relative) over a CoT-distilled baseline at 18.3x lower latency (batch=1). Per-step training supervision consistently improves accuracy across three datasets, paid once at training rather than at every recommendation.

</details>


### [43] [AraEngage: Interpretable Linguistic Correlates of Engagement in Arabic Facebook News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07926)
*Shimaa Ibrahim,Nour Elhouda Dalloua,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文构建阿拉伯语新闻帖文数据集，分析可解释语言特征与按粉丝数标准化的互动之间的关系，并指出未标注元音的阿拉伯语会使词典特征产生歧义、降低测量可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于英语的互动特征常被直接用于阿拉伯语，但阿拉伯语文本通常不标注元音，同一拼写可能对应不同词义或语法功能，导致词典指标测量失准。研究旨在考察哪些可解释的阿拉伯语语言指标与新闻帖文互动相关，并评估这些关联受发布者背景、互动定义及词汇歧义影响的程度。

Method: 构建阿拉伯语脸书新闻帖文样本，包含二〇二三至二〇二四年间、来自五十七个国家的二千四百五十一页发布的约一万条公开帖文，并配对互动计数与可解释语言特征。使用随机森林预测按粉丝数标准化的互动，以特征归因方法解释模型结果；同时审查相关词典，识别未标注元音造成的词义混淆。

Result: 随机森林在按帖文划分的数据上的决定系数为零点三八。宗教表达、消息长度、句子长度和词汇密度是主要相关指标；与英语新闻研究不同，本样本中积极情绪与较高互动相关。消息长度呈现反差：未经调整的原始互动量在长帖中最高，但模型对粉丝数标准化互动的归因更偏向短帖。词典审查发现，命令词、模糊限制词和宗教词表均存在显著歧义，可能使命令表达和模糊限制表达与互动的近零关联受到测量误差影响。

Conclusion: 研究提供了阿拉伯语新闻社交媒体互动分析的数据、语言特征、修订词典及代码，并表明语言特征与互动的关系会受到互动指标定义和阿拉伯语词汇歧义的影响。所有发现均为基于操作化指标的观察性关联，不能据此推断因果；原始帖文未公开，以遵守数据平台条款。

Abstract: Engagement features developed for English are routinely applied to Arabic, but unvocalized Arabic changes what a lexical feature measures. We introduce AraEngage, a query-defined sample of 10,000 public Arabic Facebook news posts from 2,451 pages in 57 countries (2023-2024) that pairs engagement counts with interpretable linguistic features. We use it to examine which interpretable Arabic indicators are associated with follower-normalized engagement, and how far those associations can be trusted given publisher context, the definition of engagement, and lexical ambiguity. Religious expressions, message length, sentence length, and lexical density are the leading correlates in a Random Forest model ($R^2$ = 0.38 on a post-level split) interpreted with SHAP. Unlike English news, positive sentiment is associated with higher engagement in this Arabic news sample. Message length shows a length paradox: unadjusted raw engagement is highest for long posts, whereas the model's attributions for follower-normalized engagement favor short posts. A linguistic audit identifies why standard lexicon features are fragile in unvocalized Arabic: imperative lists contain homographs such as the form read as "express!" that is also the frequent preposition "via", hedge lists conflate the possibility marker qad + imperfect ("may") with the emphatic qad + perfect ("has indeed"), and religious lists mix devotional formulas with place names in political news. These are plausible sources of measurement error that may attenuate the near-zero associations of imperatives and hedges. All findings are observational associations with operationalized indicators. We release the derived linguistic features, engagement counts, refined lexicons, and analysis code; raw posts are not redistributed, in line with CrowdTangle terms.

</details>

*Manuela-Daniela Danu,Miguel Rodríguez-Ortega,Alberto Becerra-Tome,Pablo Arancibia,Fernando Gallego-Donoso,Lorenzo Pratesi,Xi Bai,Andrei-Damian Dinescu,Salvador Lima-López,Andrea Guala,Saskia Haitjema,Jonas Mack,Michele Miranda,Wesam Al Nabki,Jan Rodríguez-Miret,Vasile George Marica,Vladimír Kincl,Kateřina Dostálová,Michael Andrej,Alicia Ujil,Corinne Allaart,Machteld Boonstra,Malin Overmars,Anoop Shah,Giovanna Ceroni,Jonathan Kossoff,Domenico D'Amario,Renzo Laborante,Antonio Iaconelli,Isabella Fumarulo,Jacopo Lenkowicz,Livia Lilli,Emma Weiss,Alex Scafa,Felix Lindberg,Milou Gamage,Eulàlia Farré-Maduell,Sébastien Bratières,Martin Krallinger,Bram van Es*

TL;DR: 研究构建了多语言心脏病学文本语料库及实体识别模型。结果表明，跨语言联合训练比单纯增加专科预训练更能提升实体识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊疗产生大量本地语言临床文本，但自然语言处理资源主要集中于英语，且缺少兼具心脏病学专科性、多语言覆盖和广泛实体类别的评测基准，限制了临床信息提取技术的研发与应用。

Method: 研究首先以五百零八篇西班牙语心脏病学病例报告为基础，通过机器翻译、标注迁移和临床专家审核，扩展为涵盖捷克语、荷兰语、英语、意大利语、罗马尼亚语和瑞典语的语料库，标注疾病、症状、医疗操作和药物。随后构建语言专属编码模型，并利用心脏病学及生物医学文本继续预训练；再基于该语料微调命名实体识别模型，并与多语言联合训练模型及规则词典基线进行比较，同时评估严格边界匹配、宽松边界匹配和临床专家评价。

Result: 专科预训练使伪困惑度最多降低至原来的八分之一，但严格跨度级别的识别效果变化不超过零点零四，收益主要出现在原始模型缺乏生物医学知识的语言。多语言联合训练模型几乎在所有语言和实体类别上取得最佳严格识别效果，相较最强单语言系统最高提升零点一三。疾病、操作和症状的错误中，实体边界识别问题较突出，采用宽松匹配后效果提高零点一至零点二三；药物的提升仅为零点零一至零点零七。临床专家对该系统的平均评分为百分之七十三，显著高于规则词典基线的百分之二十六。

Conclusion: 对于临床实体识别，专科预训练并非决定性因素；多语言联合训练在多数场景中效果更强，也更便于跨国部署。研究还表明，后续改进应重视实体边界识别，并验证机器翻译与标注迁移对不同语言数据质量的影响。

Abstract: Background: Cardiovascular care generates large volumes of clinical free-text documentation in local languages. However, clinical natural language processing (NLP) resources remain concentrated in English, and no existing benchmark is cardiology-specific, multilingual, and broad in entity coverage.
  Methods: We present two contributions. The first is CardioCCC, a corpus of 508 cardiology case reports originally published in Spanish and extended to Czech, Dutch, English, Italian, Romanian, and Swedish through machine translation, annotation projection, and clinician review, with annotations covering diseases, symptoms, procedures, and medications. The second is CardioLM, comprising CardioBERTa, a set of language-specific encoders obtained by continued pretraining on cardiology and biomedical corpora, and CardioNER, a set of named entity recognition (NER) models fine-tuned on CardioCCC.
  Results: Cardiology-specific pretraining reduced pseudo-perplexity by up to a factor of eight, yet changed strict span-level F1 by at most 0.04, with benefits concentrated in languages whose backbones lacked biomedical coverage. The jointly trained multilingual XLM-RoBERTa-large models achieved the best strict F1 in nearly every language and entity type, exceeding the strongest monolingual system by up to 0.13 F1. A large share of residual errors concerned boundary detection: relaxed matching recovered 0.10-0.23 F1 for disease, procedure, and symptom, but only 0.01-0.07 for medication. Clinicians rated CardioNER well above a rule- and dictionary-based baseline, with the mean rating across entity types rising from 26% to 73%.
  Conclusions: Specialty-specific pretraining is not the decisive factor for downstream entity recognition, whereas multilingual joint training yields the strongest results in nearly all settings and a simpler deployment path for multi-country clinical information extraction.

</details>


### [45] [Beyond the Keyword: A Human-Annotated Benchmark for Arabic Anxiety-Related Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07949)
*Shimaa Ibrahim,Mabrouka Bessghaier,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Insaf Elouard,Wajdi Zaghouani*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过“焦虑／担忧”关键词检索到的内容，并不都在表达个人焦虑，也可能承担其他话语功能。研究旨在建立经过审慎标注的基准，帮助区分这些用途，并避免把检索样本的类别比例误当作现实中的焦虑流行率或关键词检索准确率。

Method: 研究从阿拉伯语推特／Ｘ平台获取一千条含焦虑相关关键词的帖子，并依据指南标注六种话语功能。两位阿拉伯语母语标注者先独立标注，再逐条复核全部四百八十九条分歧和五百一十一条一致项，形成共识标签；另由一位标注者对随机抽取的两百条帖子进行评估。研究还测试了法纳尔二十七亿参数模型，并发布标注指南、残余类别子类型方案、固定数据划分、标签来源记录及评估代码。

Result: 独立标注的原始一致率为百分之五十一点一，科恩卡帕系数为零点三七八。复核过程中，一致项中有百分之二十三点五的标签被修改，说明仅检查分歧不足以确保标注可靠性。额外标注者与共识标签的一致率为百分之六十点五，卡帕系数为零点五一七。模型相对于共识标签的准确率为百分之七十九点八，宏平均Ｆ一值为零点七八四，卡帕系数为零点七四九。模型错误主要集中在残余类别，占全部错误的百分之四十一点一；该类精确率为百分之九十点五、召回率为百分之四十四点七。由于实际提示中的残余类别定义存在内部不一致，低召回率不能单独归因于模型。样本中社会政治焦虑占百分之二十七点一，个人情绪表达占百分之二十二点零；这只是样本标签分布，并非总体比例估计。

Conclusion: 关键词检索结果需要按话语功能细分，且标注审核应覆盖一致项与分歧项。该基准为阿拉伯语焦虑相关内容分类提供了可复用的数据和评估资源，同时显示残余类别的定义及操作提示会显著影响模型评估。

Abstract: Keyword retrieval returns expressions of personal anxiety together with many other uses of anxiety-related language. We present a benchmark of 1,000 Arabic Twitter/X posts retrieved with the keyword qalaq ("anxiety/worry") and annotated for six discourse functions. Two native Arabic-speaking annotators labeled every post independently, reaching 51.1% raw agreement (Cohen's $κ= 0.378$). We then reviewed all 489 disagreements and all 511 agreements under the same guidelines. The review changed 120 of the agreed labels (23.5%), which shows that agreements need auditing as well as disagreements. The final labels form a consensus reference; on a random sample of 200 posts, an additional annotator agreed with it on 60.5% of posts ($κ= 0.517$). Against this reference, Fanar-C-2-27B reaches 79.8% accuracy and macro-F1 0.784 ($κ= 0.749$). Its errors concentrate in the residual category (41.1% of errors; precision 0.905, recall 0.447), whose definition is internally inconsistent in the executed prompt, so the low recall cannot be attributed to the model alone. Sociopolitical Anxiety (27.1%) outnumbers Personal Emotional Expression (22.0%); these are assignment proportions in the sample, not estimates of prevalence or retrieval precision. We release the guidelines, a residual-subtype scheme, a fixed data split, label provenance, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [46] [Compression Through a Context Lens: Evaluating Adaptation, Quantization, and Distillation in Arabic Legal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07988)
*Md. Rafiul Biswas,George Mikros,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，四比特量化可显著降低模型内存占用，而蒸馏对阿拉伯语法律任务表现的影响更不稳定；无上下文评测能补充揭示模型压缩与适配的实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的法律应用场景中，压缩大型语言模型有助于部署，但其对领域能力的影响可能因评测方式而异。研究旨在比较提供法律材料与不提供外部材料时的评测结果，判断仅依赖上下文支持的测试是否足以衡量模型表现。

Method: 研究使用沙特阿拉伯语法律基准，对法纳尔九十亿参数、贾伊斯八十亿参数和通义千问二点五七十亿参数模型进行评估。评测分为有上下文和无上下文两种设置，并考察领域适配、四比特量化及知识蒸馏三个阶段，重点比较模型内存需求与无上下文选择题准确率。

Result: 对于基础模型，移除上下文后准确率最多下降二十七个百分点。四比特量化使内存占用减少百分之六十四至七十一，无上下文选择题准确率变化介于下降三点二个百分点与提升一点六个百分点之间。蒸馏结果差异较大，学生模型规模越小，准确率通常越低；由法纳尔教师模型蒸馏出的通义千问学生模型准确率为百分之七十六点六，接近教师模型的百分之七十七点二。

Conclusion: 无上下文评测是上下文支持评测的有效补充，有助于更全面地评估领域适配与压缩对阿拉伯语法律模型能力的影响。量化在大幅节省内存的同时，准确率变化相对有限；蒸馏则需谨慎权衡模型规模与性能。

Abstract: Model compression can facilitate the deployment of large language models in resource-constrained legal settings, but its effects on domain-specific performance may depend on the evaluation protocol. We investigate this issue using ArabLegalEval, a Saudi Arabic legal benchmark, under both with-context and no-context evaluation. The former provides supporting legal passages, while the latter assesses performance without external context. Removing the context produces accuracy gaps of up to 27 percentage points for the base model. We examine three stages: domain adaptation, quantization, and knowledge distillation across FANAR-9B, JAIS-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B. Four-bit quantization reduces model memory by 64-71%, with changes in no-context MCQ accuracy ranging from -3.2 to +1.6 percentage points. Distillation produces more variable results, with accuracy generally declining as student size decreases. A Qwen2.5-7B student distilled from FANAR achieves 76.6% no-context accuracy, compared with 77.2% for its teacher. Our findings suggest that no-context evaluation provides a useful complement to context-supported testing when assessing the effects of adaptation and compression on Arabic legal language models.

</details>


### [47] [Automatic keyphrase extraction and annotation: modern theoretical approaches and practical solutions for text and speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08006)
*Daria Guseva,Olga Mitrofanova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理文本与语音关键词抽取方法，指出该领域正从统计方法转向大语言模型、端到端语音处理及更具上下文意识的综合范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本与视听信息快速增长，使自动关键词抽取日益重要。然而，文本和语音领域的研究路径较为分散，方法、术语与挑战也各不相同。本文旨在整合这些研究，形成统一的分析框架，并总结领域演进与未来需求。

Method: 本文采用综述与比较分析的方法，梳理关键词抽取的流程、书面语和口语关键词的特点及相关术语；系统讨论统计、混合、机器学习和结构化方法，并重点分析大语言模型生成、语料自动标注，以及语音关键词抽取中的转写文本方法与端到端声学方法。

Result: 综述呈现了关键词抽取从传统统计方法向大语言模型和端到端语音处理发展的趋势，并比较了文本与语音任务的不同特点及方法挑战。分析还指出，语料标注仍面临方法与质量方面的问题，语音抽取则需要权衡基于转写文本和直接利用声学信息的路径。

Conclusion: 关键词抽取正朝着整合多种模态、充分利用上下文的方向发展。未来研究需重点解决低资源语言数据不足、多模态融合困难，以及生成式关键词抽取评价不够细致等问题。

Abstract: The exponential growth of textual and audiovisual information has made the task of automatic keyphrase extraction (KE) increasingly significant. This article provides a comprehensive analysis of contemporary theoretical approaches and practical solutions for KE across both text and speech modalities. The primary contribution of this work is its systematic synthesis of these often-disparate research strands into a unified analytical framework, highlighting the evolution of the field from statistical methods towards large language models (LLMs) and end-to-end speech processing. We examine the stages of KE, the characteristics of keyphrases in written and spoken language, and terminological nuances. Various methods for automatic KE are discussed and analyzed in detail: statistical, hybrid, machine learning-based, and structural. The review dedicates substantial attention to emerging paradigms, including keyphrase generation using LLMs, and provides a detailed overview of methodologies and challenges in automatic corpus annotation. Furthermore, we specifically analyze current directions and inherent difficulties in KE for spoken language, comparing transcript based and end-to-end acoustic approaches. This synthesis leads us to conclude that the field is moving towards a more integrated, context-aware paradigm. Future progress will depend on addressing key challenges such as data scarcity for low-resource languages, effective multimodal fusion, and the nuanced evaluation of generative KE systems.

</details>


### [48] [Linguistic boundaries emerge from pressure-constrained processing dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08085)
*Rui He,Philipp Homan,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出，语言边界并非仅由句法结构预先决定，而是连续加工过程中多种认知需求变化所形成的离散表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会把连续经验组织成有界单元，同时保持单元间的连贯性；语言也具有词、短语和句子等边界。然而，边界如何产生尚不清楚。本文旨在解释边界与未解决依存关系、已完成关系的整合及语义语境更新等加工需求之间的关系。

Method: 研究分三步进行：首先构建没有预设边界位置或成分标签的生成模拟，考察多种加工需求如何共同产生类句子的分段；其次检验这些需求能否预测听者在理解中标记边界的选择；最后分析自然阅读中的阅读时长，并用大型语言模型处理相同叙事，考察内部表征几何及其更新。研究还比较边界对这些现象的解释力与加工需求、句法、韵律及下一个词的意外程度等因素的关系。

Result: 模拟产生了类似句子的长度分布和不同特征时间尺度。接近边界时，未解决依存负荷下降，整合成本达到峰值，累积压力重置；结构完成可以促成闭合，但并不必然决定闭合。听者更倾向于在未解决依存较少、整合成本和语义不匹配较高、此前记忆负荷较重的位置标记边界，且这一关系超出句法和韵律线索的解释。自然阅读中，句末减速伴随记忆负荷下降以及整合成本、语义不匹配上升。语言模型在句末表现出局部内在维度上升、隐藏状态轨迹更趋直线，随后出现更大的表征更新。边界在阅读时长和隐藏状态几何方面提供了额外解释力，但对下一个词意外程度的额外解释较少。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持一种动态加工观点：语言边界是连续认知过程发生状态转变时呈现出的离散标记。边界由依存维护、关系整合、记忆负荷与语义更新等需求共同塑造，不能简单等同于句法结构的完成。

Abstract: Human cognition organizes continuous experience into bounded units while maintaining continuity across them. Linguistic elements are also organized into bounded structural units such as words and phrases, with sentence marking the final bound of a grammatical construction process. How do such boundaries emerge? We propose boundaries emerge from interacting demands to maintain unresolved dependencies, integrate completed relations, and update semantic context, with structural completion allowing but not determining closure. First, in a generative simulation without predefined boundary positions or constituent labels, these demands produced sentence-like segment-length distributions and distinct characteristic timescales, with structural completion allowing but not determining closure. Near boundaries, unresolved dependency load declined, integration cost peaked, and accumulated pressure reset. Then, we tested whether same demands predicted human listeners' boundary choices during comprehension. Listeners were more likely to mark boundaries with fewer unresolved dependencies, higher integration cost and semantic mismatch, and elevated preceding memory load, beyond syntactic and prosodic cues. Third, in naturalistic self-paced reading, sentence-final slowing coincided with declining memory load and increasing integration cost and semantic mismatch, broadly consistent with the simulation. Processing same narratives with a large language model revealed increased local intrinsic dimensionality and straighter hidden-state trajectories at sentence endings, followed by larger representational updates. Boundary explained additional variation in reading time and hidden-state geometry beyond processing demands, but considerably less in next-token surprisal. Together, these findings suggest linguistic boundaries emerge as discrete manifestations of transitions in continuous processing dynamics.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond K/V Caches: Cross-Model State Handoff in Hybrid Linear-Attention LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08133)
*Jiacheng Cai,Tao Zhang,Siqi Liang,Yun Dai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出混合状态迁移方法，在不同混合注意力模型间迁移三类推理状态；在保持较高任务效果的同时，显著加快模型切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统常在长对话中切换模型，以平衡成本与质量，但上下文越积越长，目标模型每次切换都要重新处理上下文。已有方法主要迁移全注意力层的键值缓存，未充分解决混合线性注意力模型中的循环状态与卷积历史迁移问题。尤其卷积状态扰动会影响循环层的键值及状态更新，而卷积历史被后续输入覆盖后，循环状态造成的影响仍可能持续，因此仅追求循环状态重建精度并不一定能改善后续生成。

Method: 提出混合状态迁移方法，覆盖全注意力键值缓存、循环状态和卷积历史三类状态。针对循环状态设计结构化映射，卷积历史对齐时保留历史顺序；并利用冻结目标模型在后续生成过程中的输出校准适配器。全注意力缓存映射的更新则离线合并。

Result: 在五项基准测试中，从四十亿参数模型切换至二百七十亿参数模型，平均任务保持率为百分之八十九点八八；从九十亿参数模型切换至二百七十亿参数模型，保持率为百分之九十四点一一。在源模型缓存已备妥的条件下，使用合成的一万六千令牌输入进行独立计时，切换流程主体分别实现二十一点六六倍和二十一点九一倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法将混合注意力模型中多种状态的相互作用纳入跨模型迁移，并通过面向后续生成的校准提升迁移实用性。实验显示，它能在较高任务保持率下显著降低模型切换开销；但报告的加速数据以源缓存已可用为前提。

Abstract: Production deployments often use different models to balance cost and quality through cascading, routing, and switching models during long conversations. Context accumulates across turns. Each switch requires the target to repeat prefill over this context. Existing methods transfer full-attention (FA) K/V caches across models. Hybrid linear-attention models also retain recurrent states (REC) and convolution histories (Conv). The interaction between Conv and REC complicates cross-model state transfer. Conv perturbations can change keys and values in recurrent layers and alter REC updates. Conv histories are overwritten, but changes in REC can still affect later outputs. More accurate REC reconstruction need not improve continuation. Building on this, we propose Hybrid State Transfer (HST) for all three state types. It uses structured REC maps and preserves history order during Conv alignment. We calibrate the adapters using the frozen target's outputs during continuation. FA cache map updates are merged offline. Across five benchmarks, Qwen3.5 4B$\rightarrow$27B and 9B$\rightarrow$27B achieve mean task retentions of 89.88% and 94.11%, respectively. With source caches already available, separate timing on synthetic 16K inputs yields handoff body speedups of $21.66\times$ and $21.91\times$.

</details>


### [50] [Knowing When to Teach: Learning from Teachable Prefixes in Multilingual Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08179)
*Jaewon Cho,Minseo Yoon,Jinyoung Park,Jinwoo Seo,Jaewon Chu,Alice Oh,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按推理轨迹筛选教师监督的方法，减少教师被学生错误步骤带偏的问题，并提升多语言数学推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏让模型以自身推理作为学生训练，并以参考解答条件下的同一模型充当教师。然而在多语言推理中，学生中间步骤出错后，教师有时不会纠正错误，反而顺着错误继续推理。论文将这一现象称为“教师漂移”，并发现低资源语言中漂移往往更早发生，因此不同语言需要不同的监督策略。

Method: 论文提出可教式在线自蒸馏方法，逐条判断推理轨迹的哪些部分适合接受教师监督。该方法利用教师与学生的似然信息估计教师漂移的起点，并选取此前缀作为监督范围；随后检验教师能否从该前缀恢复正确答案，仅对通过检验的前缀实施蒸馏。

Result: 在涵盖十种语言和三种模型规模的多语言数学推理实验中，该方法持续优于常规在线自蒸馏；在低资源和中等资源语言上的优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 在线自蒸馏的有效性取决于教师是否仍能提供正确、有用的反馈。通过估计漂移位置并验证教师的纠错能力，按轨迹选择监督内容可降低错误监督风险，尤其有助于改善资源较少语言的推理表现。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a student on its own reasoning, using the same model conditioned on reference solutions as a teacher. Applying OPSD to multilingual reasoning requires the teacher to provide useful feedback across languages, even when the student makes mistakes in intermediate steps. We observe a phenomenon we call Teacher Drift: as these mistakes accumulate, the teacher may follow the student's incorrect steps instead of correcting them. Our analysis shows that this shift tends to occur earlier in lower-resource languages. To account for these differences across languages, we propose Teachable On-Policy Self-Distillation (TOPSD), which determines where and whether teacher supervision should be applied to each reasoning trajectory. TOPSD uses teacher-student likelihood to estimate where drift begins and select a prefix for supervision. It then verifies whether the teacher can recover the correct answer from that prefix, applying distillation only to prefixes that pass verification. Experiments on multilingual mathematical reasoning across ten languages and three model scales show that TOPSD consistently outperforms conventional OPSD, particularly in low- and medium-resource languages.

</details>


### [51] [Agent-R$^2$: Scaling Agents for Realistic Productivity Work via Real-Source Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08219)
*Teng Pan,Zixuan Wang,Yuchen Yan,Qifei Wu,Yao Chen,Yingqi Qu,Ruiqing Zhang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以真实来源为基础的生产力任务数据合成框架，并用四千个任务实例训练四十亿参数智能体；该模型在端到端生产力表现和文件依据推理上均有提升，且在两项评测中超过更大规模的对照模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实生产力任务要求智能体遵循长流程、理解多种格式的文件，并按细致要求交付可用成果。现有智能体仍难以稳定完成此类任务，重要原因是缺少可规模化的后训练数据：任务意图不够贴近真实工作，文件环境不够真实，评价也未充分覆盖最终交付物。

Method: 提出真实来源数据合成框架。该框架以真实职业任务线索为起点，利用语料中提取的工作流程证据，并采用真实网页来源文件构建任务，而非依赖人工编写流程或由模型生成文件。框架从一万个网页语料候选中筛选出四千个任务实例，每个实例包含真实的多格式附件及针对交付成果的细粒度评分标准。随后通过监督微调和基于评分标准的强化学习训练模型。

Result: 仅使用四千个任务实例训练的四十亿参数模型，在通用生产力评测中的得分为百分之五十一，高于对照模型的百分之四十八；在办公问答评测中的得分为百分之十四点八，也高于对照模型的百分之十三点八。进一步分析显示，该方法减少了智能体在缺乏充分依据、构建或核验时就给出表面可信成果的捷径行为，并促进持续探索。

Conclusion: 研究表明，以真实职业任务、流程证据和真实文件为基础合成训练数据，能够有效提升智能体处理现实生产力工作的能力。细粒度交付物评分与相应训练机制还有助于改善文件依据推理，减少浅层应付式作答。

Abstract: Large language models have made rapid progress in agentic reasoning and tool use, raising expectations for agents that can take over realistic productivity work from humans. However, current agents still struggle to finish such tasks, where success requires following long-horizon professional workflows, understanding heterogeneous file contexts, and producing usable deliverables under fine-grained requirements. A key bottleneck is the lack of scalable post-training data that reflects real work intent, authentic file environments, and deliverable-level evaluation. To close this gap, we introduce Agent-R$^2$, a $\textbf{r}$eal-source data synthesis framework that scales training data for $\textbf{r}$ealistic productivity work. Instead of relying on manually specified workflows or LLM-generated files, Agent-R$^2$ grounds synthesis throughout the pipeline in real occupational seeds, corpus-derived workflow evidence, and authentic web-sourced files. From 10k web-corpus candidates, Agent-R$^2$ distills 4k task instances that pair real multi-format attachments with fine-grained, deliverable-level rubrics. On this data, we train Agent-R$^2$-4B via supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning, improving both end-to-end productivity performance and file-grounded reasoning. Trained on only 4k instances, Agent-R$^2$-4B surpasses Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 on both GDPval (51.0$\%$ vs. 48.0$\%$) and OfficeQA (14.8$\%$ vs. 13.8$\%$). Further analysis shows that Agent-R$^2$ mitigates shortcut behavior, where agents produce superficially plausible deliverables before sufficiently grounding, constructing, or verifying them, while promoting sustained exploration.

</details>


### [52] [From Twisted Facts to Rogue Agents: Can LLMs Reliably Detect Multi-Dimensional Hallucinations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08231)
*Hanyu Qiu,Ze Liu,Zhe Zhao,Kuang Xiong,Blaze Chen,Zhenxiang Yan,Renfen Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个大规模双语基准，用于评估大语言模型在文本事实性与智能体执行轨迹忠实性方面的幻觉检测能力。实验显示，模型能进行基本的样本级判断，但在定位、修正及智能体场景检测上仍明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛用于信息获取、内容生成和智能体任务执行，但仍容易产生隐蔽且高风险的幻觉。现有评估需要更系统地覆盖文本与智能体场景，并检验模型是否能发现、定位和修正幻觉。

Method: 构建名为“幻觉检查”的大规模双语基准，包含四万二千余个实例，覆盖文本中的事实性幻觉和智能体执行轨迹中的忠实性幻觉。基准提供多层次细粒度标注，用于评估幻觉检测、定位与修正，并涵盖不同任务和扰动程度；研究还评测了十四种大语言模型，并分析思维推理、网络搜索和监督微调的影响。

Result: 十四种模型均表现出一定的样本级幻觉检测能力，但在细粒度定位、幻觉修正以及智能体轨迹中的幻觉检测方面表现较弱。思维推理、网络搜索和监督微调各自带来部分改进，但都无法彻底解决问题。

Conclusion: 该基准为诊断和比较大语言模型在文本及智能体场景中的可靠性提供了系统基础，也表明当前模型的幻觉处理能力仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for information access, content production, and agent-based task execution, but their outputs remain vulnerable to subtle, high-risk hallucinations. To systematically evaluate and advance post-generation hallucination detection, we introduce HaluCheck, a large-scale, bilingual benchmark comprising over 42k instances spanning both factual hallucinations in text and faithfulness hallucinations in agent trajectories. HaluCheck features fine-grained, multi-level annotations for detection, localization, and correction, covering different tasks and perturbation degrees. Experiments on fourteen LLMs reveal that, while models show basic sample-level detection ability, they struggle with fine-grained localization, correction, and agent hallucination detection. Further analysis demonstrates that thinking, web search, and supervised fine-tuning each offer partial improvements, but none fully solve the problem. HaluCheck thus provides a solid basis for benchmarking and diagnosing LLM reliability across text and agent scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [Terminal-style Mathematical Modeling Bench: The First Agentic and Verifiable Benchmark for Mathematical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08237)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向完整数学建模任务的可复现评测基准，以多篇优秀论文作为参照，并通过自动化可行性检查和形式化验证筛除不合理答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学建模评测要么只考察求解过程中的局部环节，要么依赖语言模型评审整篇报告，却缺少对评分依据及答案物理可行性的可靠核验。数学建模结果既要接近可信参考答案，也必须满足现实约束，因此需要能够评估完整建模流程、并提供机器可验证信号的基准。

Method: 构建终端式数学建模基准，涵盖二〇二三至二〇二五年的十八道真实竞赛题，并在容器化终端中提供原题、官方附件和图表。智能体提交单个结构化文件，评测聚焦最终问题的数值核心，并与优秀奖论文复现结果比较。评分采用方向感知的单侧机制，不因答案优于参照值而扣分；同时设置双层可行性验证，包括六类可判定不变量的程序化检查，以及六百二十四项形式化证明义务。答案若未通过可行性硬性门槛，则得分归零。每道题使用多篇人工遴选的优秀论文作为参照，而非单一答案。

Result: 在七种多模态模型配置、每种运行三次的评测中，表现最强的配置多参照优秀论文评测得分为百分之六十四点五八。一个成本为两点五四美元的开放模型得分为百分之五十九点八一，成本约为最强配置的百分之一。

Conclusion: 该基准将数学建模答案的数值表现与可行性验证结合，为衡量智能体的长程建模能力提供了可复现、可机器核查的评测信号，也可用于收集智能体完成建模任务的过程轨迹。

Abstract: Mathematical modeling turns an open-ended real-world problem into assumptions, a model, a solution, and a defended recommendation, and its answers matter only if they are both close to a credible reference and physically feasible; existing benchmarks either score solver fragments or grade reports with an LLM judge, leaving those numbers unchecked. We introduce TB-MathModel (the Terminal-style Mathematical Modeling Bench), the first benchmark to make complete contest-style modeling problems agentic, verifiable, and terminal at once, over 18 real CUMCM and MCM problems (2023--2025). Each task ships the verbatim statement, official attachments, and figures in a containerized terminal; the agent returns one JSON artifact, scored on the numeric core of the final question against a reproduction of the Outstanding-award paper. Two design choices distinguish it: direction-aware, one-sided scoring, which never penalises beating the anchor, and two-layer feasibility verification---a programmatic gate over six decidable invariant forms plus 624 Lean~4 obligations---with a hard gate that zeroes infeasible answers. Each task is scored against a panel of human outstanding papers rather than one, so a model must be close to many accepted answers; across seven multimodal entries (three seeds each) the strongest reach a multi-reference O-Eval of 64.58\%, while the \$2.54 open model holds 59.81\% at $1/100$ the cost. TB-MathModel turns the numeric core of modeling into a reproducible, machine-checkable signal for measuring long-horizon modeling competence and for collecting agent trajectories. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/terminal-bench-math-modeling.

</details>


### [54] [Multimodal-SEA: Benchmarking AI Agent Security under Multimodal Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08281)
*Zhitao He,Chi Him Zhang,Chengru Han,Yi R Fung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向个人智能体的多模态社会工程攻击评测基准。测试显示，七种常用多模态模型均易受攻击；伪造多模态内容会显著提高攻击成功率，安全指令虽能降低成功率，却未必提升模型识别欺骗内容的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人智能体可能因虚假图像等多模态内容而受到社会工程攻击，但现有评测主要关注纯文本对话，未充分考察智能体在真实操作场景中的内在安全性。研究旨在填补这一评测缺口，并系统衡量智能体面对可适应攻击时的脆弱性。

Method: 构建包含608项专家设计任务的交互式基准，任务源自真实诈骗案例，覆盖14个领域、152种攻击情景及四个难度等级，并体现中英文环境的社会文化差异。智能体需在模拟应用中检查内容、核实请求并执行操作；攻击结果依据可验证的工具执行日志判定。研究还设计了多模态攻击测试框架，用于整合任务信息、控制攻击进程并构造多模态内容，并从六个维度评测七种常用多模态模型。

Result: 七种模型的攻击成功率均不为零，范围为7.1%至34.5%。加入多模态内容后，攻击成功率由19.9%升至29.2%，尽管模型主动核实请求的覆盖率有所提高。更强的安全指令能够降低攻击成功率，但未提升模型对欺骗性多模态内容的识别能力；部分模型较低的攻击成功率还伴随对合法请求的过度拒绝。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，个人智能体的安全评估不能局限于文本对话或表面上的核实行为，而应纳入多模态攻击、实际操作及可验证结果。当前模型仍存在显著安全风险，后续改进需同时提升欺骗内容识别与安全决策能力，并避免以过度拒绝合法请求换取较低的攻击成功率。

Abstract: Personal AI agents face social engineering attacks supported by forged multimodal content, but existing evaluations mainly focus on text-based dialogue, leaving their intrinsic security under such attacks insufficiently studied. We introduce Multimodal-SEA, an interactive benchmark comprising 608 expert-designed tasks grounded in real-world fraud cases, covering 14 domains and 152 attack scenarios with prepared multimodal content. The tasks span four difficulty levels and reflect the distinct social and cultural contexts of Chinese and English environments. Agents inspect content, verify requests, and execute actions in simulated applications, with attack outcomes grounded in verifiable tool execution logs. To enable controlled red teaming of personal agents, we design a multimodal attack harness that integrates task information, attack progression control, and multimodal content construction to evaluate their intrinsic security against adaptive social engineering attacks in simulated environments. Evaluating seven widely used multimodal models across six dimensions reveals that all are vulnerable, with attack success rates of 7.1% to 34.5%. Adding multimodal content increases attack success from 19.9% to 29.2%, despite increased proactive verification coverage. Stronger safety instructions reduce attack success without improving recognition of deceptive multimodal content, while lower attack success rates for some models accompany excessive refusal of legitimate requests.

</details>


### [55] [Measured Coupling for Parallel Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08307)
*Tal Oved,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于位置间实际耦合强度的扩散语言模型解码方法，可在准确率平均下降约一点一分的情况下显著减少解码步数，并在多数设置中加快生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型每一步可以生成多个词元，但需要判断哪些位置的预测能够安全地同时提交。既有平均场解码通过惩罚预测相似的位置来近似这种依赖关系，未能直接衡量一个位置的提交会如何影响另一个位置的模型置信度。

Method: 作者直接估计位置间的耦合，即提交一个词元对其他位置置信度的影响。方法利用主前向计算过程中的低成本探测获取耦合信息，无需额外训练或调用其他模型，并将其接入原有的平均场解码规则。

Result: 在三个模型和四个基准测试上，该方法相比平均场解码所需步数大幅减少，并在多数设置中更快；结合键值缓存时也有加速效果。平均准确率代价为一点一分。打乱耦合信息或用统一奖励替代耦合信息，都会失去这一性能收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接利用词元位置之间的实际影响关系，比依据预测相似性构造代理信号更有效。该方法无需训练且可融入现有解码框架，在较小准确率损失下提升扩散语言模型的生成效率。

Abstract: Diffusion language models can write many tokens per step, but must choose which tokens are safe to commit together. Mean-field decoding penalizes pairs of positions whose predictions look alike. We replace this proxy with the coupling itself: how much committing one token changes the model's confidence at another. Cheap probes inside the main forward pass measure it, with no training or extra model, and it plugs into the same mean-field rule. On three models and four benchmarks, our method needs far fewer steps than mean-field decoding and is faster in most settings, also with a key-value cache, at an average accuracy cost of 1.1 points. Shuffling the couplings or replacing them with a uniform bonus loses this gain.

</details>


### [56] [LLM-Assisted Label Noise Correction for EEG-Based Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08311)
*Elmira Salari,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅在训练阶段使用大语言模型筛查睡眠分期标签噪声的方法，在睡眠脑电扩展数据集上提升了分类准确率和宏平均Ｆ１分数，且不增加推理阶段的模型负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期模型的性能受训练标签质量限制。人工标注存在专家间分歧和系统性错误，可能使深度学习模型学习到错误模式，因此需要在训练阶段识别并处理不可靠标签。

Method: 先用轻量级睡眠分期网络在原始数据上训练，并提取各脑电片段的分类置信度；再将低置信度片段的频谱特征及其前后序列信息交给大语言模型，依据睡眠分期指南判断标签是否可疑。移除被判为可疑的片段后，使用清理后的数据重新训练分类器。大语言模型仅参与训练阶段。

Result: 在睡眠脑电扩展数据集的二十折交叉验证中，整体准确率由百分之八十一点八提高至百分之八十四点六，宏平均Ｆ１分数由百分之七十五点六提高至百分之七十七点四；快速眼动睡眠阶段的识别也有明显改善。推理时仍使用仅含一百三十万参数的轻量级网络。

Conclusion: 利用大语言模型辅助筛查训练标签噪声，能够改善睡眠分期模型的整体表现，同时保留轻量级模型的推理效率。由于额外的大语言模型计算仅发生在训练阶段，该方案具有用于实时可穿戴睡眠监测的潜力。

Abstract: Automatic sleep stage classification from electroencephalography (EEG) signals is a critical task in clinical sleep medicine. While deep learning models have shown promising results in this domain, their performance is fundamentally constrained by the quality of training labels, which human experts manually annotate and that are subject to inter-rater disagreement and systematic labeling errors. In this paper, we propose a novel LLM-assisted label-noise filtering pipeline that operates exclusively during training to identify and remove unreliable sleep-stage annotations before retraining the classifier. Specifically, TinySleepNet is first trained on the original dataset to extract per-epoch SoftMax confidence scores, then low-confidence epochs are submitted to LLaMA 3.1 along with their EEG spectral features and surrounding sequence context. The LLM evaluates each epoch against AASM sleep-scoring guidelines and assigns a verdict of RELIABLE or SUSPICIOUS. Suspicious epochs are removed from the training set, and TinySleepNet is retrained on the cleaned data. Evaluated on the Sleep-EDF Expanded dataset using 20-fold cross-validation, our method improves overall accuracy from 81.8% to 84.6% and Macro F1-score from 75.6% to 77.4%, with particularly notable gains in REM detection. Crucially, because the LLM operates only during training, the lightweight TinySleepNet architecture (1.3M parameters) is preserved at inference, making the proposed pipeline suitable for real-time, wearable sleep monitoring applications.

</details>


### [57] [Polarity Classification with Multitask Learning Syntax Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08323)
*Muhammad Imran,Carlos Gómez-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文比较两种在多任务学习中融入句法知识的情感分析方法。结果表明，基于对比学习的隐式句法指导在多数数据集上优于不使用句法的基线，并在适用数据集上胜过推理时执行句法分析的规则系统，同时评估速度提高约一点五至九倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感分析需要处理否定、程度强化等现象，词典方法难以充分应对；传统句法分析虽可提供结构信息，却增加计算开销。论文旨在利用训练阶段的句法标注提升情感分析效果，同时避免推理阶段进行句法分析所带来的成本。

Method: 研究提出并比较两种统一多任务学习方案。标准多任务方法使用共享编码器，并设置句法依存关系序列标注与情感极性分类两个任务专属输出层。对比多任务方法则通过对比式辅助目标和偏向句法信息的注意力池化，提供隐式句法指导，不显式预测句法标签。两种方法均在训练时使用句法标注，推理时无需外部句法分析器或人工组合规则。实验覆盖七种语言的十一项数据集，并比较四种序列标注编码方案。

Result: 采用相对编码时，对比多任务方法在十一项数据集中的十项上优于不使用句法信息的基线；在规则式句法分析流程适用的六项数据集上，均取得更好表现，评估速度快约一点五至九倍。标准多任务方法的效果则更依赖数据集和编码方案。

Conclusion: 训练阶段的隐式句法监督能够有效增强多语言情感分析，在不增加推理阶段句法计算开销的情况下，达到或超过需要推理时显式句法分析的方法。相比之下，显式联合预测句法标签的方案稳定性较弱，表现受数据集与编码选择影响更大。

Abstract: Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a fundamental NLP task concerned with the identification and categorization of subjective information in text. While lexicon-based methods offer transparency, they often struggle with linguistic phenomena such as negation and intensification. Syntactic parsing has been integrated into SA pipelines to address this, but conventional parsers introduce significant computational overhead. Recent work shows that reformulating dependency parsing as a Sequence Labeling (SEL) task can improve efficiency. We propose and compare two strategies for integrating syntactic knowledge into a neural sentiment analysis model under unified Multi-Task Learning (MTL). The first, Standard-MTL, jointly learns dependency parsing (via sequence labeling) and sentiment polarity classification using a shared encoder with two task-specific heads. The second, Contrastive-MTL, provides implicit syntactic guidance through a contrastive auxiliary objective and a syntax-biased attention pooling mechanism, without requiring explicit label prediction. Both rely on syntactic annotations during training and eliminate need for hand-crafted compositional rules or external parsing at inference. We evaluate eleven datasets spanning seven languages---the MARC corpus (English, Spanish, French, German) and seven datasets from SemEval-2022 Task 10 (English, Spanish, Norwegian, Basque, Catalan)---using four established SEL encodings. With the relative encoding, Contrastive-MTL outperforms a syntax-naive baseline on 10 of 11 datasets and surpasses the external rule-based pipeline (SELSP) that parses at inference on all six datasets where the latter applies, while being 1.5--9$\times$ faster at evaluation. Standard-MTL is more dataset- and encoding-dependent. These results show implicit syntactic supervision during training can match or exceed explicit parsing at inference without computational overhead.

</details>


### [58] [Cultural Adaptation within Repeated Social Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08334)
*Chen Cecilia Liu,Roi Reichart,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型能否在沙盒环境中通过反复社会互动持续适应文化规范；结果表明，记忆机制有助于长期学习、跨场景迁移及五种语言中的规范习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多文化知识是隐性的，通常通过具体情境中的社会互动习得。随着大语言模型被部署为服务不同社群的交互式智能体，仅用问答测量其文化能力，难以评估它们能否根据互动调整行为。因此，研究需要从“模型知道什么”转向“模型如何适应”。

Method: 研究将文化适应视为通过社会互动持续吸收规范的过程，并在沙盒环境中考察大语言模型经过反复互动后的适应能力。重点分析记忆机制在长期互动学习、向不同社会情境迁移，以及跨语言规范习得中的作用。

Result: 研究发现，记忆机制能够促进模型在重复互动中的纵向学习，帮助其将所学规范迁移到不同社会情境，并支持其在五种语言中吸收规范。摘要未提供具体实验设置或量化结果。

Conclusion: 文化适应不应只被视为静态知识测验，而应被研究为持续的互动过程。本文为构建能够通过交互不断学习和适应文化规范的大语言模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Much of human culture is tacit and is typically learned through situated social interaction. As LLMs are deployed as interactive agents across diverse communities, assessing their cultural competence demands moving beyond existing question-answering approaches, shifting from what they know to how they adapt. In this work, we broaden the scope of cultural adaptation in NLP by investigating whether LLMs can adapt through repeated interactions via a sandbox environment. By reframing cultural adaptation as a continuous process of norm assimilation through social interaction, our work demonstrates that memory mechanisms foster longitudinal learning across repeated interactions, facilitate transfer to different social settings, and enable multilingual norm assimilation across five languages. Together, our findings lay the groundwork for continuous, interaction-driven cultural adaptation in LLMs.

</details>


### [59] [SpecCTMC: Exact Speculative Simulation of Diffusion Language Model Continuous-Time Markov Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08362)
*Siva Rajesh Kasa,Yasong Dai,Sumit Negi,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出无需额外草稿模型的无训练推测采样方法，可在保持连续时间马尔可夫链目标分布的同时，将顺序模型评估次数减少约一点七三至四点三八倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型能够并行生成文本，但独立采样多个词元可能破坏词元间依赖并改变输出分布。精确的连续时间采样虽能避免这种近似，却通常需要每生成一个词元就顺序调用一次模型，效率受限。

Method: 提出推测采样方法「SpecCTMC」。该方法利用已计算的模型预测提出连续的词元更新，并并行评估这些更新对应的中间状态，再通过精确的接受与校正步骤确定结果。方法无需额外草稿模型，也无需离散化连续时间过程。理论上证明，它保留底层连续时间马尔可夫链中事件时间、生成顺序与词元取值的联合分布。

Result: 在两种掩码扩散指令模型及五个基准上的实验表明，与直接连续时间采样相比，该方法将顺序模型评估次数减少一点七三至四点三八倍，同时保持参考采样分布。

Conclusion: 该方法通过推测和并行评估缓解了精确连续时间采样的顺序计算瓶颈，并以精确校正维持原有联合分布，因此在提升采样效率的同时，不引入时间离散误差或并行解码带来的额外因子化误差。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models offer parallel text generation, but independently sampling multiple tokens can disrupt their dependencies and change the output distribution. Exact continuous-time sampling avoids this approximation, yet typically requires one sequential model evaluation per token. We introduce SpecCTMC, a training-free speculative sampler that reduces this sequential bottleneck without a separate draft model. SpecCTMC proposes successive token updates using previously computed model predictions, evaluates their intermediate states in parallel, and applies an exact acceptance-and-correction procedure. We prove that SpecCTMC preserves the joint distribution of event times, generation order, and token values of the underlying continuous-time Markov chain. It therefore introduces neither time-discretization error nor additional factorization error from parallel decoding. Experiments on LLaDA-8B-Instruct and Dream-v0-Instruct-7B across five benchmarks demonstrate 1.73-4.38x fewer sequential model evaluations than direct continuous-time sampling, while preserving the reference sampling distribution. Our code is available at: https://github.com/kasakh/specctmc

</details>


### [60] [MedGraph: Maintaining Patient State with Graph Memory for Long-Term Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08433)
*Donghua Zhang,Zizheng Wang,Ze Dong,Yiding Feng,Siyu Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出面向纵向医疗对话的图结构记忆系统，通过追踪患者状态变化、区分事实所属者并显式保留安全信息，提高医疗问答的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨多次就诊的医疗助手必须持续掌握患者不断变化的临床状态。对五种现有记忆系统的分析发现，错误主要来自三方面：检索到过时状态、混入他人的健康信息，以及遗漏作答所需的安全事实。

Method: 系统从每次对话中提取带日期的事实，并标明事实描述对象；将药物、诊断和测量记录连接成保留历史状态的链条。回答问题时，系统按患者对相关链条排序，依据问题所指的时间读取对应状态，并补充原始对话内容；同时在每个问题中附上患者档案所记录的全部安全事实。

Result: 在纵向医疗对话基准测试中，该系统搭配两种不同语言模型骨干时均取得最高总体准确率，分别比五种基线中表现最强者高出10.5和10.4个百分点。在一种骨干设置下，记忆构建与问题回答的效率也较高。

Conclusion: 将患者身份归属、时间变化、历史状态链条及安全事实纳入统一记忆与检索流程，有助于减少纵向医疗问答中的关键错误，并提升准确性。

Abstract: LLM-based medical assistants that follow a patient across visits need a memory that keeps the patient's clinical state correct as it changes. We examine the errors of five memory systems on longitudinal medical dialogue and find three recurring causes: the retrieved evidence gives an outdated state, includes another person's health facts, or lacks a safety fact the answer depends on. We present MedGraph, a graph memory built for these three cases. Construction extracts dated facts from each session, attributes each fact to the person it describes, and links medication, diagnosis, and measurement records into chains that keep earlier states. Retrieval ranks the patient's chains as units, reads each chain at the time the question asks about, and adds the original dialogue text; a patient profile lists every recorded safety fact with every question. On MedMemoryBench, MedGraph has the highest overall accuracy with both a GPT and a Qwen backbone, 10.5 and 10.4 points above the strongest of five baselines, with efficient memory construction and answering in the GPT setting.

</details>


### [61] [Understanding AI from the Inside: The State of Mechanistic Interpretability for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08438)
*Fazl Barez,Tung-Yu Wu,Hunar Batra,Gabriele Dominici,Florent Draye,Yanai Elazar,Damiano Fornasiere,Antía García,Abir Harrasse,Riley Harris,Sara Hooker,Michael Lan,Belinda Z. Li,Aaron Maiwald,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Naomi Saphra,Or David Shafran,Adi Simhi,Robert Trager,Shriyash Kaustubh Upadhyay,Ziyu Yao,Yu Zhao,Shay B. Cohen,Mor Geva,Yoshua Bengio,Stella Biderman,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述面向人工智能安全的机制可解释性，评估理解模型内部机制能否提升安全性，并提出统一的主张评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经网络能力与自主性增强，其造成社会危害的潜力也随之上升。然而，当前对模型内部运作、陌生情境下行为及可靠控制能力的理解仍不足。论文因此聚焦一个核心问题：弄清模型如何工作，是否真的能让模型更安全？

Method: 作者系统梳理机制可解释性及其与行为评估的关系，介绍特征、多义性、变换器计算和电路等基础概念，并综述特征提取与分析、电路发现和因果验证、自然语言解释中间表征、学习动态及训练数据归因等方法。论文进一步提出评估机制可解释性主张的统一框架，归纳六种研究取向，并讨论知识编辑、行为引导和监控等安全应用及其局限。作者还发布了可复现关键研究结果的代码笔记本。

Result: 这是一篇综述与框架性论文，而非报告单一实验结果。其主要产出包括对相关方法和安全应用的系统整理、用于评估机制性证据的统一框架、六种研究取向的分类，以及对失效模式、未解决问题和行为安全评估关系的分析。摘要未提供定量实验结果，也未断言机制可解释性已能可靠提升安全性。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解、预测和控制模型行为提供了潜在路径，但内部机制理解本身并不自动等同于安全保障。要判断其安全价值，需严谨评估机制性主张，并与行为层面的安全措施结合，同时正视其局限与失效风险。

Abstract: Neural networks are likely to be among the most consequential artefacts in human history. As these systems become more capable and autonomous, their potential to cause societal harm also grows, making AI safety one of the defining challenges of our time. Yet we continue to build increasingly powerful systems without fully understanding how they work, how they will behave in unfamiliar situations, or how to reliably control them. Mechanistic interpretability offers a path towards understanding these systems by studying their internal representations and computations, with the aim of evaluating, predicting, and controlling behaviour. We survey mechanistic interpretability for AI safety in order to answer the question: Does understanding how models work actually make them safer? We place mechanistic interpretability in the broader context of interpretability research and compare its mechanistic evidence with behavioural evaluation. We introduce the foundations of mechanistic interpretability, including features, polysemanticity, transformer computation, and circuits, and survey methods for feature extraction and analysis, circuit discovery and causal verification, explaining intermediate representations in natural language, learning dynamics, and training-data attribution. Beyond surveying existing methods, we propose a unified framework for evaluating mechanistic interpretability claims and characterise six research orientations by their goals, assumptions, and trade-offs. Finally, we examine how mechanistic understanding can support AI safety, including knowledge editing, steering, and monitoring, and assess its limitations, failure modes, open problems, and relationship to behavioural safety measures. We also release a repository of code notebooks with worked examples that reproduce key findings from surveyed methods, allowing readers to build intuition through hands-on experimentation.

</details>


### [62] [Label-Wording Augmentation in a Typed-Decision Encoder: Gains on Unfamiliar Words, Not on Abbreviations or Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08439)
*Vladimir Beskorovainyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，训练时增加标签表述形式增强的数据扩充，能提升模型对常见词、借词和行话标签的适应性，但对缩写和不透明代码没有可靠帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型需要为调用方临时提供的选项打分，因此性能可能受标签措辞影响，而开发者无法控制这些措辞。研究旨在检验训练时加入多种标签表面形式，能否提高模型对不同标签词汇的稳健性。

Method: 比较两个版本的俄语多语言交叉编码器，参数量为3.07亿，均在13项俄语任务上训练；较新版本额外使用了呈现多种标签表述形式的数据扩充。研究固定问题，只改变18个路由标签的措辞，构造10组标签词汇，来源于三种独立流程，并检查其与训练数据的重合情况。每题设置5个候选标签；其中8组还设置18个候选标签。评估涵盖普通词、借词与行话、缩写及不透明代码等类别。

Result: 在由普通词或借词构成、且未用于训练的标签词汇上，增强版本在所有测试设置中准确率更高，最高提升0.24。14项提升中有11项通过霍尔姆多重比较校正；行话标签的整体结果在两种候选数量下均显示标签层面区间不含零。缩写上没有可用提升，干净子集表现更差；移除与训练选项相关的标签后，不透明代码上的两个版本准确率均不高于机会水平。准确率上升的多数设置中，风险—覆盖率面积有所下降，但候选数为18时，行话和俚语上的高置信错误增加。在两个模型均未见过的数据集上，准确率分别变化−8.4和+5.6个百分点。论文还指出了四项比较两个版本时的评估风险，但摘要未具体说明其内容。

Conclusion: 多表述形式的数据扩充有助于模型适应具有语义线索的常见标签措辞，但不能据此推断模型能理解任意缩写或无意义代码。评估时应同时考察准确率、错误置信度及风险—覆盖率表现，并谨慎解释跨版本比较和跨数据集结果。

Abstract: Typed-decision models score options that the caller supplies at request time, so their accuracy depends on label wording that the model's developer does not control. We compare two releases of a 307M-parameter multilingual cross-encoder, Ru-Jev, trained on thirteen Russian-language tasks; the later release adds an augmentation that presents labels in several surface forms. Holding questions fixed and varying only how eighteen routing labels are worded, we evaluate ten label vocabularies, produced by three separate processes and checked for overlap with the training data, with five candidates per question and, for eight of them, also with eighteen. On held-out vocabularies made of ordinary or borrowed words (English-derived jargon, office slang, department names), the augmented release is more accurate in every setting, by up to 0.24. Eleven of these fourteen gains survive a Holm correction (slang with eighteen candidates and descriptive sentences with five do not), and for jargon on all labels the label-level interval excludes zero in both settings. On abbreviations and opaque codes it gives no usable gain: on abbreviations it is not better, and worse on the clean slice, and on opaque codes both releases stay at or below chance once labels related to training options are removed. In most cells where accuracy rises, the risk-coverage area falls, but with eighteen candidates confident errors rise on jargon and slang. On two datasets neither release saw, accuracy moves by -8.4 and +5.6 points. We also report four evaluation hazards from comparing the two releases.

</details>

### [63] [Formalizing inverse problems in the age of AI: possibilities, pitfalls, and an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06962)
*Christian Clason,Tapio Helin,Medet Nursultanov,Lauri Oksanen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍了一个由人工智能代理自动形式化的莱恩数学库，涵盖经典线性正则化理论及其所依赖的泛函分析，并强调审查重点应放在形式化陈述是否准确表达原意上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手可以将数学证明核验到公理层面，而人工智能代理显著降低了编写形式化证明的成本。但机器核验只能保证证明对应的形式化陈述成立，不能保证该陈述确实表达了数学家原本想说的内容。因此，核心挑战从检查证明转向核对陈述的含义。

Method: 构建一个莱恩数学库，将经典线性正则化理论及相关泛函分析结果自动形式化。库的组织方式面向人工审查：审阅者只需比较形式化陈述与其自然语言版本，无须阅读证明；同时通过针对陈述的简单自动化测试发现潜在问题。

Result: 作者报告，实际风险主要存在于定理陈述而非证明过程中。针对陈述的自动化测试能够揭示问题，包括一则教科书定理的假设条件实际上比原文声称的更严格。

Conclusion: 形式化数学的可靠性不仅取决于证明是否通过机器核验，也取决于形式化陈述是否忠实反映预期内容。该工作提供了可扩展的基础库和以陈述审查为中心的流程，并邀请反问题领域研究者继续扩充和完善该库。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean check mathematical proofs down to the axioms, and AI agents have recently made writing such proofs cheap. We describe AutoIP, a Lean library in which classical linear regularization theory, together with the functional analysis it rests on, has been autoformalized by AI agents. A machine-checked proof certifies only the formal statement it proves, so the remaining human task is to confirm that each formal statement says what was intended. We organize the library around this task: a reviewer compares the formal statements with their natural-language counterparts and never needs to read a proof. Our experience is that the risks lie in the statements rather than in the proofs, and that simple automated tests on the statements can expose problems even in the established literature. We illustrate this with a textbook theorem whose hypothesis is more restrictive than asserted. We invite the inverse problems community to extend and build on the library.

</details>


### [64] [KDASO: Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization for Liability Adjudication Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06972)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Weiming Wu,Jie Meng,Zhen Peng,Shan Huang,Qun Li,Guobing Wu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由知识与数据双重驱动的自动技能优化方法，使责任判定系统能够持续更新技能库、缩小执行偏差，并在有限上下文中高效选择技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 责任判定需要将事件描述与专业规则相结合。现有大语言模型技能方案面临三项局限：技能库难以跟上现实场景变化；模型执行技能时可能偏离专家意图；技能数量增长会超出上下文容量，导致关键技能遗漏。

Method: 提出知识与数据双重驱动的自动技能优化框架。该框架从近期判定数据中结合邻域信息归纳新技能；分析大语言模型的执行轨迹并据此修订技能，使技能描述与实际执行更一致；同时依据上下文预算进行技能路由，以支持规模化选择。

Result: 在网约车责任判定、法律责任认定和逻辑推理判断任务上的实验表明，该方法显著优于通用及领域专用基线。将技能迁移至概念相近场景时，性能提升百分之八点九六；持续优化过程中也保持稳定增益。

Conclusion: 该方法通过持续归纳、执行对齐和预算约束下的技能选择，提升了责任判定技能库的时效性、可靠性与可扩展性。实验结果还显示，生成技能具有较强的跨场景迁移能力，并能在持续优化中保持稳定表现。

Abstract: Liability adjudication tasks (e.g., legal charge determination) require analyzing event descriptions against domain-specific skills to determine whether a party should bear liability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in this area by leveraging executable skills for interpretable reasoning, existing approaches face three critical limitations: skill libraries become stale as real-world scenarios dynamically evolve, skill descriptions often suffer from a semantic execution gap where LLMs deviate from intended expert logic, and growing skill collections exceed context budgets causing critical skill omission. To address these challenges, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization (KDASO), which continuously evolves a skill library through neighborhood-conditioned induction from recent adjudication data, execution-aligned refinement based on LLM trajectory analysis, and context-budgeted routing for scalable skill selection. Experiments across ride-hailing adjudication, legal liability determination, and logical reasoning judgment demonstrate that KDASO significantly outperforms current general-purpose and domain-specific baselines. KDASO further achieves 8.96% improvement when transferring skills to concept-similar scenarios and maintains consistent performance gains through continuous optimization, underscoring the high transferability and stability of generated skills.

</details>


### [65] [BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977)
*Xiuyuan Qi,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Conde,Shanshan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 块锁在保留批量预填充共享计算的同时，仅固定受保护请求所经历的数值计算路径；在规定的部署契约下，即使同批其他请求变化，目标请求的输出仍可按位保持一致，并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现的大语言模型推理要求同一请求不因批次中其他请求变化而改变输出。现有方案要么要求所有算子采用批次不变实现，增加实现负担；要么隔离预填充并使用固定形状解码，牺牲预填充共享计算且产生解码填充开销。

Method: 提出块锁，通过固定受保护请求所见的计算几何结构来约束其数值路径，同时允许共享批量预填充。行模式采用固定宽度的行，并保留所有请求的批量解码；打包模式采用末端锚定打包与隔离的虚拟分段，随后提取受保护请求的键值状态，仅对目标请求解码。两种模式均依赖明确的部署契约。

Result: 在五种稠密模型、英伟达三种单卡部署，以及微型基准测试和函数调用基准上进行评测；另报告了双卡张量并行的初步实验。测试中，两种模式均保持目标输出序列和前二十名对数几率的位级一致。相较批次不变基线，在交付工作量相同的条件下，行模式将延迟降低百分之五十三点五至百分之五十五点零，并将吞吐量提高百分之一百一十五点二至百分之一百二十二点二。该方法无需定制算子实现，且只约束受保护请求。

Conclusion: 块锁通过局部固定目标请求的计算路径，避免了对全部算子的统一限制，并兼顾批量预填充效率与推理可复现性。其一致性保证是在特定部署契约下成立的；论文报告的结论主要由所测模型、硬件和工作负载支持。

Abstract: Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

</details>


### [66] [Resource-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Post-Training through Elastic Expert Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06984)
*Jia Wei,Ziyi Qiu,Yi Xu,Xuehai Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向混合专家模型强化学习训练的调度系统，可在不影响进行中响应所需专家计算的前提下提前释放空闲工作资源，从而提高响应生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练依赖大量响应生成，计算开销显著。在混合专家模型中，较早完成响应的工作资源可能因仍需为其他较长响应提供专家计算而无法释放；基线中约三分之一的资源占用时间发生在本地响应生成结束之后，造成资源浪费。

Method: 该系统联合调整响应调度与活跃工作资源组。它利用历史响应长度和显存约束来安排批次及提示词放置，并在确保进行中响应仍可获得所需专家计算、通信过程正确的同时，逐步释放已不再需要的工作资源。

Result: 与基线系统相比，该方法使单位资源占用时间生成的响应词元数提高约百分之六十五点四，使响应生成吞吐量提高百分之五十二点七四；实验中观察到的强化学习回报变化较小。

Conclusion: 通过减少响应生成阶段的资源闲置，该系统提高了强化学习后训练的计算效率，有望加快大语言模型及人工智能智能体的开发与迭代。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training improves the capabilities of large language models, but generating training responses is a major computational bottleneck. In mixture-of-experts models, workers that finish their assigned responses may remain occupied because their experts are still needed by workers processing longer responses. Consequently, approximately one-third of occupied worker time in the baseline occurs after local response generation finishes. Here we present FlexFloor, a system that enables earlier resource release during synchronous response generation while preserving the expert computation required by ongoing responses. FlexFloor jointly adapts response scheduling and the active group of workers. It uses historical response lengths and memory constraints to guide batching and prompt placement, then progressively releases workers while maintaining expert availability and correct communication. Compared with the verl baseline, FlexFloor generates approximately 65.40% more response tokens per unit of occupied computing time and increases response generation throughput by 52.74%, with little observed variation in rollout reward. By making reinforcement learning post-training more efficient, FlexFloor supports faster development and iterative improvement of large language models and AI agents.

</details>


### [67] [The Two-Ledger Model: Transaction-Grain Cost Accounting for Multi-Agent AI Systems Across Clouds, Models, and Systems of Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06989)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出双账本模型，将结算级账单与调用级用量记录关联起来，从而把人工智能成本归因到具体业务交易，并支持对账、发现未计费支出和实时成本控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业的人工智能业务横跨云服务、大语言模型、编排系统和业务记录系统，但财务账单通常只按产品规格和计费周期汇总。由于服务商看不到企业内部的订单、理赔或案件边界，现有账单无法回答单笔业务交易实际消耗了多少人工智能成本。统一账单格式虽改善了跨服务商的数据一致性，却没有解决结算粒度与业务归因粒度不匹配的问题。

Method: 提出双账本模型：结算账本保存符合开放成本与使用规范的账单数据，归因账本保存基于开放遥测人工智能数据构建的调用级用量记录。通过分层关联链，依次利用追踪上下文、智能体间通信及模型上下文协议元数据、业务键和概率匹配，将记录归属到业务交易，并记录关联方法与置信度。再以带版本的费率卡近实时计价，按周期将两本账与发票核对；未解释的差额用于识别未纳管的人工智能支出。论文还提出将调用级归因账本标准化为一种规范扩展。

Result: 基于已知真实值的模拟显示：即使追踪信息传递降至零，分层关联链仍可使成本覆盖率保持在百分之九十九以上；对账差额能够以不超过零点二四个百分点的偏差找回预先植入的未计量支出，并可标记服务商账单错误。交易执行期间的成本门控可使第九十九百分位交易成本最多降低百分之六十一，误暂停率为百分之二至四。

Conclusion: 双账本及分层关联机制能够弥补账单结算粒度与企业业务粒度之间的缺口，使人工智能成本可按订单、理赔或业务结果核算，并为实时成本控制、异常发现和跨服务商标准化提供基础。论文结果来自模拟，实际部署效果仍需在真实业务环境中验证。

Abstract: Enterprises run business transactions through multi-agent AI systems spanning clouds, large language models (LLMs), orchestration harnesses, and systems of record. Finance receives AI cost only at settlement grain, aggregated by SKU and charge period. The business question (what did this order, claim, or case cost in AI?) cannot be answered from billing data, because providers never see enterprise transaction boundaries. The FinOps Open Cost and Usage Specification (FOCUS) gives billing data one schema across providers but remains settlement-grain even as it adds AI token and requester attribution. We formalize this grain mismatch, derive seven requirements, and propose the Two-Ledger Model: a settlement ledger of FOCUS-conformant billing data joined to an attribution ledger of invocation-grain usage records built on OpenTelemetry GenAI telemetry. A layered correlation spine (trace context, A2A/MCP metadata, business-key correlation, probabilistic matching) assigns each record to its business transaction with recorded method and confidence; a versioned rate card prices usage in near real time; period reconciliation ties both ledgers to the invoice, and the unexplained residual identifies shadow AI. Costs are reported per order, claim, or outcome with agentic cost metrics. A simulation with known ground truth shows the spine sustaining cost coverage above 99% as trace propagation falls to zero, the reconciliation residual recovering planted unmetered spend within 0.24 percentage points while flagging provider billing errors, and mid-flight gating cutting p99 transaction cost by up to 61% at a 2-4% false-pause rate. We propose the attribution ledger as an invocation-grain FOCUS profile for standardization.

</details>


### [68] [Autonomous AI Agents Systematically Diverge From Human Preferences in Proof Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06995)
*Bálint Gyevnár,Simon DeDeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较人工智能代理与人类在形式化数学证明过程中的注意力和行动偏好，发现两者存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能生成的证明日益增多，但要让人类理解和参与这些证明，需要弄清人工智能代理与人类在证明过程中关注什么、倾向采取什么行动。论文旨在揭示双方协作中的差异及其可能造成的盲点。

Method: 作者提出“模拟协作”方法，考察人工智能代理在证明构造中的关注对象，以及其下一步行动偏好与人类偏好的差异。分析数据来自九个大型人类协作项目，涵盖形式化证明系统中的约四百七十万个定义和六千多次提交。

Result: 研究发现：证明构造过程中，人工智能代理比人类更容易被明确标出的待补空缺吸引；相对回避连接密集的证明核心，也较少偏好复杂或出人意料的证明目标；但它们较容易被出人意料的证明内容吸引，并倾向于将其规整为较常见的形式。

Conclusion: 人工智能代理与人类在证明过程中的注意力分配和行动选择并不一致。认识这些差异有助于识别人机协作的盲点，并据此设计改进措施，使人工智能更好地支持人类理解和推进数学证明。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems have ushered in a new age of proof abundance, but helping humans understand these proofs requires new attention to the differences in how AI agents and humans pursue a proof-making task. We present "simulated collaboration", a methodology that allows us to examine two key questions: (1) the relative attention AI agents pay to surface syntax or, conversely, deeper underlying semantics, and (2) how and where AI agent preferences for what to do next diverge from human preferences. On a set of nine major human collaborations in Lean with 4.7M definitions across more than 6K commits, we find that, over the course of proof construction, AI agents (1) are far more strongly drawn than humans to explicitly marked gaps; (2) show a relative aversion to densely connected proof centers and to complex or surprising proof goals; and (3) are relatively attracted to surprising proof bodies, which they tend to regularize toward baseline. Our results highlight important blind spots and potential interventions for future human-AI collaboration in mathematics.

</details>


### [69] [Trust Responsive Runtime Authorization for Agentic AI with Empirical Characterization of Policy Behavior and Parameter Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06996)
*Ahsan Habib Shihab,Imre Felde,Ramis Akhmedov,Stefan Gubo,Berik Sabdenaliyev,Madina Mansurova,Amir Mosavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出并实测一种根据运行状态调整自主智能体操作权限的策略。它在突发退化时能迅速收紧权限、恢复后较快重新授权；但面对渐进退化时反应明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体的运行状态估计如何转化为实际操作权限，通常缺乏明确规定和实证评估。研究旨在检验一种将信任、运行稳定性与情境风险综合为准备度评分，并据此调整权限的策略。

Method: 研究将信任响应式授权策略与静态、仅依据信任、仅依据风险以及对称式策略进行比较。该策略采用非对称规则：准备度连续高于权限边界并达到额外裕度后才升级权限；准备度急剧下降时则立即限制权限。研究在同一组一百条蒙特卡洛轨迹上进行比较，并开展参数单因素测试；另在策略设定冻结后，探索退化分布于五至二十个时间间隔时的表现。

Result: 在突发退化情形下，该策略能在退化开始时限制权限，并比对称式策略更早恢复授权；在自适应策略中，其稳定性最高，且描述性波动最小。不过，恢复后有更多次授权升级发生在所选信任阈值以下。成对自助法区间显示其余差异不包含零。单因素测试表明，升级裕度和所需连续观测次数增加会延长恢复时间，评分权重会改变恢复时间，而限制阈值不会改变恢复时间。渐进退化时，该策略仅在一百条轨迹中有四条因单个时间间隔的下降超过阈值而限制权限；总体上，在二十个退化间隔中，有十五点九至十八点九个间隔的授权高于准备度所支持的水平，而突发退化时为零点八个间隔，对称式策略约为十个间隔。

Conclusion: 该策略的优势依赖退化是否突发：面对急剧恶化，它能及时限制权限并较快恢复；面对渐进恶化，限制权限的效果显著减弱，可能长时间维持超出准备度所支持水平的授权。因此，实际应用需评估退化速度，并谨慎设定评分权重、升级裕度及连续观测要求。研究报告的数值可追溯至公开且带版本记录的研究发布材料。

Abstract: The policy that converts runtime estimates of an autonomous agent's condition into operational authority is rarely specified or measured. We empirically characterize the Trust Responsive Authorization Policy (TRAP), which maps an Authorization Readiness Score (ARS), a weighted combination of runtime trust, operational stability, and contextual risk, onto discrete authorization levels through an asymmetric rule: escalation requires consecutive observations above a band edge plus a margin, while a sharp drop in readiness restricts authority at once. On one matched set of 100 Monte Carlo traces, we compare TRAP with static, trust-only, risk-only, and symmetric policies. Under abrupt degradation, TRAP restricts at onset and re-escalates sooner after recovery than the symmetric policy, with the highest and, descriptively, least variable stability among the adaptive policies, although more of its post-recovery escalations occur below the selected trust threshold. Paired bootstrap intervals exclude zero for the remaining differences. In one-factor sweeps, recovery time rises with the escalation margin and run length, shifts with the score weights, and is unchanged by the restriction threshold. In an exploratory post-freeze experiment, when the same degradation is spread over 5-20 intervals, TRAP restricts only on the 4 traces that retain a single-interval drop above that threshold; averaged over all 100 traces, it holds authority above what readiness supports for 15.9-18.9 of the 20 degradation intervals, against 0.8 under abrupt degradation and about 10 for the symmetric policy. Its restriction advantage therefore depends on degradation being abrupt. All reported values are traceable to the publicly available, versioned research release.

</details>


### [70] [Adapting the Process of Adaptation: Darwinian, Lamarckian, and Agent-Directed Evolution in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07048)
*Andrew W. Lo,Chaoyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立理论框架，比较自然选择、遗传学习与智能体自主引导改进三种演化方式。自主搜索能加快适应，但若评价目标设定错误，也会更快地优化错误行为，因此需要独立评估与有效监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能智能体逐渐能够修改并评估自身行为，智能可能开始主动引导自身演化。本文关注这类改进可以有多快，以及如何确保改进过程安全、方向可控。

Method: 作者构建理论框架，研究环境发生变化后，三种演化机制的适应过程：依靠自然选择的达尔文式演化、加入可遗传学习的拉马克式演化，以及能够生成和评估行为变体、并自主决定搜索程度的智能体引导演化。框架还考察学习能力、问题难度、变体数量与实验成本如何影响适应速度和最优搜索投入。

Result: 在学习足够有效时，三种机制呈现出逐步加快的适应趋势；生成更多行为变体通常能够加速适应，而最优搜索投入取决于学习能力、问题难度和实验成本。若评价目标设定有误，增加搜索会使智能体更快收敛到错误行为，即使测得的表现有所提升。递归式自我改进还可能让这类偏差延续至后续世代。

Conclusion: 智能体能够自主引导改进，并不意味着改进方向可靠。人工智能安全的关键不仅是提升智能演化速度，还要确保评价目标正确，并让独立评估与监督能力跟上自主发展，使人类能够引导其方向、限制其速度，并在必要时介入。

Abstract: Evolution has shaped intelligence for billions of years. Artificial intelligence (AI) now raises a striking possibility: intelligence may begin to direct its own evolution. As AI agents become capable of modifying and evaluating their own behavior, how quickly could they improve, and how can that improvement remain safe? We develop a theoretical framework comparing Darwinian, Lamarckian, and agent-directed evolution following environmental change. Darwinian evolution relies on natural selection, Lamarckian evolution adds inherited learning, and agent-directed evolution allows agents to generate and evaluate behavioral variants and choose how extensively to search. When learning is sufficiently effective, these mechanisms exhibit progressively faster adaptation. Generating more variants accelerates adaptation, while optimal search effort depends on learning ability, problem difficulty, and experimentation costs. However, an agent's ability to direct its improvement does not ensure that improvements are evaluated against the intended objective. When the evaluation target is misspecified, additional search accelerates convergence toward the wrong behavior, even as measured performance improves. In recursive self-improvement, such errors may persist across generations, posing challenges for AI safety. Independent evaluation and oversight must keep pace with autonomous AI development. The central challenge is not simply to accelerate the evolution of intelligence, but to preserve humanity's ability to guide its direction, limit its speed, and intervene when necessary.

</details>


### [71] [Closed-Loop Agent Evaluation: Layered Measurement and Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07051)
*Ruifeng Ren,Gun Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向已部署智能体的闭环评估方法：将评估结论追溯到原子证据，并通过审计与回归检查持续改进评估器和智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
