These results suggest that trainable prefix tokens provide a lightweight way to improve retrieval representations.

</details>


### [140] [CacheGraft: Enhancing Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Latent-Space Document Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07393)
*Vihan Lakshman,Peter Baile Chen,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 缓存嫁接将较大模型与较小模型之间融合后的键值缓存用于检索增强生成，在五个问答基准上几乎始终优于纯文本检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往缓存到缓存的转换主要用于智能体实时通信。本文提出，这种在潜在空间中传递信息的能力也可用于检索增强生成：将文档编码为小模型可直接利用的缓存表示，从而在推理时无需部署较大的模型，尤其适合计算资源受限的场景。

Method: 系统离线处理语料中的每段文本，利用一个较大的共享模型与一个较小的接收模型，将段落编码并融合为键值缓存。查询时，把相关缓存注入小模型，由小模型生成答案；较大模型无需参与在线推理。融合器未使用检索数据进行训练，因此评估体现了其零样本迁移能力。

Result: 在五个问答基准、两套独立的本地评审中，缓存嫁接在与纯文本接收模型基线的30项比较中有29项提升答案质量，其中17项达到统计显著。实验所用共享模型约有40亿参数，接收模型约有6亿参数。

Conclusion: 结果初步表明，利用潜在空间中的文档表示丰富小模型输入，是提升检索增强生成效果的一条可行路径。该方法有望在推理阶段只能部署小模型的资源受限环境中发挥价值；不过，现有证据仍属初步，后续还需在更多模型、数据集与部署条件下验证其效果。

Abstract: Recent work in machine learning has demonstrated that transformer-based language model agents can communicate state not only through text, but also directly in latent space via key-value (KV) caches. In this paper, we argue that such cache-to-cache translation can provide utility beyond the original motivating application of real-time agent communication and can instead be utilized for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Building on the Cache-to-Cache (C2C) fusion framework, our proposed system CacheGraft encodes each corpus passage offline into a fused KV cache representation between a larger sharer model (4B parameters in our experiments) and a smaller receiver model (0.6B parameters) such that this cache can be injected into the receiver at query time with no need for the larger model. Across five QA benchmarks and two independent locally-run judges, CacheGraft improves answer quality over the text-only receiver baseline in 29 out of 30 comparisons, of which 17 of the 30 are statistically significant. Notably, these gains are zero-shot with respect to the fuser, which was never trained on retrieval data. These initial results suggest that latent-space document enrichment may be a promising avenue for enhancing the performance of RAG systems, particularly in compute-constrained settings where it is only feasible to deploy a small model at inference time.

</details>


### [141] [BRIDGE: Bridging the Semantic Quantization Gap for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07425)
*Yatong Xiao,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“桥接”方法，针对生成式推荐中量化语义信息与解码器输入之间的落差，利用码本语义锚点、邻域感知自蒸馏和分层计算分配提升推荐效果，并在离线基准和线上实验中取得收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐先将物品量化为分层语义标识序列，再由解码器逐层生成。然而，量化过程依赖码本中心、局部邻域结构和各层熵等丰富信息，解码器却只接收离散标识，因而丢失了塑造这些标识的语义结构。论文将这一问题称为“语义量化落差”，并指出其表现为语义锚点不足、物品邻域关系在逐标识监督中丢失，以及各层计算投入未考虑生成难度差异。

Method: 论文提出“桥接”方法，包含三项设计：一是将冻结量化器的码本中心作为语义锚点，在生成语义标识前注入潜在推理；二是通过带有指数移动平均机制的特权自蒸馏，将教师模型的邻域知识迁移给按自身生成结果训练的学生模型，以保留物品间几何关系并缓解暴露偏差；三是采用难度感知的循环计算，在较难的前层投入更多计算，并随生成深度增加逐步减少计算。

Result: 在三个亚马逊数据集上，相较最强基线，方法的前十项召回率和前十项归一化折损累计增益平均分别提升百分之十点九和百分之九点七，最高分别提升百分之十二点六和百分之十点七。在线上大型电商广告平台开展的一周、百分之五流量的对照实验中，每千次展示收入提升百分之零点六五。

Conclusion: 研究表明，显式补回量化过程中被解码器忽略的语义锚点、邻域结构和难度信息，能够同时改善生成式推荐的离线排序指标与线上商业效果。

Abstract: Generative recommendation retrieves items by autoregressively generating Semantic IDs(SIDs), where an RQ-VAE quantizes each item into a hierarchical SID sequence that the decoder generates level by level. We identify an overlooked bottleneck in this paradigm: the Semantic Quantization Gap(SQG). Quantization derives SIDs from rich continuous signals, including codebook centroids, local neighborhood geometry, and level-wise entropy. However, the decoder receives only discrete identifiers and loses the semantic structure that shaped them. This mismatch manifests in three ways: a grounding gap, where the decoder lacks semantic anchors when target-side information is limited; a neighborhood gap, where token-level supervision discards inter-item similarity and exacerbates exposure bias; and a difficulty gap, where computation is allocated uniformly despite generation difficulty diminishing with depth.
  To bridge the SQG, we propose BRIDGE. Codebook-grounded latent reasoning injects frozen quantizer centroids as semantic anchors before SID generation. Privileged EMA self-distillation transfers neighborhood-aware knowledge from a teacher to an on-policy student, preserving inter-item geometry while mitigating exposure bias. Effort-aware recurrence allocates more computation to the challenging early levels and progressively reduces it with depth. Across three Amazon benchmarks, BRIDGE improves Recall@10 and NDCG@10 by 10.9% and 9.7% on average over the strongest baseline (up to 12.6% and 10.7%). In a one-week online A/B experiment on a large-scale e-commerce advertising platform with 5% traffic, BRIDGE improves eCPM by 0.65%.

</details>


### [142] [OptST: Optimizing Semantic Tokenization for LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07523)
*Hanchong Chen,Jie Liu,Fuyuan Lyu,Dugang Liu,Lingjie Li,Xiuqiang He,Xing Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向推荐任务优化语义分词的方法，使语义编码既能适配下游推荐目标，也能按交互动态控制语义细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见流程先训练语义分词器，再将固定的离散编码作为推荐模型输入。推荐训练虽能学习利用这些编码，却无法调整其语义划分；离散的最近码分配使端到端适配困难，而无约束调整可能破坏预训练得到的几何结构。此外，固定的编码深度无法针对不同交互灵活提供语义分辨率。

Method: 提出“优化语义分词”方法：保持预训练编码器和基础码本不变，学习低秩形变以调整语义码分配，并通过锚定正则限制其偏离预训练结构。训练时利用直通估计器将推荐监督传递至形变模块，预测时仍采用硬最近码分配。方法还根据具体交互选择编码前缀，从而动态控制提供给推荐模型的残差信息量。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，该方法的曲线下面积指标优于传统基线和大语言模型增强基线；优势也能在不同推荐模型骨干上保持。

Conclusion: 实验表明，让语义分词面向下游任务适配，并依据交互调整语义细节，是连接大语言模型生成的用户画像与推荐模型的有效方式。

Abstract: Semantic information can enrich the identifiers and structured fields used by recommender systems. Semantic tokenization provides a structured representation of this information by converting LLM-derived user profiles into discrete tokens. In the common two-stage pipeline, the tokenizer is trained before the recommender. Its token assignments are then treated as fixed input features during recommendation training. As a result, the downstream recommender can learn how to use the assigned indices, but it cannot revise the semantic partition that produced them. Making this partition task-aware is difficult because nearest-code assignment is discrete, and unconstrained adaptation may also distort the geometry learned during pretraining. Moreover, a fixed token depth exposes the same semantic resolution to every interaction. We thus propose OptST (Optimizing Semantic Tokenization), which makes a pretrained tokenizer responsive to downstream recommendation. To revise token assignments without directly updating the pretrained encoder or base codebooks, OptST learns a low-rank deformation whose drift is limited by an anchor regularizer. Recommendation supervision reaches this transformation through a straight-through estimator, while prediction retains hard nearest-code assignments. Beyond adapting the assignments themselves, OptST selects an interaction-conditioned prefix to control how much residual detail is exposed to the recommender. Experiments on four public datasets show that OptST achieves the highest AUC among conventional and LLM-enhanced baselines. Its improvements also persist across different recommendation backbones. These results establish task-aware semantic tokenization as an effective interface between LLM-derived profiles and downstream recommendation models.

</details>


### [143] [What Gets Hacked in a Collaborative Reward for LLM Recommenders: The Part That Does Not Depend on the User](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07588)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Liang Dong,Yu Wu,Dailin Li,Yu Xia,Yunhan C. Zhang,Rui Cai,Yi Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文发现，协同过滤奖励中的物品平均得分会使语言模型推荐过度集中于少数物品；通过按物品中心化或标准化奖励，可显著改善推荐多样性，同时保持相近的排序准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同过滤模型本身能为不同用户提供多样的高排名推荐，但以其得分训练语言模型推荐器时，模型反而会集中推荐更少的物品，造成曝光坍缩。论文旨在找出这种现象的成因，并在不明显损害准确率的前提下提升物品覆盖度与曝光公平性。

Method: 作者将每个协同过滤得分分解为物品平均分量（该物品在用户历史上的平均得分）和残差分量（当前用户得分相对物品平均分的偏差），并通过受控干预检验各分量的影响。针对导致曝光坍缩的物品平均分量，提出两种奖励修正：按物品中心化，即减去估计的物品平均得分；按物品标准化，即在中心化后进一步依据估计标准差缩放残差。

Result: 在两个数据集和两种标识符方案上的实验表明，两种修正都能显著提高目录覆盖度和曝光均匀性。与使用未修正协同过滤奖励训练相比，不同用户排名第一的独特物品数量最高提升至原来的九点八倍，同时排序准确率保持相近。

Conclusion: 物品平均得分是协同过滤奖励引发曝光坍缩的重要因素。去除或校正这一分量，能够在基本维持推荐准确率的同时，让语言模型推荐器呈现更广泛、更均衡的物品曝光。

Abstract: Collaborative-filtering (CF) scores provide graded, personalized rewards for training large language model (LLM) recommenders through reinforcement learning. However, these rewards can cause exposure collapse: even though the CF model itself produces diverse top recommendations across users, an LLM optimized with its rewards concentrates on fewer items. We decompose each score into an item-mean component, the item's average CF score across user histories, and a residual component, the current user's score deviation from the average. Through controlled interventions, we identify the item-mean component as a contributor to exposure collapse. Accordingly, we propose two reward corrections: per-item centering removes each item's estimated mean score, while per-item standardization further rescales the residual using its estimated standard deviation. Experiments on two datasets with two identifier schemes show that both corrections substantially improve catalog coverage and exposure evenness. Compared with training on the uncorrected CF reward, our corrections increase the number of distinct items ranked first across users by up to $9.8\times$, while maintaining comparable ranking accuracy. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LLM4Rec-CF-Bias-27EF/.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Independent Predictions: Learning the Browsing Journey for Sequence-Level Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07591)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jingxin Hai,Zhenjia Ma,Hongbin Pei,Dagui Chen,Han Zhu,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种联合建模展示、曝光与点击过程的重排评估方法，改善候选序列的效用估计；线上测试显示点击量提升百分之零点七，收入提升百分之三点六七。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排评估器通常把用户行为拆解为各商品相互独立的预测，例如点击率。这忽略了用户必须先浏览到商品并产生曝光才可能点击，也忽略了有限注意力下商品之间的竞争，因而会造成系统性评估偏差，并导致序列选择不理想。

Method: 提出联合行为建模框架，显式描述商品从展示、曝光到点击的过程。具体而言，模型预测用户浏览深度分布，以估计各位置的曝光概率；利用预测曝光抑制未曝光商品带来的虚假上下文信息；并通过点击分布匹配目标捕捉同一序列内的行为依赖。该方法可作为即插即用模块，接入不同推荐模型。

Result: 多种模型骨干上的离线实验验证了方法的有效性与稳健性。淘宝应用为期十四天的线上对照实验中，点击量提升百分之零点七，收入提升百分之三点六七；该方法还已在全流量环境部署。

Conclusion: 准确评估重排序列需要考虑曝光与点击之间的因果关系，以及序列内商品对用户注意力的竞争。联合建模能够弥补独立商品预测的不足，并在离线实验和真实线上业务中带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Re-ranking aims to present users with an ordered list that maximizes sequence-level utility. Industrial deployments widely adopt the Generator--Evaluator framework, where the generator proposes candidate sequences, and the evaluator scores them to select the best one for display. The fidelity of the evaluator's utility estimates thus directly governs recommendation quality. However, existing evaluators predominantly follow the \textbf{Independent Behavior Modeling (IBM)} paradigm, which simplifies the continuous user \emph{browsing journey} into independent per-item predictions (e.g., CTR). This oversimplification ignores two physical realities: (i) a click can occur only after the item has been reached and impressed, and (ii) items compete for finite user attention. As a result, IBM produces systematically biased evaluations and suboptimal sequence selection. To address this, we propose a \textbf{Joint Behavior Modeling (JBM)} perspective that explicitly models the \emph{display} $\rightarrow$ \emph{impression} $\rightarrow$ \emph{click} process to capture causal and competitive dependencies within a sequence. Building on JBM, we develop the \textbf{Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)}, a plug-and-play framework that (i) predicts a browsing-depth distribution to estimate per-position impression probabilities, (ii) leverages predicted impressions to suppress spurious context from unexposed items during click modeling, and (iii) introduces a click distribution matching objective to capture intra-sequence dependencies. Extensive offline experiments across diverse backbones, together with a 14-day online A/B test in the Taobao App (+0.70\% clicks, +3.67\% revenue) and full-traffic deployment, validate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [145] [MEMOIR: A Training-Free Memory Lifecycle for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07631)
*Mayank Anand,Subham Raj,Aman Vaibhav Jha,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的长程智能体记忆管理流程：压缩前检查证据是否保留，回答后根据记忆的实际贡献调整检索优先级，并探索较少检索的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统通常将记忆压缩与检索分开处理，写入时设定的重要性分数在使用后不会更新。这可能导致压缩时丢失未来问题所需的证据，也可能让检索持续偏向过时或频繁出现的记忆。

Method: 方法名为“基于合并认证与在线重要性修订的记忆证据”。压缩前通过筛查判断答案相关证据是否得到保留；每次回答后归因分析检索到的记忆对答案的支持程度，并据此调整其检索优先级；同时加入基于置信上界的探索机制，提升较少曝光记忆的检索机会。该流程无需训练重排器，在线写入阶段也不增加大语言模型调用，合并筛查仅在定期离线整理时进行。

Result: 在去重后的洛科莫问答数据集（共1905组）上，完整检索流程相较于单纯余弦相似度检索，证据召回率前五提升11.92个百分点；带置信门控的反馈在无需参考答案或训练重排器的情况下，整体再提升1.50个百分点。不可部署的理想预言机整体提升1.84个百分点，在多跳问题上提升8.05个百分点。门控合并使文本证据召回率从58.82%升至59.08%，活动存储规模减少1.42%；100次独立审查中未观察到证据损失，对应的单侧95%损失率上界为2.95%。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种实用的记忆生命周期管理方案：使用后追溯记忆的实际价值，压缩前筛查证据能否保留。实验结果表明，该方案可改善证据检索，并以较低存储缩减幅度控制合并风险；不过，有限的审查样本仍意味着不能据此断定损失风险为零。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that preserve useful information across sessions and retrieve it when needed. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: memories are written prospectively with static importance scores that are never revised after use. This creates two coupled failure modes: consolidation can silently destroy evidence that future queries require, while retrieval continues to favor stale memories. We propose MEMOIR (Memory Evidence via Merge-certified, Online Importance Revision), a training-free memory lifecycle that unifies judge-gated consolidation with retrospective attribution. Before compressing memory, screening gates test whether answer-relevant evidence is preserved. After each query, attribution estimates which retrieved memories supported the answer and updates their retrieval priority. A UCB-style exploration bonus additionally encourages retrieval of under-exposed memories, reducing the risk that frequently retrieved memories crowd out useful but less-seen evidence. On 1,905 deduplicated LoCoMo QA pairs, the full retrieval stack improves evidence recall@5 by 11.92 percentage points over bare cosine, with confidence-gated feedback adding 1.50 points overall without reference answers or reranker training; a deployment-unavailable oracle adds 1.84 points overall and 8.05 points on multi-hop. Judge-gated merge changes the recall of text-based evidence from 58.82% to 59.08%, reduces the active store by 1.42%, and incurs 0 observed losses in 100 independently audited merges, with a one-sided 95% upper loss-rate bound of 2.95%. Together, these results outline a practical deployment path: no additional LLM call on the online write path, with merge screening confined to periodic offline consolidation. The lifecycle reduces to one principle: attribute usefulness after use, and screen for preservation before compression.

</details>


### [146] [Probabilistic Relevance Decisions for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08014)
*Bahadır Tatar,Cevat Şener*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在科学事实核查基准上，相关性判定重排提升了稠密检索效果；在本实验设置下，类型安全的判定方案表现最佳，但结果不足以证明其普遍优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索可提供可复用的语料表示，但其排序主要依赖查询与文档的语义相似度；概率式相关性判定则能针对具体查询评估文档。研究旨在比较两类方法及其组合，考察相关性判定能否作为稠密检索的有效补充。

Method: 在官方科学事实核查测试基准上评估三百个查询，比较仅使用稠密检索的ＢＧＥ－Ｍ３，以及采用类型安全判定和开放人工智能判定的重排方案。实验使用相同的、经正例增强的一百篇文档候选池，并另行进行全语料稠密检索后重排，候选深度设为五十和一百；全语料检索结果未注入正例。研究还采用配对自助法估计深度差异的不确定性，并分析精确零分数导致的并列排序敏感性、调用成本与延迟。

Result: 在全语料检索、候选深度为五十时，ＢＧＥ－Ｍ３的十位归一化折损累计增益为０．６４１３，开放人工智能判定重排后为０．７５４５，类型安全判定重排后为０．７８０５；类型安全方案在所测条件下取得最高的五位召回率和十位归一化折损累计增益。深度从五十增加到一百提高了已知正例进入候选集的召回率，但最终效果变化较小且不确定；四项配对自助法区间均包含零。独立条件下的接口评分成本约翻倍，兼容的缓存判定可降低增量支出。开放人工智能方案产生许多精确零分数，随机化并列项的次要分析显示排序可能受其影响。前一百项延迟混合了缓存与新执行；历史混合延迟则来自不同运行环境和计时边界，不能直接等同比较。

Conclusion: 结果支持在该实验协议中，将相关性判定作为稠密检索的补充，但分析属于未经调整的探索性比较。现有证据不能确立相关性判定的一般性优势，也不能证明概率分数经过校准，或认定候选深度五十与一百的效果等同。

Abstract: Dense similarity supplies reusable corpus representations, while probabilistic relevance decisions offer query-dependent document scoring. We compare dense-only BGE-M3 with TypeSafe Jev Noul and OpenAI Decisions Predicate scores on the official BEIR SciFact test benchmark. The study evaluates 300 official test queries using identical positive-enriched 100-document pools and full-corpus dense retrieval followed by reranking at candidate depths of 50 and 100. Unlike the controlled pools, neither dense prefix receives positive injection. Jev has the highest observed Recall@5 and nDCG@10 under the evaluated conditions. At depth 50, full-corpus nDCG@10 is 0.6413 for BGE-M3, 0.7545 with OpenAI and 0.7805 with Jev. Extending depth from 50 to 100 increases known-positive candidate recall but produces small, uncertain final-effectiveness changes. All four paired-bootstrap depth intervals include zero. Unique-condition API scoring cost approximately doubles, while compatible cached decisions reduce incremental expenditure. OpenAI returns many exact-zero scores, and secondary exact-tie randomization reveals ranking sensitivity without replacing deterministic primary results. Top-100 latency mixes cached and new execution. Historical hybrid latency is a saved-component sum under different execution environments and timer boundaries. These exploratory, unadjusted analyses support relevance decisions as a complement to dense retrieval within this protocol, without establishing general superiority, probability calibration or equivalence between depths.

</details>


### [147] [Domain-specific information retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08228)
*Suzan Verberne,Florina Piroi,Allan Hanbury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文综述领域专用信息检索的任务、挑战、方法与研究方向，并指出相关进展也可应用于开放领域网络搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多信息检索活动并非简单的网页搜索，而是发生在法律、学术等特定领域，服务于具体工作任务。这些场景具有独特需求，因此需要系统梳理其任务特点、数据资源、应用方式及公平性问题。

Method: 文章按章节展开：先概述领域专用检索的常见任务、数据集与应用，再分析长文档、长查询、长交互会话和特定用户需求等挑战；随后介绍针对这些问题的方法与模型，涵盖用户因素、查询处理、用户建模和交互，并讨论模型偏差与公平性。

Result: 文章总结了适用于领域专用任务与数据的多类方法，指出领域专用信息检索中的不少方法进展能够迁移到其他领域，甚至用于开放领域的网络搜索。摘要未报告具体实验结果或量化指标。

Conclusion: 领域专用信息检索应结合领域任务特点、用户需求与交互过程进行设计，同时关注偏差和公平性。未来研究可进一步探索这些挑战及方法的适用范围，并推动相关成果在不同领域和开放网络搜索中的迁移。

Abstract: Information Retrieval (IR) is much more than ad hoc web search: a large portion of the information seeking activities takes place in specific domains, often addressing work-related tasks. Examples are lawyers finding relevant prior cases for a legal case and academic researchers studying the background material for a paper. In Chapter 2 we outline the most common domain-specific tasks, datasets and applications. Domain-specific IR has a number of challenges that make set it apart from simple web search tasks: they often deal with long documents, long queries and long sessions with specific user needs. We discuss these in Chapter 3. We also address issues related to bias and fairness of models. In Chapter 4, we describe the models and methods developed for domain-specific tasks and data, addressing the specific challenges, including user aspects related to domain-specific IR tasks, dealing with queries, user models, and user interactions. We show that many of the methodological advances in domain-specific IR are transferable to other domains and even to open-domain web search. We conclude with the main takeaways and propose research directions for the near future.

</details>


### [148] [GINGER: Spicing Up Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08306)
*Daniel Salwasser,Lars Gottesbüren,Laxman Dhulipala,Jakub Łącki,Guy Blelloch,Rajesh Jayaram,Tobias Rubel,Kshiteej Sheth,Amir Zandieh,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文介绍一款开源图式近似最近邻搜索库，兼顾高查询速度、快速索引和低内存占用，并通过量化与无损压缩提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、高维嵌入数据上的近似最近邻搜索需要在查询速度、索引构建时间与内存占用之间取得平衡。现有方案难以同时做到高吞吐、快速建索引和紧凑存储，因此论文致力于改进图搜索的整体效率。

Method: 该库采用先进的邻近图构建和向量量化技术。论文提出嵌套式两级量化表示，使同一编码既能以低精度进行图导航，也能以高精度执行候选重排；该表示在接近浮点精度的同时减少约百分之六十二的内存占用。此外，论文将图重排序与基于流式整数编码改进的自适应间隔无损编码结合，使邻近图体积最多缩小至原来的五分之二，且不降低查询吞吐量。

Result: 在百分之九十五召回率下，该库的查询吞吐量平均是各数据集最快竞争方案的约一点八倍，且至少是任何单一竞争库的约二点三倍；在高维嵌入数据上，相较紧凑型库的优势可达约二点九倍。论文还报告其索引构建速度优于现有近似最近邻搜索库，同时内存占用与最节省空间的竞争方案相当。

Conclusion: 该工作通过量化、图结构压缩和图重排序，提升了图式近似最近邻搜索的速度与空间效率。实验结果表明，这些技术可在保持高召回率和较低内存占用的同时显著提高查询性能，使该库适用于大规模、高维向量检索。

Abstract: We present \textsc{Ginger}, a state-of-the-art open-source C++ library for graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS). We developed \textsc{Ginger} at Google to push the speed and memory efficiency of graph search on large and high-dimensional embedding datasets. \textsc{Ginger} surpasses existing ANNS libraries with regards to query throughput and indexing time while matching the memory footprint of the most space-efficient competitors.
  \textsc{Ginger} builds upon recent advances in proximity graph construction (PiPNN) and vector quantization (TurboQuant). We introduce Nested TurboQuant (NTQ), a two-level quantizer which supports low precision graph navigation and high precision reranking in a single code. This representation achieves near floating point precision with $2.6\times$ less memory. We also compress the size of our proximity graphs by up to $2.5\times$ without impacting query throughput. We combine graph reordering with a new lossless compression codec called AdaptiveGap, which builds upon StreamVByte. At $95\%$ recall, \textsc{Ginger} speeds up query throughput by an average of $1.8\times$ over each dataset's fastest competitor and by at least $2.3\times$ over any single library. This lead grows to $2.9\times$ over compact libraries on high-dimensional embeddings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [SteelAgent: LLM-Orchestrated, Physics-Informed Steel Property Prediction with Audited Generalization and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06953)
*Aleksandr Volkov,Roman Sultimov,Mikhail Kuzin,Yury Maximov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，钢材性能模型在随机划分下表现良好，并不代表能够可靠预测未见过的钢种或数据来源；评估时必须说明测试对象的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机划分可能使训练集和测试集包含相近的钢种或数据来源，从而高估模型对陌生合金的外推能力。论文旨在检验测试集划分方式如何影响对抗拉强度预测性能、不确定性校准及物理特征价值的判断。

Method: 研究结合冶金描述符、机器学习模型与语言模型接口，构建用于性能预测、不确定性报告和逆向设计的钢铁智能体。作者在钢铁基准数据上比较随机划分、按钢种家族留出及按数据来源留出等评估方式，并分析梯度提升模型的预测表现、预测区间覆盖率和物理衍生特征的影响；另报告分组误差，并用合成回归实验说明留出群组时校准可能恶化。

Result: 梯度提升模型的汇总交叉验证决定系数从随机划分时的0.859降至按钢种家族留出时的0.113。名义覆盖率为90%的预测区间，实际覆盖率从随机划分时的91.0%降至按数据来源留出时的37.6%。物理衍生特征的效果取决于评估设置：在17个钢种家族留出分析中，决定系数提高0.2534；在另一个四牌号实验中则降低0.358。

Conclusion: 模型性能与不确定性评估对测试集所代表的群体高度敏感，物理特征也并非在所有场景下都能改善预测。作者建议，在报告性能估计或合规评分时，同时明确评估所针对的钢种、群体或数据来源，避免将随机划分结果误解为对陌生材料的可靠泛化能力。

Abstract: Steel property models can perform well on random test splits even when they extrapolate poorly to unfamiliar alloys. SteelAgent couples metallurgical descriptors and machine-learning models with a language-model interface for property prediction, uncertainty reporting and inverse design. We examine how the choice of held-out unit changes the conclusions drawn from SteelBench tensile-strength evaluations. For gradient boosting, pooled out-of-fold $R^2$ fell from 0.859 on a random split to 0.113 when steel families were held out. Empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals ranged from 91.0% under random splitting to 37.6% on held-out sources. The effect of physics-derived features depended on the evaluation. They increased gradient-boosting $R^2$ by 0.2534 in a 17-family holdout analysis and decreased it by 0.358 in a separate four-grade laboratory analysis. We report group-level errors and a synthetic regression experiment illustrating how calibration can deteriorate on held-out groups. These results motivate reporting the evaluation population alongside a property estimate or compliance score.

</details>


### [150] [Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956)
*Tianming Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 章鱼框架让多个智能体线程共享同一模型及显存中的键值状态图，减少分支复制与信息传递开销，并提升检索研究任务的准确率和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体会把搜索与验证过程不断累积在同一上下文中；多智能体虽可并行探索，却常复制各分支的键值缓存、通过重新编码文本摘要交换信息，并在强化学习训练中丢失分支间依赖关系。因此，论文旨在兼顾并行探索、上下文共享与有效的分支信用分配。

Method: 提出章鱼框架：多个线程共用单一模型，并通过显存中的有向无环图协调键值状态。框架提供分支、读取线程、等待线程、回退和提交五种操作。其核心机制包括：零拷贝基数树分支，以共享物理缓存页的游标视图创建分支；仅在全注意力层挂载同级线程的终端键值块，避开线性循环状态，减少无关信息劫持；以及打包式有向无环图组相对策略优化训练，将各分支放入一次块稀疏注意力计算，并用逐一移除分支后的边际奖励分配反事实信用。

Result: 在稀疏词法检索语料上，以四十亿参数模型端到端训练后，章鱼在浏览比较增强任务上准确率达49.6%，比单智能体强化学习高17.5个百分点，比基于文本摘要的多智能体强化学习高12.5个百分点；在开放研究者任务上达51.2%。其表现超过规模最大达250倍的稠密检索智能体，训练吞吐量提升3.3倍，峰值键值缓存显存降低2.8倍。仅挂载注意力层状态还将无关任务劫持降低21倍，同时保留完整文本互信息的92.2%，且不额外分配键值缓存。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将多智能体协调直接建立在共享的键值状态图上，并针对推理与训练共同设计分支、信息挂载和信用分配机制，可同时提高研究任务表现与计算效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL).
  We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read_thread, wait_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\times$ ($57.9\% \to 2.7\%$) while retaining $92.2\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards.
  Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\times$.

</details>


### [151] [Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979)
*Ismail Huseynov,Mohammad Orabe,Srikantan Nagarajan,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较两类脑电／脑磁源成像贝叶斯反演方法的定位表现与不确定性校准，并发现稀疏贝叶斯学习结合稀疏基场扩展总体优于贝叶斯最小范数结合深度加权；保序校准可进一步改善可信区域覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电与脑磁源成像不仅需要准确定位脑内信号源，也需要可信的不确定性估计。然而，经验贝叶斯反演方法给出的后验可信区域很少经过实证校准，因而其名义覆盖率未必符合实际覆盖率。研究旨在系统评估这类方法的不确定性，并探索如何改善校准。

Method: 建立基于模拟的评估框架，比较贝叶斯最小范数方法与结合稀疏基场扩展的稀疏贝叶斯学习方法。该框架使用闭式条件高斯后验摘要，并考察固定或自由方向模型、不同信噪比、源稀疏度及个体化头模型。研究还推导贝叶斯最小范数方法中源方差与噪声方差的联合证据最大化更新，比较已知噪声、基线估计噪声和自适应学习噪声，并依据经验覆盖率对可信区域水平进行保序校准。

Result: 校准前，稀疏贝叶斯学习结合稀疏基场扩展具有更低的瓦瑟斯坦定位误差、更小的后验不确定性，且覆盖率更接近名义可信水平。两种方法仍存在校准偏差；保序校准均能改善覆盖率，对后验较保守的贝叶斯最小范数方法提升更明显。校准效果迁移到信噪比、稀疏度或头模型不匹配的数据时，表现取决于具体条件。不同噪声设定下，各方法内部的重建与不确定性趋势总体一致。真实听觉脑磁数据中，稀疏贝叶斯学习结合稀疏基场扩展给出更聚焦的定位和更紧致的不确定性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，脑电与脑磁反演的不确定性需要通过经验覆盖率进行评估，而不能仅依据后验形式判断其可靠性。稀疏贝叶斯学习结合稀疏基场扩展在定位精度、后验紧致性和覆盖率方面总体占优；保序校准则是改善可信区域可靠性的有效后处理方法，但其跨条件迁移能力并非稳定不变。

Abstract: M/EEG source imaging requires accurate localization and reliable uncertainty estimates, yet posterior credible regions are rarely assessed for empirical calibration. We present a simulation-based framework for uncertainty quantification and calibration of empirical Bayesian inverse methods with closed-form conditional Gaussian posterior summaries. We compare Bayesian minimum norm (BMN) with sLORETA depth weighting and sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) with sparse basis field expansions (S-FLEX). S-FLEX allows retained coefficients to contribute nonzero marginal posterior variance at locations where voxel-wise SBL yields degenerate variances. For BMN, we derive joint source- and noise-variance updates through evidence maximization. We test whether adaptive noise learning yields near-calibrated uncertainties. Both methods retain residual miscalibration, motivating post-hoc isotonic recalibration of credible-region levels using empirical coverage. Simulations assess fixed- and free-orientation models across signal-to-noise ratios (SNR), source sparsity levels, and subject-specific head models, using oracle, baseline-estimated, and adaptively learned noise. Before recalibration, SBL+S-FLEX yields lower Wasserstein localization error, smaller posterior uncertainty, and coverage closer to nominal credibility than BMN+sLORETA. Isotonic recalibration improves both methods, with larger gains for conservative BMN+sLORETA posteriors; transfer across mismatched SNR, sparsity, and head models remains condition dependent. Noise specifications yield broadly consistent reconstruction and uncertainty trends within each estimator. In real auditory MEG data, SBL+S-FLEX provides more focal localization and tighter uncertainties than BMN+sLORETA, suggesting greater statistical power for identifying bilateral auditory sources.

</details>


### [152] [Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988)
*Rishit Jain,Rishit Kar,Chetashri Bhadane,Nilesh Patil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，预测基础模型的预训练表示通常优于随机权重表示，但未能稳定胜过强大的非预训练分类器；评估时应采用有力基线和随机权重对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测基础模型常被用作冻结特征提取器，但其预训练表示是否能提供超越模型架构本身的分类信息，尤其在标注数据稀缺时是否有优势，尚不明确。

Method: 在六个大型时间序列数据集上评估两种预测模型的冻结表示，分别使用百分之一、百分之十和全部训练标签。将结果与四种成熟分类方法及相同预测模型的随机权重版本进行比较，并进一步考察恢复振幅信息、微调和使用预测误差特征的影响。

Result: 预训练表示在六个数据集中的五个上优于随机权重表示，最高提升二十三个百分点；但在十七种评估设置中，有十五种不及最强的非预训练基线，其余两种持平。恢复振幅信息可弥补部分性能差距，而微调和预测误差特征未能消除差距。

Conclusion: 预训练确实能提升预测模型用于分类时的表示能力，但这不意味着其性能优于强大的专用分类器。评估此类模型时，应设置有竞争力的任务专用基线和随机权重对照，并关注振幅信息的作用。

Abstract: Forecasting foundation models are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for time series classification, but it remains unclear whether their pretrained representations offer an advantage over strong classifiers that do not require pretraining. This raises an important question: do these representations provide useful information beyond what the model architecture alone can capture, particularly when labelled data are scarce? We present a controlled benchmarking of two forecasting models, Reverso and Chronos-Bolt, on six large datasets from the MONSTER benchmark, using 1%, 10%, and 100% of the available training labels. We compare their frozen representations against four established time series classification methods and random-weight versions of the same forecasting models. Our results show that pretraining improves classification accuracy over random-weight counterparts on five of the six datasets, with gains of up to 23 percentage points. However, the frozen representations do not reliably outperform the strongest non-pretrained baseline in any setting: they perform worse in 15 of 17 settings and tie in the remaining two. Further experiments show that restoring amplitude information recovers part of the performance gap, while fine-tuning and forecast-error features do not close it. These findings highlight the importance of strong task specific baselines and random weight controls when evaluating forecasting foundation models for classification. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachet2006/frozen-forecasters-monster.

</details>


### [153] [Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出时间中和树：在数据流训练中暂时排除当前不再相关的旧样本，并在其重新相关时恢复使用；在二十四个真实及半真实分类数据流上的评估显示，该方法在多种概念漂移情形下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流会随时间发生概念漂移，使已部署的预测模型逐渐失效，通常需要检测漂移并重新训练。传统流学习常假设样本只能使用一次，而允许保存样本的研究显示，批量学习算法可能更具优势。尽管如此，漂移检测与简单重置仍很常见，因此需要更有效地利用历史样本，以适应变化且可能反复出现的数据规律。

Method: 提出时间中和树，一种面向概念漂移的决策树架构。训练时，根据数据流当前的上下文，过滤掉暂时不再相关的旧训练样本；若这些样本之后重新变得相关，则将其重新纳入训练。该方法由此避免永久丢弃历史信息，并适应数据规律的变化。

Result: 研究在美国圣保罗大学数据流资料库中的二十四个真实及半真实分类数据流上评估了该方法及相关算法。结果显示，时间中和树在涵盖多种概念漂移类型的数据流上取得了有力且广泛的竞争优势，整体表现达到领先水平。

Conclusion: 通过按当前上下文动态管理历史样本，而非仅依赖漂移检测后的模型重置，时间中和树为非平稳数据流分类提供了一种有效方案。摘要所述实验支持其在多种漂移场景中的应用潜力，但未提供具体指标或显著性检验细节。

Abstract: Data Streams are characterised as a potentially infinite source of data subject to non-stationarity. This non-stationarity, also called Concept Drift, often results in deployed predictive models having to be perpetually retrained as the underlying data generating process of the stream changes over time. Drift Detectors are a class of methods used to identify these changes, triggering these retraining procedures. Historically, Stream Learning has operated under the assumption that instances are only seen once, and cannot be stored for later use. Contemporary work relaxes this assumption, yet drift detection and simple resetting procedures are still ubiquitous.
  Building on related work which has shown that traditional Batch Learning algorithms often outperform Stream Learning algorithms when instances can be stored, we introduce Time Neutralising Trees (TNT), a Decision Tree architecture that enables robust stream classification in settings with Concept Drift. During training, TNT will filter out ("neutralise") old training samples that are no longer relevant given the current context of the stream. If those instances become relevant again, TNT will then re-include them. We evaluate TNT, and related algorithms on 24 real and semi-real classification data streams from the USP DS repository. Results show strong evidence that TNT achieves best-in-class performance on a wide-range of data streams with varying concept drift types.

</details>


### [154] [LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025)
*Teresa Delgado,Hugo Hazard,Martin Benfeghoul,Jun Wang,Zafeirios Fountas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非对称键值缓存压缩方法：利用值向量可由对应键向量预测的特点压缩值缓存，并结合跨层键压缩；在尽量保持模型性能的同时显著降低缓存开销、提升解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码所需的键值缓存会随上下文长度线性增长，限制批处理规模与吞吐量。既有方法主要利用令牌、层、特征维度间的冗余或降低数值精度；本文进一步发现，同一注意力头中的值向量往往能由对应键向量预测，因此可利用这一互补的冗余来源。

Method: 提出值缓存摊销：在推理时为每个注意力头拟合轻量预测器，仅为预测误差较大的令牌保存残差，实现近乎无损的值缓存压缩。预测器可通过元学习初始化，以减少预填充开销；也可在允许一次性成本的场景中从头拟合。方法还结合跨层低秩键压缩，并在每个解码步骤只重建选定的一部分键值对，以控制额外计算开销；此外可与量化和缓存淘汰结合。

Result: 值缓存可实现约4倍的近乎无损压缩。结合跨层键压缩后，该方案在三类模型上均保持接近无损的效果，并在4倍压缩时将峰值解码吞吐量最高提升至原来的5倍。进一步结合量化与缓存淘汰，可实现26.9倍压缩，同时保留基线性能的94.5%。

Conclusion: 键与值具有不同的结构特性，适合采用非对称压缩策略。利用值对键的可预测性，并与键压缩、量化和缓存淘汰组合，可以在保持较高模型性能的同时大幅压缩键值缓存，并提升长上下文解码效率。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\times$ compression at $94.5\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

</details>


### [155] [Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065)
*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Yiming Zhang,Luke Snow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以随机动力系统为统一视角，分析语言模型、相互作用的模型网络及神经随机微分方程，涵盖概率预测、群体涌现行为与连续时间生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能涵盖离散序列生成、多个模型之间的信息交换，以及连续时间随机轨迹生成。本文旨在用随机动力系统框架统一刻画这些过程，并解释模型运行机制及其可能产生的集体现象。

Method: 首先，将大型语言模型表述为具有注意力依赖的高维非线性自回归模型，并将条件最大似然预训练与逐词元生成联系起来。其次，以描述网络中个体相互作用的均值场常微分方程，分析模型网络的集体动态。最后，将神经随机微分方程作为连续时间生成模型，并讨论基于粗路径分析的维纳空间求积方法，以提高训练中的估计效率，作为蒙特卡洛估计的替代方案。

Result: 摘要提出了三类模型的分析框架：大型语言模型的概率预测机制、相互作用模型网络中的优先连接与持续结构偏差（包括类型相关的晋升天花板效应），以及神经随机微分方程对不规则时间间隔随机轨迹的生成建模。摘要未报告定量实验结果或性能比较。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是以随机动力系统视角贯通生成式人工智能中的序列预测、网络交互与路径空间生成，为理解模型概率机制、群体涌现偏差和连续时间随机生成提供统一的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic dynamical-systems perspective on three classes of generative AI models. First, we formulate large language models (LLMs) as high-dimensional nonlinear autoregressive models with attention-based dependencies. This formulation connects pretraining by conditional maximum likelihood to sequential token generation and provides a probabilistic interpretation of LLM operation. Second, we consider networks of interacting LLMs and characterize their collective behavior through mean-field ordinary differential equations. These dynamics provide a framework for understanding emergent preferential attachment and persistent structural biases, including type-dependent glass-ceiling effects. Third, we consider neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) as continuous-time generative models for stochastic trajectories, including those observed at irregular time intervals. We discuss their training with particular emphasis on Wiener-space cubature methods based on rough-path analysis as an efficient alternative to Monte Carlo estimation. Together, the three settings address probabilistic prediction, information exchange and emergent behavior in interacting networks, and path-space generative modeling.

</details>


### [156] [Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069)
*Jingjuan Huang,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中的长程历史依赖，论文提出基于有限历史窗口、神经网络价值函数与时序差分学习的分析框架，并证明有限时间误差界和相对于最优价值的次线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的决策依赖完整历史，导致强化学习计算困难。满足滤波稳定性条件时，可用有限长度历史构造超状态马尔可夫决策过程近似原问题，且窗口越长，价值差距越小。然而，已有有限时间理论要么仅适用于过度简化的线性函数逼近，要么假设独立同分布采样，与实际部分可观测轨迹不符，因此难以直接用于实践。

Method: 首先建立有限状态马尔可夫决策过程的逐点逼近误差界，为算法设计与分析提供基础。随后，将部分可观测过程产生的超状态转移用于时序差分学习，并以超状态过程的贝尔曼不动点为目标，在神经网络非线性价值函数逼近下推导有限时间的评论家误差界。最后，将该误差界与策略镜像下降框架结合，分析策略学习的遗憾。

Result: 理论上，论文证明了神经网络价值函数逼近下的有限时间评论家误差界，以及相对于最优部分可观测决策价值的有限时间次线性遗憾界。作者指出，这是针对有限记忆部分可观测决策过程、基于神经网络函数逼近的此类遗憾保证中的首次成果。数值实验也支持理论结果。

Conclusion: 该研究弥补了部分可观测强化学习理论与实际非线性函数逼近之间的缺口：在有限历史近似和真实轨迹采样条件下，仍可为神经网络价值学习及策略优化提供有限时间理论保证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) is computationally challenging due to the curse of long-range dependence on history. Fortunately, recent studies have shown that a range of POMDPs that satisfy the filter stability assumption can be well approximated by a superstate Markov Decision Process (MDP) based on a truncated history in a finite-memory window, with a value gap that decays with the window length. However, existing finite-time convergence analyses on such superstate MDP surrogates are either limited to over-simplified linear function approximation or rely on an i.i.d. sampling oracle that is violated by POMDP trajectories, both of which severely limit the applicability of these theoretical results in practice. This motivates us to bridge the gap by investigating RL in POMDPs with nonlinear neural value function approximation. Our main contributions are threefold: i) We first establish a new finite-state MDP point-wise approximation error bound, which lays the foundation for our RL algorithm design and analysis in POMDPs; ii) By applying Temporal-Difference (TD) learning to POMDP-generated superstate transitions and targeting the Bellman fixed point of the superstate MDP, we prove a finite-time critic error bound with nonlinear neural value function approximation; and iii) By combining the neural critic function approximation error bound with the policy mirror descent framework, we obtain a finite-time sublinear regret against the optimal POMDP value. To the best of our knowledge, such a finite-time sublinear regret based on neural function approximation is the first in the literature for finite-memory POMDPs. Our numerical experiments also verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [157] [Shaping & Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071)
*Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal,Matt Feiszli,Satchel Grant,Andrew Lee,Chris Earls,Fatih Dinc,Vasudev Shyam,Usha Bhalla,Leon Bergen,Siddharth Boppana,Ching Fang,Michael Byun,Tristan Gallent,Nicholas Wang,Tal Haklay,Dron Hazra,Neehar Kondapaneni,Connor Watts,Ryan Panwar,Thomas Dooms,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“机制塑形”，通过训练直接塑造模型内部表征，减少模型表达或决策时依赖的指定概念，同时尽量保持任务性能；并系统分析这些概念是否仍可被监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全训练通常关注模型输出了什么，却较少约束模型依据什么作出决定。例如，模型不仅不应生成有害内容，也不应在简历筛选中依赖候选人的性别。与此同时，针对内部表征进行训练可能改变表征本身，使原有监测方法失效，因此需要区分概念只是逃过某种探针，还是确实已从表征中消失。

Method: 提出机制塑形框架，在训练过程中依据模型输出或内部表征，对指定概念进行塑造，适用于视觉、医学影像和语言任务，以及分类、监督微调、直接偏好优化和群体相对策略优化等训练范式。论文还建立监测器类别的层级，并分析三种规避机制：概念偏离监测器、概念从某层表征中被消除，以及概念以非线性高阶统计形式编码；第三种机制通过乘积码构造加以解释，并为各类机制设计相应监测方法。

Result: 在多个领域、不同模型规模（最高达三百二十亿参数）和多种训练范式中，机制塑形均显著降低了目标行为，同时大体保留任务性能。多数情况下，重新拟合的线性探针仍能恢复被塑形的概念；当梯度不经过原探针时，原探针也仍然可靠。论文观察到的概念规避现象均可由上述三种机制解释，并能通过匹配的监测器检测。

Conclusion: 研究表明，可将可解释性方法用于模型训练，不仅检查模型表达什么，也约束模型内部依赖什么。但塑形后概念是否仍可监测，取决于所采用的监测器类别，不能把单一探针失效等同于概念消失。因此，基于可解释性的训练需要建立更严谨、系统的评估与研究方法。

Abstract: We propose Mechanistic Shaping, a unified framework for shaping models through their internal representations. Mechanistic Shaping is applied during training so that models follow specifications (rules) about which concepts they express, measured via their outputs or internal representations (e.g., no harmful content), and also which concepts they rely on, i.e., what they use to make their decisions (e.g., not a candidate's gender when screening a résumé). Across multiple domains (vision, medical imaging, language), scales (up to 32B parameters), and training paradigms (classification, SFT, DPO, GRPO), we find that Mechanistic Shaping substantially reduces targeted behaviors while preserving task performance. Since training against a representation can also modify it, we ask whether shaped concepts remain monitorable, a question that depends on the class of monitors considered. We therefore introduce a hierarchy of such classes, which separates a concept that escapes one probe from a concept that is truly gone. We characterize three geometric mechanisms of circumvention: the concept can drift away from the monitor, be erased from the layer, or persist in higher-order statistics as a nonlinear encoding, which we explain through a product-code construction. Although other mechanisms may arise in different settings or at larger scales, these three account for all of our observations, and each can be detected by a matching monitor. In practice, we find that shaped concepts largely remain monitorable: refitted linear probes recover the concept in most settings, and the original probe remains reliable whenever gradients do not flow through it. Taken together, these results point to the potential of using interpretability as a tool for training, and call for a correspondingly rigorous science of interpretability-based training.

</details>


### [158] [Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078)
*Yuval Dagan,Nadav Eisen,Liad Erez,Tomer Koren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示，乐观乘法权重更新算法的遗憾表现取决于博弈中的玩家数量：两人博弈可实现与时间跨度无关的多项式遗憾上界，而三人及以上博弈可能出现指数级遗憾，甚至遗憾随时间线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已证明，所有玩家采用乐观乘法权重更新时，遗憾可以不随时间跨度增长；但已有界限是非构造性的，也未明确说明其对每人行动数量的依赖。因此，尚不清楚该算法能否获得关于博弈规模为多项式的常数遗憾界。本文进一步探究玩家人数是否会影响这一可能性。

Method: 对于两人博弈，作者提出一种时间局部化近似框架：从基于方差的遗憾上界出发，将各时间窗口内的损失近似为性质良好的函数，并控制这些函数的总变差，使其仅占累计方差的一小部分。据此选择学习率并推导遗憾上界。对于多玩家情形，作者构造具有特定收益结构的博弈，证明遗憾下界及其随玩家数、行动数和时间的增长关系。

Result: 在两人博弈中，存在合适的学习率，使每位玩家的遗憾为 O(n log n)，且与时间跨度无关，其中 n 为每位玩家的行动数。在三人博弈中，对任意不超过某个绝对常数的学习率，都存在足够大的时间跨度和相应博弈，使某位玩家的遗憾达到 2^{Ω(n)}。更一般地，对每个至少有三名玩家的情形，存在博弈使某位玩家的遗憾达到 2^{Ω(mn)}；当学习率大于 2^{-Ω(mn)} 时，该玩家的遗憾还会随时间跨度线性增长。此外，作者的框架也简化了多玩家博弈中 O(√m log n log T) 遗憾上界的证明。

Conclusion: 乐观乘法权重更新并非在不同规模的博弈中都能实现多项式规模、且与时间无关的遗憾保证；玩家数量构成关键分界。两人博弈具有较强的稳定性，可获得明确的常数遗憾界，而三人及以上博弈则可能遭遇极大的遗憾，表明仅靠调整学习率未必能保证良好表现。

Abstract: Optimistic Hedge, a multiplicative weight update with one step of extrapolation, is one of the most natural regret-minimizing dynamics for games, and characterizing its regret has been a longstanding open question. Daskalakis, Fishelson and Golowich proved that if all $m$ players in a finite general-sum game follow Optimistic Hedge with a fixed learning rate, each player's regret is polylogarithmic in the horizon $T$. Subsequently, Ha showed that an appropriate choice of learning rate makes each player's regret bounded independently of $T$. This bound, however, is non-constructive and its dependence on the number of actions $n$ is not explicit, leaving open whether Optimistic Hedge can achieve a constant regret bound which is polynomial in the size of the game.
  We show that the answer hinges on the number of players. In two-player games, there is a learning rate for which Optimistic Hedge guarantees $O(n\log n)$ regret independent of the horizon. In three-player games, however, no such bound is possible: for any learning rate up to an absolute constant and for sufficiently large horizon $T$, there is a game with $n$ actions per player in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(n)}$, and more generally, for every $m\ge3$ we construct $m$-player games in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(mn)}$; for learning rates above $2^{-Ω(mn)}$, that player's regret even grows linearly in $T$.
  Our upper bound is based on a temporal approximation framework for the analysis of Optimistic Hedge. Starting from the variance-based RVU bound of Daskalakis et al., we approximate the losses over time-localized windows by well-behaved functions, whose total variation is a small fraction of their cumulative variance. In $m$-player games, the same framework gives a significantly simplified proof of the recent $O(\sqrt{m}\log n\log T)$ regret bound due to Ha.

</details>


### [159] [Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093)
*Xue Zheng,Aylin Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种服务器端稀疏专家分配框架，从有限观测中估计各客户端相关的专家，并通过“利用—探索”机制逐步改进分配。在每个客户端仅加载五个专家的条件下，其最终平均准确率比每个客户端可使用全部二十个专家的联邦平均基线高一点三一个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备在数据分布、内存和计算能力方面存在差异，因而联邦微调大语言模型时，需要兼顾隐私、资源限制与客户端个性化需求。现有联邦混合专家方法虽采用稀疏激活或部分加载降低开销，但依赖启发式的全局观测，或主要追求系统整体负载均衡，缺乏从有限观测中可靠找回各客户端真正相关专家的保证。

Method: 提出服务器端稀疏专家分配框架，根据部分观测估计客户端特定的专家相关性，并通过利用—探索机制迭代优化专家分配。论文还给出了相关性估计误差界及精确恢复前若干高相关专家的理论保证。

Result: 在基于卷积神经网络的混合专家模型和百类图像分类任务上的实验表明，该方法能够恢复随任务变化的客户端—专家偏好，并减少不同客户端因选择相关专家而产生的冲突。与每个客户端可访问全部二十个专家、进行本地前五专家路由的联邦平均基线相比，该方法每个客户端仅加载五个专家，最终平均准确率仍提高一点三一个百分点。

Conclusion: 有限观测下的个性化专家识别可以兼顾理论可靠性与实际效果。该方法在显著减少客户端专家加载量的同时提升了分类准确率，说明针对客户端差异进行稀疏专家分配，比仅依赖全局启发式或负载均衡更适合资源受限的联邦场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly recognized for their remarkable capabilities; federated learning (FL) offers a promising framework for collaboratively fine-tuning LLMs on edge devices while achieving data privacy. However, edge devices are inherently heterogeneous: clients may exhibit diverse data distributions and resource constraints, including limited memory and computation budgets. Recent federated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) approaches address these challenges by using sparse expert activation or partial expert loading. However, existing partial-expert methods rely on heuristic full-pool observations or prioritize system-wide load balance, without guarantees for recovering client-specific relevant experts from partial observations. We propose $S^3$-MoE, a server-side sparse expert assignment framework that estimates client-specific expert relevance from partial observations and refines assignments through an exploit--explore mechanism. We also establish relevance-estimation error and exact Top-$K$ recovery guarantees. Experiments using a CNN-based MoE on CIFAR-100 show that $S^3$-MoE recovers task-dependent client--expert preferences and reduces conflicts caused by correlated expert selection across clients. Compared with Full-20 FedAvg, which gives each client access to all 20 experts for local Top-5 routing, $S^3$-MoE achieves 1.31 percentage points higher mean final accuracy while loading only $5$ experts per client.

</details>


### [160] [A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103)
*Scott Biggs,Bedionita Soro,Saumya Gupta,Bruno Andreis,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究构建了由不同数据配比训练的大语言模型集合，并训练权重生成器；结果表明，生成器可对未见过的数据配比进行零样本泛化，生成性能接近集合中的真实模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间学习正从任务专用模型和视觉模型拓展至大语言模型，但该领域缺少经过系统构建与分析的模型群体，限制了权重生成方法的研究与发展。

Method: 研究者系统调整网页文本、书籍、数学、编程和法语数据的混合比例，训练三种规模、每种各一百个的第二代生成式预训练变换器模型，规模分别为五千一百万、一亿二千四百万和三亿五千五百万参数。研究通过余弦相似度、功能多样性、留一数据配比建模和方差分解分析模型群体，并将数据配比编码为条件，在经格拉姆主成分分析压缩的权重空间中训练流匹配模型，以生成完整的预训练模型权重。

Result: 模型群体具有多样性，且模型差异与训练数据配比存在结构性关联。权重生成器能够对未参与训练的数据配比实现零样本泛化；连续秩概率得分显示其具有预测能力。生成模型在下游测试集困惑度和通用能力基准上的变化较小，整体表现接近模型群体中的真实模型。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，基于系统构建的模型群体及数据配比条件，能够在数据配比空间中生成性能相近的大语言模型，为权重空间学习与生成拓展到大语言模型领域提供了基础资源和实证支持。

Abstract: A challenge to Weight Space Learning as it expands from task specific and vision models to Large Language Models (LLMs) is the absence of a well curated and analyzed LLM population. To address this, we train such a model zoo for application to Weight Space Generation by systematically varying the data mixture composition between web text, books, mathematics, coding, and French across populations of 51M, 124M, and 355M parameter GPT-2 Models, each tier with count N = 100. We analyze these populations by cosine similarity, functional diversity, Leave-One-Mixture-Out modeling, and variance decomposition. We show that the zoo contains diverse models whose variation is structured by training mixture composition. We embed the training mixture composition to condition a Flow Matching model of complete pretrained LLM weights in Gram-PCA compressed weight space. By demonstrating zero-shot generalization to held out mixtures, measuring Continuous Ranked Probability Score skill, as well as finding minimal changes in downstream test-set perplexity and general capability benchmark scores, we show that our generator generalizes on the simplex of data mixtures to produce models that perform similarly to those in the zoo ex nihilo. Finally, we produce all source code and artifacts to support the continued expansion of Weight Space Learning and Generation into the LLM domain.

</details>


### [161] [Regulating Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand Operators with Fixed and Adaptive Penalties: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07122)
*Korbinian Kügler,Carolin Schmidt,Florian Dandl,Filipe Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用罚款监管自主按需出行运营商，抑制过高动态定价并改善不同地区的服务可得性；动态调整罚款优于同等平均强度的固定罚款，但在车队高度饱和时效果受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以利润最大化为目标的自主按需出行运营商，可能通过过高定价获取消费者剩余，并将车辆从利润较低的地区调走，造成区域服务不均。研究旨在探讨罚款监管能否限制价格过高并改善服务覆盖，同时分析监管目标、运营商盈利与乘客效用之间的权衡。

Method: 将监管建模为作用于学习型运营商的外层控制环。固定罚款情形下，运营商联合控制定价与车辆再平衡，问题表示为马尔可夫决策过程；动态监管情形下，监管者定期闭环调整各地区罚款，问题表示为部分可观测随机博弈。运营商和监管者的策略均通过基于图结构的多智能体强化学习进行训练。

Result: 基于曼哈顿真实数据的实验显示，固定的过高定价罚款将面临过高价格的乘客比例从百分之二十二降至百分之一。对于区域服务可得性，动态监管相较于平均强度相同的固定罚款，最高可使监管者奖励提升百分之十五点六。车队有闲置容量时，服务改善最明显；高峰期车队利用率很高时，服务缺口仍然存在。学习得到的监管策略会在车队容量有限、运营商难以遵从监管要求的地区降低罚款。

Conclusion: 罚款监管能够抑制过高定价，动态、分地区调整罚款也能比固定罚款更有效地改善服务可得性。但监管效果取决于车队是否有可调度余量：车队高度饱和时，单靠罚款难以弥补服务不足。因此，监管设计需要兼顾乘客利益、运营商盈利和实际运力约束。

Abstract: Profit-maximizing Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand (AMoD) operators may exploit consumer surplus through excessive prices and underserve less profitable regions by relocating vehicles away from them. We study how penalty-based regulation of an AMoD operator in a multimodal mobility system can limit surge pricing and improve regional service availability. We model regulation as an outer control loop that acts on a learning-based operator. Static regulation with fixed penalties is formulated as a Markov Decision Process for an operator that jointly controls pricing and rebalancing, and dynamic regulation as a Partially Observable Stochastic Game in which a regulator periodically adjusts region-specific penalties in closed loop. Operator and regulator policies are learned with graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning. In experiments with real-world Manhattan data, fixed surge pricing penalties reduce the share of travelers facing excessive prices from 22% to 1%. For regional service availability, dynamic regulation improves the regulator's reward by up to 15.6% over fixed penalties of equal mean intensity. The gains in service availability are strongest when spare fleet capacity exists, whereas service deficits persist in peak periods with highly utilized fleets. The learned regulator policy responds by reducing penalties where limited fleet capacity restricts compliance. The results identify trade-offs between regulatory targets, operator profitability, and traveler utility.

</details>


### [162] [Machine Learning for Live-Birth Prediction After IVF/ICSI From Structured Clinical Data: A Systematic Review, Meta-Analysis, and Critical Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07124)
*Abdul Raheem Shahzad,Muhammad Zaheer Khan,Umar Ajaib,Asad Ur Rehman,Juan Deng,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这项系统综述纳入四十八项研究，发现机器学习预测体外受精或卵胞浆内单精子注射后活产的表现差异很大；外部验证和偏倚风险显示，机器学习优势并不稳健，整体证据可信度偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究对机器学习预测活产的表现报告从接近随机猜测到近乎完美，结果相互矛盾。研究旨在系统评估这些模型的预测能力、偏倚风险与报告质量，并明确决策时点、结局、分析单位和数据来源等因素如何界定模型输出的含义。

Method: 研究依照系统综述报告规范检索三类学术数据库，纳入四十八项研究；使用预测模型偏倚风险与适用性评估工具及人工智能扩展版报告规范进行评价。研究按模型类型、决策时点和验证设计分类，并采用随机效应荟萃分析合并受试者工作特征曲线下面积，在逻辑几率尺度上进行分析。

Result: 仅内部验证的模型合并曲线下面积为零点八一四；在较晚时期或其他中心验证的模型为零点七五六，预测区间为零点五四六至零点八八九；未被评为高偏倚风险的研究为零点七六四。上述差异均无统计学显著性，且研究间异质性极高。未说明决策时点的模型合并值为零点九一零，是荟萃回归中唯一显著的调节因素。与使用相同数据拟合的逻辑回归相比，表现最好的机器学习模型曲线下面积平均提高零点零五六。四十八项研究中，四十七项存在高或不明确的偏倚风险，仅四项评估校准斜率，没有模型提供临床影响证据。

Conclusion: 现有证据对机器学习预测活产的效果支持有限，确定性为低至极低。经过验证后仍可观察到的改进，主要来自将预测对应到明确的临床决策时点并进行本地校准，而非采用更深层的模型架构。后续研究应加强外部验证、校准评估、透明报告和临床影响评价。

Abstract: Machine-learning models predicting live birth after in vitro fertilisation (IVF) or intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) from routine clinical data report performance from near chance to near perfect. We systematically reviewed these models under PRISMA 2020, searching OpenAlex, Scopus and Web of Science to 22 October 2026, appraised them with PROBAST+AI and TRIPOD+AI, and pooled their discrimination with random-effects meta-analysis on the logit scale. We define what fixes the meaning of a model's output (decision point, endpoint, unit of analysis, data source) and map the 48 included studies by model family, decision point and validation design. Models evaluated only internally pooled to an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.814, against 0.756 (95% prediction interval 0.546-0.889) for models evaluated on a later period or another centre, and 0.764 for studies not at high risk of bias; neither difference was significant, and heterogeneity was extreme. Models whose decision point was not stated pooled to 0.910, the only significant moderator in meta-regression. Against logistic regression fitted to the same data, the best machine-learning model gained a pooled 0.056 AUC (95% CI 0.017-0.090). Of 48 studies, 47 were at high or unclear risk of bias, only four assessed the calibration slope, and no model has evidence of clinical impact. The certainty of the pooled evidence is low to very low; the gains that survive validation came from staging predictions to decision points and local calibration, not deeper architectures. We provide a TRIPOD+AI-mapped checklist, a benchmark specification and a research agenda.

</details>


### [163] [Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125)
*Md Mahmudul Hasan Pathik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究汇集了291组文献中的静态熔融氯盐腐蚀实验，建立可解释且能估计不确定性的腐蚀速率预测框架；模型表现良好，并显示盐处理与杂质状况是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管熔融氯盐腐蚀已研究数十年，但由于合金成分、盐种及实验条件各异，现有知识难以可靠地跨研究预测腐蚀速率。研究旨在整合历史实验数据，并明确表征不同研究中的盐状态与实验背景，从而提升预测能力和可解释性。

Method: 研究整理了文献报道的291组静态熔融氯盐腐蚀实验，构建结构化数据集，并提出氯化物盐制备指数，将盐处理方法、气氛控制及暴露前杂质状况映射到统一的数值尺度。研究比较树模型集成方法，并结合全局敏感性分析、沙普利加性解释分析和保形预测，分别考察特征影响及预测不确定性。

Result: 融合式树模型取得最稳定的预测表现，交叉验证决定系数为0.85，平均绝对误差为每平方厘米每日0.41毫克。分级盐制备指数比简单的“纯化或未纯化”分类保留了更多信息，并成为模型中影响最大的描述变量。暴露时间、温度和合金化学成分也对预测有重要贡献，相关趋势与已知熔融氯盐腐蚀规律基本一致。保形预测区间校准良好，可为点预测提供不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 历史腐蚀数据在实验背景得到明确表征时，可以支持定量、可解释且具有不确定性评估的熔融氯盐腐蚀预测。研究同时指出，进一步统一实验信息报告方式并开展有针对性的实验，是提升未来模型可靠性与泛化能力的关键。

Abstract: Molten chloride corrosion has been studied for decades, yet reliable prediction across different alloys, salts, and test conditions remains difficult. In this work, 291 static molten chloride corrosion experiments reported in the literature were compiled into a structured dataset and used to develop an interpretable machine-learning framework for corrosion-rate predictions. To better represent the differences in salt condition across studies, a Chloride Salt Preparation Index (CSPI) was introduced to bring the reported salt-treatment method, atmosphere control, and pre-exposure impurity condition on a common numerical scale. A blended tree-based ensemble provided the most consistent predictive performance, achieving a cross-validated \(R^2\) of 0.85 and an MAE of 0.41 mg cm\(^{-2}\) day\(^{-1}\). The graded CSPI retained more useful information than a simple purified/unpurified classification and emerged as the dominant model descriptor. Global sensitivity and SHAP analyses further identified exposure time, temperature, and alloy chemistry as important contributors to the model predictions, with several observed trends consistent with the established molten-chloride corrosion behavior. The conformal prediction intervals were well calibrated, providing an estimate of prediction uncertainty in addition to the point prediction. Overall, the framework demonstrates that historical corrosion data can support quantitative, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware prediction when experimental context is represented explicitly, while also highlighting where more consistent reporting and targeted experiments are needed to improve future molten-salt corrosion models.

</details>


### [164] [Scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification for Cancer Histopathology: Calibrated Uncertainty Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07127)
*Yunru Zheng,Wun Ting Chan,Sampras Dsouza,Zhenyu Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种可扩展的贝叶斯高斯过程分类方法，在病理图像分类中提升预测校准与性能，尤其能更可靠地应对医院间分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症诊断模型要用于临床，必须提供可信的不确定性估计。然而，神经网络和变分高斯过程在跨医院应用等分布偏移场景中容易过度自信；同时，基于马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛的贝叶斯方法难以扩展到大型训练集。

Method: 在冻结的病理基础模型生成的图像嵌入上构建贝叶斯高斯过程分类器。方法结合加速随机枢轴乔列斯基分解进行低秩核分解，采用非中心参数化的哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验采样，并将分解枢轴作为诱导点用于预测，从而使约三十万训练样本上的低秩后验采样变得可行。

Result: 在乳腺癌转移检测的两个组织病理学基准上进行评估，其中一个专门考察医院间分布偏移。与随机变分高斯过程及神经网络基线相比，该方法改善了校准和预测表现：期望校准误差最多降低百分之八十八，假阴性率最多降低百分之九十；最大收益出现在分布偏移场景。

Conclusion: 低秩表示与哈密顿蒙特卡洛后验采样相结合，能够将贝叶斯高斯过程分类扩展到更大规模的病理数据，并在跨医院分类时提供更可信的不确定性估计，具有提升临床部署可靠性的潜力。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty quantification is essential for clinical deployment of cancer diagnosis models, yet neural networks and variational Gaussian process methods can produce overconfident predictions, particularly under the distribution shifts that arise when models trained at one hospital are applied at another. We present a scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification (GPC) framework that operates on frozen pathology foundation model embeddings (Phikon) and combines accelerated Randomly Pivoted Cholesky (RPCholesky) for low-rank kernel factorization, Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) with non-centered parameterization for posterior sampling, and a pivots-as-inducing-points scheme for prediction. This makes HMC posterior sampling in the low-rank latent representation tractable at N approximately 300,000 training points, enabling HC-based low-rank GPC on substantially larger datasets than prior MCMC-based demonstrations. We evaluate on two histopathology benchmarks for breast cancer metastasis detection: PCam and CAMELYON17-WILDS, where the latter tests calibration under hospital-level distribution shift. Compared to stochastic variational GP and neural network baselines, our method improves calibration and predictive performance - reducing expected calibration error by up to 88% and false negative rate by up to 90% - with the largest gains appearing under distribution shift, suggesting that HMC-based posterior sampling provides more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for cross-site pathology classification.

</details>


### [165] [Teaching Agents the "Principle", Not the "Reward"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07130)
*Shan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种直接教授行为原则的强化学习方法，使智能体无需奖励信号也能高效学习并泛化到更困难的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂任务中，即使奖励经过精心设计且样本充足，智能体仍难以学得良好、可泛化的策略。论文认为，核心困难在于奖励解码负担：设计者把期望行为编码为奖励和惩罚，智能体却必须从漫长且含噪的标量序列中自行推断这些知识。

Method: 提出规范性策略学习，直接向智能体传授任务中的高层行为原则，例如保持平衡、积累能量和稳定着陆。通过选择适当的关键量及其参考值，在每个时间步指出智能体是否遵循原则及遵循程度，从而以粗略的行为知识指导学习，而不依赖奖励信号或精确的系统动力学模型。

Result: 实验表明，仅凭简单启发式行为原则训练的智能体，无需奖励即可学得良好策略；与基于奖励的方法相比，它们使用更少样本并取得更好表现。学得的策略还能直接迁移到更困难的设置，并保持良好表现，显示出较强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了区别于传统奖励驱动学习的新途径：不必精确建模系统或设计复杂奖励，只需提供关于期望行为的粗略知识，就能帮助智能体高效学习并形成可泛化的策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved strong performance in many tasks by letting agents learn from nothing more than a reward signal. However, in more complex tasks, agents still struggle to learn good, generalizable policies, even with carefully designed rewards and abundant samples. We argue that this difficulty stems from the reward decoding burden: designers encode their knowledge of the desired behavior into rewards and penalties, and agents must infer this knowledge from long sequences of noisy scalars. To address this burden, we propose prescriptive policy learning (PPL), which directly teaches agents higher-level knowledge about the tasks, termed principles of behavior, such as how to maintain balance, how to accumulate energy, and how to land smoothly. Through appropriately selected key quantities and references, PPL can directly indicate at every step whether the agent follows the principles and to what extent. Our experiments show that agents can learn good, generalizable policies without any reward. Taught only simple heuristic principles, PPL agents outperform reward-based methods with far fewer samples. Their policies also transfer directly to harder settings and keep performing well, demonstrating their strong generalization ability. In summary, PPL provides a new approach in which rough knowledge of the desired behavior is enough for agents to learn good, generalizable policies, even when the system dynamics cannot be accurately modeled.

</details>


### [166] [Depth or Description? When a One-Step Latent Score Replaces Lookahead in Proactive Information Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07155)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究主动智能体在行动前如何选择提问，指出合适的潜在原因表示可用低成本的一步评估捕捉许多需要深度搜索的提问交互，但模型概率的跨语言模型迁移能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体需要在采取行动前选择要问的问题。精确评估多个问题组合的价值计算昂贵，并依赖拟合模型；成本较低的一步评分则可能忽略答案之间的交互。论文进一步指出，这种深度需求与隐藏原因的表示方式有关：若只对最终标签评分，可能看不出交互。

Method: 比较精确前瞻评估与一步评分，并考察不同表示方式对提问价值评估的影响：在标签上评分，或在能够表征决策的潜在原因上评分。方法强调保持潜在表示精简，并用精确价值评估核验其效果；同时检验从一种语言模型的回答中学得的概率能否迁移到另一种模型，以及直接从词元读取概率的效用。

Result: 当潜在原因能够编码决策信息时，基于该表示的一步评分可以识别标签评分容易漏掉的答案交互，并以较少搜索替代大部分深度前瞻。存在这样的情形：一步评分会失效，但精确价值评估并未改变。来自一种语言模型回答的概率，若规划时未考虑两种模型，便不能可靠迁移；直接从词元读取的概率仅相当于少量标签信息。

Conclusion: 提问规划的关键不只是搜索深度，也在于评估所用的表示。精简且贴合决策的潜在原因有望显著降低搜索成本，但应通过精确价值评估检查其可靠性；同时，不能默认语言模型间的回答概率可以迁移，也不应高估词元概率所提供的信息。

Abstract: A proactive agent must decide what to ask before it acts. Exact lookahead values combinations of questions but is expensive and leans on a fitted model; a one-step score is cheap but can miss them. Our main finding: the need for depth often lies in how the hidden cause is described. A one-step score on the label can be blind to an interaction among answers. A score on a suitable latent cause is not, and where that latent encodes the decision it replaces most of depth at a fraction of the search. Described differently, the same problem can defeat the score while exact values do not move, so keep the latent small and check it against exact values. Probabilities learned from one language model's answers do not carry to another unless planning allows for both; those read from tokens are worth only a few labels.

</details>


### [167] [A Survey of Nonlinear Filtering Methods for Data Assimilation and Recent Advances in Generative AI-Enabled Score Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07160)
*Cath Pham,Kadesha Reynolds*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述集成评分滤波器：一种以评分函数表示滤波概率密度、利用免训练蒙特卡洛估计进行更新的方法，旨在解决高维混沌系统中的状态估计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值天气预报、野火追踪等应用需要从局部且含噪的观测中推断高度非线性、混沌系统的状态。传统滤波方法各有局限：基于卡尔曼滤波的方法依赖高斯后验假设，面对非线性观测算子时可能产生偏差；粒子方法在高维情况下会遭遇维数灾难；核方法则难以在维度增长时以可承受的成本维持密度近似。

Method: 集成评分滤波器以评分函数编码滤波密度，并采用无需训练的蒙特卡洛估计来近似评分，从而更新状态分布。论文梳理该方法的数学基础，并考察十四种扩展；其中十二种保留核心的蒙特卡洛估计，另有两种采用其他方案以支持行星尺度部署。

Result: 摘要将集成评分滤波器定位为适用于非高斯、高维滤波的先进方法，并指出其可在高维混沌系统中实现稳定滤波。其扩展进一步拓宽了方法的应用范围，少数方案尤其面向行星尺度的部署需求。

Conclusion: 集成评分滤波器通过评分函数表示与免训练蒙特卡洛估计，避开了传统高斯假设、粒子退化及核密度计算成本等关键限制。论文的主要贡献是系统总结其理论基础与十四种扩展，展现其在复杂高维数据同化任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Many real-world applications, such as numerical weather prediction or wildfire tracking, involve modeling highly nonlinear dynamical systems that are chaotic and influenced by various sources of uncertainty. To improve model prediction accuracy and reduce such uncertainties, data assimilation (DA) combines the dynamical model with partial, noisy observations to estimate the evolution of the system's state. Doing so depends on solving the optimal filtering problem: computing the conditional distribution of a hidden state given a sequence of observations. Classical approaches fail along three distinct axes: Kalman-based methods assume Gaussian posteriors and produce biased estimates under nonlinear observation operators; particle-based methods represent distributions with weighted samples and collapse in high dimensions due to the curse of dimensionality; and kernel-based methods move beyond discrete particle representations but cannot maintain their density approximation affordably as dimension grows. The Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) avoids all three constraints by encoding the filtering density in a score function and approximating that score with a training-free Monte Carlo estimator, enabling stable filtering in high-dimensional, chaotic systems. This survey traces EnSF's mathematical foundations, establishes it as the state of the art for non-Gaussian, high-dimensional filtering, and examines fourteen extensions that expand its operational reach. Twelve of the fourteen extensions preserve this training-free Monte Carlo estimator at the core of the update, with only two departing from it to achieve planetary-scale deployment.

</details>


### [168] [Structural Conditional Independence Test via Neural Quantile Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07165)
*Khoa Nguyen,Bao Duong,Thin Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件分位数变形的条件独立性检验方法，既能检测复杂依赖，也能展示依赖出现的情境与分位数范围；在合成基准和真实数据上表现有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统条件独立性检验通常以单一统计量或显著性概率概括证据，难以发现微弱、局部化，或并非表现为整体均值变化的依赖关系，也无法说明条件分布究竟在哪些情境和分位数区间发生改变。

Method: 方法先用轻量级残差分位数流模型学习给定情境变量时的基准条件分位数分布，再学习加入待检验变量后产生的额外分布变形。通过比较两阶段模型的预测增益构造整体检验统计量，并采用条件随机化进行校准；同时输出随情境和分位数变化的变形图，便于诊断依赖结构。论文还构建了结构依赖基准，涵盖情境局部、尾部限定、符号反转、移动脊线及均值不变等模式。

Result: 在合成的后非线性基准和结构依赖基准上，该方法对多种结构化依赖具有较强检出能力，并评估了不同设置下的第一类错误表现。中等样本量时，提高分位数分辨率可提升合并基准上的检验效能；若诊断集中于已知受影响区域，对局部依赖的检出也会改善。在萨克斯蛋白信号数据、乳腺癌分子数据及细胞系药物反应数据上，方法表现具有竞争力，并给出可解释的分布变形模式。

Conclusion: 研究表明，以条件分位数变形刻画条件分布变化，可作为检验和诊断结构化条件依赖的实用框架。该方法不仅提供整体检验结果，还能揭示依赖发生的位置与分布区间；其实际效能仍会受到样本量、分位数分辨率及受影响区域信息的影响。

Abstract: Conditional independence testing is difficult when dependence is weak, localized, or expressed through distributional changes rather than a global mean shift. Existing tests usually return a scalar statistic or p-value, which summarizes evidence against independence but does not describe where the conditional law changes. We propose QRCIT, a quantile-resolution conditional independence test that compares P(X|Z) and P(X|Y,Z) through a quantile deformation learned by a shared model. A lightweight residual quantile-flow model first learns the baseline conditional quantile law and then learns the additional deformation induced by Y, yielding a global predictive-gain statistic with conditional-randomization calibration and an inspectable deformation field over context and quantile level. We also introduce the Structural Dependency Benchmark (SDB), which evaluates dependence structures motivated by real applications, such as context-local, tail-only, sign-reversing, moving-ridge, and mean-neutral patterns. Across synthetic post-nonlinear (PNL) and SDB benchmarks, QRCIT improves detection of structured alternatives, with empirical Type-I behavior evaluated across settings. Higher quantile resolution improves merged-SDB power at moderate sample sizes, while diagnostics restricted to known affected regions improve detection of localized dependence. On Sachs, METABRIC breast cancer, and CCLE/PLX4720, QRCIT provides competitive real-data performance and interpretable deformation patterns. These results support quantile-resolved conditional-law deformation as a practical framework for testing and diagnosing structured conditional dependence.

</details>


### [169] [Meta-Frieren: Beyond Single Level Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07169)
*Vahan Arsenyan,Arnak Dalalyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于两阶段在线优化的通用元学习框架，通过元层选择准则协调不同智能体与环境，并给出相关算法的遗憾保证及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两阶段在线优化中，元学习器先选择智能体与环境的组合，再由该智能体与环境交互并获得奖励。如何在持续探索与利用的同时有效选择组合，是提升长期累计奖励的核心问题。已有理论结果覆盖范围有限，论文旨在建立更统一的分析框架并扩展保证。

Method: 提出名为“元弗里伦”的通用框架，并推导基于上置信界的元层选择准则，使既有保证可作为特例纳入分析。进一步将高斯过程上置信界算法用于贝叶斯和频率学派设定；针对“乐观奖励式汤普森抽样”推导高概率遗憾界，并将结果推广到有限函数类与参数化函数类，最后将该方法整合进元框架。

Result: 理论分析为元层选择及相关算法提供了遗憾保证，其中包括据作者所知，首次针对“乐观奖励式汤普森抽样”给出的高概率遗憾界。实验结果支持理论分析，并表明该框架可用于跨多个模型类别的超参数优化。

Conclusion: 该框架为两阶段在线优化提供了统一且可扩展的解决思路，将多种选择策略及其理论保证纳入同一分析体系，并展示了在实际超参数优化任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We study a two-stage online optimization problem: at each round, a meta-learner first selects an agent-environment pair, then the chosen agent interacts with the environment, and receives a reward. We introduce Meta-Frieren, a general framework for maximizing cumulative reward in this setting. Our analysis yields a simple UCB-style criterion for meta-level selection and recovers prior guarantees as special cases while extending them to broader settings. We instantiate Meta-Frieren with GP-UCB under Bayesian and frequentist assumptions. We also derive, to our knowledge, the first high-probability regret bound for Feel-Good Thompson Sampling, specialize it to finite and parametric function classes, and integrate it into Meta-Frieren. Experiments support our theoretical results and demonstrate that Meta-Frieren can be successfully applied to hyperparameter optimization across multiple model classes.

</details>


### [170] [Backbone-Informed Adaptation after EEG Pretraining: A Capacity-Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07192)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Fan Ma,Daniel Moyer,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较两类脑电适配器：在参数量近似匹配的条件下，融入骨干网络结构的适配器并未稳定优于逐令牌独立处理的多层感知机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电基础模型会整合不同通道与时间的信息，因此顺应这些关系设计适配器可能有利于下游学习。但这种结构化设计是否优于其他适配器尚不明确；公平比较还需控制可训练参数量。

Method: 固定预训练编码器，比较融入骨干网络结构的原生适配器与逐令牌多层感知机适配器，并近似匹配二者的可训练参数量。在两个脑电基础模型上，评估情绪识别、睡眠分期和脑电事件分类任务；另考察同时训练脑电片段序列处理组件时的影响。

Result: 原生适配器没有表现出一致优势。对一个骨干模型，两种设计的差异相较于不同运行之间的波动较小；对另一个模型，逐令牌适配器在情绪识别和脑电事件分类上表现更好。在睡眠分期中，若同时训练片段序列处理组件，原生适配器相对逐令牌适配器的优势会缩小。

Conclusion: 适配器的相对表现取决于骨干模型、任务和下游训练方案。不能仅凭其与骨干结构相符就认定原生适配器更优；评估时应将其与参数量匹配的替代方案进行比较。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models learn representations that combine information across channels and time. This structure motivates adapters that model the same relationships during downstream learning. However, it is not clear whether such a design outperforms other candidate adapters. Moreover, in order to arrive at a fair comparison between candidate adapters, we also need to consider their trainable parameters. Here, we ask whether a backbone-informed (native) adapter makes better use of a given capacity by comparing it against a token-wise multilayer perceptron control that transforms each feature vector independently. We approximately match their trainable parameter counts and keep the pretrained encoder fixed. Across two EEG foundation models and tasks covering emotion recognition, sleep staging, and EEG event classification, the native adapter shows no consistent advantage. For one backbone, differences between the designs are small relative to variation across runs. For the other, the token-wise control performs better on emotion recognition and EEG event classification. On sleep staging, the native adapter's advantage over the token-wise control is reduced when we also train the component that processes sequences of EEG segments. The relative performance of the two adapters depends on the backbone, task, and downstream training protocol. These findings motivate evaluating backbone-informed adapters against capacity-matched alternatives.

</details>


### [171] [TrustMix: Graceful Degradation for Pretrained Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07206)
*Juliusz Ziomek,Maike Osborne,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 信任混合是一种无需重新训练的后处理方法：当分布外偏移增大时，逐步将预训练模型的预测收缩至用户指定的备用分布，从而使外推行为可控，并改善分布偏移下的不确定性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在训练分布之外进行预测时，必然依赖关于真实结果如何外推的假设；但这些假设通常隐含在网络结构中，难以理解和控制。本文旨在让外推假设明确化，并允许用户将领域知识或其他合理假设纳入预测。

Method: 信任混合在预训练模型的预测分布与用户指定的备用分布之间进行组合。组合权重由分布外偏移统计量控制：偏移信号越强，预测就越趋近备用分布。该方法无需重新训练基础模型，可用于回归与分类；备用分布既可体现领域知识，也可基于与领域无关的平稳性假设。

Result: 在多种合成及真实分布偏移任务中进行了评估，涵盖表格数据、图像和自然语言处理，以及多种卷积网络和变换器架构。实验显示，该方法在分布内表现保持竞争力，并改善了偏移条件下的预测不确定性：回归任务的预测似然有所提升，分类任务的校准有所改善。此外，将其作为贝叶斯优化的替代预测分布时，也能支持决策应用。

Conclusion: 信任混合提供了一种轻量、可控且适用范围较广的后处理方案，使模型在分布偏移加剧时能够平滑退回到明确指定的备用预测，而非完全依赖基础模型隐含的外推行为。其效果依赖偏移统计量及备用分布的合理选择。

Abstract: Predicting beyond the training domain necessarily requires assumptions about how the ground truth extrapolates, yet in most neural networks these assumptions are made only implicit through the architecture. We propose TrustMix, a lightweight post-hoc method that makes a pretrained model's extrapolative behaviour explicit and controllable. TrustMix combines the predictive distribution produced by a pretrained model with a user-specified fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$, such that as the out-of-distribution (OOD) deviation statistic grows, we collapse towards $p_0(y|x)$. TrustMix provides graceful degradation: the predictive distribution converges toward the fallback distribution as the OOD signal grows. The fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$ can reflect domain knowledge, if available, or rely on a domain-agnostic stationarity assumption. TrustMix does not require retraining of the base model and can be applied to both regression and classification problems. We evaluated TrustMix on several synthetic and real-world distribution shifts, including tabular, image, and natural language processing tasks. We tested TrustMix on datasets such as CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet and on numerous architectures including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Transformers. TrustMix remains competitive in-distribution while improving predictive uncertainty under distribution shift, with gains in predictive likelihood for regression and calibration for classification. Finally, we showed that TrustMix can provide useful predictive distributions for decision-making applications by utilizing it as a surrogate for Bayesian Optimisation.

</details>


### [172] [Structured Machine Theory of Mind from Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07208)
*Nigel F. Doering,Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,M Tanjid Hasan Tonmoy,Jacob Doan,David Danks,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种具有明确心理状态表征的行人轨迹预测框架，通过信念与欲望的结构化监督，使模型不仅能预测行为，也能直接检验改变心理状态后行为如何变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大规模轨迹数据训练的模型通常只追求预测准确性，并未表示导致行为的信念、偏好等心理状态。因此，它们虽能预测行为，却难以回答“若个体的信念或偏好不同，其行为会如何改变”这类干预与反事实问题。

Method: 研究提出结构化机器心智理论框架，在信念—欲望—意图的因果结构中，从观察到的轨迹归因并独立监督信念和欲望表征。模型的目标预测模块仅接收预测出的心理状态通道和当前轨迹嵌入，因此可以直接替换信念或欲望，观察预测结果的变化。研究在一个可构造真实心理状态的受控行人导航场景中实例化模型，并评估心理状态归因、局部通道干预及轨迹层面的反事实效果。

Result: 该模型优于仅使用轨迹信息的基线和无监督情境表征基线；与具有匹配辅助监督、但缺少可编辑心理状态接口的基线相比，性能具有竞争力。反事实实验显示，将个体的偏好替换为另一活动类型，会使预测目的地相应转向相关地点；将某地点标记为个体认为不可用，会降低该地点成为目的地的预测概率，但影响过程较为复杂。

Conclusion: 在受控导航任务中，对信念与欲望进行结构化监督，能够为纵向轨迹数据的可解释分析和反事实研究提供可行基础。该方法的主要价值不仅在于预测表现，也在于提供可编辑的心理状态接口，使研究者能够直接考察心理状态变化对行为预测的影响。

Abstract: Predictive models of human behavior trained on large-scale trajectory data optimize for statistical accuracy without representing the mental states that causally generate behavior. Such models support prediction but not principled intervention: they cannot answer how an agent's behavior would change if its beliefs or preferences were different. We introduce Structured Machine Theory of Mind (SMToM), a framework that attributes explicit, independently supervised belief and desire representations from observed trajectories within a Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) causal structure. The central architectural element is a goal head that consumes only the predicted mental-state channels and a current-trajectory embedding; counterfactual intervention on beliefs and desires is a direct operation. We instantiate SMToM on a controlled pedestrian navigation domain where ground-truth mental states are known by construction, enabling evaluation of attribution accuracy, local channel interventions, and trajectory-level counterfactual diagnostics. The resulting model, BDIBottleneck, improves over trajectory-only and unsupervised-context neural baselines. It is also competitive with a matched auxiliary-supervised context baseline while providing an editable mental-state interface that the unbottlenecked baseline lacks. Counterfactual experiments on desires confirm that substituting an agent's inferred preferences with a different activity type coherently shifts predicted destinations toward relevant locations. Counterfactual experiments on beliefs confirm that marking a location as unavailable in the agent's belief state reduces its predicted probability as a destination, though in a complex way. These results demonstrate, in a controlled navigation setting, that explicit BDI-structured supervision is a viable foundation for interpretable and counterfactual analysis of longitudinal trajectory data.

</details>
