### [173] [Canonical Correlation Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07212)
*Andrea Cavallo,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出典型相关神经网络，利用多视图数据的视图内协方差与视图间协方差作为结构偏置；理论分析和七个数据集实验表明，该方法兼具鲁棒性、参数效率与良好任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格、图等关系结构能提升神经网络的参数效率和泛化能力，但通常需要预先已知结构。协方差可在缺少先验结构时提供替代偏置，现有协方差神经网络却只适用于单视图数据，无法充分利用多视图数据中的视图内关系及视图间联系。

Method: 提出典型相关神经网络，将视图内协方差和视图间协方差作为不同的关系偏置，用于直接完成下游任务。谱分析显示，该架构与典型相关分析存在联系；但不同于典型相关分析旨在提取共享表示，该方法直接针对任务训练。论文还分析有限样本协方差扰动下的输出误差，并以可控的滤波器利普希茨常数刻画稳定性。

Result: 在脑成像和运动传感领域的七个数据集上，该方法达到或超过单视图、双视图及典型相关分析基线，且通常使用更少参数。即使协方差由较少样本重新估计，模型也能保持性能。理论上，其稳定性受可控的滤波器利普希茨常数影响，而非典型相关分析稳定性所依赖的奇异值间隔。

Conclusion: 该研究将协方差关系偏置从单视图推广至多视图，为多视图下游任务提供了兼具理论依据、参数效率和有限样本鲁棒性的神经网络方案。

Abstract: Relational inductive biases such as grids or graphs allow neural networks with fewer trainable parameters and improved generalization, but require a known structure beforehand. When no structure is available, the covariance matrix provides a principled substitute, and coVariance Neural Networks (VNNs) have shown that using it yields models that are theoretically grounded in Principal Component Analysis, stable to finite-sample estimation errors and parameter-efficient. This framework, however, is limited to single-view data, whereas many applications observe the same phenomenon through multiple views, each with its own in-view covariance and a cross-view covariance linking them. To incorporate these matrices as inductive biases in a principled way, we introduce CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs), architectures that use in-view and cross-view covariances as distinct relational biases to solve downstream tasks. Via spectral analysis, CANEs draw analogies with Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA), a classical technique to project two-view data onto a correlated subspace. However, while CCA's objective is to compute shared view representations, CANEs are trained to solve a downstream task directly. We bound their output error under finite-sample covariance perturbations and show that it depends on controllable Lipschitz constants of the learned filters in place of the singular-value gap that governs the stability of CCA, thus guaranteeing better robustness. Experiments on seven datasets from brain imaging and motion sensing show that CANEs match or outperform single-view, two-view and CCA-based baselines, often with fewer parameters, and retain their performance when covariances are re-estimated from fewer samples.

</details>


### [174] [Fractional physics-informed neural networks to model social media virality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07215)
*Alex Koong,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种结合分数阶动力学与物理信息神经网络的方法，从社交媒体互动数据中学习记忆效应和非线性增长规律，并以符号回归生成可解释方程。对噪声进行正确建模并将时间窗口分段后，模型的轨迹重建效果优于经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交媒体传播模型通常预先规定互动量的增长形式，或假设过去活动影响未来互动的机制固定，因此难以同时揭示时间记忆效应与非线性增长动力学。研究还关注短观测窗口和噪声条件下，能否可靠地识别这些机制并形成可解释方程。

Method: 构建分数阶物理信息神经网络，同时学习分数阶数所表示的时间记忆特性，以及描述互动增长的非线性反应规律，再通过符号回归将学习到的动力学转化为方程。研究先以每小时观测、共二十四小时的合成数据检验方法，并针对噪声导致标准逐点物理残差将分数阶数推向下界的问题，提出将观测噪声经分数阶算子传播的完全白化残差。随后将平台X上的三百四十八条帖子数据按二十四小时窗口划分为四段，并采用前一段观测值驱动后一段建模。

Result: 在无噪声合成数据上，模型能够联合恢复分数阶数和反应规律；有噪声时，标准残差会产生尺度伪影，而完全白化残差旨在解决这一问题。在真实数据上，单窗口模型虽估计出强记忆依赖动力学，却难以准确重建留出轨迹。分段模型的分数阶数从前段的零点二三逐步升至末段的零点九九，并为各阶段得到可解释的增长规律。该模型将百分之六十四的留出轨迹重建至均方误差低于零点零一；九种经典基线中最强者的比例为百分之四十四。

Conclusion: 分数阶物理信息方程发现有望从社交媒体数据中提取兼具记忆效应与非线性增长特征的可解释动力学。可靠估计不仅需要考虑噪声在分数阶运算中的传播，也需要适应互动过程随时间变化的分段建模。

Abstract: Existing models of social media virality either prescribe the functional form of engagement growth or assume a fixed mechanism by which past activity influences future engagement. We present a fractional physics-informed neural network that learns both the temporal memory and nonlinear growth dynamics of social media engagement, using symbolic regression to translate the learned dynamics into interpretable equations. Synthetic experiments at the deployment resolution of 24 hourly observations demonstrate that the fractional order and reaction law can be jointly recovered from clean data. Under observation noise, however, the standard pointwise physics residual drives the fractional order toward its lower bound through a scale artifact. We address this failure using a fully whitened residual that propagates observation noise through the fractional operator. Applying the framework to 348 posts from X (formerly Twitter), we find that a single-window model estimates strongly memory-dependent dynamics but fails to accurately reconstruct held-out trajectories. Partitioning the 24-hour window into four teacher-forced segments substantially improves performance, yielding segment-specific fractional orders that increase from 0.23 to 0.99 and interpretable growth laws for each phase. The segmented model reconstructs 64% of held-out trajectories within a mean squared error of 0.01, compared with 44% for the strongest of nine classical baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of fractional physics-informed equation discovery to uncover interpretable engagement dynamics while highlighting the importance of noise-aware estimation and temporal segmentation.

</details>


### [175] [Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216)
*Hong kyu Lee,Kai Shu,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的内部干预方法，检验视觉语言模型的组合式安全是否只是表面行为。实验表明，模型虽能识别有害内容并在基准测试中拒绝作答，但其拒绝机制仍可能被定向干预轻易削弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式危害来自图像与提示语的跨模态组合，即使两者单独看都无害，也可能共同诱发有害请求。现有安全评估主要依赖语言模型评审观察输出行为，难以判断安全机制是否稳固。论文因此主张通过解除对齐来审计模型的安全鲁棒性，并指出相近的基准分数并不意味着相同的内部安全强度。

Method: 作者提出一种无需训练的组合式安全解除对齐方法：以模型对“拒绝”与“配合”回答的对数概率差衡量拒绝意图，计算该指标相对于中间层激活的梯度，再与部分无害的对照样本进行差分，以提取跨模态交互效应。利用八十个样例的一次反向传播即可得到干预方向；沿该方向调整模型，可逐步将拒绝转为配合。

Result: 在两种七十亿参数视觉语言模型上，该方法最多使组合式安全分数下降六十五分，同时超过百分之九十八的输出仍保持连贯，效果优于基于梯度的基线方法。所得方向还可迁移至文本明确表达的危害，并能削弱经过防御训练加固的模型。对比实验还发现，简单监督微调即可达到与多阶段强化学习流程相近的评审分数；九种常见方向识别方法虽能以最高零点九九的曲线下面积识别组合式危害，但消融这些方向要么无法破坏拒绝，要么会损害模型连贯性。

Conclusion: 组合式安全表现良好，并不代表其已稳固地整合进模型内部。通过针对跨模态交互机制进行定向干预，可以在基本保留输出连贯性的情况下解除拒绝行为。因此，解除对齐可作为传统行为评估的补充，用于揭示模型安全机制的脆弱性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are vulnerable to compositional harm: an image and a prompt that are benign in isolation become harmful through the model's cross-modal reasoning. Recent alignment methods report strong scores on compositional safety benchmarks, but an LLM judge only measures observed behavior and cannot reveal how robustly safety is integrated into the model. We argue that un-alignment provides a complementary audit of alignment robustness. We first show that minimal supervised fine-tuning reaches an LLM-judge score comparable to a multi-stage reinforcement-learning pipeline, so similar benchmark scores cannot establish robustness by themselves. We then show that recognition and refusal of compositional harm are decoupled: nine standard direction-finding methods detect compositional harm with up to AUC=0.99, yet ablating the recovered directions either leaves refusal intact or destroys model coherence. We therefore propose CMaG, a training-free un-alignment method for compositional safety. CMaG measures refusal intention with a refusal-versus-compliance log-probability margin, takes its gradient with respect to mid-layer activations, and contrasts it against partially benign pairs to isolate the cross-modal interaction term. A single backward pass over 80 examples yields a direction along which steering monotonically converts refusal into compliance. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.5-7B, CMaG reduces compositional safety scores by up to 65 points while over 98% of outputs remain coherent, outperforming gradient-based baselines; the direction also transfers to text-explicit harm and un-aligns models hardened by defensive training. Compositional safety can thus appear behaviorally strong while remaining fragile to targeted internal intervention. Warning: this paper contains unsafe model outputs, including references to self-harm.

</details>


### [176] [TileQuant: Tile as the Precision-Allocation Unit for LLM Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07217)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向硬件对齐二维权重块的训练后量化方法，在有限高精度预算下保留最敏感的权重块，并兼顾模型质量与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的权重敏感度并不均匀，统一位宽或按整列保留高精度，可能无法准确匹配局部敏感性；整列保留还会浪费高精度预算，而运行时检测异常值又会增加解码开销。因此，需要一种能刻画局部敏感性且适合高效硬件执行的精度分配方式。

Method: 提出训练免费的量化框架，将权重划分为与硬件对齐的二维块。离线校准通过前向统计评估输入激活相关的重要性，或利用损失敏感度获得更强的质量保持能力。对每个投影层，在既定高精度预算内选择得分最高的权重块保留为十六位，其余量化为八位。随后依据精度分配图重建紧凑权重缓冲区，并由计算内核复用，以原生整数和十六位运算及局部累加降低内存流量。

Result: 实验显示，该方法的生成吞吐量达到原始模型的二点六六倍。在语言建模、推理、长上下文检索和问答任务中，它能够保持模型质量，并在多项任务上优于量化模型及原始模型。摘要未提供具体基准、模型规模或精度损失数据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将精度分配单位从整列细化为硬件友好的局部权重块，有助于更精准地保护敏感参数，并同时提升推理效率。该方法为大语言模型的训练后量化提供了兼顾质量与部署成本的方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces large language model (LLM) deployment costs, but preserving quality requires accounting for uneven weight sensitivity. Uniform PTQ uses one weight bit-width, importance-aware PTQ adjusts scales or reconstructs weights within that width, and column-wise mixed precision retains selected columns at higher precision. However, sensitivity varies within columns, so whole-column retention also protects less sensitive weights, while runtime outlier detection adds decoding overhead. We therefore introduce TileQuant, a training-free framework that assigns precision to hardware-aligned 2D weight tiles. Offline calibration provides input-activation-aware scoring from forward statistics for lower calibration cost and gradient-informed scoring from loss sensitivity for stronger quality preservation. Within each projection's retention budget, the highest-scoring tiles remain at 16-bit and the rest use 8-bit. The resulting map guides reconstruction into compact weight buffers, which the kernel reuses for native integer and 16-bit computation with local accumulation to reduce memory traffic. Experiments show that TileQuant reaches 2.66x the original model's generation throughput. Across language modeling, reasoning, long-context retrieval, and question answering, it preserves quality and surpasses quantized and original models on multiple tasks. These results support selecting precision units that capture local sensitivity while enabling efficient hardware execution. The implementation is: https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileQuant

</details>


### [177] [Loopback Transformer: Towards a Unified View of Residual Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07226)
*Xuan Ouyang,Zefan Cai,Chenlong Zhang,Cheng Luo,Ke Wan,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出回环变换器，通过分别控制输入信息与各轮新增更新的权重，在减少参数量的同时提升验证损失和下游任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环变换器通过重复使用共享模块来减少参数，但参数共享本身并未解决重复计算结果如何保存、读取和组合的问题。若直接对模块完整输出加权，输入中已累积的历史信息也会被再次加权，难以独立控制输入与各轮更新的贡献。

Method: 论文从残差连接的深度—记忆视角分析信息流。回环变换器将共享堆栈的输入保留在独立路径上，并分别读取每轮产生的新更新，从而分开控制输入强度、更新总权重及其在不同轮次间的分配。更新总权重由几何衰减设定，并引入偏向近期更新的先验；注意力机制则学习如何分配这些权重。

Result: 在攀升混合基准上，训练数据量和实际计算深度相同的条件下，回环变换器在参考规模为1.91亿至30亿参数时，使用标准变换器基线40%至75%的变换器参数（不含词嵌入和输出头），仍取得更低的验证损失。在14亿和30亿参数规模下，其13项下游任务的平均准确率更高。在1.91亿、3.74亿和7.78亿参数规模下，它在标准变换器及七种循环残差设计中，同时取得最低验证损失和最高的13项任务平均表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，循环架构的效果不仅取决于是否共享参数，也取决于如何读取和加权重复计算产生的信息。将输入与各轮更新分路处理，并分别控制更新总量及轮次分配，有助于以较少参数获得更好的语言建模和下游任务表现。

Abstract: Looped Transformers reduce parameter count by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but parameter sharing alone does not determine how the results of repeated computation are reused. We take the depth-memory view of residual connections and analyze which representation is stored after each block application and how later reads weight it. A full block output contains both its input and its new update, so weighting that output also reweights the history already carried by the input. Reading individual updates instead, with the shared stack's input kept on a separate path, gives separate controls over input strength, total update weight, and allocation across updates. The Loopback Transformer implements these controls: geometric decay sets the total update weight and supplies a recency prior, and attention learns how to allocate that weight. On ClimbMix, Loopback reaches lower validation loss than standard Transformer baselines with 40-75% of their Transformer parameters (excluding token embeddings and the output head) at reference scales from 191M to 3B, with training tokens and executed depth matched. It also has higher mean accuracy over thirteen downstream tasks at 1.4B and 3B. At 191M, 374M, and 778M, it has the lowest validation loss and the highest thirteen-task mean among the standard Transformer and seven looped residual designs.

</details>


### [178] [Neural Compression over Equivalence Classes: Exact Gauges and Functional Rate-Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07233)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文利用神经网络参数表示的冗余，在不更换压缩器的前提下寻找更易压缩的参数表示；在小幅改变模型功能时，检查点压缩体积最多可减少百分之二十三点四。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一或近似相同的神经网络功能，往往对应许多不同的参数表示，而部署时通常直接保存训练得到的参数。论文希望将这种表示自由度转化为存储压缩资源，而非重新设计压缩算法。

Method: 研究者搜索保持函数完全不变的精确变换，以及允许受控功能扰动的变换，并使用原有压缩器评估、选择压缩成本更低的参数表示。精确变换用于严格审计下的零失真对照；受控扰动则探索更大的压缩收益。

Result: 在斯莫尔语言模型二代和通义千问三代上，使用赞斯坦德压缩时，检查点体积最多减少百分之二十三点四。使用其他常见压缩器时，也观察到相近比例的缩减。

Conclusion: 神经网络参数表示的冗余不仅是理论性质，也可作为实际存储优化资源。通过搜索等价或近似等价的参数表示，即使不改变物理压缩器，也能降低模型检查点的存储成本。

Abstract: Neural networks admit many parameterizations of the same or nearly the same function, but deployment typically stores whichever parameter vector training happens to produce. We show that this representational freedom can itself be used for compression. Rather than redesigning the codec, we search over exact function-preserving transformations and controlled functional perturbations, using the unchanged physical compressor itself to select cheaper representations. Exact gauge families provide a zero-distortion reference under the strict audit; allowing small functional changes exposes much larger rate-distortion gains. Across SmolLM2 and Qwen3, our method reduces Zstandard-compressed checkpoint size by up to 23.4\%, with similar relative reductions under gzip, Brotli, and ZipNN, establishing trained-network representational freedom as a practical storage resource.

</details>


### [179] [$D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241)
*Bowen Fang,Dingmin Wang,Hengzhi Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种改进的在线策略蒸馏方法，通过分别控制目标分布的外推方向与距离，在数学推理基准上优于现有方法，最高提升九点四五分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外推式在线策略蒸馏将外推方向和距离耦合，导致目标分布移动多远主要由教师模型的概率变化幅度决定，难以独立、稳定地控制外推。论文旨在解决这一问题，并增强学生模型的学习信号。

Method: 提出D²在线策略蒸馏，将外推方向与距离解耦：对每个前缀施加硬性相对熵约束，限制目标分布与教师分布之间的距离；再结合教师训练后的概率变化以及学生与教师初始差距，自适应确定外推方向。随后以正向相对熵训练学生，使低概率词元也能获得学习信号。

Result: 在三种设置下进行数学推理基准评测：标准在线策略蒸馏表现良好、基础模型初始化下表现受限，以及缺少教师参考模型。该方法在三种设置中均取得最高分，较最强基线最高提升九点四五分。消融实验验证了各组成部分的作用。

Conclusion: 通过独立控制外推方向和距离，并采用正向相对熵优化，D²在线策略蒸馏能更有效地利用教师信息，在多种蒸馏条件下提升数学推理表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Extrapolative OPD further extends the teacher's log-probability shift relative to a reference policy to construct stronger learning targets. However, existing methods couple extrapolation direction and distance, allowing the magnitude of the teacher's shift to dictate how far the target moves. We propose $D^2$-OPD, decoupling these two factors: a per-prefix hard KL constraint bounds the target's distance from the teacher, while an adaptive combination of the teacher's post-training shift and the initial student--teacher gap determines the extrapolation direction. We optimize the student toward this target using forward KL, providing learning signals even for low-probability tokens. We evaluate three settings: where standard OPD performs well, where it struggles with base-model initialization, and where no teacher reference is available. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, $D^2$-OPD attains the highest score in all three settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.45 points. Ablations validate the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [180] [MAC-v2: Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07245)
*Kwanyoung Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向像素输入离线强化学习的潜在世界模型方法，通过无重建表征学习与动作分块，实现更可靠的长时域模型价值扩展，并在多类视觉基准上取得较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素环境中的长时域模型价值扩展比基于状态的环境更困难：智能体既要学习视觉表征，也要学习环境动力学；表征或动力学的误差都会沿着想象轨迹递归累积。研究旨在探索该方法能否有效用于像素输入的离线强化学习。

Method: 提出一种不依赖图像重建的潜在世界模型，以预测和控制目标共同学习表征，避免编码与控制无关的视觉细节对动力学预测造成干扰。同时采用动作分块，使每次模型预测覆盖一段动作，从而减少价值扩展所需的递归预测次数，缓解误差累积。

Result: 在视觉离线强化学习基准上，包括视觉奥运会基准、运动匹配基准二、机器人操作基准和自由环境基准十，该方法相较于无模型及基于模型的离线强化学习基线取得了较强表现。分析还表明，长时域模型价值扩展对其性能至关重要；动作分块与潜在表征学习目标都是可靠长时域想象的重要组成部分。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过无重建的潜在表征学习与动作分块，可以提升像素环境中长时域模型价值扩展的可靠性，为离线强化学习提供有效框架。

Abstract: We study whether \emph{long-horizon model-based value expansion} can serve as an effective framework in pixel-based offline reinforcement learning (RL). Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels is substantially harder than state-based environments, as the agent must learn both the representation and its dynamics, and errors from either can accumulate recursively over imagined trajectories. We introduce MAC-v2, which enables long-horizon model-based expansion from pixels by training a reconstruction-free latent world model with action chunking. By learning representations from prediction and control objectives, MAC-v2 does not need to encode control-irrelevant visual details that can hinder the dynamics prediction. Action chunking further mitigates the prediction error by reducing the number of recursive model predictions required for value expansion. Across diverse visual benchmarks including visual OGBench, MMBench2, Robomimic, and LIBERO-10, MAC-v2 achieves strong performance against model-free and model-based offline RL baselines. Our analyses show that long-horizon model-based value expansion is critical for the performance of MAC-v2, and that both action chunking and latent learning objectives are essential for reliable long-horizon latent imagination.

</details>


### [181] [KG-Neighbor: Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Construction for Embedding Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07246)
*Chang Won Kim,Jaewook Lee,Haeun Min,JunNyeong Jeong,Minwoo Sim,Barom Kang,Kyohoon Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用知识图谱关系修正嵌入空间邻域的方法，并用内容与类别标签核查残余错配；实验显示该方法在八种数据集与编码器条件下均能提升至少一项布局指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化中，彼此接近的样本常被理解为语义相关，但仅由输入嵌入构建的邻域未必符合样本内容之间的关系。以此类邻域图为基础的降维方法可能延续这一偏差。此外，投影后的邻域与标签不一致，不一定意味着投影失败，也可能源于标签本身缺乏内容支持。

Method: 提出知识引导的邻域图构建方法：将每个样本的关系知识表示为知识图谱三元组，以样本间共享三元组的程度作为语义证据，调整嵌入距离并重新构建前若干近邻图；知识抽取和邻域构建均不使用类别标签。另提出独立诊断方法，分别判断样本内容是否支持其给定标签，以及是否支持邻域中的多数类别，从而分析残余错配。

Result: 在八种文本与图像数据集—编码器条件下，研究考察了不同引导强度及四种知识图谱相似度度量。遍历引导强度时，八种条件下的归一化互信息和二维局部结构指标的最高值均超过对应的近邻图基线；最大提升分别达到零点六八和一点三五。提升幅度与邻域重构程度因条件而异，且在基线得分较低的编码器条件下通常更大。诊断结果显示，最大幅度的减少集中于布局问题候选；剩余待复核样本中，标签问题和未解决案例占比较高。

Conclusion: 知识图谱关系可帮助缩小嵌入邻域与内容支持的语义关系之间的差距。对剩余错配进行细分也很重要：它不应被一概视为投影失败，还需判断问题是否来自布局、类别标签或现有证据无法判定。

Abstract: In embedding visualizations, nearby instances are interpreted as semantically related, although neighborhoods derived from input embeddings may not reflect content relationships. Neighborhood-based dimensionality reduction (DR) methods can propagate this limitation by optimizing layouts from embedding-based neighborhood graphs. We propose Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Graph Construction (KGN), which represents relational knowledge derived from each instance as a set of knowledge graph (KG) triples. It uses the degree of triple sharing as pairwise semantic evidence to adjust embedding distances and reconstruct the top-k neighborhood graph; neither KG extraction nor neighborhood construction uses class labels. We also introduce IDProfile (IDP), which interprets residual mismatches by independently assessing whether the source content supports the assigned class label and the neighborhood-majority class. Across eight text and image dataset-encoder conditions, we characterize KGN over the full guidance-strength sweep and four KG similarity measures. Across this sweep, the maximum NMI and LT_2D each exceed the corresponding kNN baseline in all eight conditions, with the largest gains reaching 0.68 and 1.35, respectively. The gains and the extent of neighborhood reconfiguration vary across conditions and are larger under encoder conditions with lower kNN baseline scores. The IDP analysis shows that the largest reductions are concentrated in Placement Issue candidates, whereas Label Issue and Unresolved Case instances account for a larger share of the remaining review set. These results indicate that KGN can narrow the evaluated gap between embedding-based neighborhoods and content-supported semantic relations, while IDP shows that a residual mismatch should not automatically be interpreted as a projection failure.

</details>


### [182] [Using Transformers for Teaching and Learning via Symbolic Distillation of Neural A-life Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07254)
*Shuwei Wang,Vadim Bulitko,Matthew R. G. Brown,Michael Youngblood,Ramon Lawrence,Roscoe Smith,Shinichi Nakagawa,William Yeoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用预训练的变换器模型，将人工生命智能体的神经策略蒸馏为紧凑、可理解且便于共享的符号策略，以降低蒸馏成本并促进群体学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工生命智能体的神经网络虽能通过多代演化提升生存能力，却难以被人类理解；网络权重也难以迁移到采用不同神经架构的智能体。将神经策略转化为符号策略，有望提升行为可解释性，并以紧凑形式在群体中传播知识。然而，传统方法需要在庞大的符号策略空间中进行不完备的随机搜索，计算开销较高。

Method: 作者提出先利用易于生成的随机感知—动作数据预训练变换器模型，再将其用于神经策略到符号策略的蒸馏，以加速这一过程。变换器的具体架构通过神经架构搜索确定。研究还比较不同蒸馏方法，并考察符号策略空间如何影响人工生命群体的动态，包括自我反思、教学与学习。

Result: 摘要报告，该方法能显著加快策略蒸馏，并通过实验比较了不同蒸馏方法及符号空间对群体动态的影响。摘要未提供具体的数值结果或各方法的详细优劣。

Conclusion: 预训练变换器为人工生命智能体的策略蒸馏提供了一种更高效的途径。紧凑的符号策略不仅可能让智能体行为更易理解，也有助于跨个体共享知识；其效果同时受到符号策略空间设计及群体学习过程的影响。

Abstract: Neural networks controlling behavior of A-life agents can evolve over multiple generations. While effective in making the agents survive, such networks are generally opaque to a human observer. Furthermore, the neural weights cannot be transferred between agents with different neural architectures. Distilling such neural networks into compact symbolic policies can help humans understand agent behavior in A-life. Additionally, it allows the agents to share their knowledge in a compact fashion, improving population-level learning. Distilling a neural policy into a symbolic policy is typically performed by searching the space of symbolic policies to find one that approximates the neural policy. However, even with a domain-specific language the space of policies can be large, necessitating incomplete stochastic search such as genetic algorithms. Yet the distillation remains computationally expensive. We propose to instead use a transformer pre-trained on readily available random percept-action data. Doing so speeds up distillation substantially. We derive the transformer architecture via a neural architecture search. Empirical results compare different distillation methods and the influence of the symbolic space on A-life population dynamics through self-reflection and teaching and learning.

</details>


### [183] [TriAttention: Unified Sparse Mixed-Precision Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07256)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按注意力交互重要性分配跳过、四位或八位计算的方案，在降低计算与缓存开销的同时维持模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏化和量化都能降低注意力计算成本，但两者通常没有区分不同交互的需求：有些交互可用低精度计算，却不能被删除。论文希望同时利用这种差异，并将策略以紧凑方式应用于长上下文场景。

Method: 该方法为每个注意力头设置两个对角距离边界，将查询与键的计算区域划分为跳过、四位和八位三种策略；边界数量固定，因此策略存储不随上下文长度增长。离线校准会评估候选边界对的归一化投影输出误差及完整计算块的成本，再由模型级分配器在共享计算预算下为各注意力头选择边界。预填充阶段依据策略生成按精度分组的遍历，并通过融合内核跳过指定计算块、合并两种精度的贡献以得到归一化注意力输出。随后，量化后的键和值用于压缩缓存，供解码阶段重复读取。

Result: 实验显示，该方法相较于火炬注意力实现，端到端生成速度最高提升七点八八倍；相较于半精度基线，最高提升二点六五倍。同时，在通用任务和长上下文任务上，模型质量与原始模型相当。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将保留与精度策略结合，并依据校准误差和计算预算进行分配，可以把局部敏感度转化为实际的注意力计算及缓存访问优化，在维持任务质量的同时显著提升生成效率。

Abstract: Sparsity and quantization reduce the cost of attention by skipping interactions and lowering the precision of those retained. However, useful interactions differ in their precision requirements: some tolerate low-precision scores while remaining sensitive to removal. We therefore introduce TriAttention, which assigns query-key (QK) tiles to skipping, 4-bit, or 8-bit computation, using 4-bit to retain quantization-tolerant interactions at lower cost. To represent compactly, we encode each head's computation regions with two diagonal-distance boundaries, keeping policy storage independent of context length. Offline calibration then evaluates candidate boundary pairs using normalized projected-output errors and complete-tile execution costs. Using these measurements, a model-wide allocator selects each head's boundaries to minimize calibration error under a shared computation budget. During prefill, the selected boundaries generate precision-grouped traversals for a fused kernel that skips omitted tiles and combines contributions from both QK precisions into one normalized attention output. The quantized keys and values prepared during prefill are then reused in a compressed key-value (KV) cache for decoding, reducing repeated reads of prompt data. Experiments show that TriAttention achieves end-to-end generation speedups of up to 7.88x over the Torch attention and 2.65x over the BF16 baseline. Meanwhile, TriAttention maintains quality comparable to the original models on general-purpose and long-context tasks. These results show how a compact retention-and-precision policy translates local sensitivities into attention computation and repeated KV-cache access. The implementation is https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TriAttention.

</details>


### [184] [Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood Learning: A Genetic Algorithm for Instance-Dependent k Optimization in Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07257)
*Isaac Touza,Kaladzavi Guidedi,Kolyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种按样本自适应选择邻居数量的方法，以局部数据特征决定分类时采用的邻居规模；实验显示其表现与经过调优的全局邻居数方法相当，并优于默认设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最近邻分类器通常为所有样本统一设定邻居数，但偏差与方差的权衡会随局部数据结构变化：稠密、少噪声区域可能适合较小邻域，稀疏或噪声较多区域则可能需要较大邻域。论文认为，仅对单一全局参数进行遗传搜索收益有限，因此转向优化随样本变化的邻域选择策略。

Method: 提出进化自适应邻域选择方法，利用低维策略将局部密度、标签熵、投票差距和邻居集中度等特征映射为各样本对应的邻居数量。采用实数编码的精英遗传算法优化该策略，以留一法准确率为目标，并加入邻域平滑惩罚；同时预先计算邻居投票，以降低适应度评估成本。论文还分析其训练准则下相对全局方案的优势、进化收敛性、策略正则性及泛化保证。

Result: 在具有空间异质结构的合成数据和十二个公开数据集上，论文通过嵌套交叉验证评估预测表现、鲁棒性与计算成本。摘要报告称，该方法总体上可与调优后的全局邻居数基线竞争，并优于默认邻居数设置；同时考察了训练与测试表现之间的差距。摘要未提供具体数值，因此无法判断提升幅度及统计显著性。

Conclusion: 研究将邻居数量选择从全局参数调优扩展为基于局部特征的自适应策略，并结合进化优化与理论分析。初步实验支持其相较默认设置的实用价值，但其相对精细调优基线的优势并非摘要所能确认；训练与测试差距及计算成本仍是评估实际收益的重要因素。

Abstract: The number of neighbours k is the central hyperparameter of the k-nearest neighbour (k-NN) classifier and is usually selected globally through cross-validation or evolutionary search. However, the bias-variance trade-off is inherently local: dense and noise-free regions may favour small neighbourhoods, whereas sparse or noisy regions may require larger ones. Since global-k optimization is one-dimensional, replacing exhaustive search with a genetic algorithm offers little computational advantage. We therefore optimize an instance-dependent neighbourhood policy instead. We propose EANK (Evolutionary Adaptive Neighborhood K), which uses a low-dimensional policy to map local neighbourhood descriptors, including density, label entropy, vote margin, and concentration, to an adaptive number of neighbours k(x). A real-coded elitist genetic algorithm optimizes this policy using leave-one-out accuracy with a neighbourhood-smoothness penalty. Precomputed neighbour votes reduce fitness evaluation costs. We investigate theoretical properties of the method, including dominance over global-k solutions on the training criterion, evolutionary convergence, policy regularity, and generalization guarantees. Experiments on synthetic data with spatially heterogeneous structure and twelve public datasets evaluate predictive performance, robustness, and computational cost. Under nested cross-validation, EANK achieves competitive performance relative to tuned global-k baselines and improves upon the default k=5. We also examine the gap between training and test performance to assess the practical value of adaptive neighbourhood selection.

</details>


### [185] [Reweighting is not Harmless: Understanding the Role of Dataset Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07258)
*Amalia Ionescu,Jose G. Hernandez,Emily F. Wong,Anil K. Saini,Jason H. Moore,Tiffani J. Bright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，重加权虽能改善群体间的分类公平指标，却可能通过加重高基准事件率群体的漏判来实现；总体准确率不变，因而这种伤害容易被忽视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重加权常用于缓解模型对代表性不足群体的系统性低估，但其公平效果可能受群体规模和事件基准发生率影响。论文旨在检验该方法是否会在改善某一群体处境的同时，把低估问题转移给另一群体。

Method: 研究通过受控模拟改变群体规模与事件基准发生率，生成三千组数据集，事件发生率为百分之七至百分之十四；随后在六类分类器上比较重加权前后的分类结果、公平指标与总体准确率。

Result: 重加权改善了群体间正向分类比例和漏判率的均等程度，但部分改善来自高基准事件率群体的正向分类减少、漏判率上升。当该群体同时是少数群体时，漏判率上升六个百分点，即每一万名群体成员约多漏判八十二例；约四分之三的漏判率均等改善源于这一恶化，而非另一群体得到改善。该现象在六类分类器中均出现，总体准确率基本不变。

Conclusion: 公平指标改善不一定意味着各群体都受益，也可能掩盖伤害在群体间的转移。应用重加权前，应结合数据特征与实际目标评估其影响；若目标是在不加重其他群体低估的前提下减少某一群体的低估，重加权未必适用。

Abstract: Reweighting is a widely used pre-processing method for promoting discrimination-free model behavior by assigning different weights to data points during model training. While it is often credited with improving fairness, particularly by mitigating systematic underestimation for underrepresented groups, it can also introduce unintended harms under certain data conditions. Using controlled simulations that vary group size and baseline event prevalence, we show how reweighting can yield misleading improvements in fairness by reducing underestimation for one group while introducing it for another. Across 3,000 simulated datasets with event prevalence of 7-14%, reweighting improved parity in positive classification and false negative rates (FNR) between groups, but did so partly by decreasing positive classifications and increasing FNR for the group with higher baseline prevalence. These effects were most pronounced when that group was a minority: its FNR rose by 6 percentage points, approximately 82 additional missed cases per 10,000 group members, and roughly three-quarters of the improvement in FNR parity came from this increase rather than from gains for the other group. The pattern held across six classifiers, from logistic regression to neural networks, while overall accuracy was essentially unchanged, leaving the shift invisible in aggregate performance metrics. These findings mirror patterns reported in real-world applications and underscore the critical role of data characteristics in shaping the impact of this fairness intervention. While reweighting may create desirable outcomes in some applications, practitioners should critically evaluate it relative to their problem area and goals. Specifically, when the goal is to reduce underestimation in one group without introducing underestimation in another, reweighting may not be the appropriate approach.

</details>


### [186] [Energy Measurement and Optimization of TinyML in Resource-Constrained Embedded Systems: A Systematic Mapping Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07264)
*Clariele Almeida,Renan Beserra,Rafael Moura,Danilo Araújo,Bruno Nogueira,Sérgio Chevtchenko,Ermeson Andrade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理面向能耗的微型机器学习研究，归纳常见模型、能耗评估方法、影响因素与优化策略，并指出实验标准化不足妨碍研究复现和结果比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备在网络边缘产生的数据不断增加，但微控制器和智能传感器受存储、算力及能源限制。尽管微型机器学习支持此类设备执行机器学习任务，现有研究在算法、能耗测量、评价指标、影响因素和优化方法方面较为分散，缺少统一整理。

Method: 研究采用系统性映射方法，梳理并分类相关文献中的主要模型与学习方法、能耗评估方法及指标、能耗影响因素，并将优化技术归纳为结构化分类体系。

Result: 研究发现，卷积模型和轻量化架构较常见；能耗评估通常采用直接测量或分析估算。能耗影响因素包括模型架构、硬件、通信、数值精度和输入特征。优化策略可分为模型压缩、硬件感知设计、系统与通信优化，以及数据与处理优化。实验平台、测量流程和报告方式缺乏标准化，限制了结果复现与横向比较。

Conclusion: 本文提供了能耗感知微型机器学习领域的最新研究概览，并为设计和评估更节能的解决方案提供分类框架与参考。未来研究需加强实验平台、测量程序和报告规范的统一，以提升可复现性和研究结果的可比性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) has increased the volume of data generated at the network edge, where many devices operate under severe memory, processing, and energy constraints. In this context, Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) enables machine learning (ML) tasks to be executed on microcontrollers, smart sensors, and other resource-constrained platforms. However, the literature remains heterogeneous regarding the algorithms evaluated, energy-measurement methods, reported metrics, influencing factors, and optimization strategies. This study presents a systematic mapping of energy-aware TinyML research. It identifies the main models and learning approaches, classifies energy-evaluation methodologies and metrics, examines the technical factors affecting energy consumption, and organizes the reported optimization techniques into a structured taxonomy. The findings highlight the frequent use of convolutional and lightweight architectures, direct measurements and analytical estimations, and factors related to model architecture, hardware, communication, numerical precision, and input characteristics. The optimization strategies were grouped into model compression, hardware-aware design, system and communication optimization, and data and processing optimization. The results also reveal limited standardization in experimental platforms, measurement procedures, and reporting practices, limiting reproducibility and direct comparison across studies. This mapping provides an updated overview of the field and guidance for developing and evaluating more energy-efficient TinyML solutions.

</details>


### [187] [How Deep is the Signal? Time-Context up to 30 Years for Colorectal Cancer Prediction in a Primary-Care EHR Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07265)
*Achille Raviez,Lan Wang,Brendan Delaney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出“时间上下文”这一评估维度，系统比较电子健康记录模型回看二至三十年的预测表现；对结直肠癌预测而言，性能约在十八年后趋于平稳，约八成提升在十年内获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录基础模型通常以令牌数衡量上下文长度，但令牌数无法说明模型需要回看患者多少年的病史，才能捕捉最早的预测信号。研究旨在明确长期病史对临床预测的价值，并将日历时间跨度作为独立于令牌上下文的设计与报告维度。

Method: 研究者使用伦敦西北部三百六十万名基层医疗患者的一百四十亿条编码医疗事件，预训练一个三千四百六十万参数的仅解码器变换器，并微调以预测新发结直肠癌。评估队列包括一千零八名病例和九千五百八十二名匹配对照，所有患者均有至少三十年登记记录；研究在同一批患者中设置二至三十年共十五种嵌套回看窗口，并采用诊断前十二个月作为缓冲期。此外，研究将模型与树提升基线比较，并模拟旋转位置编码对长时间跨度的表示特性。

Result: 模型区分能力随回看时间延长而提升：受试者工作特征曲线下面积从两年的零点五五一升至十八年的零点五九零，之后趋于平台；约百分之八十的性能增益在十年内实现。树提升基线达到相近水平，最高为零点五八九。模拟显示，默认基频下的旋转位置编码在约二十年后可能出现时间位置混叠，提示需要调整位置编码的基频参数。

Conclusion: 时间上下文能够衡量病史时间跨度对预测性能的影响，是不同于令牌上下文的模型设计维度。对于本研究的结直肠癌预测任务，延长回看窗口有益但收益逐渐递减，且长期时间位置编码可能需要专门校准。研究建议临床预测模型报告其时间上下文，并指出结论仍应结合具体任务与数据集验证。

Abstract: EHR foundation models read medical events within a fixed context window, and long context has so far been measured only in tokens. How far back in time a model must look to capture the earliest predictive signal is unknown. We introduce time-context, the calendar lookback window before the prediction point, and provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic evaluation of decade-scale time-context for an EHR foundation model. We pretrained a decoder-only transformer (34.6M parameters) on 1.4B coded events from 3.6M primary-care patients in North West London and fine-tuned it to predict incident colorectal cancer under a 12-month pre-diagnostic buffer. A frozen cohort of 1,008 cases and 9,582 matched controls, all registered for at least 30 years, was evaluated at 15 nested lookback windows from 2 to 30 years, so that every truncation is compared within the same patients. Discrimination rose from an AUROC of 0.551 at 2 years to 0.590 at 18 years, then plateaued, with about 80% of the gain reached within 10 years; an XGBoost baseline converged to the same level (peak AUROC 0.589). A simulation further suggests that rotary position embeddings (RoPE) over elapsed time alias beyond about 20 years at the default base frequency, motivating a retuned $θ$. Time-context is a measurable design axis for clinical prediction models, distinct from token-context, and should be reported.

</details>


### [188] [Satisficing as competitive Bayesian weighting: a minimal model of goal-directed choice under an unattained target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07270)
*Moto Shinriki,Yu Kono,Tatsuji Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将风险敏感满足规则重新表述为一种贝叶斯式估计模型，说明未实现的目标如何约束选择，并预测停止选择的选项会逐渐向目标水平重新估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物和人类的选择往往不是为了最大化收益，而是围绕尚未达到的目标或期望水平展开。论文关注的问题是：尚未实现的目标如何组织行为？作者据此将目标视为限制选择的约束，而非某个特定的最终状态。

Method: 作者在多臂赌博机这一简化形式框架中研究风险敏感满足规则。该规则依据每个选项过去被尝试的比例，以及其结果相对期望水平的盈余或不足来评价选项。论文进一步将其改写为一种类似贝叶斯估计的竞争性注意模型：期望水平充当所有选项的先验均值，有限的试验预算则决定观察证据能够在多大程度上修正先验。

Result: 模型带来两项结果。第一，某个选项停止被采样后，其估计值会逐渐回归期望水平；这并非删除旧数据，而是目标对过往观察的相对影响增强，因此称为“伪遗忘”，有别于显式设置遗忘率的模型。第二，当期望水平无法达到时，探索不会稳定下来。伯努利多臂赌博机模拟还显示，根据证据调整期望水平能够降低后悔值，即预期损失。

Conclusion: 该模型以可分析的方式刻画了未实现目标与有限注意资源如何共同约束行为，并提出可检验的预测：生物体会如何重新评价已经放弃的选项。

Abstract: Animals and humans often do not maximize; they act relative to goals (aspiration levels) that are frequently not yet met. How can a goal that is not (yet) realized organize choice? Deacon (2012) calls such absence-referenced organization \emph{ententional}, and García-Valdecasas and Deacon (2024) argue that ends are best understood as constraints rather than as particular states. We examine this idea in a minimal formal setting: Risk-sensitive Satisficing (RS), a bandit learning rule that values each action by its share of past trials multiplied by its surplus or shortfall relative to an aspiration level. We show that RS can be rewritten as a Bayesian-like estimator, Attention Competitive Bayes (ACB), in which the aspiration level is the prior mean of every action and the finite budget of trials sets how strongly evidence can override it. Two consequences follow. First, actions that are no longer sampled are partially overridden by the goal rather than by past observations: their estimates relax toward the aspiration level without any data being discarded. This ``pseudo-forgetting'' distinguishes ACB from models with an explicit forgetting rate. Second, when the aspiration is unattainable, exploration does not settle; in Bernoulli bandit simulations, adjusting the aspiration level from evidence reduced regret (expected loss). ACB offers an analyzable model in which an unattained goal and a limited attentional resource jointly constrain behavior, and it yields testable predictions about how organisms revalue options they have abandoned.

</details>


### [189] [Graph Neural Networks for Resource Allocation in Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07277)
*Junyu Liao,Lili Chen,Tian Han,Jamie Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的车辆通信感知融合功率分配方法，在满足感知约束的同时提升通信速率，并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆通信感知融合网络中的服务车辆需要在有限功率预算下同时服务多个感知目标和通信接收端。最大化通信总速率并满足感知信干噪比约束属于非凸优化问题，传统迭代算法计算成本较高，且每次网络布局变化时都需重新求解。

Method: 提出图神经网络功率分配算法，通过两类边分别表示不同服务提供方之间的干扰，以及同一服务提供方内部的功率预算共享。算法采用带感知惩罚项的总速率损失进行无标签训练。此外，针对该问题改造加权均方误差最小化算法，并加入感知功率预留步骤作为比较方案。

Result: 仿真显示，该图神经网络方法在感知链路违约率较低的情况下，取得接近改造后加权均方误差最小化算法的通信总速率，同时计算量显著更低。它还优于已有的异构图神经网络方法和不利用信道信息的基线，并能推广至更大规模网络而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 利用区分干扰关系与功率共享关系的图结构，可以有效学习车辆通信感知融合网络中的功率分配策略。该方法兼顾通信性能、感知约束与计算效率，并具备扩展到更大网络的能力。

Abstract: In vehicular integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) networks, each service-provider vehicle uses a limited power budget to serve multiple sensing targets and communication receivers. Maximizing the communication sum-rate subject to sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) constraints is nonconvex, and iterative optimization is costly to rerun for every network layout. This paper proposes a graph neural network (GNN)-based power allocation algorithm, termed GNN-ISAC. Concretely, GNN-ISAC addresses the aforementioned problem by considering two distinct edge types in the graph representation, one capturing the inter-provider interference, the other representing the intra-provider power-budget sharing. GNN-ISAC is trained without labels using a sensing-penalized sum-rate loss. We also adapt the weighted mean-square error minimization (WMMSE) algorithm to this problem by introducing a sensing-power reservation step. Simulation results show that GNN-ISAC achieves communication sum-rates close to WMMSE with low sensing-link violation rates and much less computation. It also outperforms an existing heterogeneous GNN and a channel-blind baseline, and generalizes to larger networks without retraining.

</details>


### [190] [Scenario Augmentation for Rare Financial Events via Multimodal Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07282)
*Mahdi Arab Loodaricheh,Anita Raja,Indranil SenGupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用多模态潜变量生成扩充稀缺的极端市场样本，提升金融风险分类效果，并生成可用于压力测试的情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端市场波动样本数量少、类型多样，历史数据类别不平衡，使模型难以学习少数风险状态。研究关注生成式数据扩充能否改善基于条件风险价值标记的罕见市场窗口表征与分类，同时保留不同金融数据表示之间的互补信息。

Method: 将每个市场窗口表示为三种对齐模态：结构化市场变量、空间图像变换和投资组合收益序列。分别使用变分自编码器将各模态压缩为潜变量，再训练类别条件扩散模型学习这些潜变量的联合分布，并依据条件风险价值定义罕见窗口、引导模型生成少数类样本。实验覆盖美国科技股的四种窗口长度，并与表格数据扩散基线比较。

Result: 生成样本在保真度和支持度方面优于表格扩散基线。使用真实样本与生成样本训练的极端梯度提升分类器，受试者工作特征曲线下面积达到0.953至0.960，高于表格生成器方案及相应的仅使用真实数据的基线。

Conclusion: 多模态潜变量生成能够有效扩充稀缺的金融极端风险样本，并改善风险分类表现，显示其可为数据不足的金融风险模型提供有用的合成压力情景。

Abstract: Extreme market movements are scarce, heterogeneous, and difficult to learn from imbalanced historical data. We study whether generative augmentation can improve the representation and classification of CVaR-labeled rare financial windows while retaining the complementary structure exposed by different financial representations. Each market window is expressed through three aligned modalities: structured market variables, a spatial image transformation, and a sequence of portfolio returns. Separate variational autoencoders map the modalities to compact latent codes, and a class-conditional diffusion model learns their joint latent distribution. Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) provides a risk-aware definition of rare windows and directs generation toward the minority regime. Across four window lengths on U.S. technology equities, the resulting synthetic samples improve fidelity and support relative to tabular diffusion baselines. An XGBoost classifier trained with real and generated samples attains AUCs of 0.953 - 0.960, exceeding both tabular generators and the corresponding real-only baselines. These results show that multimodal latent generation can provide useful synthetic stress scenarios for data-scarce financial risk models.

</details>


### [191] [How do Transformers Perform Low-Level Control In-Context?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07291)
*Ebonye Smith,Aidan Andrews,Phoenix Wilson,Alexis Frias,Ayush Pandey,Gireeja Ranade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究变换器能否仅凭系统类别信息和无控制状态轨迹，在参数未知时实现不稳定非线性系统的上下文控制；实验表明该能力可行，且增加上下文通常能提升控制表现，并具备一定分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换器展现出的上下文学习能力启发了“上下文控制”问题：控制器能否根据系统演化数据，在线推断未知系统特性并采取控制，而不依赖具体参数信息或推理阶段的专家示范？这对参数未知、动力学复杂的系统控制具有潜在价值。

Method: 采用多头、类生成式预训练变换器的架构，研究倒立摆和双连杆杂技机器人两类经典非线性系统。模型参数在训练中未知；训练阶段提供专家控制器生成的轨迹，推理阶段则不提供专家轨迹。上下文仅包含系统未受控时的动力学轨迹。研究还分析了训练检查点，以识别训练低层控制策略所需的关键技术，并考察上下文长度及分布外系统上的表现。

Result: 检查点分析显示，变换器能够进行上下文控制；总体而言，提供更长的上下文会改善策略表现。模型还能够泛化到训练分布之外的系统并实现稳定控制。摘要未提供具体数值结果或各训练技术的细节。

Conclusion: 研究表明，变换器可以利用无控制轨迹作为上下文，在缺少系统参数和推理阶段专家示范的条件下控制不稳定非线性系统。这为将上下文学习扩展到动力系统控制提供了初步证据，同时也凸显了适当训练技术的重要性。

Abstract: One of the remarkable abilities of Transformer-based neural networks is in-context learning. This motivates us to think about "in-context" control. Can we control a system knowing only (1) that it belongs to a particular family of systems and (2) some data on system evolution (provided as the context trajectory) but no specific parameter information? Specifically, we investigate the ability of Transformer-models to perform "in-context" control of unstable, nonlinear dynamical systems, where context consists only of the unforced system dynamics. We leverage a multi-head, GPT-2-style architecture, and we evaluate performance for two classical nonlinear systems -- the cartpole system and the two-link acrobot system, where the system parameters are unknown to the Transformer. We consider a setting where trajectories from an expert controller are available at training time but not at inference time. Here, we identify training techniques that are essential to train Transformers to perform low-level control. Through an analysis over checkpoints, we show that in-context control is possible, and the learned policy performance generally improves as context increases. Furthermore, the Transformer models can generalize to stabilize systems that are out-of-distribution.

</details>


### [192] [Provably Efficient Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07295)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Shengpu Tang,Wanyun Si,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向低秩约束马尔可夫决策过程的无模型表示学习算法，在不显式估计转移核的情况下，实现奖励与约束的联合优化，并给出与状态空间规模无关的次线性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束马尔可夫决策过程中，学习者既要最大化累计奖励，又要控制累计效用约束的违反程度。现有方法难以同时处理未知特征表示、低秩转移结构与约束优化；尤其是未知特征情形下，缺少奖励遗憾和约束违反的次线性理论保证。

Method: 提出低秩约束马尔可夫决策过程的无模型表示学习算法。算法结合最小二乘价值迭代与置信上界方法，以及原始—对偶优化机制，以协调奖励表现和约束满足；通过函数逼近奖励项增强价值估计，并直接学习从表示到价值函数的映射，避免显式估计转移核。

Result: 理论分析表明，以高概率计，经过 K 轮部署后，奖励遗憾和累计约束违反均为带对数因子的 K 的四分之三次方量级，且该界不依赖状态空间大小。论文称，这是针对未知特征的约束马尔可夫决策过程首次给出的奖励遗憾与约束违反次线性保证。

Conclusion: 该方法将表示学习、低秩结构、置信价值估计与原始—对偶约束控制结合，为未知特征下的约束决策提供了无需显式建模转移动态的理论方案。其核心贡献是同时保证奖励性能与约束控制的次线性表现。

Abstract: We study representation learning in low-rank Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs), where both value and reward functions are approximated by a set of unknown representation vectors, and the transition dynamics admit a low-rank factorization. The objective is to maximize expected cumulative rewards while complying with constraints on the expected cumulative utility. To achieve this, we propose \texttt{MFRLC} (Model-Free Representation Learning for Low-Rank CMDP), a model-free algorithm that uses a primal-dual scheme to effectively balance reward regret and constraint violations. To the best of our knowledge, \texttt{MFRLC} is the first representation-learning method for low-rank CMDPs that combines the Least-Squares Value Iteration with Upper Confidence Bound (LSVI-UCB) and primal-dual techniques. \texttt{MFRLC} further enhances value function estimation with bonuses for function approximation and learns the mapping from representations to value functions directly, avoiding explicit estimation of the transition kernel. We prove that, with high probability, \texttt{MFRLC} achieves $\widetilde O(K^{3/4})$ reward regret and cumulative constraint violation over $K$ deployment rounds, independent of the size of the state space. To our knowledge, these are the first sublinear regret and violation guarantees for CMDPs with unknown features.

</details>


### [193] [WeightLoop: Weight-Space Programming with Experimental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07319)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Weijing Zhao,Chengyang Zhang,Boyu Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zelin Zang,Victor C. M. Leung,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeightLoop 将实验测量结果转化为预测模型的持久参数更新，使模型能在不重新训练、也不反复调用大语言模型的情况下逐步改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型参与实验设计时，可以从已完成实验中获得预测误差，并据此决定后续测试。然而，常规反馈往往只影响下一步决策，不会直接改变已部署的预测器。论文旨在建立一条由实验反馈驱动模型持续改进的路径。

Method: 先由大语言模型检查任务说明和模型实现，并生成一个受限、可复用的参数编辑程序。之后，每当新测量结果到达，该程序便识别预测偏差，利用实际观测验证候选参数修改，并将选定修改写回原模型。编辑程序保持固定，预测器则随证据逐步演化；整个过程无需梯度训练或后续大语言模型调用，并保留原有推理接口。

Result: 离线回放实验显示，在八种终点与实验预算组合中，针对性参数编辑有七种提升了平均排序效用；与随机编辑相比，八种组合均有提升。此外，在三个连续任务中，WeightLoop 提高了平均最终发现水平。

Conclusion: WeightLoop 将实验结果直接转化为模型参数变化，并让更新后的模型继续指导后续决策，连接了权重空间编程与受反馈驱动的递归自我改进。结果支持这一机制在所测任务中的有效性，但摘要未提供更广泛任务上的验证或具体提升幅度。

Abstract: Weight-space programming (WSP) shows that language models can reason about and rewrite neural network weights; recursive self-improvement (RSI) motivates feedback-driven improvement. Models guiding experiments receive concrete feedback: completed assays reveal prediction errors and inform what to test next. We introduce WeightLoop, a framework that turns experimental feedback into persistent updates to deployed predictors. GPT-6 Astra inspects a task specification and the model implementation, then writes a bounded, reusable parameter-editing program. As new measurements arrive, the program identifies prediction discrepancies, validates candidate edits using observed outcomes, and writes the selected changes back to the original model. The editor remains fixed while the predictor evolves, enabling repeated updates without further LLM calls or gradient retraining while preserving the original inference interface. In offline replay, targeted edits improve mean ranking utility in seven of eight endpoint-budget settings (all eight versus random edits) and mean final discovery across three sequential tasks. WeightLoop connects WSP with an RSI-inspired improvement loop, making experimental evidence a direct driver of model change and subsequent decisions.

</details>


### [194] [Rethinking Data Selection: The Importance of Coverage over Difficulty in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07323)
*Lalchand Pandia,Kanishka Misra,Allyson Ettinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型微调选数时，覆盖输入分布往往比挑选高难度样本更重要；基于聚类的覆盖式选样在多领域任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据选择能够降低大语言模型微调的计算成本。以往方法常依据样本难度打分，认为难例更有信息量，但这类方法在大语言模型微调中往往不如随机选样。研究旨在解释这一现象，并寻找更有效的选数原则。

Method: 研究分析不同选样方法的表现，发现随机选样的优势与其对底层输入空间具有更好的代表性和覆盖度有关。为验证覆盖度的重要性，作者设计了简单的聚类选样方法，从数据中选择更能代表输入分布的样本，并在多种单领域任务及图鲁三多领域混合数据上进行评估。

Result: 在多种单领域任务中，聚类选样的表现达到或超过随机选样；在随机抽样难以保证领域均衡的图鲁三多领域混合数据上，聚类选样优于所有对比方法。基于难度的选样方法则常常无法胜过随机选样。

Conclusion: 大语言模型微调的数据选择应优先考虑对输入分布的覆盖与代表性，而非单纯追求高难度样本。覆盖式选样尤其适用于领域多样、随机抽样容易造成分布失衡的场景。

Abstract: Selecting high-quality training data can reduce computation cost for LLM fine-tuning. Prior work on data selection developed a variety of difficulty-based scores, motivated by the intuition that harder examples are more informative. While effective for smaller classification models, we find that such methods often fail to outperform random selection for LLM fine-tuning. Our analysis reveals that random selection performs better because it is more representative of the underlying input space---i.e., has better coverage. To validate the importance of coverage, we implement a simple clustering-based selection method which selects data that is more representative of the underlying input distribution. On a set of diverse single-domain tasks, this method yields performance at or above that of random selection. On the diverse, multi-domain Tülu 3 mixture, where random sampling no longer guarantees a balanced subset, our method outperforms all methods. Overall, our results indicate that prioritizing coverage over difficulty leads to more effective data selection for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [195] [Permanent and Transient Representations for Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07327)
*Nishanth Anand,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将持续强化学习中的长期稳定学习与短期快速适应分开处理：长期价值函数采用参数化表示，瞬态价值函数采用非参数近似，并在图像任务和《工艺世界经典版》中取得优于竞争基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习智能体面对新情境时，往往难以在吸收新知识的同时保留旧知识，形成稳定性与可塑性之间的权衡。互补学习系统为此提供思路：分别设置长期学习系统与快速适应系统。已有工作虽采用永久价值函数和瞬态价值函数，但两个系统使用相同特征，未充分利用它们不同的学习需求。论文据此提出，长期学习适合依靠参数化表示进行广泛泛化，而瞬态学习更适合依靠非参数表示针对具体情境快速调整。

Method: 论文从理论和实证两方面研究上述表示分工，并针对复杂任务提出一种新的非参数近似方法，用于估计瞬态价值函数。该方法支持在线学习，同时与长期学习系统形成互补；摘要未提供具体算法细节或理论结论。

Result: 实验显示，该方法在基于图像的任务以及《工艺世界经典版》中优于有竞争力的基线方法，表明以非参数表示实现瞬态适应具有实际效果。摘要未报告具体数值或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 将长期学习与瞬态适应进行系统层面的分解，并为两者配置不同类型的表示，有助于缓解持续强化学习中的稳定性与可塑性冲突。论文结果支持长期采用参数化表示、短期采用非参数表示这一设计方向。

Abstract: Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL) agents struggle to adapt to new situations while retaining prior knowledge, leading to a stability-plasticity trade-off. One approach to solving this problem is by using complementary learning systems: one for slow, long-term learning and another for quick, transient adaptation. Anand & Precup (2023) instantiated this idea using permanent and transient value functions, but used the same features in both systems. Intuitively, long-term learning should rely more on parametric representations for broad generalization, while transient learning would be best served by non-parametric representations for situation-specific adaptation. In this paper, we explore this idea both theoretically and empirically. By introducing a novel non-parametric approximation for estimating the transient value function in complex tasks, we demonstrate that our method enables online learning and outperforms competitive baselines on image-based tasks and Craftax-Classic, underscoring the effectiveness of our system-level decomposition.

</details>


### [196] [AtlasULP: Domain-aware Universal Link Prediction via Relation Atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07347)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Heng Tao Shen,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于关系图谱与领域引导提示的通用链接预测框架，可在未见图上进行免训练推理，并提升跨领域预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统链接预测方法通常需要针对每个数据集单独训练，计算与维护成本较高。现有通用方法依赖子图采样，难以有效捕获高阶及长程结构信息；同时预测模型缺乏领域适应能力，因此在生物网络等依赖长程信号的图上表现受限。

Method: 提出通用链接预测框架“阿特拉斯通用链接预测”。该方法构建关系图谱，从全局和局部视角刻画节点对之间的结构关联，以综合表示不同图领域中的连接信号；进一步根据上下文结构生成领域感知的提示词元，并通过提示引导的上下文学习进行自适应链接预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在多个不同图领域中持续优于现有先进方法。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 关系图谱有助于弥补子图采样方法对高阶与长程信息刻画不足的问题，领域引导提示则增强了模型对不同图领域的适应能力。该框架为未见图上的通用、免训练链接预测提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Link prediction (LP) is a widely applied task in graph learning. Conventional LP approaches typically follow a dataset-specific paradigm, requiring independent training for each graph and incurring high computational and maintenance costs in large-scale applications. Motivated by these limitations, recent work explores Universal Link Prediction (ULP), aiming to enable training-free inference on arbitrary unseen graphs. However, existing methods primarily rely on subgraph sampling strategies to construct universal representations, which are unable to capture higher-order information due to the exponential growth of higher-order neighborhoods. As a result, they often under-perform on graphs where long-range signals are essential, such as biological networks. Moreover, their prediction models are typically domain-agnostic and lack the ability to adapt to different graph domains, leading to limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose an Atlas-based Universal Link Prediction framework (AtlasULP), which introduces the relation atlas to capture structural associations between node pairs from both global and local perspectives, enabling a comprehensive characterization of connectivity signals across diverse graph domains. Building upon this representation, we develop a domain-guided prompting ULP model, which generates domain-aware prompt tokens from contextual structures and performs prompt-guided in-context prediction for adaptive link inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AtlasULP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse graph domains.

</details>


### [197] [Acoustic Anomaly Detection for Early Identification of Drill Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07356)
*Maria Beatriz Silva,Lucas Edson Silva de Araújo,Sergio F. Chevtchenko,Ermeson Andrade,Danilo Araujo,Saeed Afshar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于超声声学特征的数控加工钻头卡滞预警系统。自编码器在跨钻头测试中优于监督式极端梯度提升模型，成功检出所有测试中的卡滞事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻头卡滞会损害工件质量与刀具完整性，并可能造成非计划停机和安全风险。由于不同钻头之间存在数据分布差异，研究旨在寻找能够提前识别机械失稳、且适用于跨刀具场景的检测方法。

Method: 使用超声传感器采集数控加工过程中的声学信号，并公开包含十七支钻头时域与频域声学特征的数据集。研究比较基于自编码器的半监督正常状态建模与监督式极端梯度提升分类，并采用留一钻头验证评估模型的跨钻头泛化能力。

Result: 自编码器的召回率为一点零零，综合F1值为零点八一，综合曲线下面积为零点八二九四。监督式极端梯度提升模型的平均F1值为零点五五，曲线下面积为零点七五五九，且在部分钻头测试中未能检出故障。结果表明，自编码器对正常与故障前声学模式的区分更好，并检出了所有测试中的卡滞事件。

Conclusion: 超声频谱能够为识别早期机械不稳定提供有效信息。相较于监督式分类，自编码器在存在跨钻头数据分布偏移时表现出更强的检测能力，可为加工安全提供较可靠的预警保障；不过，结论目前基于十七支钻头的数据，仍需在更广泛的设备与工况中验证。

Abstract: In the context of Industry 4.0, predictive maintenance is essential to minimize unplanned downtime and ensure operational safety. In machining processes, drill jamming is a critical failure that compromises both workpiece quality and tool integrity. This work proposes an acoustic anomaly detection system using ultrasonic sensors to identify pre-failure patterns in Computer Numerical Control (CNC) machining. We release a public dataset of time- and frequency-domain acoustic features from 17 drills, and benchmark semi-supervised autoencoder-based normality modelling against supervised XGBoost classification using Leave-One-Drill-Out validation. The results demonstrate that the Autoencoder is highly effective for industrial safety, achieving a Recall of 1.00 and a consolidated F1-score of 0.81. Furthermore, the threshold-independent analysis shows that the Autoencoder better separates normal and pre-failure acoustic patterns, achieving a consolidated AUC of 0.8294, compared with 0.7559 for the supervised model under inter-tool covariate shift. In contrast, the supervised XGBoost achieved an average F1-score of 0.55 and failed to detect failures in some of the evaluated drills. The findings highlight that the ultrasonic spectrum provides a reliable window for identifying incipient mechanical instabilities, and the AE model offers a robust safety envelope by anticipating all jamming events across the tested drill bits.

</details>


### [198] [Test-Time Self-Play: Scaling without a Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07367)
*Akira Yoshiyama,Akmal Ashirmatov,Jonas Hübotter,Ido Hakimi,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出无需正确性验证器的测试时自博弈方法，在代码问题上相较基线表现更好，且验证器越弱，优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通过在部署阶段投入额外计算来提升模型在困难任务上的表现，但现有方法通常依赖能够判断答案是否正确的验证器。实际应用中，针对目标任务的高质量验证器往往难以获得，因此需要一种不依赖验证器的扩展方法。

Method: 提出测试时自博弈：在推理阶段让模型进行非对称自我对抗，以额外计算改善候选解，而不依赖外部验证器。论文还在经过难度校准的代码题子集上，考察该方法在不同验证器能力下的表现。

Result: 在代码基准第六版的难度校准子集上，测试时自博弈在所有评估的验证器能力水平下均优于所比较的方法；验证器越弱，其相对最强基线的优势越大。在完全没有验证器时，其八次采样平均指标绝对提升三十五个百分点。若基础策略几乎无法生成正确答案，则上述收益不再稳定成立。

Conclusion: 非对称自博弈有望在缺少强验证器时，为测试时扩展提供有效途径。不过，该方法的收益取决于基础策略是否具备一定的正确答案覆盖能力；若这一能力近乎为零，自博弈难以弥补。

Abstract: Test-time scaling methods spend additional compute at deployment to improve a large language model's performance on a hard target problem. Most methods rely on a verifier that scores candidate solutions for correctness, using it to select among or refine the model's outputs. In many deployment regimes, however, a strong verifier on the target problem is unavailable. We introduce Test-Time Self-Play (TTSP), an approach to test-time scaling using asymmetric self-play without reliance on a verifier. On a difficulty-calibrated subset of LiveCodeBench v6, TTSP is the best-performing method at every verifier strength we evaluate, and the gap to the strongest baseline widens as the verifier weakens; in the no-verifier limit, TTSP yields a 35\% absolute improvement in mean@8. The gains hold across a range of verifier strengths, except when the base policy has near-zero coverage of the correct answer.

</details>


### [199] [When Scores Conflict with Preferences: Pre-Alignment Diagnosis and Adaptive Correction of Constraint Drift in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07375)
*Ping Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，直接偏好优化可能因偏好判断与点式约束指标冲突而产生约束漂移，并提出依据数据集统计量调整训练的冲突感知方法，以减少漂移并尽量保留偏好学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际训练数据常同时包含成对偏好和回答长度、正确性或安全性等单点评分。当偏好胜出的回答在单点评分上更差时，标准直接偏好优化仍将其与两类指标一致的样本同等处理，可能导致约束成本上升。论文旨在刻画并诊断这种约束漂移，判断数据集是否会自行纠正，或需要训练干预。

Method: 论文分析小幅参数更新下两类样本的作用：冲突样本倾向于增加约束成本，一致样本则倾向于修复约束。基于两类样本的相对比例，作者构造了可由数据集统计量计算的闭式平衡诊断指标，用于判断数据集是否具有自我纠正能力。在此基础上，提出冲突感知偏好优化：根据该指标确定冲突样本的降权强度，而非通过调参确定；同时加入自适应间隔偏移，以处理训练过程中的残余漂移。

Result: 实验显示，在冲突占主导的领域，约束漂移会随冲突样本暴露程度单调增加。平衡诊断指标对所有领域的干预判断均与实际漂移一致，包括一个被判定为可自我纠正的领域。在主要比较涵盖的四个领域中，所提方法均降低了漂移，同时大体保留了标准直接偏好优化的偏好学习能力；与硬筛选以及近期的约束优化和多目标基线相比，它也取得了更有利的漂移—偏好权衡。

Conclusion: 偏好优化中的约束漂移与冲突样本和一致样本的数量平衡密切相关。通过训练前诊断数据集的自我纠正能力，并在训练中针对冲突样本降权、动态调整间隔，可以有效减轻漂移，且较少牺牲偏好学习效果。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is increasingly used in settings where pairwise preferences coexist with pointwise criteria such as response length, answer correctness, or safety scores. When the two disagree, standard DPO treats pairs whose preferred output scores worse on the pointwise criterion (conflict pairs) in the same way as the remaining (matching) pairs. We characterize a systematic failure mode, constraint drift: for sufficiently small updates, conflict pairs tend to increase constraint cost while matching pairs tend to repair it, and the net change depends on their balance. This balance yields a closed-form pre-alignment diagnostic, $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$, computable from dataset statistics, that indicates whether a dataset is self-correcting or requires intervention. Building on this diagnostic, we propose Conflict-aware DPO (CoDPO), which down-weights conflict pairs with a strength set from $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$ rather than tuned, and adds an adaptive margin shift that addresses residual drift during training. Empirically, drift grows monotonically with conflict exposure on the conflict-dominated domains, and the intervention decision given by $ρ^{*}_{\mathrm{eq}}$ matches the observed drift on every domain, including one that it identifies as self-correcting. On all four domains of the main comparison, CoDPO reduces drift while largely preserving the preference learning of vanilla DPO. Compared with hard filtering and recent constrained and multi-objective baselines, CoDPO also achieves a more favorable drift-preference trade-off.

</details>


### [200] [Adversarial Multiplicity: Cherry-Picked Models and the Limits of Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07378)
*Cassandra C. Chou,Benjamin Q. Huynh,Allison Koenecke,Elizabeth T. Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究指出，预测多重性虽可用于优化公平等次要指标，却也能被有动机的参与者利用来操纵个体预测乃至总体估计；现有审计与监管未必能发现此类操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个预测性能相近的模型可能对同一个体给出不同结论，这种预测差异可用于兼顾公平等目标，但也为诉讼当事人、消费者或追求特定目标的开发者提供了操纵空间。论文旨在分析这类对抗性利用的方式、影响及其能否通过现行监管审查。

Method: 论文区分两类操纵：软操纵，即通过看似无害的模型选择、超参数调整或特征工程寻找有利预测；硬操纵，即直接修改模型参数。研究比较其对个体预测、总体估计和模型间分歧调和结果的影响，并评估其通过黑盒或白盒审计及现行监管要求的可能性。

Result: 足够灵活的模型可经少量参数改动逆转任意个体预测，同时几乎不损害预测性能，并可能通过黑盒审计。软操纵影响较小，但仍可能通过要求公开代码和数据的白盒审计。气候变化对野火影响的估计也可被硬操纵或软搜索完全反转。调和算法通常能消除大部分模型分歧，但若对立模型由操纵者刻意挑选，调和结果更常偏向操纵者，而非随机选择对立模型时的情况。

Conclusion: 预测多重性带来的灵活性具有明显的双重用途，单靠预测准确率或常规审计不足以防范操纵。论文建议采用事先注册、提供模型训练过程证明、开展敏感性分析与多指标评估，并承诺使用带版本记录的公开模型，以提高操纵的可发现性和问责能力。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity -- where similarly accurate models disagree on individual predictions -- can be leveraged to optimize secondary metrics (e.g., fairness) without loss in predictive performance. However, exploiting multiplicity opens the opportunity for motivated parties (or coding agents gaming their objectives) to steer predictions toward their own ends, such as in litigation or consumer disputes with automated decision-making tools. In these adversarial multiplicity settings, we distinguish soft manipulation, where parties optimize over innocuous model choices (e.g., hyperparameter tuning, feature engineering), from hard manipulation, where parties directly perturb model parameters. We find a sufficiently flexible model can be manipulated to reverse any individual prediction with minimal change in predictive performance while passing black-box audits, making hard manipulation viable in most regulatory settings. Soft manipulation has smaller effects but can pass white-box audits requiring full access to code and data. Even aggregate estimates can be manipulated: a model's estimated effect of climate change on wildfires can be fully reversed by hard edits and soft search. We find the Reconcile algorithm typically resolves most disagreements between models, but more often in favor of the manipulator when the opposing model is chosen adversarially compared to at random. We map which manipulations can plausibly pass existing regulations and disclosure policies, and recommend mitigations such as pre-registration, proof of model training, sensitivity analyses, multi-metric evaluations, and committing to versioned public models.

</details>


### [201] [Spectral symmetry fingerprints in equivariant representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07410)
*Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Ning Lin,Zihe Wang,Ji-Rong Wen,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用等变表示的奇异值谱构建对称性指纹，可识别离散点群并量化连续对称性变化，且不依赖特定模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何深度学习中的对称性会对等变表示施加秩约束，限制等变图神经网络的表达能力。论文将这一限制转化为分析工具：表示的奇异值谱能够反映结构对称性及其破缺。

Method: 提出谱对称性指纹方法。对于离散点群，通过将奇异值谱与不同对称群对应的秩模板匹配来识别群；对于连续对称性，则统计超出目标秩约束的谱能量，以衡量对称性偏离。该方法具有方向不变性，可参数无关地用于图神经网络；谱还可复用于多个目标群，并构造满足子群单调性的泄漏分数。

Result: 在合成结构、分子、配位复合物和大型生物组装体上，方法能够准确识别点群、跟踪受控的对称性破缺，并揭示全局几何指标可能掩盖的局部及按节点度区分的对称性变化。

Conclusion: 等变表示的谱结构可作为跨物理系统刻画对称性的通用框架。论文展示了表示秩约束不仅是表达能力的限制，也能用于识别和量化结构对称性。

Abstract: Symmetry governs the organization of physical structures, while symmetry breaking reveals their transformations. In geometric deep learning, symmetry imposes rank constraints on equivariant representations, limiting the expressive capacity of equivariant graph neural networks (GNNs). Here we show that these constraints can instead be exploited: the singular-value spectra of equivariant representations provide a unified fingerprint of structural symmetry. We introduce spectral symmetry fingerprints (SymFP), which enable discrete point-group identification through rank-template matching and continuous symmetry analysis by quantifying spectral energy beyond target rank constraints. SymFP is orientation invariant and architecture agnostic, enabling a parameter-free GNN implementation. Its spectra can be reused across multiple target groups, and the resulting leakage scores satisfy subgroup monotonicity. Across synthetic structures, molecules, coordination complexes and large biological assemblies, SymFP accurately identifies point groups, tracks controlled symmetry breaking and reveals local and degree-resolved symmetry changes that can be obscured by global geometric measures. These results establish the spectral structure of equivariant representations as a general framework for characterizing symmetry across physical systems.

</details>


### [202] [IMUDINO: Self-distilled Foundation Model for Inertial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07437)
*Zhoutong Ye,Chengwen Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Xufeng Jian,Zhe He,Qingyang Wan,Xutong Wang,Jiaqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Yu Mei,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向惯性传感的通用基础模型，借助自监督学习与适配可变输入的架构，在多类任务和不同传感设置下实现较强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有惯性基础模型往往局限于单一领域，在部分任务上不及先进的监督方法；同时，它们常要求固定输入长度和采样率，且难以适应未见过的传感器佩戴位置。因此，研究需要一种覆盖多种惯性感知任务、对输入变化和传感配置更具适应性的通用模型。

Method: 研究将自监督学习范式迁移到惯性数据上，并设计保留运动语义的变换，以模拟运动模式和传感器设置的变化。模型采用标准变换器编码器，并使用感知频率的位置编码，从而原生支持不同输入长度与采样率，同时学习全局和密集特征。

Result: 摘要称，该模型在人体活动识别、健康、动作捕捉和里程计等任务上取得有竞争力的表现，并能泛化到不同用户、传感器设置和任务；在机器人里程计中，还能适应不同机器人实体。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标或数值结果。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了通过语义保持的数据变换和适配频率的编码机制，构建通用惯性传感基础模型的可行性。作者希望该模型成为惯性感知任务的通用骨干，并推动相关通用基础模型的发展。

Abstract: We present IMUDINO, the first foundation model (FM) for inertial sensing to achieve competitive performance across diverse sensing tasks including human activity recognition (HAR), health, motion capture, and odometry. Prior inertial FMs often focus on one subfield of inertial sensing and sometimes underperform state-of-the-art supervised methods (e.g., in HAR), making their scope considerably narrower than FMs for vision and natural language. Moreover, they often require fixed input length and sampling rate, and generalize poorly to unseen sensor locations. To build a general-purpose foundation model for inertial sensing, we adapt the DINO self-supervised learning (SSL) paradigm to inertial data through a set of semantics-preserving motion transformations that simulate variations in motion pattern and sensor setup. For model architecture, we use a plain transformer encoder with frequency-aware positional encoding that natively supports varying input length and sampling rate. This simple setup proved remarkably effective for learning global and dense features generalizable across users, sensor setups, tasks, and, in the case of robot odometry, embodiments. We hope IMUDINO can serve as a backbone for inertial sensing and kickstart the development of general-purpose inertial FMs.

</details>


### [203] [Two-stage residual learning for calibrated uncertainty in solar irradiance forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07444)
*RR Flanagan,E Kasha,PW Talbot,AS Epiny,MR Deinert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种两阶段太阳辐照度概率预测方法：先预测期望辐照度，再单独建模预测误差，以更准确地估计预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电预测不仅需要准确的点预测，也需要可信的不确定性估计，尤其是在较长预测时域。现有机器学习方法往往只给出单一预测值；直接预测假定概率分布的参数，也可能无法充分反映实际预测误差。

Method: 采用两阶段建模。第一阶段使用长短期记忆网络预测全球水平辐照度的期望值；第二阶段另用网络预测该预测的误差分布，并将误差信息与期望值结合，得到预测结果及其不确定性。研究使用美国国家太阳辐射数据库的历史气象数据，并通过概率积分变换评估预测分布。

Result: 显式建模预测误差后，预测标准差与历史标准差之间的偏差明显缩小：单独使用长短期记忆网络时降低百分之三十四点六；与高斯过程回归结合时降低百分之五十六点五。后者是所测试配置中表现最可靠的方案。

Conclusion: 将预测误差与期望辐照度分开建模，有助于更完整地捕捉实际预测所需的不确定性。与仅估计模型自身不确定性的随机失活方法相比，该方法针对总预测误差进行建模，更适合为决策提供不确定性信息。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of renewable generation requires not only point predictions but also reliable estimates of the uncertainty attached to them, particularly at extended horizons. Machine learning models are increasingly used to predict solar output, but they typically report a single value and capture the associated uncertainty poorly. The standard remedy is to train a model to predict the parameters of an assumed probability distribution directly. Here we develop an alternative two-stage method, motivated by synthetic solar signal generation, in which the forecast error is modeled separately from the expected irradiance. A first long short-term memory (LSTM) network predicts the expected global horizontal irradiance, and a second network models the distribution of the forecast error, which is added back to the mean to give both the forecast and its uncertainty. Using historical weather data from the National Solar Radiation Database and a probability integral transform evaluation, we show that explicitly modeling the error brings the predicted standard deviation markedly closer to the historical value, reducing the deviation between the two by 34.6 percent for a standalone LSTM and by 56.5 percent when the LSTM is coupled to Gaussian process regression, the most reliable of the configurations tested. Because the method models the total forecast error directly, it captures the full predictive uncertainty that matters for decisions, rather than only the model contribution that dropout-based approaches estimate.

</details>


### [204] [Echoes of Error: Residual-Directional Local Rollout Consistency for Timeseries Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07473)
*Zheng Wang,Kaixuan Zhang,Xiaonan Lu,Wanfang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向时间序列预测的正则化方法，抑制模型自身预测误差在后续滚动预测中的传播，从而提升预测精度与轨迹稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见训练方式主要关注单步预测误差，但在自由滚动预测中，模型会将自身输出反馈为后续输入，早期误差可能扰动状态并持续影响未来预测。直接刻画残差如何映射到下一状态，往往需要针对输入与输出维度、组成或模型结构设计复杂的对齐算子。

Method: 论文以局部滚动一致性为出发点，比较教师强制与自由运行时产生的下一步预测。为避免显式构造残差到状态的对齐算子，采用一阶局部近似，对预测器沿实际反馈扰动方向的雅可比—向量积进行惩罚，使模型降低对自身误差的敏感度。论文还从预测流形的几何视角解释该目标：控制残差诱发、探测流形法向方向的扰动，并据此给出自由运行轨迹保持在流形附近稳定管道内的理论条件。

Result: 摘要报告称，在时间序列预测实验中，该正则化方法改善了预测准确性，并支持其关于抑制误差回响的解释；摘要未提供具体数据集、基线或量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: 训练预测模型不应只追求较小的单步误差，还应降低模型自身误差对后续预测的影响。通过约束反馈扰动方向上的局部敏感性，可以改善自由滚动预测的稳定性与准确性。

Abstract: In time-series forecasting, rollout errors are not merely one-step fitting discrepancies: once fed back into the evolving state, they can perturb future predictions. We formalize this effect through local rollout consistency, which compares the next prediction produced under teacher forcing with that produced under free running. In principle, this consistency can be enforced directly by tracking the residual-induced perturbation of the next state. In practice, however, the residual-to-state alignment operator is often difficult to design when the input state and output block have different dimensions, heterogeneous components, or architecture-dependent layouts. We therefore use a first-order local surrogate that penalizes the Jacobian-vector product of the predictor along the actual feedback perturbation, suppressing precisely the directions through which self-generated errors remain visible to future predictions. We further give the same loss a geometric interpretation near a predictive manifold: by controlling residual-induced directions that probe the normal bundle, the objective yields theoretical conditions under which free-running trajectories remain inside a stability tube around the manifold. Experiments on time series forecasting tasks support this view, showing that the proposed regularization improves predictive accuracy. More broadly, our results suggest a training principle for time-series forecasters and sequential predictors: a model should not only make small one-step errors, but also learn not to let its own errors echo into the future. Code is available at https://github.com/Wangzzzzzzzz/Echoes-Of-Error.

</details>


### [205] [Adversarial Robustness of Kernel Machines: A Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07474)
*Rahul Kumar Yadav,Tunir Ghosh,Sudhendu Ahir,Parthe Pandit,Arjun Bhagoji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估核方法模型的对抗鲁棒性，并提出一种近似对抗训练算法；实验显示，核方法模型面对扰动时通常比神经网络性能下降得更缓慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管核机器学习模型应用广泛，其面对对抗样本的鲁棒性此前缺乏全面研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，考察不同核方法模型在多种攻击条件下的表现。

Method: 在二分类与多分类任务中，作者针对图像和表格数据，使用白盒与黑盒规避攻击，实证评估核机器、随机特征分类器及递归特征机器的原生鲁棒性；此外，还提出一种用于核机器的近似对抗训练算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，核方法模型通常比神经网络具有更强的原生对抗鲁棒性：在对抗扰动增强时，其分类性能下降得更加平缓。

Conclusion: 研究表明，核方法模型是值得关注的鲁棒分类方案；其面对对抗扰动时的性能退化相对温和。论文还提供了近似对抗训练方法，以探索进一步提升核机器鲁棒性的途径。

Abstract: Kernel machines are an important class of models in machine learning. However, the question of how robust modern kernel machines are to adversarial examples has not been studied comprehensively. In this paper, we provide a thorough empirical investigation of the adversarial robustness of kernel-based models for both binary and multiclass classification tasks, via white-box and black-box evasion attacks. We evaluate the native robustness of kernel machines, random feature classifiers, and the relatively newer Recursive Feature Machines. In addition, we provide an algorithm for approximate adversarial training of kernel machines. Our experiments on classification tasks on image and tabular datasets strongly indicate that kernel machines are natively more robust than neural networks, i.e., their performance degrades more gracefully against adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [206] [Localization Does Not Imply Steerability: A Mechanistic Study of Sign Errors in LLM Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07478)
*Shradha Agarwal,Tariq J.,Deepak Rajbhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，多步数学推理中的符号错误通常在网络后段由多层感知机写入；但错误位置相同并不意味着都容易被干预纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多步数学计算中，常因中途写错一个正负号而导致最终答案错误，尽管后续运算都正确。研究旨在定位网络中符号错误的形成位置，并检验该机制能否通过因果干预得到纠正。

Method: 研究考察了参数规模从百亿级到数千亿级的六种开放模型，逐层读取其符号表征，以定位错误符号的写入位置；再通过修改残差流中的符号方向，测试干预对答案的因果影响，并与随机方向及对照层干预比较。核心主张的检验标准在查看留出数据前预先登记。

Result: 在各模型和任务中，错误符号通常由网络末段四分之一的多层感知机层写入；在受测模型中，这一现象也出现在指令微调前的基础模型。不同模型的机制有所差异：有的层会固定推动负号，有的会放大已有符号倾向，还有的模型存在抵消该倾向的后续层。在一种模型上，干预纠正了留出的十四个负号错误中的十三个，并在其他模型中也产生了不同程度的复现；具有针对性的干预基本不影响正确的正号答案，而随机方向和对照层干预无效。然而，另一种模型虽在相近深度具有清晰定位的符号回路，同一干预却几乎不能翻转其输出，因为符号表征分散写入约十五层，且决策优势更大。预先登记的二十九项检验中有二十三项通过，未通过项亦全部报告。

Conclusion: 多步数学中的符号错误可在网络后段形成，并且在部分模型中能够通过针对性表征干预纠正。但定位到某个回路不等于该回路容易操控：不同模型即使在相似位置写入符号，其冗余程度和可翻转性也可能大不相同。

Abstract: When large language models do multi-step mathematics, a characteristic failure recurs: the model writes one wrong sign token mid-computation -- a cofactor sign, the minus of a recursive integration-by-parts step -- then executes the rest of the arithmetic correctly, and the answer comes out wrong. We ask where inside the network that sign is written. Across six open models spanning 14B to 675B parameters, a per-layer readout shows the wrong sign is committed late -- by MLP layers in the last quarter of the network (70-100% per model and task), a pattern present, where tested (Qwen), in the base model before instruction tuning. The mechanism varies: some models carry fixed "minus-stamp" layers that push a minus regardless of the right answer, others carry amplifiers of whichever sign was already chosen, and Qwen adds a downstream rescue layer that opposes the stamp. The circuit is causal: subtracting a single sign direction from the residual stream flips 13 of 14 held-out minus-written integration-by-parts errors on Llama-3.3-70B, with flips replicating on Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral-Small (24-70% per cell), and with representation-level specificity -- the same dose breaks correctly-minus answers while sparing correct-plus answers where the direction is surgical (0/72 on Llama), and matched random-direction and control-layer interventions are null. Core claims were preregistered with pass criteria frozen in git before held-out data was seen: 23 of 29 checks passed, all misses reported. But causality is not uniform: Phi-4 implements an equally well-localized circuit at the same late depth, yet the identical intervention flips almost nothing -- the sign is written redundantly across ~15 layers with margins several times larger (median 17.8 logits). Localization, we conclude, does not imply steerability: two models can share a circuit location yet differ enormously in how overturnable it is.

</details>


### [207] [Learning Multi-Agent Orchestration:Higher-Order Interactions in Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07495)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出交互感知策略优化方法，显式建模多智能体编排决策之间的相互作用；在多个推理与代码任务上，该方法以更少的令牌达到最强基线系统的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别依据各项决策的优势来训练编排器，仿佛其他决策保持不变。但在多智能体编排中，一项决策的效果取决于此前的决策，因此联合更新带来的收益并不等于各项决策单独收益之和。这种忽略决策交互的做法可能低估有效改进。

Method: 作者提出交互感知策略优化框架，将两种策略之间的性能差异精确分解为单项决策效应与决策交互效应。理论分析表明，交互效应可占可实现收益的一半。该方法利用采样轨迹同时优化这两类效应，并为每次策略更新提供改进保证。

Result: 在数学文字题、竞赛数学题、阅读理解及代码生成任务的长输入版本上，作者将方法与十五种已发表的多智能体系统和工作流搜索系统进行比较，并使用相同的七十亿参数执行模型。部署后的计划以更少令牌达到最强对比系统的准确率。代码任务消融实验显示，移除交互项会使准确率下降七至十七个百分点。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排中的决策交互是影响最终表现的重要因素，不应仅将各项决策孤立归因。显式建模并优化交互效应，有望在保证更新改进的同时，以更高的令牌效率实现强劲表现。

Abstract: Learned orchestration has emerged as a promising paradigm for large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems, in which a central orchestrator selects agents and controls the information they receive throughout a task. Despite rapid empirical progress, existing methods train the orchestrator by crediting each decision with its own advantage, implicitly assuming that the other decisions in the episode stay fixed. This assumption fails in orchestration, where the contribution of each decision to the final outcome depends on the decisions preceding it, so the gain of a joint update is not the sum of its per-decision effects. To address this challenge, we introduce \textbf{IAPO}, an interaction-aware policy optimization framework that explicitly accounts for interactions among decisions. Specifically, IAPO exactly decomposes the performance difference between two policies into the effects of individual decisions and their interactions, which we prove can account for half of the attainable gain, and then optimizes both from sampled trajectories while certifying that every update is an improvement. On long-input versions of GSM8K, MATH, DROP, MBPP and HumanEval, against fifteen published multi-agent and workflow-search systems on the same 7B executor, IAPO's deployed plans reach the strongest system's accuracy with fewer tokens, and removing the interaction term costs 7 to 17 points of accuracy on code.

</details>


### [208] [Scaling Laws for SAE Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07756)
