*Nikita Koriagin,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器训练无需持续增加激活数据：缓冲区达到质量平台后，重复使用其中的数据即可；实验中存储量减少八至六十四倍，质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练稀疏自编码器时，保存大量激活数据常成为内存与存储瓶颈。研究关注在总训练预算固定的情况下，需要多少种不同激活，才能兼顾模型质量与数据存储成本。

Method: 系统改变激活缓冲区规模，并在固定训练预算下比较训练质量，考察数据多样性与重复使用之间的权衡，并以有限数据缩放规律描述这一关系。实验覆盖不同字典规模、词元预算、模型层次与模型规模，以及多种稀疏自编码器结构；同时改变激活来源，包括数学文本与代码。评估指标包括解释方差、重建均方误差、特征吸收、自动化可解释性和稀疏探测。

Result: 随着缓冲区扩大，模型质量起初迅速提升，随后进入平台期；在平台期，重复使用缓冲区中的激活与持续使用新激活效果近似。超过该规模后，增加独特激活对重建质量和可解释性的收益很小。上述趋势在多种模型、层次、训练预算及自编码器结构中得到验证。采用平台期所需的最小缓冲区，可将激活存储量降低八至六十四倍，且质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 实用训练策略是先确定质量达到平台所需的最小激活缓冲区，再重复使用该缓冲区完成剩余训练预算。激活数据规模不必随训练预算同步增长，从而能显著降低存储需求。

Abstract: Activation storage is often a bottleneck in sparse autoencoder (SAE) training. We study how many unique activations an SAE needs when its total training budget is fixed. Quality improves rapidly as the buffer grows, then reaches a plateau where replaying the buffer is nearly as effective as using fresh activations throughout training. Beyond this point, additional unique activations bring little benefit to reconstruction or interpretability, so the buffer need not grow with the training budget. We describe this tradeoff between diversity and repetition with a scaling law for limited data. We validate it across dictionary sizes and token budgets, early, middle, and late layers of Llama-3.1, Qwen3 model sizes, and BatchTopK, TopK, and JumpReLU SAEs. Sweeps using mathematical text and Python code test the effect of changing the activation source. Evaluation covers explained variance (EV), reconstruction MSE, feature absorption, automated interpretability, and sparse probing. These results give a practical training rule: choose the smallest buffer that reaches the quality plateau and replay it for the remaining budget. In our experiments, this reduces activation storage by $8\text{--}64\times$ with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [209] [RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512)
*Chao Zhou,Shenghua He,Jiaxin Mo,Qiangguo Yu,Fengchuang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于在线强化学习的稀疏决策攻击方法，可在有限查询预算下动态选择扰动支持集缩减策略及其粒度，并显著提升攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策型稀疏攻击只能利用受害模型预测的标签来判断攻击是否成功。随着扰动支持集不断缩小，适宜的恢复策略也会变化：攻击初期适合粗粒度移除，后期则需要更细致、考虑历史状态的判断。固定策略难以适应这一变化，而查询预算又限制了可尝试的次数。

Method: 论文提出一种在线强化学习方法，用于调度支持集缩减执行器及其粒度，并在硬性查询预算内进行决策。方法采用带掩码的双重对决深度行动价值网络，从七种策略与尺度组合中选择动作。每次宏观决策后，系统依据当前支持集重新划分恢复分组；执行器只提交仍能维持对抗性的恢复操作，并将支持集缩减收益和查询成本反馈给决策模块。

Result: 在仅提供预测类别的相同查询接口下，该方法在柯达十类数据集、稀疏度为0.01时，针对性攻击成功率达到61%，高于稀疏进化方法的25%和逐点方法的0%。在残差网络和视觉Transformer上，稀疏度为0.005时成功率分别达到75.5%和73.0%，也高于对比方法。固定探针测试还显示，无需任何更新，经过40张图像后，中位数L零范数可由165降至23。状态盲化与状态打乱对照实验的效果明显下降，表明策略确实利用了攻击状态；主要收益来自状态条件化的执行器调度和动态重新分组。

Conclusion: 实验表明，在决策型稀疏攻击中，根据攻击状态动态调整缩减策略与粒度，能够有效利用有限查询预算并提升攻击成功率。尤其是执行器调度与分组方式的动态更新，是方法取得主要性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Decision-based sparse attacks must reduce perturbation support using only the victim's predicted label. The best restoration strategy varies as support contracts: coarse removal works early, while tail states need finer, history-aware tests. We propose RLGPA, an online reinforcement learning method that schedules support-reduction executors and their granularity under a hard query budget. A masked Dueling Double DQN selects among seven stable policy--scale actions; each action produces support-relative restoration groups that are rebuilt after every macro decision. A feasibility-preserving executor commits only adversarial restorations and feeds support gain and query cost back to the planner. Under the matched top-1-label oracle, RLGPA reaches 61\% targeted ASR at sparsity 0.01 on CIFAR-10 (vs. 25\% SparseEvo, 0\% Pointwise), and 75.5\%/73.0\% on ResNet50/ViT at sparsity 0.005 (vs. 29.0\%/15.5\%). A fixed-probe test reduces median \(L_0\) from 165 to 23 after 40 images without any update. State-blind and state-shuffled controls degrade significantly, confirming that the learned policy uses attack state; the main gains come from state-conditioned executor scheduling and dynamic repartition. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Acui001/RLGPA.git.

</details>


### [210] [CoRD: Coupled Rollouts Fill the Blind Spot of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07959)
*Guhan Chen,Bohan Li,Jun Rao,Hejin Wang,Songtao Tian,Yexin Xie,Xiaojun Meng,Peng Yuan,Jiansheng Wei,Xuebo Liu,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出协同轨迹蒸馏，通过少量、针对性的替换，让学生模型在更接近教师模型的状态分布上接受训练，同时保留原有的在线策略蒸馏损失；在五个数学基准上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏使用学生模型自身生成的轨迹作为训练状态，因此教师模型会到达、但学生模型尚未到达的状态很少得到监督。这种状态分布偏差可能限制学生的学习效果。论文旨在调整训练状态的分布，同时避免改变在线策略蒸馏的损失形式。

Method: 协同轨迹蒸馏将学生模型与教师模型的轨迹分布进行几何插值，并利用推测解码中的最大耦合生成训练轨迹。若两者对某个词元的分布一致，就保留学生模型的词元；若分布不一致，则从学生模型低估、而教师分布所支持的部分进行替换。该策略使干预尽可能少，并且随着学生模型逐步学习，替换会自然减少。

Result: 在来自两个模型系列、参数规模为六亿至四十亿的六个学生模型上，与相同步数和数据量的在线策略蒸馏相比，该方法在五个公开数学基准上的平均单次通过率提高零点九至二点零个百分点，八次尝试通过率提高一点二至三点四个百分点。对于教师模型曾到达的固定状态，四十亿参数学生模型用十六步训练即可达到在线策略蒸馏八十步仍未达到的差距缩小程度。干预频率不低于该方法、但采用其他替换方向的规则，表现均不及该方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在线策略蒸馏的训练状态分布可以通过稀疏且方向明确的替换向教师模型靠近，而无需改变原有损失。协同轨迹蒸馏在多个模型规模和数学任务上提升了效果，并能更高效地学习学生模型原本较少接触的教师轨迹状态。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a student on its own rollouts, so states the teacher would reach but the student does not are seldom supervised. We introduce CoRD (coupled-rollout distillation), which keeps the on-policy loss and moves only the distribution of training states toward the teacher, drawing rollouts from a geometric interpolation of student and teacher through the maximal coupling of speculative decoding. The coupling keeps the student's token wherever the two distributions agree and otherwise substitutes a token from the part of the target the student underweights, so interventions are as rare as any exact sampler allows and recede as the student learns. Across six base students from two model families (Qwen3 and Qwen3.5, 0.6B to 4B), CoRD raises average pass@1 over on-policy distillation by 0.9 to 2.0 points and pass@8 by 1.2 to 3.4 points on five public math benchmarks at equal steps and data. On frozen teacher-reached states, a 4B student trained with CoRD reaches in 16 steps a gap closure that on-policy distillation does not reach in 80, and substitution rules intervening at least as often in other directions fall short of CoRD.

</details>


### [211] [Understanding Data Influence in Transportation Networks: A Physics-Constrained Influence-Function Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07526)
*Hamidreza Attari,Yueyue Fan,Johannes Royset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向物理约束学习的影响函数框架，用于评估训练样本如何在满足网络约束的条件下影响交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统影响函数主要针对无约束学习，未能显式处理交通网络中耦合不同组成部分的物理约束。因此，它们可能无法准确刻画训练样本扰动在网络中的实际传播方式，尤其是扰动只能沿物理可行方向影响模型参数和预测的情形。

Method: 构建硬物理约束影响函数框架，在网络约束所定义的物理可行空间内分析训练数据的影响。以满足流量守恒约束的交通预测为案例，并通过正交分解，分别量化扰动经由流量守恒约束在网络中的传播部分，以及被约束投影消除的部分。

Result: 理论分析表明，物理约束会改变训练样本影响的大小、方向和空间分布。交通预测案例中，新框架识别出的重要训练样本与经典影响函数识别出的样本存在系统性差异；正交分解还揭示了扰动在网络中的传播与消除情况。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在遵循物理约束的前提下评估训练数据对模型预测的影响，为受约束交通学习中的数据诊断、传感器评估和针对性数据采集提供了物理一致的分析方法。

Abstract: Understanding how individual training observations influence model predictions is important for developing reliable and interpretable data-driven transportation systems. Existing influence-function methods, however, are primarily formulated for unconstrained learning and do not explicitly account for physical constraints that couple different components of a transportation network. Under such constraints, perturbing a training observation can affect model parameters and predictions only through physically feasible directions, potentially changing how its influence propagates across the network. This paper develops a hard physics-informed influence-function framework for constrained learning, in which training-data influence is characterized within the physically feasible space defined by network constraints. The analytical results reveal how physical constraints alter the magnitude, direction, and spatial distribution of training-sample influence relative to conventional influence measures. Using traffic prediction under flow-conservation constraints as a case study, we show that influential observations identified by the proposed framework differ systematically from those identified by classical influence functions. Through orthogonal decomposition, we quantitatively characterize how much of a perturbation in the training data is propagated through the network via the flow-conservation constraints and how much is removed by the constraint projection. The proposed influence framework provides a physically consistent approach for evaluating training-data influence in constrained transportation learning problems, with potential applications to data diagnostics, sensor evaluation, and targeted data collection.

</details>


### [212] [Learning Dynamics of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08016)
*Guan Zhe Hong,Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Liang Chen,Yangchen Pan,Junchi Yu,Jialin Yu,Philip Torr,Yi Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从机制层面分析在策略蒸馏如何通过参数更新和后续生成轨迹形成反馈，并影响学习与泛化；离线蒸馏虽未必立即提升留出集准确率，却能为后续在策略蒸馏打下基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏结合教师的密集监督与学生自身生成的轨迹，但每次参数更新都会改变学生的预测及后续训练数据分布。反馈如何影响学习和泛化尚不清楚，也难以解释学生对教师信号响应有限，以及训练可能逐渐退化等现象。

Method: 对在策略蒸馏的学习动态进行机制分析，将反馈过程拆分为两个阶段：一是蒸馏信号如何转化为参数更新；二是参数更新如何传播至不同前缀上的预测，并改变后续生成轨迹的分布。研究据此考察教师监督、学生参数几何结构与生成分布之间的相互作用，并分析离线蒸馏对后续训练的作用。

Result: 分析发现，即使学生与教师的预测存在明显分歧，学生也可能对蒸馏信号响应有限。此外，某些更新虽能提升局部的教师一致性，却可能提高失败续写的概率，使后续生成轨迹暴露于不可靠监督之中，导致训练退化。仅进行在策略蒸馏可能损害生成表现；经过充分离线准备后，在策略蒸馏则能带来显著的准确率提升，尽管离线阶段本身未必明显提高留出集准确率。

Conclusion: 在策略蒸馏的效果并非只由教师监督强弱决定，还取决于学生如何将信号转化为更新，以及这些更新如何改变后续生成分布。离线蒸馏可作为有效准备，即使其即时准确率收益有限，也可能提升后续在策略蒸馏的表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is emerging as an important paradigm for language-model post-training, combining dense teacher supervision with training on student-generated trajectories. This creates a feedback loop where each update changes the student and thereby the training distribution that generates subsequent updates. Yet, how this feedback shapes learning and generalization remains poorly understood. We conduct a mechanistic analysis of OPD's learning dynamics, distinguishing the two feedback loop stages: conversion of the distillation signal into parameter updates, and broadcast through which these updates change predictions across prefixes and reshape the rollout distribution. This framework helps us understand both limited student responsiveness despite substantial teacher-student disagreement, and degeneration when updates that improve local teacher agreement also increase the probability of unsuccessful continuations, exposing the student to unreliable supervision in subsequent rollouts. Finally, we show that offline distillation can prepare a student for OPD without significantly improving immediate held-out accuracy. While OPD alone may degrade generation, it can yield substantial accuracy gains after sufficient offline preparation. Together, our results connect teacher supervision, student geometry, and the evolving rollout distribution in determining the learning dynamics of OPD and its effects on downstream performance.

</details>


### [213] [Provable Zeroth-Order Safe Exploration for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07561)
*Weiqin Chen,Xinmeng Huang,Dongsheng Ding,Santiago Paternain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于人类偏好的安全强化学习方法，在训练过程中确保所有执行策略（包括探索策略）始终满足安全约束，并证明其收敛性与样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原始—对偶优化的安全强化学习方法，虽可能最终得到满足约束的策略，却无法避免训练期间出现违规。本文关注更严格的安全探索要求：训练中每一个策略都必须保持可行，同时直接利用人类偏好反馈，避免依赖奖励模型。

Method: 提出零阶对数障碍策略梯度方法，直接从人类偏好构造零阶梯度估计，并优化对数障碍目标。为应对反馈噪声，方法采用保守的约束裕度估计、耦合式轨迹采样，以及自适应步长，以防更新越过约束边界。

Result: 理论上，方法以高概率保证所有迭代策略及探索策略严格可行，并能全局收敛至近似最优的受约束策略。轨迹样本复杂度为约 Õ(ε⁻⁶＋d³ε⁻⁵)，人类查询复杂度为约 Õ(ε⁻⁸＋d⁵ε⁻⁷)，其中 ε 表示精度，d 表示维度。合成约束马尔可夫决策过程和语言模型对齐实验支持其训练期安全保证。

Conclusion: 该研究将安全要求从最终策略扩展到整个训练过程，提出了无需奖励模型、基于人类偏好的安全探索方案，并给出相应的可行性、收敛性和复杂度保证。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning from human feedback (Safe-RLHF) seeks to improve helpfulness while satisfying harmlessness constraints. Existing primal-dual approaches can converge to feasible policies but do not prevent constraint violations during training. We study a stronger requirement, safe exploration for RLHF, under which every policy executed during training, including auxiliary exploration policies, remains feasible. We propose Zeroth-Order Log-Barrier Policy Gradient (ZO-LBPG), a reward-model-free method that optimizes a log-barrier objective using zeroth-order estimates derived directly from human preferences. To ensure safety under noisy feedback, we combine conservative constraint margin estimates with coupled rollouts and an adaptive step size that prevents updates from crossing constraint boundaries. We prove that, with high probability, all iterates and exploration policies remain strictly feasible, and that ZO-LBPG globally converges to an approximately optimal constrained policy. We establish a sample complexity of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-6}+d^3\varepsilon^{-5})$ trajectories and $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-8}+d^5\varepsilon^{-7})$ human queries. Experiments on a synthetic CMDP and a language-model alignment task corroborate the training-time safety guarantees.

</details>


### [214] [Gabor Gabor: E-Gaborformer, a Strictly Causal Speech Recognizer with Gabor-Dictionary Convolutions and Delta-Rule Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08092)
*Nir Krakowski,Tamir Cohen,Ohad Zohar,Chen Zarfati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种严格因果的流式语音识别编码器，在不等待未来音频、且每路通话内存不随时长增长的前提下，实现更快收敛和更低识别错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时语音代理需要低延迟识别器，但常见因果注意力模型会随着通话时间增长而累积缓存，造成内存开销不断上升。论文旨在同时满足严格因果、固定内存和高识别精度，并提升训练效率。

Method: 提出“E-伽博福默”编码器，将门控增量网络第二版循环层与滑动窗口注意力按三比一组合；所有时间卷积均由固定字典中的因果伽博尔原子构成。模型每路通话的流式状态保持恒定，生产规模下连同预测网络约占19兆字节。另提出将循环神经网络传感器的格结构改写为二分类形式，在保持损失及梯度完全相同的情况下加速训练。

Result: 在利布里语音语料库的受控因果实验中，该模型更早走出初始性能平台，达到指定准确率最多快18%，且表现持平或优于采用相同前端与训练方案的分支式编码器。生产规模的六亿参数希伯来语和英语流式模型，在英语语音代理流量上的词错误率比同规模英伟达流式识别模型低28%至31%；希伯来语词错误率较此前生产系统最多降低20%。二分类训练形式使训练速度提升2.6倍。相同模型权重也可用于严格因果解码和480毫秒前瞻解码，准确率不受影响。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合循环计算、局部注意力和固定因果卷积基元，可以构建适用于实时语音代理的低延迟、固定内存识别器，并在多项实验中提升精度或训练效率。

Abstract: Real-time voice agents need a speech recognizer that never waits for future audio and whose memory per call does not grow with the length of the call. We present E-Gaborformer, a strictly causal encoder that combines Gated DeltaNet-2 recurrent layers with sliding-window attention in a 3:1 ratio, and builds every temporal convolution from a fixed dictionary of causal Gabor atoms. Its streaming state is constant, about 19 MB per call at production scale including the prediction network, where a causal-attention encoder of the same size accumulates over 1 GB after ten minutes. In a controlled causal study on LibriSpeech, E-Gaborformer leaves the initial plateau earlier, reaches a given accuracy up to 18% sooner, and matches or beats an E-Branchformer trained with the identical front end and recipe. At production scale, a 0.6B-parameter Hebrew and English streaming transducer built on E-Gaborformer has 28 to 31% lower word error rate (WER) than NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 ASR Streaming, a model of the same size, on English voice-agent traffic, and up to 20% lower Hebrew WER than our previous production system. The same weights serve strictly causal and 480 ms-lookahead decoding with no loss in accuracy, and a two-class formulation of the RNN-T lattice trains the transducer 2.6x faster with exact loss and gradients.

</details>


### [215] [MambaTG: Candidate-Aware State Space Modeling for Future Link Prediction on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07569)
*Nguyen Hong Quan,Sitao Luan,Yilun Zheng,Lihui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向时序图未来连边预测的候选感知框架，同时利用交互序列的演化信息与候选连边和历史的匹配程度，提升重复连边与未见连边的预测能力，并兼顾计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图模型往往依赖重复交互或历史重叠等信号，这些信号有助于预测曾经连接过的节点对，却难以用于预测从未交互过的节点对，导致未见连边预测表现明显下降。因此，需要超越历史重复模式，挖掘能够支持泛化的时序信息。

Method: 提出一种基于选择性状态空间机制的候选感知框架，将预测依据分解为两类互补信号：一是源节点按时间排序的交互序列及其演化依赖；二是候选节点对与源节点动态历史之间的兼容性。模型通过选择性递归刻画序列演化，并根据候选输入自适应调整历史状态，从而联合建模这两类信号。

Result: 在十三个时序图数据集上与十三种代表性基线进行的大量实验表明，该方法在重复连边和未见连边预测上均取得较强且稳定的表现，同时保持较高的计算效率。摘要未提供具体指标或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 未来连边预测不应只依赖历史重复交互，还应同时建模交互序列的时序演化及候选连边与历史的适配关系。该研究表明，候选感知的序列建模有望改善模型对未见连边的泛化，并兼顾预测性能与效率。

Abstract: Future link prediction on temporal graphs involves both repeated interactions between previously connected node pairs and first-time interactions between pairs with no prior interaction. While repeated-edge prediction can exploit direct pairwise evidence, unseen-edge prediction must generalize beyond historical recurrence. Existing temporal graph models often exploit signals tied to recurring interactions or historical overlap, which are highly informative for repeated edges but become limited or unavailable for unseen edges, contributing to substantial performance degradation on unseen-edge prediction. Based on this observation, we identify two complementary signals for modeling sequential dynamics beyond historical recurrence: (1) historical-sequence evolution, which captures dependencies among the source node's chronologically ordered interactions, and (2) candidate--history compatibility, which captures how well a candidate fits the source node's evolving history. Based on the decomposition, we propose MambaTG, a candidate-aware Mamba-based framework that jointly models these two signals. Mamba provides a natural mechanism for this design: its selective recurrence captures the evolution of ordered interaction histories, while its input-dependent state transitions allow the history state to adapt to different candidate inputs. Extensive experiments on 13 temporal graph datasets against 13 representative baselines show that MambaTG achieves strong and consistent performance across both repeated and unseen edges while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [216] [ACSeL: Adaptive Configuration Search for Hardware-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07570)
*Bokun Wang,Hung-Yueh Chiang,Diana Marculescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向资源受限部署的大语言模型配置搜索框架，在满足内存与时延预算的前提下，寻找生成质量较高的模型配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备等部署环境受内存和时延服务等级目标约束，需要联合选择模型规模、批量大小、量化算法及混合精度方案。配置组合数量庞大，穷举搜索不可行；而此类场景通常优先满足资源预算，再在可行配置中追求更高生成质量。

Method: 框架以候选模型与参考模型在少量校准语料上的下一词概率分布差异，构造相对模型质量退化指标，用于低成本估计生成质量，并训练轻量级代理模型直接预测该指标。时延方面，采用少量端到端性能测量校准基于算子性能档案的估计。另设计结构化搜索算法，探索逐层混合精度配置，并筛选预测上同时满足资源约束且质量较高的方案。

Result: 在英伟达锐龙工作站级显卡和英伟达杰特森索尔边缘平台上的实验显示，相对模型质量退化指标评估开销较低，且与下游基准表现高度相关。少量端到端测量校准显著提高了配置满足部署约束的判定效果；结合校准的结构化搜索，在评估配置集合中取得了最高的可行性，并使相对于实测最佳配置的质量退化差距最小。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将低成本质量代理、少量实测时延校准与结构化混合精度搜索结合，可以有效应对资源受限大语言模型部署中的配置选择难题，在满足内存和时延预算的同时，较好地逼近高质量配置。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under tight memory and latency service-level objectives (SLOs), including on edge devices, requires jointly selecting the model size, batch size, quantization algorithm, and mixed-precision quantization recipes. Exhaustive search over this combinatorially large space is infeasible. Unlike data-center serving, which often prioritizes throughput or generation speed, resource-constrained deployments typically set memory and latency budgets as constraints and then seek the configuration with the highest generation quality among feasible ones. To this end, we propose ACSeL, a framework that identifies configurations predicted to satisfy deployment constraints while achieving high predicted generation quality. To enable efficient quality estimation, we use Relative Model Quality Degradation (RMQD), a proxy based on the divergence between the candidate and reference models' next-token distributions over a small calibration corpus. Across the evaluated model sizes and precisions, RMQD is inexpensive to evaluate and strongly correlated with downstream benchmark performance. ACSeL combines (i) a lightweight quality surrogate that predicts RMQD directly from a configuration; (ii) Few-shot Median Calibration (FMC), which calibrates operator-profile-based latency estimates using a small number of end-to-end profiles; and (iii) a structured search algorithm BandPareto for exploring layerwise mixed-precision configurations. Experiments on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 workstation and an NVIDIA Jetson Thor edge platform show that FMC substantially improves deployment feasibility, while BandPareto + FMC achieves the highest feasibility and the lowest RMQD gaps to the best measured configurations in the evaluation set.

</details>


### [217] [Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453)
*Zeyu Leo Liu,Yang Li,Bo Pang,Emre Can Acikgoz,Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Anurag Koul,Srijan Bansal,Erik Nijkamp,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较在线策略蒸馏与组相对策略优化的训练效率。外部教师通常不能让蒸馏更省计算；但若教师是学生自身经组相对策略优化得到的检查点，蒸馏可高效恢复其能力，并能将多个领域的专长整合到单一模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏在学生自身生成的回答上，以冻结教师提供逐词监督，信号更密集；论文检验这种方式是否比强化学习更省训练计算量，并进一步探究教师来源、任务领域和模型规模等因素如何影响效率。

Method: 在不同任务难度、学生与教师规模、领域及优化设置下，系统比较反向散度形式的在线策略蒸馏与组相对策略优化，并以训练浮点运算量和留出集准确率衡量效率。实验还考察两类教师：外部后训练教师，以及学生自身经组相对策略优化训练得到的检查点；最后测试将多个领域专家蒸馏到单一学生的多教师方案。

Result: 在匹配设置下，使用外部后训练教师的蒸馏通常不如组相对策略优化节省计算，且常导致回答变长。若教师是学生自身的组相对策略优化检查点，蒸馏在数学任务上可用少五十倍的蒸馏计算恢复该检查点的准确率；更大模型、代码任务及另一模型系列中也观察到类似效率。多教师蒸馏的阶段计算量，在两个领域和四个领域时分别仅为专家训练计算量的百分之五点二和百分之八点零；计入专家训练后，两领域方案相较直接联合训练仍减少百分之四十二的总计算量。

Conclusion: 在线策略蒸馏的效率并非由监督信号密集这一点自动保证，关键在于教师是否提供适合学生学习的能力。单领域中，蒸馏已有的优化检查点会增加总计算；跨领域时，不同专家各有所长，多教师蒸馏则能以较低额外成本整合能力，并比直接联合训练更省计算。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under a frozen teacher's per-token supervision. Does this dense signal reduce the training FLOPs needed to reach a target accuracy compared with reinforcement learning? We systematically compare vanilla reverse-KL OPD with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across task difficulty, student and teacher size, domain, and optimization choices, measuring held-out accuracy against training FLOPs. With an external post-trained teacher, OPD generally loses to GRPO in accuracy per training FLOP at matched settings, even when the teacher is further trained with GRPO, and often suffers response-length inflation. Yet a different teacher changes the picture: when the teacher is the student's own GRPO checkpoint, OPD recovers that checkpoint's accuracy on math with $50\times$ fewer distillation FLOPs than GRPO needs to reach the same accuracy. Following the same teacher setting, we consistently observe this high efficiency with a larger student, on code, and in a second model family. On a single domain, however, the GRPO checkpoint is already the desired model, so distilling it adds to total compute. Across domains, separate GRPO checkpoints hold different capabilities, and no single checkpoint serves every domain. Multi-teacher OPD (DeepSeek-AI, 2026) consolidates per-domain GRPO experts into a single student that matches each expert's accuracy, with the distillation stage costing only 5.2% (on 2 domains) and 8.0% (on 4 domains) of the specialist training FLOPs. Counting specialist training and distillation together, this pipeline reaches joint GRPO's performance on the 2-domain mix with 42.0% fewer total FLOPs.

</details>


### [218] [Binding Paraphrased Instructions to Encountered Objects in sLLM Agents under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07599)
*Chanyoung Kim,Harim Song,Byoungchan Ko,Keonyoung Lee,Myungwon Lee,Dahuin Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出释义鲁棒性基准与免训练的指代绑定方法，显著提升经强化学习微调的小型大语言模型在具身任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型大语言模型智能体在具身任务上虽可媲美更大型模型，但用户换用不同说法时，智能体的表现可能不稳定。研究发现，物体同义词尤其容易导致失败：智能体即使知道同义词指向什么，也可能在观察到对应物体后仍未能正确选择。部分可观测环境又增加了指代消解与物体绑定的难度。

Method: 研究构建受控释义评测基准，系统测试指令改写对任务表现的影响；并提出免训练框架「绑定意图」，依据指代表达与已观察标签之间的语义接近度，以及候选物体仍未出现的可能性，以概率方式将表达绑定到物体标签。该方法无需额外训练或调用大语言模型。

Result: 释义会使成功率下降21%至52%，主要原因是物体同义词。所提方法使小型大语言模型在同义词释义任务上的成功率提高17.5至49.1个百分点，每步仅增加0.4毫秒的中央处理器开销。应用于七十亿参数的吉格波智能体后，整体成功率从61.8%升至77.4%，超过经提示使用的二百四十亿参数大语言模型。

Conclusion: 具身智能体的关键短板不只是缺乏同义词知识，还包括无法将表达可靠地绑定到环境中的具体物体。通过结合语义相似度与部分可观测条件下的候选估计，免训练的概率绑定机制能够以极低开销提升释义鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of LLM agents requires grounding diverse user expressions in their environments. Although RL-finetuned smaller large language model (sLLM) agents rival larger prompted LLMs on embodied tasks, their robustness to instruction paraphrases remains unclear. We introduce ALFWorld-Para to evaluate this robustness under controlled paraphrases, revealing 21-52% declines in success rates (SRs), predominantly driven by object synonyms. Despite retaining knowledge of synonym referents, agents often fail to take the corresponding objects even after observing them, exposing a gap between referential knowledge and object binding. Partial observability further complicates binding, as referents may remain unseen or have already been encountered under different labels. To address this challenge, we propose BINDIntent, a training-free framework that probabilistically binds referring expressions to observed labels using semantic proximity and estimated persistence against unseen candidates. Without additional training or LLM calls, BINDIntent improves synonym-paraphrase success of RL-finetuned sLLMs by 17.5-49.1 percentage points (pp) with only 0.4 ms of CPU overhead per step. It also raises GiGPO-7B's overall SR from 61.8% to 77.4%, surpassing a prompted 24B LLM. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/cau-hai-lab/ALFWorld-Para.

</details>


### [219] [Monotone Viability Learning for Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07616)
*Ziqian Wang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出单调可行性学习方法，从固定的离线转移数据中学习长期安全可行集，并用于部署时的动作安全认证；实验显示该方法兼具较好的奖励与成本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线安全强化学习依赖固定数据，无法通过在线探索补充信息，因此需要从有限数据中可靠估计长期安全性。现有哈密顿—雅可比可行性学习面临三项挑战：保持安全语义、保证收敛，以及避免数值不稳定；尤其是折扣机制可能使不同时间跨度的学习目标逐渐衰减。

Method: 作者提出单调可行性学习方法，通过单调的有限时域集合递推，从离线转移数据中学习可行集。该递推保留有限时域安全语义，并收敛到无限时域可行核；使用二元监督，使不同时间跨度的目标尺度保持固定，避免折扣造成的数值衰减。论文还给出恢复由行为数据支持的可行子集的充分条件，并将学习到的可行性估计用于部署阶段的动作认证。

Result: 杜宾车实验验证了所学可行集的数值准确性。在安全强化学习基准测试中，该方法取得先进的奖励—成本综合表现；在每项任务上均满足平均成本约束，并在比较方法中实现最低的平均成本。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于单调有限时域递推的离线可行性学习能够兼顾安全语义、长期收敛与数值稳定性，为缺乏可靠动力学模型且不适合在线探索的安全关键系统提供了一种有潜力的长期安全分析与动作认证途径。

Abstract: Offline safe reinforcement learning requires reliable long-horizon safety estimates from fixed data. Learning Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) viability in this setting calls for semantic preservation, convergence, and numerical stability. To address these challenges, we propose Monotone Viability Learning (MoViL), which learns viable sets from offline transitions through monotone finite-horizon set recursion. The recursion preserves finite-horizon safety semantics and converges to the infinite-horizon viability kernel. Binary supervision maintains a fixed target scale across horizons, avoiding discount-induced numerical attenuation. We further establish sufficient conditions for recovering behavior-supported viable subsets. The learned viability estimates are used to certify actions at deployment. Dubins car experiments validate the numerical accuracy of the learned viable sets. Evaluations on safe RL benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art reward-cost performance. MoViL satisfies the average cost constraint on every task and achieves the lowest average cost among the compared methods. These results highlight MoViL's potential to extend long-horizon HJ viability analysis to safety-critical systems where reliable dynamics models are unavailable and online exploration is unsafe.

</details>


### [220] [Online Learning with Ordered Experts (via Two-point Feedback)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07629)
*Shiliang Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究专家建议会任意变化、重排时的两点反馈在线学习，并提出利用端点流估计器将局部观测转化为全体当前建议的无偏损失估计；在两类损失结构下均获得接近全信息基准的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多在线决策场景中，学习者每轮只能选择并观察少数选项，无法获得所有专家建议的损失；同时，建议所在的全序空间可能动态变化或被任意重排。论文旨在利用损失沿序关系的结构，以仅观察两个建议的代价，降低相对于最佳专家的累积遗憾。

Method: 论文分析两种反馈模型：一是损失可分解为隐藏的单调非减分量与单调非增分量，且查询能分别揭示两者；二是损失有界，并沿全序具有有界变差，查询只能揭示总损失。核心方法是边际保持的端点流估计器：两个观测提供序关系上的局部损失增量，再通过随机路径构造望远镜恒等式，为所有当前建议生成无偏损失估计；可逆的成对采样器则保持两次行动各自符合学习者预期的边际分布。进一步利用估计器的二阶矩与稳定性界进行在线更新。

Result: 在可分离单调损失问题中，两个付费观测即可实现遗憾上界 O(log(|ℱ|T)√(T log|ℱ|))，与经典全信息界相比仅差对数因子。在有界变差问题中，遗憾为 Õ(√T+|ℱ|)。论文还将方法应用于专家建议下的动态定价，证明仅提供两个选项的菜单即可达到与全信息速率相差对数因子的表现。

Conclusion: 即使每轮只能观察两个建议，若损失具有单调分解或有界变差结构，仍可通过结构化估计实现较强的在线学习保证。端点流估计器是连接局部反馈与全体建议损失估计的关键，也使该框架适用于动态定价等实际决策问题。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with expert recommendations in a totally ordered action space, where the experts' recommendations may change and be reordered arbitrarily. Our focus is two-point feedback: on each round the learner plays two current recommendations, observes both, and pays their average. We study two problems. In the separated monotone problem, the loss is the sum of a hidden nondecreasing component and a hidden nonincreasing component, and a query reveals the two components separately. In the BV problem, the losses are bounded and have bounded variation (BV) along the order, and a query reveals only the loss.
  In the separated monotone problem, two paid observations achieve \[ O\bigl(\log(|\cF|T)\sqrt{T\log|\cF|}\bigr) \] regret against the best of $|\cF|$ experts, within a logarithmic factor of the classical full-information bound. For the BV problem, we obtain $\wtO(\sqrt T+|\cF|)$ regret. We give an application in dynamic pricing with expert advice, showing that two-option menus matches the full information rate up to $\log$ factors.
  The main technical ingredient is a marginal-preserving endpoint flow estimator. Two observations reveal a loss increment along the order; random paths supply telescoping identities that turn this local information into unbiased loss estimates for all current recommendations. A reversible pair sampler preserves the learner's intended marginal at both played actions. The estimator's second-moment and stability bounds support online updates for both feedback models.

</details>


### [221] [Measuring the Marginal Value of Verifier-Based RL Beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07639)
*Amit Sagtani,Saloni Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，监督微调贡献了绝大部分性能提升；在此基础上，可验证奖励强化学习对同分布表现没有显著增益，但能在分布外测试中带来小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究常将可验证奖励强化学习视为监督微调的替代方案或后续阶段，并默认其本身带来额外收益。本文旨在通过可完整运行、精确对照的实验，直接测量强化学习相对于示范数据的独立贡献。

Method: 研究者构建了一个确定性诊断基准，包含一千八百七十七项合成的图形处理器与大语言模型训练故障诊断任务，覆盖三十种类型，并由可给部分分的程序验证器评分。实验比较监督微调与群组相对策略优化强化学习；预试验后，研究者在正式实验前修订研究问题，重点考察强化学习在示范数据之外的增益。正式实验采用六十步强化学习训练，并另设从基础模型直接启动的完整训练组；同时评估同分布与分布外测试表现。

Result: 一千二百条高质量示范已使监督微调在困难测试集上达到零点七八五。正式实验中，强化学习对同分布表现的提升为零点二二个百分点，置信区间包含零，因而未发现显著改善；对留出的分布外测试集，提升为零点三三个百分点，置信区间未包含零，表明存在幅度较小但可测量的收益。完整训练的冷启动实验也印证了预试验结论：单独强化学习在该任务上未能有效提升表现。监督微调遗留的错误多为样本间一致的系统性错误，可能削弱组内学习信号。

Conclusion: 在这一可复现的受控诊断任务中，示范数据是主要性能来源，可验证奖励强化学习并未显著改善同分布表现，只在分布外泛化上带来有限增益。结果提示，强化学习的附加价值取决于示范数据留下的错误类型及其能否提供有效学习信号；研究结论的适用范围仍受该基准与实验设定限制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is usually evaluated as an alternative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT), or as a stage that follows it, with the implicit assumption that the RL stage contributes gains of its own. We measure that contribution directly, in a controlled setting where both stages can be run to completion and compared exactly. We built gpu-train-debug, a deterministic benchmark of 1,877 synthetic GPU and LLM training failure diagnosis tasks across 30 archetypes, graded by a programmatic verifier with partial credit, and calibrated so that a mid-tier model scores in a 0.60 to 0.75 band. A pilot produced a surprise: 1,200 gold demonstrations nearly solved the benchmark (SFT 0.785 on the hard split), while 15 steps of GRPO from the base model did not move it (0.314 vs 0.311 base). We therefore amended the study, in writing and before the full run, to ask the sharper question: what does verifier-based RL add beyond demonstrations? At the pre-registered scale (60 GRPO steps, learning rate 1e-6), the answer is: no detectable improvement in-distribution (+0.22 points, 95% CI [-0.06, +0.51]), and a modest but measurable improvement under distribution shift (+0.33 points on a held-out OOD split, CI [+0.02, +0.68]). SFT accounted for most of the available improvement, while verifier-based RL produced only modest additional gains under distribution shift. The residual errors SFT leaves are mostly systematic, identical across samples, which likely reduces the within-group learning signal available to GRPO. A full 60-step cold-start GRPO arm confirms the pilot at the full horizon. The whole study cost $8.69 of compute and is fully reproducible from archived generations, frozen configs, and tagged code.

</details>


### [222] [WaSP: Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline for Solver-Free Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07646)
*Ryan Liu,Chuwei Wang,Ricardo Baptista,Andrew Stuart,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出瓦瑟斯坦数据合成流程，通过逐步缩小合成训练数据与目标输入分布之间的差异，提升无需传统数值求解器的神经算子训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子可快速近似偏微分方程的解，但训练通常依赖成本高昂的输入—解配对数据。现有免求解器方法各有不足：物理信息学习需要优化方程残差，较强的残差范数虽有助于控制解误差，却会增大微批次方差、妨碍优化；人工构造数据则可能与目标分布相互奇异，导致模型在目标分布上精度不佳。

Method: 该流程先构造一条逐步趋近目标输入分布的中间分布路径，并沿路径分阶段训练。每一阶段由上一阶段的神经算子生成解，再用精确的偏微分方程算子反推出对应输入，形成下一阶段的训练配对数据。论文还给出了合成过程中分布偏移所导致的逐步误差传播上界。

Result: 在艾伦—卡恩方程、库拉莫托—西瓦辛斯基方程和金兹堡—朗道方程等问题上，该方法相较现有免求解器方法将解误差降低了约三点九至九点五倍；对于其中两个混沌系统，也更准确地再现了统计观测量。

Conclusion: 逐步迁移合成数据分布，可缓解人工构造训练数据与目标分布不匹配的问题，并在理论分析和实验结果上显示出提升神经算子免求解器训练精度的潜力。

Abstract: Neural operators offer fast surrogates for solving PDEs, but training them typically requires expensive input-solution pairs. Two solver-free alternatives include physics-informed learning and training on manufactured data. Physics-informed learning approximates solutions by minimizing PDE residuals. While the residual norm must be strong enough to control solution error, stronger norms increase mini-batch variance and hinder optimization. Manufactured data constructs input-solution pairs for learning by applying the PDE operator to artificial solutions. However, the resulting training distribution can be mutually singular with the target distribution, yielding poor accuracy on the target distribution. We introduce the Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline (WaSP), which trains on manufactured input-solution pairs while gradually mitigating the distribution shift. WaSP synthesizes new pairs by applying the learned operator to a path of intermediate distributions converging to the target input distribution. Training proceeds in stages along this path. At each stage, the previous surrogate generates solutions, and the exact PDE operator derives corresponding inputs, yielding training pairs for the next surrogate. Theoretically, we prove a stepwise error-propagation bound for the distribution shift introduced during synthesis. Empirically, WaSP reduces solution error by $3.9\times$ to $9.5\times$ compared to existing solver-free methods on PDEs such as Allen-Cahn, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, and Ginzburg-Landau, and better reproduces the two chaotic systems' statistical observables.

</details>


### [223] [Simple Meta-Learning for HPO and CASH Using Performance Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07659)
*Emiel Witting,Cheng Yan,Tom Julian Viering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以配置在历史任务上的表现作为表示的元学习方法，用于从固定候选集迭代选择待评估配置；在多模型联合选择与调优任务中，它以更少试验达到相同性能，且实现简单、成本较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化常用显式超参数编码作为代理模型输入，但在高维搜索空间中，代理模型难以据此学到有效的性能规律。论文希望利用跨任务的历史表现构造替代表示，降低对搜索空间编码及复杂元学习方案的依赖。

Method: 将每个配置表示为其在既往任务上的历史表现，并将该表示直接输入标准代理模型。作者还将此表示用于最大边际相关性这一多样性重排序准则，使其成为不依赖后验分布的候选选择规则。研究范围限于固定候选集中的迭代选择，不涵盖完整自动化机器学习系统中的运行时间估计、成本感知预算分配或集成等功能。

Result: 在塔布竞技场基准的多模型选择与调优任务中，历史表现表示搭配线性贝叶斯岭回归，优于专门的元学习基线，并大约只需一半试验次数即可达到相同调优性能。在单模型超参数优化任务中，该方法仍具竞争力，排名第二。两个基准上的遗憾表现均与对照方法接近或更好，同时复杂度和成本更低；方法无需预训练或额外调参，且扩展性不受搜索空间维度影响。

Conclusion: 以跨任务历史表现取代显式超参数编码，是一种简单而有效的配置表示方式，可提升多模型选择与调优效率，并在单模型优化中保持较强表现。其优势在于减少试验和实现开销，但结论针对固定候选集的迭代选择，不能直接视为完整自动化机器学习流程的评估。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization for Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) and Combined Algorithm Selection and Hyperparameter Optimization (CASH) often relies on explicit hyperparameter encodings as surrogate inputs. In high-dimensional search spaces, however, such representations can make it difficult for standard surrogates to learn useful performance models. We instead study an alternative meta-learning representation that describes each configuration directly as its historical performance across prior tasks. This representation can be paired directly with standard surrogate models. Our scope is the iterative selection component of AutoML, deciding which configuration to evaluate next from a fixed candidate set, rather than a complete framework with features such as runtime estimation, cost-aware budgeting and ensembling. We further apply this representation to maximal marginal relevance (MMR), a diversity-based re-ranking criterion from recommender systems, as a posterior-free acquisition rule. When selecting and optimising across multiple models on the TabArena benchmark, the performance-based representation paired with a linear Bayesian ridge regression model outperforms dedicated meta-learning baselines, achieving the same tuned performance in roughly half the number of trials. For single-model HPO, it remains a competitive second. Across both benchmarks this method closely matches or improves on regret at a fraction of the complexity and cost, requiring no pre-training or tuning and scaling independently of search space dimensionality. Code for reproducing our results is available at https://github.com/EWitting/simple-metalearning.

</details>


### [224] [An Information Bottleneck for Post-hoc Memorization Mitigation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07669)
*Dina EL Zein,Shashi Kumar,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在冻结的大语言模型中加入非参数变分信息瓶颈，以减少训练数据记忆内容的提取，同时尽量保持语言建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能记忆并复现受版权保护的训练数据，引发隐私与知识产权风险。重新训练或修改模型本体成本较高，因此论文探索能否通过外接模块，在不改动底层模型的情况下缓解记忆问题。

Method: 提出一种模块化、即插即用的方案：在冻结模型的最终隐状态与语言建模输出层之间加入非参数变分信息瓶颈。该模块压缩用于预测下一个词的表示，目标是在保留通用语言知识的同时过滤高度具体的记忆序列。研究通过书籍段落记忆提取任务评估记忆可提取性，并用标准语言建模基准衡量效用变化。

Result: 实验表明，该信息瓶颈层显著降低了书籍内容的可提取性，同时在基准任务上维持了有竞争力的表现。摘要未提供具体数值，因此无法判断降低幅度及不同任务间的详细权衡。

Conclusion: 在冻结的大语言模型上后置加入信息瓶颈，可能是一种实用且不依赖特定架构的记忆缓解途径。不过，其有效性仍需结合具体指标、任务范围及不同模型上的实验结果进一步评估。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce copyrighted training data, raising serious privacy and intellectual property concerns. In this work, we investigate whether memorization can be mitigated without modifying or retraining the underlying model through an information bottleneck. We propose a modular plug-and-play framework that inserts a nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) between a frozen LLM backbone and its language modeling head. By compressing the final hidden representations before token prediction, the proposed bottleneck aims to preserve general linguistic knowledge while filtering out highly specific memorized sequences. We evaluate our approach on memorization extraction tasks involving book passages and on standard language modeling benchmarks to measure the trade-off between memorization reduction and downstream utility. Our experiments show that the NVIB layer substantially reduces extractability while maintaining competitive performance on benchmark tasks. These results suggest that adding an information bottleneck offers a practical and architecture agnostic direction for post-hoc memorization mitigation in pretrained LLMs.

</details>


### [225] [Calibrated Target Noise Recovers Curvature from the Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07671)
*Arash Jamshidi,Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich,Aristides Gionis,Kai Puolamäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法通过扰动训练目标并分析批次梯度的协方差，仅凭梯度即可估计实用的曲率矩阵，并可用于预条件优化和提前停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲率信息对优化预条件、采样和提前停止等任务很重要，但实际训练中往往只能获得聚合的小批次梯度，无法访问逐样本梯度或二阶导数。论文旨在解决仅依赖梯度估计曲率的难题。

Method: 向训练目标中注入均值为零、方差依据局部输出曲率校准的噪声，再计算由此产生的批次梯度协方差。该协方差无需逐样本梯度、二阶导数或其他网络内部量，即可恢复曲率矩阵。对于广泛的非线性模型，该方法可恢复广义高斯—牛顿矩阵；对于前馈整流线性单元网络，还可恢复总体海森矩阵的对角块。

Result: 实验显示，该估计器能够准确恢复曲率信息；用于预条件时可加快优化，并且估计得到的广义高斯—牛顿矩阵有助于提前停止。论文还指出，该方法轻量、易于融入现有训练流程，并具有理论上的最优性保证。

Conclusion: 通过对目标进行恰当扰动，梯度协方差能够成为实用的曲率估计工具。该方案降低了获取曲率信息对二阶计算和内部模型信息的依赖，为优化加速及训练过程控制提供了简洁途径。

Abstract: Estimating curvature information from gradients alone is a fundamental problem in machine learning. The challenge becomes particularly acute in settings where only aggregate mini-batch gradients are available, yet curvature information is still required for applications such as preconditioning in optimisation, sampling, and early stopping. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that a simple target-perturbation scheme suffices to recover such curvature information from gradients alone. Our method injects zero-mean noise into the targets, with variance calibrated to the local output curvature. The covariance of the resulting noisy batch gradients then recovers practical curvature matrices, without requiring per-sample gradients, second-order derivatives, or other internal network quantities. For a broad class of non-linear models, our scheme recovers the Generalised Gauss--Newton (GGN) matrix; for feed-forward ReLU networks, it additionally recovers the diagonal blocks of the population Hessian. We demonstrate that the resulting estimator is simple, lightweight, provably optimal, and easy to integrate into existing training pipelines. Experiments demonstrate accurate curvature recovery, faster optimisation when the estimator is used for preconditioning, and the utility of the estimated GGN for early stopping.

</details>


### [226] [Instrumental Variables in Continuous-Time Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07674)
*Lars Pennig,Søren Wengel Mogensen,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为连续时间随机系统建立基于随机过程型工具变量的因果识别与估计框架，并将其扩展到非线性因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工具变量方法主要适用于静态或离散时间情形，尚不能直接处理连续时间动态；在存在未观测混杂时，现有极大似然方法和离散时间工具变量方法可能产生系统性偏差。

Method: 针对由随机微分方程描述的平稳连续时间随机过程，在漂移项的无穷小尺度刻画结构稀疏性与因果效应，并将这些条件与离散化过程上的工具变量假设加以区分。利用路径值工具变量推导矩约束；对奥恩斯坦—乌伦贝克过程给出简化形式、识别条件及一致估计方法；进一步借助签名核处理非线性因果效应。

Result: 摘要报告，所提方法能够在存在混杂的连续时间系统中恢复因果效应；相比之下，基于极大似然的估计和离散时间工具变量方法会出现系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 研究将工具变量因果推断拓展到连续时间随机动力系统，提供了从结构假设、识别条件到一致估计的完整框架，并展示了利用路径信息处理非线性因果关系的可能性。

Abstract: Causal questions often arise in systems that evolve in continuous time. While instrumental variable (IV) methods provide a principled approach to identify causal effects in static or discrete-time settings, even in the presence of unobserved confounding, they do not yet extend to continuous-time dynamics. In this work, we develop an IV framework for stationary continuous-time stochastic processes parameterized by stochastic differential equations (SDE), where the instrument itself takes the form of a stochastic process. We encode the structural sparsity and causal effects directly at the infinitesimal level of the drift, and contrast these assumptions with the IV assumptions on the discretized version of the same process. Based on these structural assumptions, we derive moment restrictions using path-valued instruments. We show how these restrictions simplify for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes and present an identification condition together with a corresponding consistent estimation method. Finally, we extend our estimation framework to nonlinear causal effects by expressing the path-based moment restrictions leveraging the signature kernel. Empirically, our method recovers causal effects in confounded continuous-time systems where maximum-likelihood-based estimation and discrete-time IV methods are systematically biased.

</details>


### [227] [$O(1/n)$ Convergence Rate for RVI $Q$-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07676)
*Avik Kar,Siddharth Chandak,Rahul Singh,Shalabh Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：对有限弱沟通马尔可夫决策过程，采用合适步长的同步相对价值迭代动作价值学习，其贝尔曼跨度残差均方和贪心策略期望次优性均以迭代次数倒数的速度下降，因此达到期望精度 ε 只需与 ε 倒数同阶的迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步相对价值迭代动作价值学习虽用于平均回报问题，但其有限时间收敛速度及策略质量的理论保证仍不充分。本文旨在建立适用于有限弱沟通马尔可夫决策过程的定量收敛界，并将迭代误差进一步转化为策略次优性保证。

Method: 首先研究有限维赋范空间中受随机噪声驱动的克拉斯诺谢尔斯基—曼迭代。对于非扩张且线性正则的映射，在噪声满足鞅差条件、条件二阶矩至多随残差平方线性增长时，证明末次迭代的均方残差界。使用阶数为迭代次数负幂的多项式步长，可得到相应的残差收敛速率。随后验证有限弱沟通马尔可夫决策过程的中心化贝尔曼算子满足线性正则性与噪声条件，并应用一般收敛结果分析学习算法。

Result: 采用合适步长时，贝尔曼跨度残差的均方为迭代次数倒数阶。利用最优策略与次优确定性平稳策略之间的正收益差，可进一步证明贪心策略的期望次优性为迭代次数倒数阶。因此，达到期望 ε 最优所需的同步迭代次数为 ε 倒数阶。收敛界中的常数依赖于具体问题，包括命中时间参数和第二优策略的次优性差距。

Conclusion: 本文为有限弱沟通马尔可夫决策过程中的同步相对价值迭代动作价值学习提供了有限时间收敛保证，并建立了从迭代残差到贪心策略性能的联系。其效率保证具有问题依赖性，实际常数会受到状态转移所需时间及策略间收益差距的影响。

Abstract: We establish finite-time convergence guarantee for synchronous relative value iteration (RVI) $Q$-learning for finite weakly communicating Markov decision processes (MDPs). For stochastic Krasnosel'skiĭ--Mann iterations driven by a non-expansive, linearly regular map on a finite-dimensional normed space, we establish last-iterate mean-squared residual bounds under martingale-difference noise whose conditional second moment grows at most linearly with the squared residual. Suitable polynomial stepsizes of order $n^{-a}$ yield an $O(n^{-a})$ rate for $0<a\leq1$. We verify linear regularity and the noise condition for the centered Bellman operator for finite weakly communicating MDPs. Applying the general result gives an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the mean-squared Bellman span residual of RVI $Q$-learning iterates when a suitable stepsize sequence is used. The positive gap between optimal and suboptimal stationary deterministic policies then yields an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the expected suboptimality of the greedy policy. Consequently, $O(ε^{-1})$ synchronous iterations suffice for an $ε$-optimal policy in expectation. These guarantees are instance-dependent, with constants that depend on the hitting-time parameters and the suboptimality gap of the second-best policy.

</details>


### [228] [Non-Parametric Mutual Information Estimation with Vine Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07693)
*Sigurd Holmsen,Berit Omli Øksnes,Ingrid Hobæk Haff,Sylvia Richardson,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于简化D藤 copula 的非参数互信息估计方法，在低至中维数据上兼顾较高精度与较低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经互信息估计方法可能计算开销较大。论文希望利用联合分布的结构特点，构造更灵活且高效的非参数估计方法，并检验其在不同数据条件下的表现。

Method: 以简化D藤 copula 近似联合密度，将密度比化简为一系列成对 copula 的乘积，再对这些成对 copula 进行非参数估计，由此得到互信息估计器VINE。

Result: 在简化D藤假设成立及不成立的多种数据集上，VINE与先进神经互信息估计器进行比较。摘要指出，VINE在低至中维数据上具有有竞争力的估计精度，同时计算成本更低；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 借助简化D藤结构，VINE将互信息估计转化为对成对依赖关系的非参数建模，在一定维度范围内实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce VINE: a non-parametric mutual information (MI) estimator based on simplified D-vine copulas. Approximating the joint density with a simplified D-vine allows the density ratio to simplify to a product of pair copulas, which we can estimate non-parametrically. This structural simplification yields VINE, a flexible and computationally efficient MI estimator. We compare VINE against SotA neural MI estimators on a range of datasets, both under settings where the simplified D-vine assumption holds and where it is violated, demonstrating competitive accuracy at a lower computational cost for low- to medium-dimensional datasets.

</details>


### [229] [Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695)
*Shi Yin,Zujian Dai,Xinyang Pan,Lixin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在直积空间中直接处理哈密顿量算符块的等变学习框架，并构建包含逾十一万种材料结构的数据集；实验显示其预测精度与推理效率均优于现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现有等变模型在不可约球张量的直和空间中传递特征，而哈密顿量本身更自然地表示为局域轨道与自旋基上的直积空间算符块。两种表示不匹配会使连续矩阵块碎片化，妨碍轨道耦合的直接操作，并带来繁复变换、低效的硬件执行和内存管理。

Method: 提出一种直接在哈密顿量式算符块上进行消息传递的等变框架。理论上，采用基于李代数生成元的方向嵌入，以及带不变权重的左右乘积与收缩，使模型在不显式进行克莱布施—戈丹分解或逆重构的情况下，保持对三维旋转、二重旋转和空间反演的等变性。具体实现为变换器架构：矩阵或张量特征在直积空间中演化，并由不变标量路由控制结构化门控、方向传播、注意力及环境嵌入的上下文交互。另构建了包含逾十一万种材料结构、覆盖六十八种元素并提供哈密顿量与波函数数据的大规模数据集。

Result: 在新构建的数据集及另一含自旋—轨道耦合的材料哈密顿量数据集上，实验表明该方法相较当前先进方法显著提升了哈密顿量预测精度，并提高了推理效率。摘要未提供具体数值，因而无法进一步量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: 直接在与目标物理算符相匹配的直积表示中学习，有望同时缓解表示不匹配与计算效率瓶颈。该方法将等变性与面向硬件的矩阵运算结合，为高效、准确的材料哈密顿量预测提供了新的路径。

Abstract: Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [230] [Evaluating Language Model Features for Zero-Shot Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Isolation Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07720)
*Romain Hermary,Nesryne Mejri,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在零样本条件下，将大语言模型作为特征提取器用于多变量时间序列异常检测，并通过孤立森林评估所得特征的异常区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测缺乏标注数据，同时要求精确识别局部异常。现有大语言模型方法常依赖预测或重构等辅助目标，但模型表达能力增强后，可能连异常也一并预测或重构，从而削弱检测依据；常见的分词方式还可能丢失时间细节与变量结构。

Method: 研究不再让大语言模型执行预测或重构，而是直接分析其提取的特征空间，考察异常特征能否与正常特征分离。采用每个时间步对应一个词元的简易分词方案，以保留局部信息；随后使用无监督孤立森林评估特征对异常检测的适用性，并探究模型表现是否源于语言预训练。

Result: 摘要未提供具体数值或模型间比较结果。作者称研究给出了大语言模型表征多变量时间序列并用于异常检测的实证认识，并考察了时间与上下文层面的区分能力及语言预训练的作用。

Conclusion: 本文提出一种直接利用大语言模型特征进行多变量时间序列异常检测的零样本研究路径，避免将预测或重构作为检测代理目标。其核心价值在于评估特征空间本身的异常可分性，并为跨模态基础模型在时间序列任务中的应用提供经验依据。

Abstract: Time-Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD) is a fundamentally challenging task: it is unsupervised, requires high local precision capabilities, and suffers from limited labelled data. Recent work has explored adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to time-series via cross-modal transfer. However, existing approaches are poorly suited to Multivariate TSAD (MTSAD), as tokenisation often obscures fine-grained temporal and variables structures, and most methods optimise auxiliary objectives such as forecasting or reconstruction. We posit that these surrogate objectives become unreliable as model capacity increases. Highly expressive models risk reconstructing or forecasting anomalies, undermining the foundations of both strategies. Instead, we propose to use LLMs purely as feature extractors and directly analyse their induced feature spaces. In this work, investigate the ability of LLMs to provide useful representations for MTSADs in a zero-shot setting through classification. To this end, we study the separability of the extracted features, under the assumption that anomalous features should be isolated from normal ones. We analyse their temporal and contextual discriminative ability using simple step-wise tokenisation schemes, with one time-step per token, which preserve fine-grained local information. Their usability for MTSAD is assessed using an unsupervised Isolation Forest, enabling a direct evaluation of the resulting representation quality. We further investigate whether the observed performance can be attributed to the language-related pre-training of the LLMs or arises independently of it. Our results provide empirical insight into the ability of LLMs to represent multivariate time-series for anomaly detection and highlight broader applications of cross-modal foundation models.

</details>


### [231] [Reinforcing What the Student Has Learned: Teacher-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07722)
*Bozhen Fang,Linhao Zhong,Xuehao Wang,Huanyi Zheng,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用学生模型自身后训练变化来调节蒸馏信号的方法，在五组实验配置中显著提升数学推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏利用教师模型对学生生成内容提供密集反馈，但反馈强度存在明显偏差：在数学推理回答中，风格相关位置的教师与学生对数概率差绝对值均值，是数学内容位置的三点五倍。因此，直接按差异大小加权更新，可能将过多训练力度用于模仿表达风格，而非提升数学推理能力。

Method: 提出学生差分在线策略蒸馏方法，以学生模型当前版本与其预训练基础版本之间的差异作为参照，捕捉学生在后训练中形成的词元偏好。方法将学生差异居中处理，并仅保留其符号与居中后的教师—学生反馈方向一致的系数；每轮训练均重新计算学生信号，以适应学生不断变化的状态。

Result: 在五组教师—学生配置中，学生模型规模覆盖十七亿至八十亿参数。相较于常规在线策略蒸馏，该方法在六项数学任务上的平均首答正确率指标（十六次采样平均）提高七点七六至十点三七个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学生自身后训练形成的偏好可作为有效参照，帮助蒸馏更新减少对教师表达风格的过度追随，并更有效地改善数学推理表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model reasoning through dense teacher feedback on student-generated responses.We first observe an amplitude disparity in this feedback: on math reasoning responses, style-related positions exhibit 3.5 times the mean absolute teacher--student log-probability difference of mathematical positions.Scaling OPD updates by these discrepancies thus directs much of the update strength toward matching the teacher's expression. We instead take the student's own pretrained base as a reference: the student--base difference records the token preferences acquired during post-training and provides supervision that does not depend on the student's distance from the teacher. Building on this reference, we propose Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation (SD-OPD), which uses centered student--base differences as update coefficients, keeps each coefficient only when its sign agrees with centered teacher--student feedback, and recomputes the student signal every training round. Across 5 teacher--student configurations with Qwen3 students of 1.7B to 8B parameters, SD-OPD improves six-task mathematics average pass@1 (avg@16) over OPD by 7.76 to 10.37 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/aim-uofa/sd-opd.

</details>


### [232] [Computation, not weight access: large language models as auditors of clinical prediction networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07847)
*Zhiyu Liu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，借助计算工具并通过明确指令引导，前沿大语言模型能够仅凭小型神经网络的预测结果审计模型关系与群体偏差；单纯把权重放进提示中则效果有限。对于这些审计任务，计算能力和任务引导比直接读取权重更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有展示声称，大语言模型可以直接读取、编辑和编写小型神经网络的权重，因此可能只凭模型文件审计临床预测模型。但相关展示往往没有区分模型究竟是在解读权重，还是借助代码实际运行计算。本研究通过预先设定的模拟实验，分离这些条件，检验模型审计能力的来源及其适用范围。

Method: 研究者训练了权重规模为105至38,401个参数的双曲正切神经网络，数据模拟自蛛网膜下腔出血患者队列，并设置真实关系、无关系以及某亚组死亡被低记录的偏差情形。前沿模型及其他四种同系列模型分别在默认或明确的系统指令下完成单轮任务；实验条件包括不使用工具、通过计算工具查看权重，或仅查看网络预测结果。另将精心设计的脚本与模型审计和修复表现进行比较。

Result: 不使用工具时，Astra模型能在353个权重的网络中找出预设关系，但在3,457个权重时表现不稳定；默认指令下，它对12项审计与修复任务中的11项以无法识别为由拒绝作答，明确指令则消除了这些拒答。使用工具后，该模型在规模不超过38,401个权重的网络中，29次试验均找回预设关系；在15次无关系试验中没有误报，并在6次试验中全部发现存在偏差的亚组。仅提供预测结果的黑盒条件与提供权重的白盒条件表现相当，其他同系列模型的读取准确性也相近。设计良好的脚本在审计任务及最佳模型修复结果上达到相当水平。

Conclusion: 在本研究涉及的小型临床预测网络中，大语言模型审计效果主要取决于是否能进行计算以及指令是否清晰，而不是能否直接访问网络权重。仅凭模型文件审计的设想因此需要谨慎界定：不借助工具时能力有限，而计算工具与恰当引导可显著提升表现；简单脚本也可能提供同等水平的审计能力。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have been reported to read, edit and write the weights of small neural networks, raising the prospect of auditing clinical prediction models from their files alone. Public demonstrations, however, mixed reading raw weights with running code. We separated these conditions in internally pre-specified simulation experiments. Tanh networks of 105 to 38,401 weights were trained on simulated cohorts of patients with subarachnoid haemorrhage, each with a planted relationship or none, and one network was trained on labels that under-recorded deaths in a subgroup. GPT-6 Astra and four other GPT models answered single prompts under a default or an explicit system instruction, either with the weights in the prompt and no tools, or with a calculation-only tool that exposed the weights (white box) or only the network's predictions (black box). Without tools, Astra recovered planted relationships in 353-weight networks but became unreliable at 3,457 weights, and under the default instruction it declined 11 of 12 audit and repair tasks as unidentifiable; the explicit instruction removed these refusals. With the tool, it recovered the planted relationship in all 29 runs on networks of up to 38,401 weights, raised no false alarm in 15 runs on null networks, and found the biased subgroup in 6 of 6 runs. Black-box access performed as well as white-box access, and other GPT-6 models read as accurately. A well-designed script matched the comparator models on the audit and the best LLM repair. For auditing these networks, what mattered was computation and elicitation, not access to the weights.

</details>


### [233] [Static Recovery Is Not Dynamic Stability: Dynamics-Aware Benchmarking of Protein Motif Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07723)
*Emile de Bruyn,Moritz Schäffler,Jannik Schneider,Sebastian R. Schmidt,Chanwoong Hwang,Aleena Siji,Utkarsh Upadhyay,Stefan Bauer,Alexander Schug,Stefan Kesselheim,Artur Yakimovich,Alina Bazarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两套互补的蛋白质基序支架评测方法：一套扩大测试基序范围，另一套用分子动力学检验设计的动态稳定性。结果表明，静态折叠成功并不等于支架具有可靠的动态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质基序支架基准通常依赖少量人工挑选的基序，并以静态结构折叠结果判定成功。然而，测试对象的选择会影响模型比较结论，而蛋白质功能往往还取决于构象集合及其转变。因此，需要扩大基准覆盖范围，并将动态行为纳入成功标准。

Method: 作者构建了两套评测体系。第一套从与蛋白质家族数据库条目关联的实验结构中系统筛选结构保守的功能基序，形成包含二百二十个案例、涵盖一百七十四种基序模式的基准，并评估六种近期支架生成模型的目标覆盖率和独特成功方案产出。第二套建立基于参考结构的全原子分子动力学评测框架，针对既有基准中静态折叠成功的设计，比较基序偏移、支架偏移、基序接触保持情况，以及基序二面角自由能景观。

Result: 不同模型呈现出不同优势：射线扩散模型和蛋白质生成模型解决的目标更多，而序列—结构联合模型在其成功解决的目标上产生了更多独特支架。在五十五个静态折叠成功的模型—问题组合中，三十一个通过分子动力学支持的标准，只有八个通过全部检查。紧凑且相对自足的基序较常获得动态支持；类似界面或依赖周边环境的基序则更容易失败。

Conclusion: 静态折叠结果能够为局部基序是否恢复提供有用证据，但不足以证明整个支架具有动态保真度。评估基序支架生成应同时考察目标覆盖率、独特成功方案产出和分子动力学支持的动态保真度。

Abstract: Protein motif scaffolding benchmarks assess whether generative models can build scaffolds around functionally important residues. Their conclusions depend on which motifs are tested and which criteria define success. Existing benchmarks typically use small hand-curated motif sets and static refolding criteria, but protein function often depends on conformational ensembles and transitions. Here, we address both benchmark scope and success criteria. We introduce BILBY-PRO, a systematically derived benchmark of structurally conserved functional motifs from PROSITE-linked experimental structures, comprising 220 cases from 174 PROSITE motif-pattern entries. Evaluating six recent scaffold-generation models reveals distinct patterns in target coverage and unique-solution yield: RFDiffusion and Proteina solve more targets, whereas ESM3 produces more unique successful scaffolds on the targets it solves. We further introduce BILBY-MD, a reference-based all-atom molecular dynamics evaluation framework. Applied to statically solved designs from the original MotifBench benchmark, it compares motif drift, scaffold drift, motif-contact preservation, and motif-dihedral free-energy landscapes against reference simulations. Of 55 statically solved model-problem pairs, 31 satisfy our MD-supported criterion and only 8 pass all checks. Compact, self-contained motifs are often MD-supported, whereas interface-like or context-dependent motifs fail more frequently. Static refolding success therefore provides useful evidence of local motif recovery but does not establish dynamical fidelity of the full scaffold. BILBY highlights three complementary axes of motif-scaffolding evaluation: target coverage, unique-solution yield, and MD-supported dynamical fidelity.

</details>


### [234] [When, Where, How: Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07886)
*Christian M. M. Frey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表格基础模型在逐步获取标注数据时，何时以及如何选择样本最有效；结果显示，早期应优先扩大样本多样性，整体上结合目标样本的邻近关系与预测信息增益能取得最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型可利用上下文中的已标注数据适应新任务，但现实中的高质量标签往往需要昂贵的领域知识。主动学习在此场景下还有一个特点：每加入新标签，负责指导后续选样的模型状态也随之改变。因此，研究需要厘清随着上下文增大，多样性、预测不确定性和面向目标的信息增益各自何时最有价值。

Method: 作者研究了两种选样策略：面向目标的近邻选择，优先标注与目标输入相关的池中样本；以及上下文中的期望预测信息增益，衡量标注某个样本对目标预测的预期帮助。研究将它们与经典主动学习规则进行比较，并在二十个基准任务上评估两种表格基础模型及经典梯度提升决策树。

Result: 实验发现：一是多样性驱动的选样在最初几轮效果最好；二是选择能够服务于目标输入的池中样本至关重要；三是期望预测信息增益在标签预算用尽时表现最佳。在池规模最大的八个基准上，仅标注池中百分之五至百分之十的数据，模型平均即可保留相对于机会水平校正后的全数据性能的百分之八十至百分之八十七。此外，这种选样也能提升梯度提升决策树的预测表现。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的主动上下文学习需要根据标注进程调整选样重点：早期重视多样性，随后关注与目标输入相关的信息，并以预期预测收益指导选择。有效的选样不仅能显著减少标注需求，也可能惠及其他传统表格模型。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) adapt to a new task only through the labeled rows in their context. However, labels are expensive to acquire in real-world scenarios due to the need for highly specific domain knowledge to ensure high-quality data. Active in-context learning transfers classical acquisition rules to this setting, in which the model that guides acquisition changes with every label added. We ask how the value of diversity, predictive uncertainty and target-aware information gain changes as a frozen TFM acquires more context. For that, we study Target-Aware Nearest Neighbor (TANN) acquisition and the Expected Predictive Information Gain in Context (EPIC) and evaluate them against classical rules. Our study across twenty benchmarks with two well-established TFMs and classical GBDTs highlights three findings: (i) When: diversity-based acquisition is best during the first acquisition iterations; (ii) Where: labeling pool rows that serve the target inputs via a simple target-aware nearest-neighbor rule matters substantially; (iii) How: EPIC yields the best models at the label budget, retains on average 80--87% of the chance-corrected full-data performance with only 5-10% of the pool labeled on the eight benchmarks with the largest pools, and selects labels that also improve the predictive performance of gradient-boosted decision trees.

</details>


### [235] [Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741)
*Qiufu Li,Huibin Xiao,Linlin Shen,Weijia Fan,Xi Jia,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出分类准确率不等同于特征质量，并重新审视二元交叉熵与交叉熵在特征学习中的作用；适中的归一化空间尺度因子有助于兼顾可靠分类与良好特征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督特征学习通常以分类准确率作为特征质量的替代指标，但准确率主要反映模型对单个样本的局部判断，无法充分体现特征在整个数据集上的类内紧凑性和类间可分性。论文旨在从特征学习的整体目标出发，分析损失函数与特征几何结构之间的联系。

Method: 从特征学习的基本原理出发，重新设计二元交叉熵损失，并在归一化特征空间中分析二元交叉熵与交叉熵损失的极小值，考察它们如何形成神经坍塌。进一步研究不同尺度因子下两种损失的收敛行为，以比较分类准确率和特征质量的变化。

Result: 分析表明，两种损失均可促使不同类别的样本特征达到类内最大紧凑、类间最大分离的理想状态。尺度因子过大时，模型虽可实现完美分类，但特征几何结构趋于理想状态的速度较慢；尺度因子过小时，分类结果容易受到微小扰动影响。适中的尺度因子更有利于兼顾分类可靠性和特征结构质量。实验还显示，二元交叉熵在长尾识别和开放集识别中具有优势。

Conclusion: 分类准确率不能单独作为特征质量的充分衡量标准。训练时应同时关注分类表现与数据集层面的特征结构，并合理设置归一化空间的尺度因子。由于二元交叉熵与特征学习的整体要求更加契合，它在长尾及开放集识别任务中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $γ$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $γ$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $γ$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $γ$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

</details>


### [236] [DISCERN: Distributional Synthetic Replay for Retain-Free Incremental Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07892)
*Nicolás Segura-Kunsagi,Juan Luis Suárez-Díaz,Iván Sevillano-García,Francisco Herrera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需保留集的增量类别遗忘方法，通过为各类别拟合并选择合适的概率分布来合成保留类嵌入，尤其能稳定处理变换器模型中的连续删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中的删除请求往往陆续到达，因此模型需要逐次遗忘类别，同时确保已删除类别不会重新出现，并尽量保留其余类别的性能。现有原型回放通常假设每类嵌入围绕质心呈各向同性高斯分布，但不同架构的潜在表示几何可能不同，尤其变换器嵌入往往集中在超球面上的类别方向附近。

Method: 提出一种基于干预的无保留集增量类别遗忘方法，以每类参数化分布生成的合成嵌入替代真实保留数据。方法针对每类拟合欧氏分布（高斯、拉普拉斯、斯图登特分布）和方向分布（冯·米塞斯—费舍尔、费舍尔—宾厄姆），先通过统计检验选择分布；若均未通过，则依据交叉验证对数似然进行选择。合成嵌入用于支持剩余类别，降低连续遗忘时已删除知识再次出现的风险。

Result: 在手写数字、柯达十类和柯达百类数据集上，研究使用两种卷积网络和两种变换器骨干，并评估单类、多类及增量遗忘。该方法在同时删除类别时具有竞争力；在变换器上的增量遗忘中稳定性最佳。以视觉变换器大模型为例，连续删除二十类后，其调和平均值为零点七九七，而同时删除设定中表现最强的方法降至零点零四八。配对消融实验显示，逐类选择分布能显著减少已遗忘类别重新出现，但其作用主要是稳定累积遗忘，并非对所有性能指标均带来普遍提升。

Conclusion: 无保留集的合成嵌入回放可用于逐次类别遗忘，而依据类别及模型潜在几何自适应选择分布，是提升变换器模型遗忘稳定性的关键。该方法的主要价值在于抑制已遗忘知识的回流，尤其适用于连续删除场景。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. In real deployments, however, deletion requests arrive over time, calling for incremental class unlearning (ICU): classes are removed sequentially, previously forgotten knowledge must remain forgotten, and the remaining classes must be preserved. We present DIstributional SynthetiC rEplay for Retain-free iNcremental unlearning (DISCERN), an intervention-based class unlearning method that replaces access to the retain set with synthetic embeddings sampled from per-class parametric distributions. Unlike prototype-based replay, which assumes an isotropic Gaussian around each class centroid, DISCERN fits Euclidean (Gaussian, Laplace, Student's $t$) and directional (von Mises--Fisher, Fisher--Bingham) families to each class and selects among them through statistical tests and, when none of them accepts, cross-validated log-likelihood. This design is motivated by the architecture-dependent latent geometry: transformer embeddings lie approximately on a hypersphere and concentrate around class directions, unlike those of convolutional networks. We evaluate DISCERN against seven retain-free baselines on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 with two convolutional and two transformer backbones, under single-class, multi-class, and incremental unlearning. DISCERN is competitive when classes are removed at once and is the most stable method in ICU on transformers: on ViT-B/16, after 20 sequential deletions it retains a harmonic mean of $0.797$, whereas the strongest method in the simultaneous setting drops from $0.918$ to $0.048$. A paired ablation shows that per-class distribution selection substantially reduces the resurfacing of previously forgotten classes, acting as a stabilizer of accumulated forgetting rather than a uniform improvement.

</details>


### [237] [Ordering Multivariate Time Series by Volatility Memory: A Temporal Convolutional Approach to Volatility Heredity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07746)
*Kaoutar El Azaar,Mohamed Hamlich,Moncef Garouani,Franck Ravat,Asmae Chakir,Mohamed Tabaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合波动率记忆估计与时序卷积学习的多变量时间序列排序方法，用于识别变量间的先后关系，但不用于推断因果图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动率变化可能揭示条件均值中看不出的时间关系，但单个变量自身的持续性会掩盖其与其他变量的依赖，因此难以提取可靠的方向性信息。

Method: 该方法将准最大似然估计得到的持续性参数，以及矩阵铅笔法提取的时间模式，构造成网络中的结构化滤波器；再结合学习得到的滤波器，表征目标变量的历史信息并比较变量间的方向性证据，最终汇总为全局先后排序。该排序不代表因果关系图。

Result: 在包含线性与非线性波动交互的合成系统，以及具有已知参考关系的金融和功能性磁共振成像基准上进行了评估。在四种合成波动机制中，该方法的平均排序质量高于外部领先—滞后排序方法及其平方输入变体；但在其中两种机制下，相对于平方输入变体的优势尚不确定。论文还描述性地报告了其在外汇、黄金和加密货币市场中的应用。

Conclusion: 结合波动率持续性估计、时间模式与时序卷积学习，有助于提升多变量时间序列的先后排序表现。该方法提供的是变量间的方向性排序，而非因果推断；其相对优势在不同波动机制下并非都确定。

Abstract: Volatility dynamics can carry information about temporal relationships that is not apparent in the conditional mean. Extracting this information is difficult because the persistence of each variable can obscure its dependence on other variables. This paper presents QS-TCN, a method for ordering multivariate time series that combines statistical estimates of volatility memory with temporal convolutional learning. Persistence estimates obtained by quasi-maximum likelihood and temporal modes extracted by the Matrix Pencil method define structured filters within the network. Together with learned filters, they represent the history of the target and support comparisons of directional evidence between variables. These comparisons are combined into a global order of precedence, which does not identify a causal graph. The method is evaluated on synthetic systems with linear and nonlinear volatility interactions and on the Finance and fMRI benchmarks, whose reference relations are known. Across the four synthetic volatility regimes studied, QS-TCN reaches a higher mean ordering quality than an external lead--lag ordering and its squared-input adaptation, although the gain over the adaptation is uncertain in two regimes. Applications to currency, gold and cryptocurrency markets are reported descriptively.

</details>


### [238] [AttnSR: Attention Step Reward for Long-Horizon Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07932)
*Mengsong Wu,Tianchen Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhenhua Liu,Zhe Cui,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用策略模型注意力分配步骤级奖励的方法，在不增加奖励模型或环境交互的情况下，提升了长程智能体强化学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体需要连续执行多个决策步骤，但仅依据最终结果给予奖励，难以直接指导各个中间步骤。为步骤学习额外训练奖励模型或增加采样交互，又会带来成本。论文因此探索能否利用策略自身已有的信息，判断哪些早期步骤与最终结果相关。

Method: 该方法从训练过程的回放数据中提取策略模型的注意力信息，并沿注意力关系反向追踪，估计早期步骤对最终回答的相关性，据此分配步骤级奖励。最终步骤的表现相对于动态基线进行衡量，用来决定对相关步骤进行强化还是惩罚。整个过程不需要单独训练奖励模型，也不需要额外采集交互轨迹。

Result: 在受控的七十亿参数指令模型实验中，该方法在所有评估检查点上的《异世界》任务表现均优于近端策略优化基线，最终留出测试成功率从百分之九十一点二提升至百分之九十七点一。在《科学世界》任务中也于各检查点取得提升，同时将环境交互次数减少百分之二十九点九。消融实验显示，收益取决于中间奖励的分配方式，而非简单地随时间累加步骤奖励。

Conclusion: 研究表明，策略注意力可作为长程智能体强化学习中步骤级奖励分配的实用先验，帮助弥补结果奖励缺乏过程监督的问题，并在所测任务中提升成功率或减少环境交互。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents make multi-step decisions, yet outcome-reward-only reinforcement learning provides little direct supervision for individual steps. We introduce AttnSR, a step-level reward augmentation method that derives reward allocations from policy attention extracted during training replay, without a separately trained reward model or additional rollouts. AttnSR traces backward through the policy model attention graph to estimate the relevance of earlier steps to the final response. The terminal step, measured relative to a running baseline, determines whether these rewards reinforce or penalize the selected steps. In a controlled Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct study, AttnSR consistently outperforms the PPO baseline across all evaluated checkpoints on ALFWorld, raising final held-out success from 91.2% to 97.1%. The ablation experiment shows that the benefit depends on how intermediate rewards are assigned, rather than simple accumulation of step rewards over time. AttnSR also improves ScienceWorld at every checkpoint while cutting environment interactions by 29.9%. These results support policy attention as a practical prior for step-level reward allocation in long-horizon agentic RL. Code is available at https://github.com/fairyshine/AttnSR .

</details>


### [239] [ConvPE: Convolutional Position Embedding Improves Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07749)
*Zehao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 卷积式位置嵌入通过轻量因果卷积为注意力加入依赖内容的局部位置信息，提升了模型在语言建模、长上下文扩展和视觉任务中的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在改善变换器的位置建模能力，同时避免引入过重的计算开销，并使方案能够适配不同任务、注意力掩码及缓存拼接等常见设置。

Method: 在隐藏状态的低维投影上施加带窗口的因果卷积，并将其形成的、依赖输入内容的偏置注入注意力分数。该方法可与旋转位置编码、注意力掩码和键值缓存拼接兼容，也支持通过高维卷积构造高维位置嵌入。

Result: 复现实验显示，该方法优于五种一维基线和八种二维基线。摘要还报告其在语言建模、长上下文扩展和视觉场景中兼具性能与效率优势，但未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 卷积式位置嵌入是一种轻量、可插拔且适用范围较广的位置建模方案；现有摘要所述比较结果支持其有效性，但具体提升幅度及实验条件仍需结合论文全文判断。

Abstract: ConvPE (Convolutional Position Embedding) improves both the performance and efficiency of Transformer models in language modeling, long-context extension, and vision. It injects a windowed, content-dependent bias into attention scores through a lightweight causal convolution over a low-dimensional projection of the hidden states. As a lightweight plug-in, ConvPE is compatible with RoPE, attention masks, and KV concatenation. It also supports high-dimensional position embeddings via high-dimensional convolutions. In a reproduction, ConvPE outperforms five 1D baselines and eight 2D baselines. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzhagi/convpe.

</details>


### [240] [Can an LLM Actually Reason and Program about Neural Network's Weights or is it just memorization on steroids? Exploring "Weight-Space Programming"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08070)
*Nazmus Sakib Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，模型读取神经网络权重的能力更像是局部数值计算与熟悉数据集先验的结合，而非可靠追踪权重中的信息流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有研究称大型语言模型能够直接读取和编写神经网络权重。本文检验模型所谓的“读取”能力，究竟源于对权重信息流的推理，还是局部计算能力与对公开数据集的记忆先验。

Method: 研究者测试克劳德·奥普斯5.5：向模型提供约十二万五千个参数的小型卷积网络的全部权重文本。这些网络在五种图像数据集上训练；测试不提供工具、前向传播结果、数据集名称或类别名称。研究者考察模型对训练步数、类别结构、数据集及特征语义的判断，并核验其部分推断。

Result: 模型能完成一些局部计算：它根据批归一化统计量推断出训练仅进行了约三步，也能从输出层权重符号中恢复十类图像数据集中“车辆／动物”的划分。但它通常只细读首尾层、抽查中间层，若干基于不完整追踪作出的判断未通过核验。语义判断明显依赖数据集先验：它把快餐图像网络也误认成熟悉的十类图像数据集，把日文字符数据集误称为手写数字数据集，还误判卫星图像网络及特征所对应的类别。打乱类别标签后，模型仍能识别原有输出划分，却把类别对全部解释错误，显示其依赖记忆中的标签顺序。

Conclusion: 在本研究的零样本测试中，模型读取权重更适合描述为局部算术与数据集先验的结合，而非可靠的权重信息流追踪。研究结果属于探索性证据，主要来自单次实验，且未进行直接前向传播测试；作者因此提出了用于审计未来权重空间能力主张的评估流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to read and write the raw weights of neural networks directly, a capability termed weight-space programming. We ask whether the reading side of this capability reflects reasoning about weight flows or a combination of local arithmetic at the network's edges and memorized priors over public datasets. We test Claude Opus 5.5 on small convolutional networks of about 125,000 parameters trained on five image datasets, providing every weight as plain text with no tools, no forward passes, and no dataset or class names. The model performs accurate local calculations: batch-normalization statistics lead it to infer about three optimizer steps on networks stopped after three updates, and output-layer sign patterns recover the vehicle/animal split of CIFAR-10. The sessions report reading the first and last layers closely and sampling the middle, and several claims built on partial tracing fail verification, although we did not run a direct forward-pass test. Semantic judgments lean on familiar datasets: the model names CIFAR-10 for CIFAR-10 networks but also for all three fast-food networks, relabels KMNIST as MNIST, reads a 99.57%-accurate SAT-6 network as CIFAR-10 animal photos, and calls "frog" the output favored by a feature that fires most on trees. Permuting CIFAR-10's labels preserves the output split at the new indices, but every class pair the model interprets is then wrong, indicating reliance on remembered label order. These are exploratory results from mostly single runs. We conclude that zero-shot weight reading by this model is better described as local arithmetic plus dataset priors than as weight-flow tracing, and we provide a protocol for auditing future weight-space claims.

</details>


### [241] [Foresee to Trade: Forecast-Augmented Policy Optimization for Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07750)
*Zeyu Liu,Xuanzhi Feng,Haoran Xu,Lai Sing Kwong,Yang Li,Hualei Zhang,Hao Chen,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyi Pang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种同时利用资产未来机会与账户交易结果进行监督的投资策略优化方法，在五类投资组合设置中取得了最高的平均累计收益和年化收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一市场机会未必对应所有账户采取相同交易行动。投资智能体不仅需要识别哪些资产可能表现较好，还需要结合账户初始状态与市场路径，判断应如何调整具体持仓。若只监督资产机会或只优化账户结果，都可能无法充分连接资产判断与账户决策。

Method: 提出预测增强相对策略优化方法，在共同学习的表示中分别监督资产机会和账户决策。训练时，金融状态预测头学习未来相对收益目标，提供资产层面的机会监督；同路径组相对优化则在相同市场路径和初始账户条件下比较不同交易轨迹，提供账户层面的相对结果监督。自适应门控根据预测方差和梯度对齐程度协调辅助监督的影响。金融状态预测头仅用于训练，推理时由编码器和策略网络执行交易。

Result: 在道琼斯工业平均指数三十只成分股、东京证券交易所核心三十只成分股及三种投资风格组合上，研究进行了十个随机种子的评估。摘要报告称，该方法在全部五种设置中的平均累计收益和平均年化收益均高于所比较的方法；摘要未提供具体收益数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将未来资产机会的监督与账户交易结果的监督结合起来，有助于强化投资智能体的账户级交易决策。该方法在训练期间引入额外资产预测监督，而推理阶段无需保留预测头。

Abstract: Online investment services must translate asset opportunities into investment outcomes for users' accounts, yet the same market opportunity can warrant different account actions. We study how supervision from future asset opportunities can strengthen account-level trading decisions and propose Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization (FRPO). FRPO separately supervises asset opportunities and account decisions through a jointly learned representation: a Financial State Head (FSH) learns future relative-return targets to provide asset-level opportunity supervision, while Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization compares trading trajectories under the same market path and initial account to provide account-level relative-outcome supervision. An Adaptive Gate coordinates auxiliary supervision using predictive variance and gradient alignment. FSH is used only during training; inference uses the encoder and policy for trading. Across DJIA30, TOPIX Core30, and three investment-style portfolios, ten-seed evaluations show that FRPO achieves the highest mean cumulative and annualized returns among the compared methods in all five settings. These results support strengthening investment agents' trading decisions through joint supervision from asset opportunities and trading outcomes.

</details>


### [242] [Value or Attention: Investigating Layer-wise Relevance Propagation for Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08088)
*Ken Schreiber,René P. Klausen,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Naujoks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将图注意力网络中的相关性分解为内容贡献与注意力路由贡献，指出两者应分别解释；不同分解权重可能使同一节点呈现负、正或近零相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图注意力网络逐渐应用于医学、材料科学等需要理解模型推理过程的领域，但现有解释方法难以揭示注意力层中模型如何选择信息。层次相关性传播虽沿着模型计算过程追踪贡献，却未充分区分节点提供的内容与节点对信息路由的影响。

Method: 提出一个带有自由权重的统一乘积规则，将注意力层传递到节点的相关性拆分为两部分：内容路径相关性衡量节点相对于零基线提供了多少内容；注意力路径相关性衡量节点如何相对于邻居改变注意力门控及信息传递。该规则可通过不同权重设置涵盖已有方法，并为两条路径分别给出解释规则。

Result: 在一个具有真实标签的分类任务中，研究发现，采用该规则的三种设置时，同一节点可分别获得负相关性、正相关性或近零相关性。分离后的相关性图还支持一种关于模型行为的假设：模型可能因不读取该节点而获益。

Conclusion: 解释图注意力网络时，应区分节点提供了什么内容，以及节点如何影响信息路由；将两种贡献合并为单一数值，可能掩盖其不同机制与符号含义。

Abstract: Graph Attention Networks (GATs) are increasingly used in domains where scientists need to read a model's reasoning, from medicine to materials science, yet the routing they learn remains opaque to most explainers.
