Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) follows the model's computation, but in an attention layer the relevance reaching a node is the sum of two mechanically different quantities: its content relevance through the value path and its routing relevance through the attention path. Existing rules either drop one or add them into a single number. We write a single product rule with free weights on the two factors that recovers current approaches as special cases. The value path measures what content a node supplied against a zero baseline; the attention path measures how the node shaped what was passed relative to its neighborhood, and its sign records whether the node's features moved the gating the way the prediction demanded. On a ground-truth classification task, we show that the same node receives negative, positive and near-zero relevance under three settings of the rule. The separated relevance maps yield a hypothesis about the model: it gains by not reading that node. We give reading rules for both paths and argue that they should be interpreted separately.

</details>


### [243] [Neural Collapse on Imbalanced Datasets and Open-Set Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07753)
*Qiufu Li,Huibing Xiao,Linlin Shen,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从理论上说明：采用类别均衡的损失优化时，即使数据类别不平衡，特征与分类器仍会形成标准神经坍塌结构；在开放集情形及重平衡对比损失下也得到相应结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经坍塌描述分类模型损失达到最小时，样本特征和分类器向量呈现的几何结构。然而，类别不平衡通常会扭曲这一结构，开放集中的未知类别也缺乏充分理论解释。论文旨在重新分析这些情形下神经坍塌何时仍能成立。

Method: 论文不采用以往研究中的全局平均损失，而是研究按类别分别平均的损失，并分析其达到最小值时的几何性质。研究涵盖交叉熵和二元交叉熵，并扩展到一种开放集设定：损失中包含没有训练样本、但仍有分类器向量的类别。此外，论文还分析按类别样本频率倒数重加权的软最大和逐元素逻辑函数对比损失。

Result: 理论证明表明，在平衡或不平衡数据集上，只要类别平均损失达到最小值，交叉熵和二元交叉熵均会产生标准神经坍塌。开放集设定中，有训练样本的已知类别仍呈现标准神经坍塌；无训练样本的未知类别，其分类器向量彼此重合，并与已知类别的分类器向量正交。对于按样本频率倒数重加权的两类对比损失，在平衡或不平衡数据集上的最小值处，不同类别的样本特征也形成标准神经坍塌。

Conclusion: 论文指出，类别不平衡本身并不必然排除标准神经坍塌；采用类别平均损失进行分析时，标准结构仍可在多种分类损失、开放集设定及重平衡对比损失下得到理论保证。

Abstract: Neural collapse (NC) describes the elegant geometric structure that sample features and classifier vectors exhibit when a classification model's loss reaches its minimum. Class imbalance, however, generally distorts standard NC. In this paper, we theoretically reformulate NC on imbalanced datasets and in open-set settings. Our analysis is based on an optimization problem over class-wise averaged losses, rather than the global averaged losses used in prior NC studies. We prove that when class-wise averaged losses attain their minima, standard NC is achieved on balanced or imbalanced datasets, for both cross-entropy (CE) and binary cross-entropy (BCE) losses. We further extend the analysis to an open-set-inspired scenario, where some classes contain no training samples yet are still assigned classifier vectors in the losses. We find that in this scenario, when class-wise averaged losses reach their minima, standard NC still emerges on the known classes with training samples, while the classifier vectors of unknown classes without training samples overlap with each other and become orthogonal to those of the known classes. Finally, we analyze Softmax- and Sigmoid-based contrastive losses re-balanced by inverse sample frequency, proving that at their minima on balanced or imbalanced datasets, sample features of different classes likewise form standard NC.

</details>


### [244] [Gradient Boosting for Efficient Rank Discovery in Tensor Network Kernel Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08093)
*Albert Saiapin,Kim Batselier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逐步集成固定秩张量学习器的方法，以提升模型容量并降低秩选择的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络核机能够以低秩表示高效建模复杂关系，但确定合适的张量秩并不容易。传统交叉验证秩搜索需要训练多个候选模型，因而耗费较多计算资源。

Method: 针对典范多元分解参数化，提出梯度提升框架：按序添加固定秩学习器，逐步提高预测模型容量，并依据验证集表现确定集成规模，从而避免显式搜索张量秩。

Result: 在真实回归基准任务上，该方法相较于基于交叉验证的传统秩选择方法，训练速度最高提升至六倍，峰值内存使用最多降低一半，同时预测性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过逐步集成固定秩学习器，梯度提升可替代显式秩搜索的一种计算密集型流程，在维持预测效果的同时提升张量网络学习的效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Tensor network kernel machines (TNKMs) combine nonlinear features with low-rank tensor representations to model complex relationships efficiently. However, selecting an appropriate tensor rank remains challenging, as conventional rank-search methods require training multiple candidate models, increasing computational costs. In this work, we focus on a canonical polyadic (CP) tensor network parameterization and propose GB-CPKM, a gradient boosting framework that builds a higher-capacity predictor by sequentially adding fixed-rank learners. This approach provides a new perspective on rank selection by incrementally increasing model capacity, with ensemble size selected based on validation performance rather than through explicit rank search. Experiments on real-world regression benchmarks show that GB-CPKM trains up to 6x faster and uses up to 2x less peak memory than a conventional rank-selection method based on cross-validation, while achieving comparable predictive performance. These results highlight the potential of gradient boosting to improve the computational efficiency and scalability of tensor-network learning.

</details>


### [245] [Optimal Learning in Games with Arbitrary Delays: Best-of-Both-Worlds for Self-Play and Adversarial Opponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07773)
*Emmanuel Esposito,Andrew Jacobsen,Hao Qiu,Alexander Ryabchenko,Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种适用于反馈延迟零和博弈的算法，在双方协同学习时实现快速收敛，同时对任意对手保持最优阶的个体后悔保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈延迟会使博弈中的学习与决策更困难。理想算法既要在平滑零和博弈的双方自我对弈时取得快速收敛，又要在面对任意对手时维持稳健的最坏情形保证；常见的黑箱式延迟消除方法无法同时达到这两项目标。

Method: 提出多锚点乐观在线镜像下降算法，专门处理反馈延迟，并允许延迟序列任意且事先未知。该算法兼顾双方协同学习时的快速后悔界，以及对任意对手时的个体后悔界；研究还将方法扩展到多玩家博弈和各玩家延迟序列不同的情形。

Result: 在平滑零和博弈中，若双方都采用该算法，则社会后悔和个体后悔均为最大未处理梯度数量加一的量级。面对任意对手时，个体后悔为轮数与总延迟之和的平方根量级。论文还证明，标准的黑箱式转换无法同时实现上述最优速率。

Conclusion: 该算法在延迟反馈下兼顾协同自我对弈的快速学习与对抗任意对手的最优保证，并适用于更广泛的多玩家及异质延迟场景。

Abstract: We study learning in zero-sum games with delayed feedback. We introduce Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent (MAO-OMD), a single algorithm which obtains optimal fast rates in self-play on smooth games, while simultaneously maintaining minimax optimal guarantees against arbitrary opponents. In particular, in smooth zero-sum games with arbitrary and unknown feedback delays, MAO-OMD obtains $O(σ_{\max}+1)$ social and individual regrets when used by both players, where $σ_{\max}$ denotes the maximum number of outstanding gradients, while also guaranteeing minimax optimal $O(\sqrt{T+d_{\mathrm{tot}}})$ individual regret against arbitrary opponents, where $d_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total delay. Moreover, we show that these optimal rates cannot be obtained simultaneously using the standard black-box reduction to the undelayed setting. We also extend our results to multi-player games, and to games with player-dependent delay sequences.

</details>


### [246] [Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154)
*Sina Raeisadigh,Henning Müller,Abderrahmane Hedjoudje*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究显示，急性缺血性卒中患者发病首日的液体衰减反转恢复成像和表观扩散系数成像特征，可能在临床变量基础上补充三个月预后信息；但样本量较小，结果仍需外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中后的功能结局预测对治疗决策和患者管理具有重要意义。研究旨在评估发病首日多模态磁共振成像是否能在临床资料之外增加对三个月功能结局的预测价值，并比较不同成像模态与临床变量融合的效果。

Method: 研究纳入66名急性缺血性卒中患者，使用表观扩散系数成像、弥散加权成像、液体衰减反转恢复成像及临床资料。研究从各成像模态提取三维预训练残差网络特征，经严格限定在各交叉验证训练折内的主成分分析降维，再与临床变量及表观扩散系数病灶指标组合。采用线性支持向量机，在分层分组的八折交叉验证中评估不同模态组合，并分析特征重要性及液体衰减反转恢复成像的激活区域。

Result: 仅使用临床资料的预测表现较强，受试者工作特征曲线下面积为0.899±0.151；仅使用磁共振成像的模型区分能力有限，约为0.67至0.73。融合模型中，液体衰减反转恢复成像与临床资料组合表现最高，曲线下面积为0.948±0.061；表观扩散系数成像与临床资料组合为0.941±0.094。将全部三种成像模态融合并未稳定优于单一成像模态与临床资料的组合。年龄、美国国立卫生研究院卒中量表分项、表观扩散系数病灶负担及液体衰减反转恢复成像特征是重要预测因素；激活图显示模型对疑似病灶区域有响应。

Conclusion: 发病首日的液体衰减反转恢复成像和表观扩散系数成像特征可能为临床变量提供互补的预后信息，其中液体衰减反转恢复成像与临床资料的组合在本研究中表现最佳。结果支持采用紧凑的影像特征开展可解释、节省数据的卒中结局预测，但研究样本有限且交叉验证结果存在波动，尚不能据此确认临床应用效益；仍需更大规模、独立队列验证。

Abstract: This study evaluates the added prognostic value of day-1 multimodal MRI for predicting three-month functional outcome after acute ischemic stroke (AIS). Sixty-six AIS patients with ADC, DWI, FLAIR, and clinical data were analyzed. Three-dimensional pretrained ResNet embeddings were extracted from each MRI modality, reduced using fold-isolated principal component analysis, and combined with structured clinical variables and ADC-derived lesion biomarkers. Linear support vector machines were evaluated using eight-fold stratified group cross-validation across MRI modality combinations. Clinical-only modeling achieved strong performance (AUC = $0.899 \pm 0.151$), while MRI-only models showed limited discrimination (AUC $\approx$ $0.67$-$0.73$). Among multimodal configurations, FLAIR-clinical fusion achieved the highest mean cross-validated performance (AUC = $0.948 \pm 0.061$), followed by ADC-clinical fusion (AUC = $0.941 \pm 0.094$). Full ADC-DWI-FLAIR fusion did not consistently outperform single-modality clinical fusion. Feature-importance analysis identified age, NIHSS subscores, ADC lesion burden, and learned FLAIR representations as key contributors, while qualitative FLAIR activation maps showed responses over lesion-like regions. These findings suggest that compact day-1 FLAIR and ADC representations provide complementary prognostic information beyond clinical variables and support interpretable, data-efficient outcome prediction in AIS.

</details>


### [247] [Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775)
*Moritz Grävinghoff,Konstantin Wehmeyer,Aron Asefaw,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将低秩适配器视为可学习的数据模态，发现非线性潜空间既保留权重空间已有能力，又能提升对扰动的稳健性，并支持混合秩适配器共享表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究发现，低秩适配器分布在低维流形上，线性主成分分析已能支持采样、编辑和反演等操作。本文进一步探究：学习得到的非线性潜空间，能否在这些已有能力之外带来额外价值，并缓解固定维度权重表示对适配器秩的限制。

Method: 研究者使用同一批身份适配器训练变换器自编码器，将适配器映射到潜空间；针对采样、属性编辑等操作，将潜空间中的结果与直接在原始适配器权重上执行相同操作进行比较。此外，扩展序列式自编码器，使其能够在共享潜空间中表示不同秩的适配器。

Result: 潜空间保留了权重空间已有的能力，能够生成多样身份并编辑个体属性。相比权重空间，它对潜变量位移更稳健：受到扰动时，生成结果会变形，但仍保持为有效人脸，而不是退化成纹理。扩展后的方法还能够在单一共享潜空间中容纳混合秩适配器。

Conclusion: 非线性潜空间不仅可以复现权重空间中的适配器操作，还能提升对扰动的鲁棒性，并为跨秩适配器的统一表示提供可行路径。学习型潜空间有望进一步支持架构各异的适配器群体，推动适配器权重成为一种通用数据模态。

Abstract: LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

</details>


### [248] [Scaling Neural Motor Decoding via Decoupled Behavioral Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08263)
*Divyansha Lachi,Vinam Arora,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Alexandre Andre,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可混合利用配对神经—行为数据与非配对行为数据的解码框架，证明易获取的行为数据能够提升神经解码表现及跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解码方法依赖大量配对神经与行为记录，但这类数据采集成本高、具有侵入性且难以扩展；行为数据则更丰富、易采集。研究旨在解除两类数据必须配对使用的限制，探索如何借助行为数据扩大解码模型的训练规模。

Method: 提出一个支持多会话训练的灵活框架，可混合使用任意比例的配对神经—行为记录和非配对行为数据。行为解码器直接从行为轨迹中学习运动动态；在有神经活动数据时，通过交叉注意力层将其纳入模型，使行为监督与神经监督能够独立扩展。

Result: 在多个非人灵长类的皮层内运动数据集与任务上，加入非配对行为数据可提升解码性能，尤其有助于迁移到新任务；再加入少量目标任务行为数据，迁移效果还能进一步改善。此外，仅用行为数据进行预训练，也能达到与使用规模大得多的神经数据预训练相近的性能。

Conclusion: 将行为表征学习与神经表征学习解耦，为神经解码提供了新的扩展途径。由于行为数据比配对神经记录更便宜且易于采集，扩大行为数据规模有望成为提升跨受试者、跨任务泛化能力的实用且经济的方法。

Abstract: Recent neural decoding approaches achieve strong performance by training on large collections of paired neural-behavioral recordings, but such datasets are expensive, invasive, and difficult to scale. In contrast, behavioral data is abundant and easy to collect, raising the question of whether neural decoding can benefit from decoupling behavioral and neural representation learning. We introduce BEEMO, a flexible multi-session framework that enables training with arbitrary mixtures of paired neural-behavioral recordings and unpaired behavioral data. A behavior decoder learns movement dynamics directly from behavioral trajectories, while neural activity is incorporated through cross-attention layers when available, allowing behavioral and neural supervision to scale independently. Across multiple intracortical motor datasets and tasks in nonhuman primates, we show that incorporating unpaired behavioral data improves decoding performance, particularly when transferring to new tasks, and that adding even a small amount of behavioral data from the target task further improves transfer. By decoupling neural and behavioral data, we unlock a new dimension for scaling: unpaired behavioral data, which is substantially cheaper and easier to collect than paired neural recordings, can be used directly to improve decoding performance. We further show that behavior-only pretraining, without any neural pretraining, achieves performance comparable to models pretrained on substantially larger neural datasets. Together, our results suggest that scaling behavioral data offers a practical and cost-effective path toward neural decoding models that generalize across subjects and tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/nerdslab/beemo.

</details>


### [249] [PBPO: Prefix-Block Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07783)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Si Zhang,Hanqing Zeng,Tianxin Wei,Chenglin Wei,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向扩散式大语言模型的块级策略优化方法：保留块级条件建模，同时按单个词元进行重要性加权与裁剪；实验显示其表现具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式大语言模型通常按块自回归生成，并在每个块内迭代去噪多个词元，因此块级策略优化看似适用。但完整块的精确似然需要对去噪中间状态和揭示顺序求和，难以计算。直接使用块乘积权重还会放大梯度贡献的方差，因此需要兼顾块级条件结构与稳定优化的方案。

Method: 论文提出前缀块策略优化。方法构造包含已生成前缀与当前块的状态，并通过一次模型评估，为块似然建立因子化的一步近似。该块近似比率等于各词元近似比率的乘积，但实际重要性加权和裁剪分别施加于每个词元，使单个位置的变化在裁剪前能够影响整个块，同时避免直接采用块乘积权重。此外，训练时重新采样提示和已揭示前缀的受损视图，以增加重复更新的多样性。

Result: 在两种基于拉达架构的模型初始化、六项基准测试和三种生成长度设置下，该方法相较现有扩散式大语言模型强化学习方法取得了具有竞争力的表现。理论分析还指出，在其假设条件下，块乘积加权会比词元局部加权带来更高的梯度贡献方差。

Conclusion: 该研究为扩散式大语言模型提供了一种兼具块级条件建模与词元级稳定优化的策略优化框架。实验结果支持其有效性，但摘要未提供具体分数，也未说明其在所有任务或设置下均优于已有方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) commonly decode responses by proceeding autoregressively across blocks while iteratively denoising multiple tokens within each block. This structure makes block-level policy optimization a natural adaptation of autoregressive policy optimization. However, the exact likelihood of a completed block is intractable because it marginalizes over the intermediate states and reveal orders of the denoising process. To realize the block-level formulation, we construct a prefix-block state and use a single model evaluation to define a factorized one-step surrogate for the block likelihood. The resulting block surrogate ratio is a product of its token surrogate ratios, allowing variation at individual positions to propagate across the entire block before clipping. We therefore introduce Prefix-Block Policy Optimization (PBPO), which preserves block-level conditioning but applies surrogate importance weighting and clipping separately to each token. PBPO further diversifies repeated updates by resampling corrupted views of the prompt and revealed prefix, a design motivated by masked-dLLM pre-training. Under our analysis assumptions, block-product weighting amplifies the variance of gradient contributions relative to token-local weighting. Across two LLaDA-family initializations, six benchmarks, and three generation lengths, PBPO achieves competitive performance against existing dLLM RL methods.

</details>


### [250] [Accelerating Safe Reinforcement Learning with Massive Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08273)
*Joonyoung Lim,Younghwan Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模并行、短时域训练与完整回合安全约束之间的不匹配，提出一种利用分阶段成本估计和约束收紧的安全强化学习方法，在维持相近性能的同时缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行环境可提高数据吞吐量并减少实际训练耗时，但每批数据只包含较短轨迹片段，而约束马尔可夫决策过程中的安全约束通常针对完整回合的累计成本。环境错开重置会使短轨迹覆盖不同回合阶段，若直接据此估计完整回合成本，可能产生偏差，进而影响安全约束的满足。

Method: 将错开重置的环境视为从不同阶段抽取短轨迹片段形成的阶段混合分布，分析阶段覆盖造成的估计偏差；据此构造阶段聚合成本估计器，用短时域批次重建回合级成本估计。进一步将有限样本不确定性与分布不匹配纳入约束收紧框架，并使该方法能够与在策略优化结合。

Result: 在基于大规模并行物理仿真的安全健身房实验中，所提方法的表现与非大规模并行实现相近，同时显著降低了实际训练时间。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 通过校正短轨迹阶段覆盖带来的成本估计偏差，并对估计不确定性和分布差异进行约束收紧，可以将安全强化学习扩展到大规模并行训练场景，在保持竞争性表现的同时提升训练效率。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of scaling safe reinforcement learning (safe RL) to massively parallel training regimes, where a large number of environments are exe- cuted with short rollout horizons. Although this setting improves data throughput and wall-clock efficiency, it creates a mismatch with constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs), whose safety constraints are defined over full-episode cost returns. We address this mismatch by analyzing staggered environment resets as a phase-mixture distribution over short trajectory segments. This analysis charac- terizes the bias induced by phase coverage and motivates a phase-aggregated cost estimator that reconstructs episode-level cost estimates from short rollout batches. We further incorporate finite-sample uncertainty and distribution mismatch into a constraint-tightening framework, yielding a scalable Safe RL method compatible with on-policy optimization. Using an MJX-based implementation of Safety Gym, our experiments show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to non-massively parallel implementations while substantially reducing wall-clock training time.

</details>


### [251] [EnergyBench: a live, gate-aligned benchmark of day-ahead forecasts for European power markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07784)
*Evgenii Genov,Ander Martinez Alonso,Christoph Bergmeir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 能源基准通过每日实时、统一信息集的预测竞赛评估能源模型，发现天气预报协变量大幅提升基础模型表现；集成模型排名靠前，但电网运营商在风电和光伏预测上仍更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源预测研究常依赖回测和固定公开测试集。回测可能使用预测时点尚不存在的数据，而反复使用的测试集也可能造成基准过拟合，甚至已被基础模型预训练吸收。因此，需要在真实市场截止时间下，以统一且可核验的信息条件持续评测模型。

Method: 该基准每日在欧洲中部时间或夏令时间十一点三十分启动预测，距日前市场关门三十分钟，预测六个竞价区域次日每十五分钟的电价、风电和光伏发电量及负荷。基准方自行运行所有模型，并冻结、公开截止时点的信息集，其中包含三个数值天气预报模型，使不同模型可在相同天气信息条件下比较。评测涵盖基线、线性模型、梯度提升模型、十八个时间序列基础模型、集成模型及电网运营商预测；误差相对季节朴素基线汇总，并以埃洛等级分制的布拉德利—特里评级排序，同时通过按天自助抽样估计不确定区间。

Result: 在最初三十二个实时评测日中，对十个同时进行有无天气协变量对照的基础模型而言，加入协变量使误差降低百分之三十七至五十一；风电和光伏预测的降幅分别为百分之六十四至七十三、百分之四十八至六十一。零样本基础模型的表现可与使用相同协变量训练的梯度提升模型相当或更好。模型参数量从三百万增至三亿三千一百万，对表现的影响相对较小；使用协变量模型的中位数集成排名最高。电网运营商的闸门前预测在风电和光伏上仍领先，最佳单一基础模型误差高出百分之十六至二十二；负荷预测差距为百分之四至六，处于统计区间之内。

Conclusion: 实时、统一信息集的评测有助于减少回测泄漏和固定测试集过拟合，使模型比较更贴近实际市场决策。天气协变量对基础模型尤其关键，集成也能进一步提升排名；不过，当前基础模型尚未在风电和光伏预测上超越电网运营商的实际预测。

Abstract: Energy forecasting models are usually compared on backtests and fixed public datasets. Backtests can use data that did not yet exist when the forecast would have been made, and a fixed test set, reused across studies and possibly contained in the pretraining data of foundation models, invites overfitting to the benchmark. EnergyBench evaluates every entry live instead. Each day at 11:30 CET/CEST, thirty minutes before the day-ahead (SDAC) gate closes, every model forecasts the next day's price, wind and solar generation and load on the 15-minute grid for six bidding zones (BE, NL, FR, DE-LU, DK1, DK2). Unlike existing live benchmarks, EnergyBench runs the models itself, on one information set that it freezes at the cut-off and publishes, including three numerical weather prediction models, so that foundation models can be compared with and without the same weather covariates under the actual market deadline. Errors are pooled per series against a seasonal-naive baseline, and models are ranked by a Bradley-Terry rating on the Elo scale with a day-bootstrap interval. The field spans baselines, linear and gradient-boosted models on the frozen weather, eighteen time-series foundation models from eleven families, ensembles, and the grid operators' own forecasts. Over the first thirty-two live days, the covariates reduced the error of each of the ten foundation models run with and without them by 37-51% on matched races (64-73% on wind, 48-61% on solar). Used zero-shot, these models match or outperform a LightGBM model trained on the same covariates. Model size, from 3M to 331M parameters, has a much smaller effect, and median ensembles of the covariate models rank highest. The grid operators' pre-gate forecasts remain ahead on wind and solar, where the best single foundation models have 16-22% more error; on load the gap is 4-6%, within the intervals.

</details>


### [252] [AutoCompress: Automated Hardware-Guided Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08290)
*Sheldon Liang,Ali Taghibakhshi,Saurav Muralidharan,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Ashwath Aithal,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 自动压缩框架依据具体硬件、工作负载和服务引擎约束搜索模型结构，在满足延迟、吞吐量与显存要求的同时，提升压缩模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝流程通常以参数量为目标，并依赖人工挑选模型规模；但参数量无法可靠预测延迟、吞吐量和显存占用，这些指标还受模型结构、工作负载、硬件及服务引擎影响。因此，需要将实际部署约束直接纳入模型压缩过程。

Method: 提出硬件引导的自动压缩框架，将延迟、吞吐量和显存约束融入可微分架构搜索，联合优化全局结构与各层结构，从而生成异构模型架构。框架使用服务引擎成本模型指导搜索，并通过端到端测量验证模型预测的延迟、吞吐量和显存占用。

Result: 在长上下文本地编程、数据中心批量服务和游戏内低延迟交互等场景中，相较米尼特龙剪枝框架，该方法在八项基准上的平均准确率分别提升：五〇七〇显卡上6.57个百分点、五〇八〇显卡上2.35个百分点、H100显卡上0.57个百分点。此外，框架将一千二百亿参数的内梅特龙三点五超级模型压缩至七百四十亿参数，使其能够在单张B200显卡上以脑浮点16格式处理128K上下文，同时保留平均基准分数的95%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，面向具体硬件和工作负载进行结构搜索，比单纯依据参数量剪枝更能兼顾模型质量与部署约束。该框架在多种服务场景中改善了准确率，并展示了在超长上下文部署中大幅压缩大型模型的潜力。

Abstract: LLM pruning must preserve quality while meeting hardware- and workload-specific constraints on latency, throughput, and memory. Existing pruning workflows often target parameter counts, requiring practitioners to manually select model sizes that meet latency, throughput, and GPU memory limits. However, parameter count alone does not predict these metrics, which depend on the model architecture, workload, hardware, and serving engine. We introduce AutoCompress, a hardware-guided compression framework that directly incorporates such constraints into differentiable architecture search. AutoCompress jointly optimizes global and per-layer structure to produce heterogeneous architectures, guided by serving-engine cost models validated against end-to-end latency, throughput, and memory measurements. We evaluate AutoCompress on long-context local coding, batched datacenter serving, and latency-sensitive in-game interaction. Compared to the state-of-the-art Minitron pruning framework, AutoCompress improves average accuracy across eight benchmarks by 6.57, 2.35, and 0.57 percentage points on RTX 5070, RTX 5080, and H100, respectively. AutoCompress also compresses Nemotron 3.5 Super from 120B to 74B parameters for single-B200 BF16 serving at 128K context, retaining 95% of its mean benchmark score.

</details>


### [253] [Same Answer, Different Future: Matched Predictions Do Not Imply Matched Belief Updating in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07913)
*Weihan Li,Junhao Wu,Xinlei Chen,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型当前给出相似预测，并不意味着其内部信念相同；信念的形成方式和提取方式会影响后续如何吸收新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可能通过观察结果、阅读摘要、接受指令或内部激活干预，获得相似的即时概率预测。但即时预测一致，并不能说明这些状态面对后续证据时会作出相同更新，因此需要区分预测一致与信念状态等价。

Method: 研究者在伯努利推断任务中，以解析贝叶斯模型为参照，考察三个开放权重语言模型。通过四种文本途径和两种激活层面的干预构造模型状态，分别测量其后续结果续写和明确概率估计，再从每种状态追加相同的新证据。研究以“有效证据权重”衡量初始信念的持续影响，即该信念相当于多少条新观察。

Result: 证据呈现形式决定其内容主要反映在哪类读数中：结果序列主要影响续写，摘要和明确给出的比例主要影响显式概率估计。不同途径形成的有效权重差异显著；模型赋予摘要的权重仅随摘要所述观察数量弱增长，而提问方式会使推断出的权重相差数个数量级。在一次性激活引导实验中，干预虽能复现自然记录带来的即时预测，但加入后续证据后，其影响大多消失。

Conclusion: 仅凭当前预测相符，不能判定模型具有等价的信念状态。信念如何形成，以及通过何种方式读取，都会共同塑造其后续证据更新动态；评估模型信念应考察其对新证据的响应，而不应只比较即时预测。

Abstract: Language models can produce similar probabilistic predictions after observing outcomes, reading summaries, receiving instructions, or undergoing activation interventions. Whether these routes induce equivalent belief states remains unclear: agreement in current predictions does not establish how a model will respond to subsequent evidence. We investigate this distinction in a Bernoulli inference task with an analytic Bayesian reference. Across three open-weight language models, we prepare states through four textual routes and two activation-level interventions, measure both next-outcome continuations and explicit probabilistic estimates, and append identical new evidence from each prepared state. We quantify the persistence of the initial belief through its effective evidence weight, the number of new observations it is worth. We find that the format of evidence decides which readouts express its content: outcome sequences primarily affect continuations, whereas summaries and stated rates primarily affect explicit estimates. Moreover, effective weights differ substantially across routes, with summaries receiving weights that scale weakly with their stated observation counts and elicitation questions changing inferred weights by orders of magnitude. In a one-shot steering setting, an intervention that reproduces a natural record's immediate prediction loses most of its effect under subsequent evidence. These results show that agreement in current readouts is insufficient to establish equivalence in belief updating: how a belief is acquired and how it is read jointly determine its subsequent dynamics.

</details>


### [254] [Hybrid Machine Learning for Imbalanced Landing-Phase Safety Event Prediction from Flight Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08386)
*Taha Asghar,Bilal Mufti,Syed Qasim Zaheer,Luqman Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种可解释的特征融合框架，用于从严重类别不平衡的进近数据中识别着陆阶段安全事件；在三个机场的数据上，重复评估的宏平均召回率均高于百分之九十。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着陆阶段的异常事件较少，飞行数据类别分布严重不平衡。模型既要捕捉多个飞行参数随时间变化的耦合关系，也要区分不同机场运行环境下的事件特征，因此需要兼具可靠性、可解释性与适应性的预测方法。

Method: 研究使用正常飞行窗口训练长短期记忆自编码器，以重构误差表征偏离常规进近行为的程度；再将这一连续误差特征与各飞行参数的统计历史特征融合，输入采用类别加权的极端梯度提升分类器，识别正常、高速、航迹偏高和襟翼放出过晚四类窗口。研究基于三个机场共五万三千七百五十个进近窗口，比较跨机场汇总训练与机场专属训练，并开展组件基准对比、重复训练和特征归因分析。

Result: 重复划分与训练的结果显示，各机场的宏平均召回率均超过百分之九十。组件对比表明，类别加权具有重要作用，连续特征融合优于仅使用重构误差或采用硬阈值门控的方法。机场层面的比较发现，数据量较大的两个机场更适合本地专属建模，而数据量最少的机场从汇总训练中受益。特征归因结果还指出，垂直航迹、飞机构型、速度及进近环境变量与预测密切相关。

Conclusion: 该框架能够结合时序异常表征与可解释的飞行历史特征，实现对少数类着陆安全事件的有效识别。研究同时说明，训练策略应依据机场数据规模调整：数据充足时可采用机场专属模型，数据有限时则可利用跨机场汇总训练。

Abstract: Landing-phase safety-event prediction requires the detection of rare abnormal approaches within strongly imbalanced multivariate flight data. Capturing coupled temporal behavior while distinguishing event-specific signatures across airport operating contexts remains a central modeling challenge. This study proposes an interpretable hybrid feature-fusion framework for multiclass landing-phase safety-event prediction. A long short-term memory (LSTM) autoencoder trained on nominal flight windows characterizes deviations from routine approach behavior. Its continuous reconstruction-error descriptor is fused with parameter-specific statistical flight-history features and supplied to a class-weighted XGBoost classifier to distinguish Normal, Speed High, Path High, and Flaps Late windows. The framework is evaluated using 53,750 NASA DASHlink approach windows from three arrival airports. Pooled and airport-specific learning strategies are compared alongside component-level benchmarks, repeated pipeline retraining, and feature-attribution analysis. The results demonstrate strong class-balanced detection, with mean macro recall above 0.90 across all airports under repeated partitioning and retraining. Component comparisons establish the importance of class weighting and show that continuous feature fusion outperforms reconstruction-error-only and hard-gate alternatives. Airport-level comparisons reveal benefits from local specialization at the two airports with larger datasets and from pooled training at the airport with the smallest dataset. Feature attributions associate predictions with operationally relevant vertical-path, configuration, speed, and approach-context variables. The framework provides an interpretable approach to rare-event detection and practical guidance for adapting flight-data monitoring models to airport-level data availability.

</details>


### [255] [Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929)
*Fuma Kimishima,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以 Flux 生成模型为压缩先验的零样本图像编解码方法，并发现基于常微分方程的重建在低码率下尤其有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，预训练扩散模型可用于零样本压缩，但编码路径与重建方式如何影响码率、失真和感知质量之间的权衡仍不清楚。本文旨在系统分析这些因素，并检验 Flux 是否能作为有效的压缩先验。

Method: 提出基于 Flux 的零样本编解码方法，并从三个方面进行分析：编码成本与比特分配、重建方法，以及低码率失真表现。重建方案比较了祖先采样、单步常微分方程重建和多步常微分方程重建；实验涵盖高斯信源与自然图像。

Result: 在信噪比相同的条件下，不同编码路径不会改变理想信息率，但会改变各时间步之间的比特分配。常微分方程重建的失真可低于祖先采样，且低码率时优势更明显。实验结果支持这一分析；该方法整体表现具竞争力，部分情况下优于近期的神经图像压缩方法。

Conclusion: 研究表明，编码路径主要影响比特在时间步上的分配，而重建策略会显著影响失真表现，尤其是在低码率区间。Flux 可作为一种有理论依据且实用的零样本压缩先验。

Abstract: We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. We analyze FluxC along three axes: coding cost and bit allocation, reconstruction method, and low rate distortion. At matched signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that the path choice does not affect the ideal information rate, but changes how bits are allocated across time steps. For reconstruction, we compare ancestral sampling, one-step ODE reconstruction, and many-step ODE reconstruction. Our analysis shows that ODE-based reconstruction can achieve lower distortion than ancestral sampling, especially in the low rate regime. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

</details>


### [256] [Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08465)
*Celeste De Schamphelaere,Juan J. Vazquez,Ari Spiesberger,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Gavin Leech,Euan Ong,Jiahai Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出最大激活示例元模型：根据语言模型残差流中的方向生成能强烈激活该方向的文本示例，并以可验证的方式辅助理解模型内部表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解残差流方向的一种方法，是寻找能强烈激活该方向的文本；但从语料库检索示例受限于语料中已有的内容，难以覆盖多样领域、语言及模型内部特征。论文希望借助元模型生成更丰富的示例，并避免依赖人工标签。

Method: 训练一种语言模型，将目标模型残差流中的方向映射为文本示例。训练目标是最大化给定方向与生成文本在目标模型中所产生激活之间的余弦相似度。该目标可通过强化学习优化，不需要人工标签；生成的示例还可在目标模型上进行前向计算验证。

Result: 实验显示，该方法生成的示例具有较强信息量：在反推未见过的激活方面优于语料检索和自然语言自编码器；在多数未见语言与领域中，优于使用一千万词元进行的语料检索；还能激活百分之八十九的未见稀疏自编码器特征。它对引导向量所代表概念的识别能力与自然语言自编码器相近，更常从低秩适配器后门中恢复有效载荷，并能将部分模型工作区中的内在独白转化为语言。

Conclusion: 最大激活示例元模型能够突破固定语料的覆盖限制，为理解模型内部方向和特征提供可扩展的文本示例。由于生成示例可通过目标模型的前向计算核验，这种方法也为训练元模型提供了一种有依据、可验证的替代方案。

Abstract: To interpret a direction in a language model's residual stream, we can look for text that strongly activates it. But when such text is found by scanning a corpus, it is limited to what that corpus contains. We develop Maximally-Activating Example Metamodels (MAEMs), language models which map residual stream directions from a subject model to text exemplars that strongly activate those directions. Our objective is to maximise the cosine similarity of a supplied residual stream direction with the activations of the text generated by the MAEM. This label-free RL objective helps to scale MAEMs on diverse sources of residual stream directions. Empirically, we find the resultant metamodel produces informative exemplars: a Qwen3.6-27B MAEM inverts held-out activations better than corpus search or a natural-language autoencoder (NLA), beats corpus search (at 10M tokens) on a majority of held-out languages/domains, and activates 89% of held-out SAE features. It also identifies concepts behind steering vectors about as well as NLAs, recovers the payloads of LoRA backdoors more often than corpus search, and can even verbalise some model workspace inner monologue. Unlike explanations produced by other metamodels (e.g. NLAs), MAEM exemplars can be verified with a forward pass on the subject model, making the MAEM objective a grounded alternative for scaling the training of metamodels.

</details>


### [257] [The Blindfolded Controller: Decision Model-Guided Training for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07934)
*Muhammad Ibrahim Khan,Aniket Dixit,Faizan Ahmed,James Brusey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出决策模型引导训练方法，用决策模型为强化学习策略提供有效初始化，使智能体从一开始就能较快获得奖励，并进一步通过奖励反馈改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励和探索困难会使强化学习从零开始学习高效策略的过程缓慢且样本效率低。决策模型能够依据环境与任务描述直接行动，却无法从奖励中学习并持续优化，因此需要结合两者的优势。

Method: 决策模型引导训练使用包含环境和任务信息的描述，让决策模型生成可供强化学习策略采用的初始行为。智能体因此不必从零开始探索，能够在早期回合到达目标并获得奖励；随后，强化学习利用奖励反馈继续改进策略，力求超越决策模型的表现。论文还通过消融实验总结了撰写环境与任务描述的指导原则。

Result: 在七项任务的实验中，该方法在全部七项任务上优于强化学习基线，在其中五项任务上优于决策模型。摘要未提供具体数值或显著性检验信息。

Conclusion: 以决策模型初始化强化学习策略，有望兼顾决策模型在稀疏奖励任务中的即刻行动能力与强化学习通过奖励持续改进的能力，从而提升学习效率和最终表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a framework for continuous improvement through interaction with an environment. However, RL agents struggle against tasks that offer exploration challenges and sparse rewards, because learning efficient policies from scratch is slow and sample-inefficient. In contrast, decision models (DMs), given a description of the environment and task, can perform well in sparse-reward tasks, but, as they do not learn from reward, they are not able to improve towards the optimal solution. In this paper, we present Decision Model-Guided Training (DMGT), a technique that uses a DM with a description of the environment and task to initialise an RL policy, combining the DM's ability to act without training with RL's ability to improve from reward. Instead of exploring from scratch, the agent reaches the goal and receives reward from its first episodes, and RL then improves its policy beyond what the DM achieves. We demonstrate through experiments on seven tasks that our approach outperforms RL in all seven and DM in five. In addition, we provide comprehensive guidelines, derived from ablation studies, for describing an environment and task to a DM.

</details>


### [258] [Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08469)
*Linqian Wu,Diaoxuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何从有限、局部且含噪的干预数据中识别和验证机制层面的量，重点分析整合信息理论中的整合信息量。研究发现，可观测性受实验设计限制，该量对机制变化也可能不连续；但仍可用有限数据认证其下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究、人工智能审计和机器意识评估，日益依赖描述系统内部因果计算机制的量，而非仅看输入输出。然而，实验只能通过有限、局部和含噪的干预观察因果转移规律，因此需要明确：何时目标量可识别、对机制变化是否连续，以及能否用有限数据加以认证。

Method: 作者以整合信息理论中的整合信息量为案例，在具有已知真实机制的四单元和五单元随机网络中，系统考察可识别性、连续性与有限样本认证。研究比较不同观测与干预方案，并检验机制边界附近的变化、拟合模型的误报风险，以及基于受控转移数据构造下界认证的方法。

Result: 仅凭输入输出行为可能无法区分内部机制：一组循环网络与前馈网络在响应延迟处具有相同输入输出规律，但整合信息量分别为零点二八比特和零比特；内部记录可在四至五十三次试验中将其区分。访问不完整也会造成无法靠增加样本消除的精度下限，示例中为零点三零至一点零二比特；在特定奇偶网络中，多个隐藏单元只能通过其可靠度乘积被观测，单次特定干预可打破这种对称性。该量存在明显不连续边缘：在随机网络中，约百分之九十九的已发现边缘处，机制发生无穷小变化即可使其改变百分之二十五至百分之百；约百分之一的几乎完美拟合干预数据的替代模型，仍会造成超过百分之二十的误报。另一方面，边缘两侧的数据都能认证该量的下界；作者还证明，在一个六十四维机制区域内该量始终大于一点零零四比特，并展示可用约一千万次受控转移进行认证，且错误认证概率不超过预设的α。

Conclusion: 整合信息量并非总能从行为数据或不完整观测中可靠确定，其不连续性也使点估计容易受机制微小变化影响。因此，实验设计必须针对目标机制量安排观测与干预。尽管完整识别可能受限，经过设计的有限实验仍可认证有意义的单侧下界；机制量的可测性可以被定量分析，而非仅凭经验判断。

Abstract: Interpretability, AI auditing and assessments of machine consciousness increasingly rest on mechanism-level quantities: functionals of a system's causal transition law that describe how it computes, not what it outputs. Experiments see that law only through finite, partial and noisy interventions. We ask when such a quantity is identifiable from an experiment's access, continuous in the mechanism and certifiable from finite data, and answer exactly for a deliberately demanding case - the integrated information $\varphi$ of integrated information theory - in stochastic networks of four and five units with known ground truth. Behavior can be silent: a recurrent and a feed-forward network with identical input-output laws at their response delay carry 0.28 and 0 bits, yet internal recordings separate them within 4-53 trials. Partial access can be just as blind: in our examples, units left out of a one-step recording impose precision floors of 0.30-1.02 bits that no sample size lowers, and in all-but-self parity networks, recordings of any length see several hidden units only through the product of their fidelities - a symmetry that one intervention broke in exact checks. The measure has sharp edges: because $\varphi$ selects a partition, and the cause and effect it specifies, by one criterion and reports another, infinitesimal changes of mechanism that cross an edge change it by 25-100% at 99% of the edges we found in random networks, and about 1% of surrogate models that fit interventional data almost perfectly misreport it by more than 20%. One-sided claims survive: the same data certify a lower bound on both sides of an edge, and we prove $\varphi>1.004$ bits throughout a 64-dimensional region of mechanisms and certify it from about $10^7$ controlled transitions with false-certificate probability at most $α$. Measurability is thus a design question with computable answers.

</details>


### [259] [Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950)
*Dang-Khoa Bui,Quang-Huy Tran,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui,Duc-Tuan Ngo,Thanh-Trong Tran,Tuan-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，胸片与电子健康记录预测基准存在患者级数据泄漏和标签错配；在按患者隔离、校正标签后，类别不平衡处理方法几乎没有带来增益，影像信息与诊断史则更有预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有多模态胸片预测研究常按影像记录划分数据，同一患者的多张胸片可能同时进入训练集和测试集，使评估结果受到患者信息泄漏影响。研究旨在审查这类基准是否可靠，并检验类别不平衡策略在更严格评估下是否有效。

Method: 研究审计了包含一万零四百七十九名患者、二万九千零八十三张胸片及八种诊断目标的基准。首先，以记录级划分评估标签查表基线和多种模型；随后追溯标签生成过程，依据住院记录重新对齐并细化标签。最后采用患者级隔离评估，比较不同类别不平衡策略、树集成模型、逻辑回归、电子健康记录变量、影像嵌入及诊断史，并分析影像编码器与诊断史结合后的效果。

Result: 记录级划分中，仅凭相同电子健康记录向量查找并复制开发集标签的基线，就几乎达到最佳模型表现；对未见患者时，各模型表现降至“全部预测为阳性”的基线水平。标签审计发现，每名患者的标签源自单次住院的诊断代码，经复制后用于其所有胸片，因此约三分之一胸片的标签与其实际对应住院记录的诊断代码不一致。患者级评估及标签校正后，所测类别不平衡策略相较不增强数据的方案，平均宏平均二次调和指标提升均不超过零点零一；树集成模型也未稳定优于调优后的逻辑回归。已发布的电子健康记录变量仍接近全阳性基线，而影像嵌入和成像时可获得的诊断史超过该基线。采用细化标签时，一个未见过这些胸片的外部数据集训练编码器也有预测能力；另一自监督编码器则在预训练中见过百分之九十六点六的胸片。将任一编码器加入诊断史，均未显著提升二次调和指标；在曲线下面积指标上，自监督编码器带来的提升显著，另一编码器则未显著。

Conclusion: 记录级划分和跨影像复制标签会造成误导性评估；可靠的多模态预测研究应按患者划分数据、将标签与对应住院记录对齐，并明确审查预训练数据暴露情况。该研究还提供了可复用的诊断工具、避免泄漏的评估流程和检查清单。

Abstract: Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

</details>


### [260] [XStack: A Benchmark for ML Repo Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08472)
*Linrong Cai,Taiming Lu,Haozhe Chen,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用于评估智能体将机器学习代码从一种框架与硬件生态迁移至另一种生态的基准；现有智能体的迁移成功率仍不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代码通常受限于最初采用的框架和硬件生态，研究者若想利用不同硬件，往往需要迁移并测试整个代码仓库。此类迁移既有现实价值，也适合作为评测任务，因为它跨越框架与硬件，涉及完整仓库，且需要长期、复杂的操作。

Method: 研究提出迁移评测基准：向智能体提供一个基于一种框架的代码仓库及一台干净的目标硬件机器，要求其生成使用另一种框架的仓库。评测标准依据原仓库已发表的结果制定，并经过研究者验证与校准。

Result: 迁移仍不可靠。摘要报告，表现最佳的智能体通过率为百分之七十五，另一种模型为百分之四十五，其余智能体均不超过百分之四十。通过条件以评测标准为准，而非仅判断代码是否完成转换。

Conclusion: 该基准使跨框架、跨硬件的整仓迁移能力能够被系统衡量，为提升机器学习代码迁移的可靠性提供评测基础；现阶段的结果也表明，智能体距离稳定完成此类任务仍有差距。

Abstract: ML code should run where researchers choose, not where it was first written. Today, machine learning code mainly lives in two ecosystems, PyTorch on NVIDIA GPUs and JAX on Google TPUs. Migrating repositories between them means building and testing across frameworks and hardware. This migration is (1) a practical need, since it unlocks different hardware for researchers, and (2) a natural benchmark: it involves different frameworks and hardware, covers whole repositories, and is long-horizon and challenging. We introduce XStack, a benchmark that evaluates agents on migrating ML repositories between these two ecosystems. Each agent receives a PyTorch repository and a clean TPU machine and must deliver a JAX repository. Its output passes if it meets our grading criteria, based on the source repository's published result, authenticated and calibrated by us. Migration by agents remains unreliable: Claude Opus 5 passes 75%, GPT-5.6-sol 45%, and every other agent at most 40%. XStack is a step toward freeing ML code from its ecosystem. We release XStack to make progress toward reliable migration across frameworks and hardware measurable.

</details>


### [261] [Invariance before structure: two modes of emergent compression in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07952)
*Hardik Rajpal,Dan Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种分析学习表征涌现的框架，发现表征对网络底层参数变化的不敏感性，与表征对任务时间结构的预测能力，在训练中分阶段形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人工神经网络即使具有不同的实现基质，并受到参数漂移、异质性和初始化差异影响，仍常呈现相似的学习表征。论文旨在解释这种表征不变性如何形成，并区分底层网络变化所致的不变性与任务结构所致的表征组织。

Method: 提出“涌现式自适应表征学习”框架，区分两类涌现：状态空间压缩，指整体表征对自身未来状态的预测能力超过单个网络单元；参数空间压缩，指微观参数不同的网络趋向相同表征。作者将两者形式化为费舍尔敏感度，并为每类现象构造基于集成的信息论度量；随后在带有训练瓶颈的循环储备池网络中进行分析。

Result: 训练最初数个阶段内，参数空间压缩便有所增强，且部分独立于具体任务。约三十个训练周期之后，状态空间压缩才开始增强，并且只在网络开始学习任务的时间结构时出现。

Conclusion: 表征对实现基质或微观参数差异的适应，以及任务特定结构的形成，并非同一过程，而是在学习的不同阶段涌现。该框架为研究跨网络表征不变性及其与任务学习的关系提供了可量化的分析工具。

Abstract: Learned representations show striking invariance across biological and artificial neural networks, with different substrates and variations driven by drift, heterogeneity and initialisation. To understand this invariance, we propose Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning (EARL), a framework that distinguishes two modes of emergence in learned representations: state-space compression, where a learned representation gains predictive power over its own future beyond what the network's units can predict individually, and parameter-space compression, where networks with different micro-level parameters converge to the same representation. We formalise both as Fisher sensitivities and introduce ensemble-level information-theoretic measures for each. In a recurrent reservoir with a trained bottleneck, parameter-space compression rises within the first few epochs of training and partly independently of the task, whereas state-space compression rises about 30 epochs later and only when the network begins to learn the temporal structure of the task. These results suggest that invariance to the substrate and task-specific emergent structure are acquired in distinct phases of learning.

</details>


### [262] [Tight Asymptotic Bounds on the Maximum Number of Linear Regions of Piecewise-Linear Kolmogorov--Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07973)
*Ye Su,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为连续分段线性科尔莫戈洛夫—阿诺德网络的线性区域数量建立匹配的上下界，并证明在相同深度和参数预算下，其区域数量相较整流线性单元网络具有多项式级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究只给出了连续分段线性科尔莫戈洛夫—阿诺德网络线性区域数量的较松上界，缺少下界，因此无法确定其区域复杂度随每条边的仿射分段数增长的真实阶数。本文旨在刻画这一复杂度，并在统一的深度与参数预算下比较该网络与整流线性单元网络的表达能力。

Method: 针对具有固定层数和宽度向量的网络，设每条边至多包含若干仿射分段。作者推导线性区域数量的匹配上、下界，其增长指数由各层之前出现过的最小宽度决定。随后，将可训练结点位置计入参数预算，在固定非线性深度和稠密参数化条件下，对网络宽度及每条边的分段数进行优化，并比较两类网络可达到的最大区域数量。

Result: 若第ℓ层之前的最小宽度记为ρₗ₋₁，则区域数量关于每条边分段数k的紧确增长阶为Θₙ(k^{Σₗ₌₁ᵀρₗ₋₁})。当所有隐藏层宽度均不小于输入维度d时，该阶数简化为Θₙ(k^{dT})。相较已有上界k^{Σₗ₌₁ᵀnₗ₋₁nₗ}，本文上界在指数上更紧；只要存在宽度大于一的隐藏层，渐近改进即为严格的。在参数预算Λ趋于无穷时，两类网络最大区域数量之比为Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d⌊T/2⌋})；因此当T≥2时，科尔莫戈洛夫—阿诺德网络具有多项式级优势。

Conclusion: 本文首次以匹配上下界刻画了这类网络线性区域数量的紧确增长规律，并证明其在固定深度、相同参数预算下可实现显著高于整流线性单元网络的几何复杂度。这些结果为理解其表达能力及指导网络结构设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Schoots et al.~\citep{schoots2025relating} (AISTATS 2025) established a generally loose upper bound on the number of linear regions of continuous piecewise-linear (CPWL) Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs), without providing a lower bound. We consider a $T$-layer network with width vector $\mathbf n=(n_0,\ldots,n_T)$, input dimension $n_0=d$, output width $n_T=1$, and at most $k\geq2$ affine segments per edge. Our matching upper and lower bounds determine the tight growth order $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}})$ in $k$, where $ρ_{\ell-1}:=\min_{0\leq s\leq\ell-1}n_s$. When every hidden width is at least $d$, the tight order simplifies to $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{dT})$. The upper bound of \citep{schoots2025relating} is $k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell}$. Our upper bound improves it by a factor of $Θ_{\mathbf n}\!\left(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell-\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}} \right)$, with strict asymptotic improvement whenever a hidden layer has width greater than one. We further compare CPWL KANs with rectified linear unit (ReLU) networks using dense parameterizations with at most $Λ$ parameters per model and the same fixed nonlinear depth $T$. Counting trainable knot locations and optimizing the widths and $k$, we prove that the maximum region count of CPWL KANs divided by that of ReLU networks is $Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d\lfloor T/2\rfloor})$ as $Λ\to\infty$, yielding a polynomial advantage for KANs when $T\geq2$. These tight bounds characterize the attainable geometric complexity of CPWL KANs, providing a foundation for understanding their expressive power and guiding architectural design.

</details>


### [263] [QRAE: Query-Relevant Attention Entropy for Context Compression in DeepSearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07976)
*Yingjie Tan,Haiquan Qiu,Quanming Yao,Manyi Zhong,Xianzhi Yu,Yaqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于查询相关注意力熵的在线上下文压缩方法，在减少检索内容占用的同时保留有用证据，使任务成功率平均提升二点三五个百分点，并将端到端延迟和报告的令牌用量分别平均降低百分之三十和百分之四十五。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索语言模型会反复检索资料，并将检索结果带入后续推理，导致上下文不断膨胀、令牌消耗和延迟上升；然而，许多检索内容并不会帮助后续推理。论文旨在筛除当前不相关的信息，同时避免丢弃可能在后续步骤中有用的证据。

Method: 提出一种在线上下文压缩方法，利用稀疏证据评估注意力头判断哪些完整检索片段应保留。方法对有界候选批次执行成对预填充：通过无查询对照抑制共享注意力响应，再以归一化熵衡量每个片段中正向响应的分布广度。高分片段进入持久证据白名单并纳入推理上下文；不确定片段暂不进入推理上下文，而是保存在候选缓存中，以便之后重新评估。该方法无需额外的生成式压缩过程，也不修改注意力算子。

Result: 在多个深度搜索基准和推理模型上的实验表明，该方法使任务成功率平均提高二点三五个百分点，端到端延迟平均降低百分之三十，报告的令牌用量平均降低百分之四十五。效果还推广至参数规模接近一千亿的模型。

Conclusion: 根据摘要，基于注意力响应筛选并延迟处理不确定证据，能够在保留后续可用信息的同时压缩推理上下文，从而兼顾任务表现、延迟和令牌效率；该方法也展现出跨模型规模的适用性。

Abstract: DeepSearch LLM agents repeatedly search and carry retrieved sources into subsequent reasoning steps. This expanding context increases token consumption and latency, although many results do not contribute to later reasoning. We propose Query-Relevant Attention Entropy (QRAE), an online context compression method that uses sparse evidence-assessment heads to decide which intact retrieved chunks should persist across reasoning steps. It scores bounded candidate batches through paired prefills: a query-free control suppresses shared attention responses, while normalized entropy measures the breadth of the positive response within each chunk. High-scoring chunks enter a persistent evidence whitelist and are included in the reasoning context. Uncertain chunks remain outside the reasoning context but are retained in a candidate cache, where they can be retrieved for later reevaluation. QRAE requires neither an additional generative compression pass nor modification of the attention operator. Across multiple DeepSearch benchmarks and reasoning models, QRAE improves task success by an average of 2.35 percentage points and reduces end-to-end latency and reported token usage by an average of 30% and 45%, respectively. These benefits generalize to a model with nearly 100B parameters.

</details>


### [264] [Towards Multiscale Molecular Representation Learning through Geometry-Based Fragmentation for Polymer Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07998)
*Makoto Takamoto,Chaitanya Sampara,Viktor Zaverkin,Daniel Oñoro-Rubio,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种不依赖预设化学规则的几何分子片段划分方法，并将多尺度片段注意力与三维构象信息结合，用于提升聚合物性质预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子中的不同子结构对性质的贡献并不均等，片段表示有助于引入化学先验。但依赖固定化学规则的划分方式，可能不适用于陌生结构，也常忽视不同尺度的结构信息。

Method: 将分子片段划分建模为受半径约束、受聚类中心思想启发的优化问题，使片段数量由指定半径决定。方法主要依据几何信息划分分子或材料结构，并可通过调节半径控制片段尺度。研究进一步构建几何片段注意力机制，整合多尺度结构信息，并将其用于结合聚合物简化分子线性表示与三维构象信息的多模态模型。

Result: 在聚合物基准数据集上的实验表明，相比仅使用聚合物简化分子线性表示的模型，加入三维构象信息后，若干性质的预测准确度得到提高。可解释人工智能分析还显示，模型学习到的表示具有一定的物理相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种灵活、以几何信息为基础的多尺度分子片段表示框架，能够减少对预设化学规则的依赖，并通过融合三维构象信息改善部分聚合物性质预测。

Abstract: It is well established that molecular substructures can contribute unequally to chemical and physical properties, with specific motifs or side chains often playing dominant roles. Fragment-based representations have therefore emerged as an effective strategy for incorporating chemically meaningful inductive biases, as exemplified by molecular fingerprints and fragment-based attention mechanisms. However, many existing fragmentation-based machine learning methods rely on predefined chemical rules, which may yield suboptimal fragmentation for previously unseen or chemically unfamiliar structures and often overlook the importance of multiscale molecular representations. To address these limitations, we formulate molecular fragmentation as a radius-constrained optimization problem inspired by the k-center formulation, where the number of clusters is not fixed a priori but emerges from the specified radius, and propose a geometry-based fragmentation method that partitions arbitrary molecular or material structures using largely geometric information, without relying on predefined chemical fragmentation rules. The method provides flexible control over fragment size, enabling the construction of multiscale fragment representations for scale-adaptive modeling. Building on this framework, we develop a geometric fragment-based attention mechanism that integrates multiscale structural information and incorporate it into a multimodal machine learning model combining p-SMILES representations with three-dimensional (3D) conformational information. Experiments on a benchmark polymer dataset show that incorporating 3D conformational information improves prediction accuracy for several properties over models based exclusively on p-SMILES representations. Explainable artificial intelligence analyses further provide insights into the physical relevance of the learned representations.

</details>


### [265] [GTAFL: Addressing Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning in Medical Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08021)
*Guangyu Chen,Jingyun Zeng,Hui Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对医疗联邦学习中训练数据长尾且测试分布未知的问题，提出综合框架，通过训练阶段的分类器与特征校正，以及推理阶段的专家集成，提升模型在未知测试分布下的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据在不同机构及总体层面常呈现长尾分布，导致模型偏向多数类别、特征空间发生扭曲。现有长尾联邦学习方法通常假设测试集分布均匀，但实际医疗系统中的测试分布往往未知且难以预测，因此模型可能无法可靠应对真实应用场景。

Method: 论文提出面向测试分布未知的长尾联邦学习框架。训练阶段采用自适应重采样缓解类别不平衡，通过重新训练专家分类器校正分类偏差，并利用自监督学习改善受长尾分布影响的特征空间。推理阶段则集成多个重新训练的专家分类器，以应对未知的测试数据分布。

Result: 在通用图像数据集和两个医疗数据集上的实验表明，该框架整体优于其他先进方法。摘要未提供具体指标或逐项对比结果，因此无法进一步判断提升幅度及不同数据集上的表现差异。

Conclusion: 研究将测试分布未知纳入长尾联邦学习问题设定，并通过训练阶段的偏差校正与推理阶段的专家集成提升模型的分布适应能力。实验结果支持该方法的有效性，但其在更多真实医疗场景中的泛化表现仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: In healthcare, protecting patient privacy is crucial due to the sensitivity of medical data and its extensive accessibility. Federated Learning (FL) offers a decentralized and privacy-preserving training paradigm, making it an ideal solution for healthcare applications. A critical challenge in healthcare is that real-world medical data often exhibits long-tailed distributions in local and global views. Existing methods addressing long-tailed FL problem typically assume that the model will be evaluated on uniform test data distribution. However, practical test data in healthcare systems is often agnostic and unpredictable, leading to potential model failures in real-world scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a novel task termed Test-Agnostic Long-Tailed Federated Learning and propose GTAFL, a comprehensive framework to address this challenge. During the training stage, GTAFL employs adaptive re-sampling, expert classifier retraining, and self-supervised learning to correct biased classifiers and distorted feature spaces caused by long-tailed training distributions. During the inference stage, an ensemble mechanism combines retrained expert classifiers to handle test data with unknown distributions. Extensive experiments on CIFAR10 and two medical datasets manifest that our framework outperforms other state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/cgy-lavine/GTAFL

</details>


### [266] [Reproducing Kernel (Quasi-)Banach Spaces with the $\ell^p$ Coefficient Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08032)
*Tianfang Wang,Guohui Song,Haizhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将再生核空间理论扩展至一般的 ℓᵖ 正则化，重点研究非凸的 0＜p＜1 情形，并比较不同 p 值下的存在性、稀疏性与表示定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作为 ℓ¹ 范数下的再生核巴拿赫空间奠定了基础。为获得更强的稀疏性、使解更接近 ℓ⁰ 伪范数所偏好的稀疏结构，本文进一步考察 ℓᵖ 准范数，尤其是非凸的 0＜p＜1 区间，同时研究 p＞1 时相应理论的差异。

Method: 对于 0＜p＜1，构造采用 ℓᵖ 准范数的再生核拟巴拿赫空间，并在适当假设下分析插值与正则化学习问题；利用广义勒贝格常数刻画线性表示定理和松弛表示定理，并根据核函数衰减、样本间距及稳定性推导界。对于 p＞1，基于固定的可数字典构造再生核巴拿赫空间，证明极小解存在，并通过样本外系数算子确定最优松弛表示因子。

Result: 理论结果给出了 0＜p＜1 情形下插值和正则化学习解的存在性及稀疏性结论，并建立了表示定理及其相关界；对于 p＞1，证明了极小解存在并确定最优松弛表示因子。数值实验比较了 p＜1、p＝1 和 p＞1 三种情形下解的精度与稀疏程度。

Conclusion: 本文拓展了再生核空间在 ℓᵖ 正则化下的理论，揭示不同 p 区间在空间构造、解的存在性、稀疏性和表示性质方面存在实质差异。非凸的 0＜p＜1 正则化为追求更强稀疏性提供了理论框架，而其适用性仍依赖相应的假设条件。

Abstract: This paper advances the theory of reproducing kernel Banach spaces (RKBSs) by investigating the general $\ell^p$ regularization framework, with a particular focus on the non-convex regime $0<p<1$.
  While our preceding work established the foundation for RKBSs with the $\ell^1$ norm, the pursuit of stronger sparsity---closer to the $\ell^0$ pseudo-norm---naturally motivates this exploration into $\ell^p$ quasi-norms.
  For $0<p<1$, we construct reproducing kernel quasi-Banach spaces (RKQBSs) with $\ell^p$ quasi-norms and establish existence and sparsity results for interpolation and regularized learning under appropriate assumptions.
  We characterize the linear and relaxed representer theorems in terms of a generalized Lebesgue constant and derive bounds under kernel decay, sample spacing, and stability assumptions.
  For $p>1$, we construct RKBSs using a fixed countable dictionary, establish minimizer existence, and identify the optimal relaxed representer factor through an off-sample coefficient operator.
  These results reveal fundamental differences between the regimes $0<p<1$, $p=1$, and $p>1$.
  Numerical experiments compare the accuracy and sparsity of solutions for these three cases.

</details>


### [267] [Should We Pay This Much for Robustness? Efficient Proxy Certificates with Marginal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08035)
*Soroush H. Zargarbashi,Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法用低成本代理函数近似昂贵的鲁棒性证书，并通过统计校准控制其错误放行率，从而降低认证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器容易受到微小输入扰动影响。随机平滑等模型无关的鲁棒性认证方法虽然有较强保证，但通常需要对每个输入执行大量模型前向计算，部署成本高。论文旨在降低认证成本，同时保留有意义的鲁棒性保证。

Method: 使用可交换的无标签校准集，通过稳健共形风险控制调整低成本代理函数，使其错误放行率在未来输入的边际分布上受到约束：即统计控制代理函数放行、但扰动实际会导致预测翻转的概率。代理证书既可单独使用，也可作为回退机制：代理拒绝时才调用昂贵的原始证书。论文还提出单样本和多样本校准方案。

Result: 实验显示，单独使用代理证书时，测试阶段的认证成本可降至每个输入一次模型前向计算，并在较小扰动半径下保留随机平滑的大部分认证性能。作为回退机制时，该方法能够恢复随机平滑的认证比例，同时获得较为适中的加速效果。该方法也适用于视觉变换器和语言模型等较大架构。

Conclusion: 通过对代理证书进行统计校准，可以在明确控制边际错误放行风险的前提下显著降低鲁棒性认证成本。其保证是针对未来输入总体的边际保证，而非对每个单独输入的逐点保证；代理证书可独立使用，也可与昂贵证书结合以平衡效率和认证性能。

Abstract: Modern classifiers remain sensitive to small input perturbations, while strong model-agnostic robustness certificates such as randomized smoothing are often too expensive for deployment, requiring an extensive number (e.g. 2000) of model forward passes per input. We propose a framework that delegates certification to a cheap proxy function while statistically controlling its deviation from the original sound certificate. Given an exchangeable unlabeled calibration set, we tune the proxy via robust conformal risk control to guarantee a bounded marginal (rather than pointwise) false-approval rate: the probability, over future inputs, that the proxy accepts an input whose prediction is flipped due to perturbation. The resulting proxy certificate can be used either as a standalone marginal certificate or as a fallback mechanism that invokes the expensive certificate only when the proxy rejects. We provide both single-sample and sample-heavy calibration recipes. Empirically, as a standalone certificate our method reduces the test-time certification cost to a single model forward while retaining much of the certified performance of randomized smoothing at small radii, and as a fallback it recovers the certified ratio of randomized smoothing at a more modest speedup. This enables cheap yet effective robustness guarantees for larger architectures such as vision transformers and language models.

</details>


### [268] [Performance metrics in machine learning: a scenario-based guide from the confusion matrix to calibration, regression and uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08048)
*Mehmet Baygin,Sengul Dogan,Turker Tuncer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可控情景说明：同一组预测可能因类别比例、判定阈值、汇总方式及软件约定不同而得到不同评价结论，并提供指标选择表与报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类与预测性能指标容易被误读：类别比例变化会影响部分指标，阈值和汇总规则也会改变评价结果，软件实现约定还可能造成差异。本文旨在以可复核的示例解释这些机制，帮助研究者选择和报告合适的指标。

Method: 作者将二十二种混淆矩阵指标整理为按类别比例编码的关系图，并设计十五个情景，涵盖二分类、多分类、分数判别、概率校准、回归、不确定性及一致性检查。每个情景使用已发布文件中的固定输入，仅改变一个因素；共进行二百零五项验证检查，其中二百零二项为独立检查、三项为内部检查。另提供基于评价目标的指标选择表和报告清单。

Result: 示例显示，在正类比例为百分之一时，始终判为负类的规则准确率可达百分之九十九，但平衡准确率仅为百分之五十；在灵敏度和特异度固定时，正类比例从百分之五十降至千分之一，精确率从百分之八十八点九降至百分之零点八。三种概率预测的受试者工作特征曲线下面积相同，均为零点八二五，但校准斜率分别为二点零八、一点零四和零点五二。参与者人数仅为十人时，参与者多数决准确率为百分之七十，其百分之九十五区间较宽，为百分之三十四点八至百分之九十三点三。此外，若使用不恰当的合并测试集模型，按折叠平均的分数可能被错误判定为不一致。所有验证检查均通过。

Conclusion: 性能指标并非脱离数据条件而独立成立；类别比例、阈值、聚合方式和软件约定都可能影响结论。评价时应依据研究目标选择指标，结合校准、判别能力及不确定性等方面报告，并明确所用方法与约定。本文的情景用于解释作用机制，并不估计这些问题在已发表研究中的发生频率；文献审查和真实数据示范仅被列为未来方案，尚未实施。

Abstract: Performance metrics can yield different conclusions from the same predictions. This methodological tutorial explains the effects of class prevalence, decision thresholds, aggregation rules and software conventions through controlled scenarios. Twenty-two confusion-matrix metrics are organized in a prevalence-coded map. Fifteen scenarios cover binary and multi-class classification, score discrimination, probability calibration, regression, uncertainty and consistency checks. Inputs are fixed in released files, and one factor is varied in each scenario. All 205 verification checks passed; 202 were independent and 3 were internal. An always-negative rule achieved an accuracy of 0.990 at a prevalence of 0.010, with balanced accuracy of 0.500. At fixed sensitivity and specificity, precision fell from 0.889 to 0.008 as prevalence fell from 0.5 to 0.001. Three probability variants shared a ROC-AUC of 0.825 but had calibration slopes of 2.08, 1.04 and 0.52. With 10 participants, participant-majority accuracy of 0.700 had a 95% interval of 0.348-0.933. Fold-averaged scores were falsely flagged as inconsistent under an inappropriate pooled-test-set model. A goal-based metric table and a reporting checklist are provided. The scenarios illustrate mechanisms and do not estimate their frequency in published studies. A literature audit and a real-data demonstration are specified as future protocols and were not executed.

</details>


### [269] [Optimal Competitive Ratios for Chasing Convex Functions with Quadratic Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08078)
*Linzhe He,Sifan Yang,Wei Jiang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对凸函数追逐问题，在欧氏移动成本下将竞争比上界改进至量级为条件数三分之一次方，并在平方欧氏移动成本下确定了满足二次增长条件时的最优无维竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸函数追逐要求学习者逐轮应对对手给出的凸函数，同时承担函数损失和改变决策的移动成本。既有结果存在不足：欧氏移动成本下，上下界之间有多项式差距；平方欧氏移动成本下，虽已有无维保证，但最优竞争比仍不明确。

Method: 对于欧氏移动成本，提出裁剪近端下降方法，将平衡近端步骤与朝当前极小点方向的裁剪修正结合起来，并分析其竞争性能。对于平方欧氏移动成本，对正则化在线平衡下降方法进行紧致竞争分析，同时构造针对随机算法的匹配下界。

Result: 在满足二次增长条件的光滑凸函数上，裁剪近端下降取得量级为条件数三分之一次方的竞争比，优于此前条件数平方根量级的上界，并匹配已有的条件数三分之一次方下界。对于平方欧氏移动成本，论文确定了满足二次增长条件的凸函数追逐问题的精确最优无维竞争比；摘要未给出该比值的具体数值。

Conclusion: 论文分别解决了两类移动成本下的关键竞争分析问题：欧氏移动成本下缩小并闭合了渐近上下界差距；平方欧氏移动成本下则通过算法分析与随机算法下界相结合，确立了最优无维保证。

Abstract: Chasing convex function has attracted much attention in recent years, which can be formulated as a game between a learner and an adversary. In each round, the adversary reveals a convex function, and the learner selects a decision and incurs a hitting cost and a movement cost for changing its decision. The performance of the learner is measured by the \emph{competitive ratio}, which compares its cumulative cost with that of an optimal offline decision sequence. For Euclidean movement, existing upper and lower bounds for $κ$-well-conditioned functions exhibit a polynomial gap. For squared Euclidean movement, dimension-free competitive guarantees have been established under quadratic growth, but the optimal competitive ratio remains unclear.
  In this paper, we establish optimal dimension-free competitive ratios for convex function chasing under two types of movement costs. For Euclidean movement, we propose Clipped Proximal Descent (CPD), which combines a balanced proximal step with a clipped correction toward the current minimizer. CPD achieves an $O(κ^{1/3})$ competitive ratio for smooth convex functions satisfying quadratic growth, improving the previous $O(\sqrtκ)$ upper bound and matching the existing $Ω(κ^{1/3})$ lower bound. For squared Euclidean movement, we establish the exact optimal dimension-free competitive ratio for convex functions satisfying quadratic growth. Our result is based on a new tight competitive analysis of Regularized Online Balanced Descent (R-OBD) and a matching lower bound against randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [270] [SAE Interprets MoE: Sparse AutoEncoders Reveal That MoE Experts Exhibit Human-Interpretable Fine-Grained Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08089)
*Anyuan Zhuo,Shaohua Zhang,Yuan Lin,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过稀疏自编码器分析混合专家模型中各专家对特征的实际贡献，发现专家分工比路由选择所呈现的更集中、更细致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究常依据专家在不同领域语料中的选择频率推断其职责，但这种方法依赖预先设定的领域分类，粒度较粗，而且衡量的是专家被选中的频率，而非其对模型表征的实际贡献。

Method: 在混合专家层的输出上训练稀疏自编码器。由于该层输出是各专家输出的路由加权和，且编码器为仿射形式，每个潜在特征的预激活都能精确分解到各个专家。研究据此统计语料中各专家主要驱动的潜在特征，并分析这些特征集合及其重叠情况。

Result: 在通义千问三百亿参数、三十亿激活模型的第4、30和46层中，专家对特征的贡献比路由选择集中得多；多数潜在特征主要由一至两个专家驱动，主导特征集合的重叠则集中于少数专家对。自动解释结果显示，专家分工细于宽泛领域，例如分别专精于爪哇语言与前端网页代码，或几何与算术、多项式；不同宽泛领域所对应的专家数量也相差显著。

Conclusion: 通过从特征贡献而非选择频率的角度分析，可以更细致地揭示混合专家模型内部的专家分工。研究表明，专家职责具有明确的特征级结构，且传统领域分类难以完整刻画这种结构。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a few of many experts, yet what an individual expert is responsible for remains unsettled. Existing analyses count how often an expert is selected on domain-labeled corpora, an instrument that fixes the taxonomy in advance, stays coarse, and records selection rather than contribution. We instead study specialization in feature space by training a sparse autoencoder (SAE) on the output of an MoE layer. Since this output is a router-weighted sum of expert outputs and the SAE encoder is affine, the pre-activation of every latent decomposes exactly over the experts. Aggregated over a corpus, this gives each expert a dominant set of latents whose activation it principally drives. On layers 4, 30 and 46 of Qwen3-30B-A3B, contribution is far more concentrated than gating, most latents have only one or two dominators, and the overlap between dominant sets concentrates on a few expert pairs. Auto-interpreting these sets reveals a division of labor below coarse domains, with distinct experts for Java and front-end web code, or for geometry and for arithmetic and polynomials, while the coarse domains themselves take very unequal numbers of experts. Our SAEs and code are available at https://github.com/Bytedance-Seed/SAE-Interprets-MoE.

</details>


### [271] [PersonaAgent: Retrieval-Augmented Transaction Anomaly Scoring with Refreshable Multi-Entity Persona Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08094)
*Zhengyu Fang,Liang Wang,Yingtong Dou,Yuzhong Chen,Shubham Jain,Junpeng Wang,Xiran Fan,Menghai Pan,Jiarui Sun,Yan Zheng,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可刷新记忆增强的交易异常检测框架，用最新实体行为特征补充基础分类器，在保持推理高效的同时提升检测效果与业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型支付网络中的卡片、商户等实体及其交互是识别异常交易的关键。但实体间交易模式变化快于生产模型更新，且实体嵌入表面临实体基数高、交互数据稀疏的问题，导致模型难以及时捕捉最新行为。

Method: 框架将实体的最新行为信号概括为“角色画像”，并存入可刷新的记忆库。离线阶段每周运行批处理任务，提取并更新行为信号；推理阶段由轻量适配器从记忆库检索每笔交易相关实体的行为信号，用以增强基础异常分类器的评分。

Result: 在多种异常检测模型上的综合基准测试中，该框架可在中央处理器上以每批一千笔交易耗时一至二毫秒的速度完成评分。部署后，精确率—召回率曲线下面积提升百分之零点六五至百分之六点五八，生产业务指标相对提升百分之八点九三至百分之十四点二四。

Conclusion: 通过将最新实体行为信号存入可定期刷新的记忆，并在推理时按需检索，该方法缓解了行为变化快、嵌入稀疏及实体基数高带来的挑战。实验与部署结果表明，它能以较低推理开销提升异常检测效果和业务表现。

Abstract: In large-scale payment networks, entities (card, merchant, etc.) and their interactions play a key role in transaction anomaly detection. However, transaction patterns between different entities usually change more frequently than the production model refresh schedule. Meanwhile, entity embedding tables suffer from high-cardinality and sparse interaction issues. To resolve the above challenges, we present PersonaAgent, an agentic transaction anomaly detection framework which represents the latest entity behavior signals as personas and saves them in a refreshable memory. Offline, a weekly scheduled batch job is used to extract the latest behavior signals and update them in the memory. At inference time, a lightweight adapter retrieves the corresponding entity behavior signals of each transaction from memory and uses them to augment the base anomaly classifier's scoring. Through comprehensive benchmarking with different anomaly detection models at Visa, PersonaAgent is able to score transactions efficiently (a 1,000-transaction CPU batch in 1-2 ms), lifting the PR-AUC by 0.65-6.58% and the production business metric by 8.93-14.24% relatively in deployment.

</details>


### [272] [How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136)
*Hyojun Yi,Geunyoung Kim,Hogeon Seo,Seunghyoung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练神经网络的异常检测框架：利用固定随机非线性特征和基于正常样本的马氏距离评分，在表格检测与视觉定位任务中取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测通常被视为表征学习问题，研究者往往认为需要学习特征或针对异常检测进行优化。论文指出，这种观点可能忽视了异常样本与正常参考样本本身的可分性，因此探究未经训练的神经网络能否有效完成异常检测，并作为衡量学习表征增益的基线。

Method: 框架将固定的随机非线性特征与正常参考样本的马氏距离评分相结合，分别用于表格异常检测和密集视觉异常定位。模型不进行神经网络权重优化，也不使用预训练表征。论文还从理论上分析非线性变换如何影响马氏评分差距中的均值与协方差因素，并通过实验考察异常分数的可分性。

Result: 在异常检测基准集合的47个表格数据集上，表格版本取得86.4%的平均受试者工作特征曲线下面积，排名第一；平均精确率—召回率曲线下面积为65.9%，排名第二。在两个视觉基准的27个类别上，视觉版本的图像级和像素级平均受试者工作特征曲线下面积分别为90.4%和96.4%。上述结果均未使用神经网络权重优化或预训练表征。

Conclusion: 未经训练的神经网络结合正常参考样本评分，也能成为有竞争力的异常检测方案。该框架不仅提供了表格检测和视觉定位方法，也可作为学习表征之前的基线，用于评估学习所得表征对异常检测究竟带来了多少额外价值。

Abstract: Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

</details>


### [273] [Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148)
*Ioannis Dravilas,Miruna Stoicescu,Roope Tervo,Carlos Horn,Oriol Hinojo Comellas,Sina Montazeri,Andrea Meraner,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较仅使用静止气象卫星红外观测与额外加入再分析资料的锋面识别模型；加入再分析资料后，识别表现显著提升，低层风场和水汽信息尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气锋面是性质不同的气团之间的过渡区域，与中纬度降水及强对流天气密切相关。研究旨在评估机器学习识别锋面的能力，并量化再分析资料对识别效果的贡献。

Method: 针对北大西洋和欧洲区域，比较同一像素级锋面分类模型的两种配置：一种仅输入静止气象卫星红外观测，另一种在此基础上加入二十八种第五代欧洲中期天气预报中心再分析资料。研究还评估卫星观测对不同气象变量的表征能力，并通过移除变量分析其对识别性能的影响。

Result: 仅使用卫星观测的模型在一百五十公里容差下，邻域临界成功指数为零点四零二，处于不同气象服务机构分析结果的一致性范围内。加入再分析资料后，该指数升至零点五七五，提高零点一七三。卫星表征低层风场的能力最弱，决定系数为零点二三；对温度的表征较强，决定系数为零点八八。移除风场或水汽信息会造成最大的识别性能下降。

Conclusion: 卫星观测能够提供有价值的锋面识别信息，但难以充分刻画低层风场等关键动力特征。融合再分析资料可明显提升识别效果，其中风场和水汽信息对性能贡献突出。

Abstract: Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. Our analysis shows that low-level winds are the least predictable from the satellite representation ($R^2 = 0.23$, compared with $0.88$ for temperature), while removing winds or moisture causes the largest decreases in detection performance.

</details>


### [274] [BUMP: Brief Unexpected Mid-Interpolation Peak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08182)
*Melinda Flóra Kiss,Márton Muntag,Adrián Csiszárik,Dániel Varga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，两个经过排列对齐的神经网络进行权重线性插值时，测试准确率会在插值端点附近短暂上升，随后向中点下降；据此提出结合权重混合与预测混合的集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有插值图中可见端点附近的准确率提升，但这一现象此前未被明确命名或系统研究。作者希望厘清它是否普遍存在、能否由简单正则化效应解释，并探索其对模型集成的启发。

Method: 系统考察排列对齐网络之间的线性权重插值，检验该现象在不同数据集、架构和训练设置下的稳定性，并通过消融实验排查简单正则化解释。进一步提出权重—预测混合集成：同时结合权重空间的混合与预测空间的混合。

Result: 端点附近的短暂准确率提升在多种数据集、架构和训练条件下均稳定出现，在使用较早训练检查点或进行大幅剪枝后仍然存在；消融结果表明，它不能简单归因于正则化效应。所提出的集成方法在实验中持续优于标准对数几率集成的测试准确率，并改善校准效果与准确率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 端点附近的准确率峰值是具有稳健性的插值现象，也可转化为实用的集成策略。结合权重空间与预测空间的信息，能够发挥二者的互补优势；其具体机制仍有进一步研究价值。

Abstract: We identify an intriguing phenomenon when linearly interpolating between permutation-aligned neural networks: test accuracy briefly improves near the interpolation endpoints before dropping toward the midpoint, which we term BUMP (Brief Unexpected Mid-interpolation Peak). We observe that BUMP appears consistently across datasets, architectures, training regimes, and remains robust under substantial perturbations such as early checkpoints and extreme pruning. Moreover, controlled ablations suggest that it cannot be attributed to trivial regularization effects. While similar near-endpoint gains are visible in prior interpolation plots, they have not been explicitly documented or systematically and thoroughly analyzed. Leveraging this effect, we propose a simple, novel ensembling scheme: weight-prediction mixed ensembling that combines weight mixing with prediction-space mixing. Across our experiments, the resulting ensembling method consistently outperforms standard logit ensembling in test accuracy and yields improved calibration trade-off, highlighting complementary benefits of weight- and prediction-space mixing.

</details>


### [275] [Do sEMG Foundation Models Improve Cross-Subject Generalisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08199)
*William Raftery,Dario Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，表面肌电基础模型的预训练收益主要体现在同一采集会话内；面对跨受试者评估时，收益大幅减弱，受试者数量较少时传统机器学习方法也能达到或超过其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号容易受到受试者差异和采集条件变化影响，导致模型跨受试者泛化困难。已有评估难以判断预训练本身能否缓解这一问题，因此需要在不同数据划分和微调策略下系统检验基础模型的实际贡献。

Method: 研究评估了三种表面肌电基础模型在三个手势分类数据集上的表现，分别采用同一会话内划分和跨受试者划分，并使用三种微调策略，以区分预训练对下游性能的贡献；同时将模型表现与传统机器学习基线进行比较。

Result: 在训练与测试样本来自同一采集会话时，预训练能够提升性能；在跨受试者评估中，这一优势大多消失。对于受试者数量较少的数据集，预训练带来的收益很小或不存在，传统机器学习基线的表现可与基础模型相当，甚至更好。

Conclusion: 表面肌电基础模型的预训练并不能普遍解决跨受试者过拟合问题，其效果取决于评估场景和数据规模。评估此类模型时应采用跨受试者划分，并与传统方法进行比较，避免仅凭同会话结果高估其泛化能力。

Abstract: sEMG Foundation Models aim to learn general purpose representations over diverse subjects and recording conditions that boost performance on a variety of downstream tasks. However, existing evaluations make it difficult to discern whether pretraining alone is sufficient to combat the cross-subject overfitting which makes machine learning on sEMG so challenging. We conduct a systematic evaluation of three sEMG foundation models across three gesture classification datasets under both intra-session and inter-subject splits, using three fine-tuning strategies designed to isolate the contribution of pretraining. We find that pretraining boosts performance when train and test samples are drawn from the same recording session, but this benefit largely disappears under inter-subject evaluation. In low subject count datasets, pretraining provides little to no benefit and classical ML baselines match or exceed foundation model performance.

</details>


### [276] [Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236)
*Carlos Boned Riera,David Romero Sanchez,Oriol Ramos Terrades*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可分离高斯和空间算子，以低于二次方的计算开销替代稠密自注意力，并在准确率、参数效率、推理速度和理论性质方面展现优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的计算成本随令牌数量平方增长，限制了视觉变换器处理高分辨率输入的能力。此外，点积自注意力在紧致输入域之外的利普希茨常数无界，使连续深度模型缺乏普遍的全局解保证。论文旨在设计一种计算更高效、具备更好理论性质的替代空间算子。

Method: 提出可分离高斯和空间算子，以学习得到的轴向因式分解高斯核混合替代稠密自注意力，将单层计算成本从令牌数的平方级降至次平方级。对于固定核，该算子具有全局利普希茨连续性；结合皮卡—林德勒夫定理，可保证其连续深度版本在整个积分区间内存在唯一解。论文在离散变换器、连续深度变换器和循环共享权重变换器中进行评估，并比较高分辨率推理表现。

Result: 在分辨率为二百二十四乘二百二十四的彩色图像分类数据集上，离散与连续深度设置中，该方法在三种模型宽度下将最高类别准确率提高二点二至四点一个百分点，同时减少百分之八点八至十六点六的参数量；循环变换器设置中，准确率提高五点四至八点一个百分点。高分辨率测试最高达到一千零二十四乘一千零二十四：连续深度架构的吞吐量最高提升百分之一百五十四，峰值内存降低超过百分之六十；离散和循环设置的吞吐量也提高超过百分之三十。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，可分离高斯和空间算子能够作为稠密自注意力的实用替代方案，在不同变换器架构中兼顾准确率、参数效率与高分辨率推理效率，并为连续深度模型提供全局唯一解的理论保证。

Abstract: Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\% higher throughput and over 60\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.

</details>


### [277] [FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240)
*Haitao Lin,Minsi Ren,Yang Li,Xingtong Ge,Zheyuan Hu,Peiyan Hu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套将强化学习目标系统适配到少步扩散模型的方法，并发现不同方法的效果取决于奖励是否可微及其梯度计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型强化学习方法通常每轮都要用当前策略进行多步采样，训练开销很大。少步模型虽能降低采样成本，但预测的是一段时间内的平均速度，而传统目标多针对多步采样中的瞬时速度设计，因此不能直接沿用。

Method: 方法分为三个阶段：首先将不同目标统一改写为样本空间中的策略预测，使基于概率密度、速度或奖励的目标能够比较；其次将各目标的梯度分解为向参考值回归的部分与奖励增强部分，从而归纳为锚点和权重的选择；最后利用少步模型在输运终点或轨迹切线处计算锚点，并通过网络本身或辅助网络估计瞬时速度。该框架将多种已有方法纳入同一模块化实现。

Result: 实验显示，奖励梯度可微且计算成本可接受时，直接利用奖励梯度的方法效果最好；否则，类似扩散噪声过滤式训练的方法通常能以较少迭代获得较高奖励，但单轮成本有时更高。训练后增加推理步数并未带来一致的整体收益，不过部分生成结果在推理步数为训练时的二至四倍时有明显改善。

Conclusion: 本文提供了将多类扩散强化学习目标迁移到少步模型的统一分析与实现框架。方法选择应权衡奖励梯度的可用性、计算成本与训练效率；增加推理步数的收益则具有任务和样本依赖性，不能视为普遍有效的策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation.
