The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

</details>


### [278] [Geometric Relevance Is Not Sufficient: Evaluating Focused Post-Training Supervision in Diffusion Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08249)
*Michael Brown,Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Mehdi Yazdani-Jahromi,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Ivan Garibay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较不同后训练监督策略对扩散式分子对接的影响；在所测试的训练预算下，广覆盖续训的置信度筛选首选姿势成功率最高，聚焦监督未带来稳定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式分子对接需要在广泛的扰动分布上学习校正分数场，但不同姿势误差对分子结构的影响程度并不相同。后训练因此面临取舍：维持对广泛分数场的覆盖，还是将监督集中于看似更符合对接几何的校正。

Method: 以扩散式分子对接模型为基础，在固定训练预算下比较广覆盖续训、按笛卡尔坐标后果聚焦的扭转角监督、按条件靶标一致性聚焦的扭转角监督，以及广覆盖与聚焦监督的混合策略。评估分别考察固定样本上的分数预测保真度、生成姿势质量、十次采样中的最佳恢复能力，以及经置信度筛选后的首选姿势对接表现。

Result: 以误差小于五埃为成功标准时，在训练后的策略中，广覆盖续训取得最高的平均首选姿势成功率；聚焦策略和混合策略在所测预算下均未显示稳定优势。初始模型的固定分数预测保真度更高；广覆盖续训模型的平均首选姿势成功率更高，而两者十次采样的总体最佳恢复能力相同。

Conclusion: 在本研究的预算和评估设置下，将监督集中于结构后果更显著或条件指定更一致的校正，并未稳定提升最终返回姿势的成功率；广覆盖续训是表现更好的训练后策略，但其优势不代表分数预测保真度或最佳采样恢复能力也同步提高。

Abstract: Diffusion-based molecular docking requires learning a corrective score field across a broad corruption distribution, yet individual pose errors can differ substantially in their structural consequences. This creates a post-training conflict between preserving broad score-field coverage and concentrating supervision on corrections that appear more relevant to docking geometry. Using DiffDock, a fixed-budget study compares broad continuation with torsional supervision focused on Cartesian consequence or conditional target consistency, and with a broad/focused mixture. Evaluation separates fixed score fidelity, generated-pose quality, best-of-10 sampling capacity, and confidence-selected Top-1 docking performance. At <5 Angstrom, broad continuation had the highest mean Top-1 success among trained policies, and focused or mixed supervision showed no consistent advantage under the tested budget. The starting model had better fixed score fidelity, whereas the evaluated broad-continuation model had higher mean Top-1 success with the same aggregate best-of-10 recovery. Focusing supervision on structurally consequential or more consistently specified corrections did not consistently improve returned-pose success over broad continuation.

</details>


### [279] [Nowcasting of Tropical Cyclone Wind Fields and Intensity from Geostationary Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08255)
*Simon Donike,Pritthijit Nath,Cristina Radin,Arthur Avenas,Ruben Cartuyvels,Emiliano Diaz,William K. Jones,Anna Jungbluth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究利用静止气象卫星云顶影像预测热带气旋近地表风场与强度指标；联合预测风场和标量指标，尤其有助于快速增强事件中最大风速及其半径的反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径雷达观测可提供高分辨率热带气旋近地表风速，有助于风暴结构和强度建模，但观测在时空上稀疏，且难以近实时获取。静止气象卫星能够连续观测云顶，却不能直接测量近地表风，因此需要建立从云顶影像推断风场的方法。

Method: 提出简单的神经网络，以静止气象卫星影像为输入，同时预测二维风场和风暴尺度的强度指标。通过一系列消融实验，评估联合预测以及利用模型潜在表示预测标量指标的效果。

Result: 实验表明，从模型潜在表示联合预测二维风场和标量强度指标，可改善最大风速及其对应半径的反演；这种提升在快速增强事件中尤为明显。

Conclusion: 静止气象卫星影像有望用于连续推断热带气旋近地表风场与强度。联合预测风场和强度指标是提升关键风暴参数反演效果，特别是应对快速增强事件的有效策略。

Abstract: High-resolution tropical cyclone surface wind speeds derived from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations can support storm structure and intensity modelling, but remain spatially and temporally sparse and unavailable near-real-time. Geostationary satellites capture cloud tops continuously in space and time, but do not directly resolve surface winds. Here, we present simple neural networks to predict 2D wind fields and storm-scale intensity metrics from geostationary imagery. Through a series of ablation studies, we find that joint prediction of 2D fields and scalar metrics from the model latent space improves the retrieval of the (radius of) maximum wind, especially for rapid intensification events.

</details>


### [280] [Dynamic Optimistic Constrained OCO with Memory via Delay Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08302)
*Mohammed Abdullah,George Iosifidis,Salah Eddine Elayoubi,Tijani Chahed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带记忆和时变约束的在线凸优化，提出利用不可信预测的乐观算法，并在动态遗憾与累计约束违反方面获得适应环境变化和预测准确度的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带记忆和时变约束的在线凸优化能够刻画具有状态依赖性的在线学习、非随机控制及预算约束问题，但相关研究较少。已有方法在适应环境非平稳性和预测信息方面存在不足，且依赖函数可分离等限制性假设。本文旨在建立更通用的算法与理论保证。

Method: 提出一种融合不可信预测的乐观算法，以控制普适动态遗憾和累计约束违反。分析上，将记忆效应约化为反馈延迟，并将依赖状态的梯度视为延迟反馈；进一步结合环境路径长度与预测准确度分析性能，同时研究记忆到延迟的约化，以及带时变约束的延迟在线凸优化。

Result: 本文给出了首批同时适应环境非平稳程度和预测准确度的界，并取消了以往的函数可分离等限制。算法具有次线性的最坏情形保证；预测完全准确时，遗憾可达到常数量级。路径长度未知时，累计约束违反为平方根量级；路径长度已知或可在线观测时，可降至对数级。记忆消失时，算法恢复最优的相关问题界。

Conclusion: 该工作为带记忆和时变约束的在线凸优化提供了更完整的理论框架，说明不可信预测能够在不放弃最坏情形保障的前提下改善动态性能，并通过记忆到延迟的约化处理状态依赖反馈。

Abstract: Online Convex Optimization with memory and time-varying constraints (COCO-M) is a recently-introduced framework that captures the dynamics of stateful online learning and non-stochastic control with budget constraints. Despite its expressive power, COCO-M remains largely understudied. In this work, we tackle this problem in its full scale, providing an optimistic algorithm that incorporates untrusted predictions to tame universal dynamic regret and cumulative constraint violation. Our analysis leverages a potent reduction from memory to delay and treats state-dependent gradients as delayed feedback. We establish the first bounds that adapt to both environment non-stationarity (path length $P_T$) and prediction accuracy, while lifting previous restrictive assumptions such as functional separability. En route to these results, we derive findings of independent interest for the memory-to-delay reduction, and for delayed OCO with time-varying constraints. The proposed framework ensures sublinear worst-case guarantees that improve with prediction accuracy, reaching $\mathcal O(1)$ regret under perfect predictions, with cumulative constraint violation (CCV) $\mathcal O(\sqrt{T})$ for unknown $P_T$ and $\mathcal O(\log T)$ for known or online observable $P_T$ , while recovering the optimal COCO rates when memory disappears.

</details>


### [281] [Interpretable many-body interaction laws for collective motion from few trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08314)
*Timon S. Gutleb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可解释、可系统改进的多体交互学习方法，能从少量数据中恢复群体运动规律，并揭示仅看下一步预测误差不足以评估交互学习质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体运动模型常将个体间作用简化为两两交互，但真实集体行为可能包含显著的三体及更高阶效应。论文旨在建立能够表达这些多体作用、适用于不同空间维度且便于解释的学习框架。

Method: 作者将原子簇展开思想改造为适用于自驱动个体的方向性原子簇展开，用来分解并拟合个体加速度。该方法具有严格的对称等变性，可将作用唯一拆分为自项、一体、二体、三体及更高阶贡献；自项用于刻画个体自身过去运动，并经过专门拟合以避免吸收个体间交互。

Result: 在二维和三维、交互规则已知的模拟数据上，该方法能用较少数据逐阶恢复真实作用规律；相较于神经网络基线，在多体或手性规则上的误差更小，且不局限于二维。在斑马鱼和金色鲦鱼实测数据中，分割数据集两半后学得的规律一致性更高。金色鲦鱼的结果还显示，邻域变密时三体作用会抵消部分两体响应。研究也发现，单步预测准确率主要反映自驱动与噪声，而非交互规律本身。

Conclusion: 方向性原子簇展开为群体运动中的多体交互学习提供了数据效率较高、可解释且适用于二维和三维的方案。评估交互学习时，不应只依赖下一步预测误差，而应结合更能反映交互规律恢复质量的指标。

Abstract: Models of collective motion usually reduce social interactions to pairwise forces, despite empirical evidence for many-body effects. We introduce the directional atomic cluster expansion (dACE), which adapts the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) of interatomic potentials to self-propelled agents. dACE provides a systematically improvable, exactly equivariant expansion of an agent's acceleration that splits uniquely into 1-, 2-, 3-body and higher contributions, along with a self term for the agent's own past motion that is fitted so that it does not absorb the interactions. On simulated collectives in two and three dimensions with known rules, dACE recovers the interaction law body order by body order from little data, while the neural model SocialNet, the current closest competitor, needs more data, has much larger errors on many-body or chiral rules and is restricted to 2D. On real data sets of tracked zebrafish and golden shiners, the laws learned by dACE agree better between disjoint halves of the data than SocialNet's. The learned laws are interpretable, and in the shiners the 3-body term opposes the pairwise response as neighbourhoods get dense. Finally, we show that next-step prediction accuracy mostly measures self-propulsion and noise rather than the interactions, and suggest more robust combinations of error measures for interaction learning.

</details>


### [282] [Discovering Learning Rules that Shape Others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08335)
*Hong Jun Lee,Ziyan Wang,Jun Wang,Stefanos Leonardos,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种通过元学习发现更新规则的方法，使智能体仅凭自身经验塑造对手行为，并在锦标赛中取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和博弈中的自利学习者可能陷入双方都不利的结果。已有的对手塑造方法虽能促进合作或利用对手，却通常需要访问对手的策略、参数、奖励或学习规则，限制了部署适用性。论文希望实现不依赖这些对手信息的对手塑造。

Method: 作者提出“对手塑造的发现式更新规则”。该方法通过元学习训练一套独立于策略的更新规则，以最大化智能体整个生命周期的预期回报；执行时，规则仅读取智能体自身的经验，并据此更新其内部网络，从而在一般和博弈中学习如何影响对手。

Result: 实验显示，发现的更新规则呈现出与对手塑造相符的行为，同时无需读取对手的策略或参数。在参赛者锦标赛中，该方法的归一化平均得分排名第一，自我对弈表现也排名第一。

Conclusion: 研究将基于元学习发现更新规则的思路扩展到一般和博弈，提出了一种仅依赖自身经验的对手塑造方法。摘要所报告的结果表明，该方法兼具较强的竞赛表现与较低的对手信息依赖；其适用范围和在更多博弈环境中的稳健性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: In general-sum games, self-interested learners can reach outcomes that are poor for everyone, which has motivated methods that shape the opponent's learning. Methods like Learning with Opponent-Learning Awareness (LOLA), which differentiates through the opponent's learning process, and Model-Free Opponent Shaping (M-FOS), which uses model-free optimisation methods for long-horizon opponent shaping, have enabled mutual cooperation and exploitation of learners. However, these methods require access to the opponent's parameters, policy, rewards or learning rule during deployment. In single-agent reinforcement learning, update rules discovered by meta-learning have achieved state-of-the-art results (e.g. DiscoRL). These rules are separate from the policy they train and read only the agent's own experience. We hereby extend this framework to general-sum settings by introducing Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping (DUOS). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method to discover an update rule for an agent in general-sum games. DUOS meta-trains the rule to maximise the agent's expected return over its lifetime, and updates an inner network using the agent's own experience. We show that our discovered rule exhibits behaviour consistent with opponent shaping without reading opponent policies or parameters, and ranks 1st in normalised mean score and 1st in self-play among tournament entrants.

</details>


### [283] [StrataFlux: Memory-Hierarchy-Native Low-Bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08363)
*Jake Hyun,Ziyu Zhao,Jae-sun Seo,Mohamed S. Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种精度分层与选择性传输框架，在保留低比特量化所带来的显存和速度优势的同时，恢复部分精度损失；其核心是不必将全部高精度数据常驻显卡，也能利用主机内存和固态硬盘提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型量化可降低推理所需的显存和带宽，但低比特权重与键值缓存会损害模型准确性。若通过传输高精度数据来弥补这一问题，数据调入延迟又可能阻塞生成。因此，研究旨在打破准确性、显存占用与解码速度之间的折衷。

Method: 提出精度分层框架，联合设计精度补充数据的表示、选择和传输方式。框架从主机内存或固态硬盘中选择性读取额外精度数据，减少传输量；通过传输与计算重叠，以及跨解码步骤复用已读取的数据，降低等待开销。研究评估了二至四比特权重、二至三比特键值缓存，并涵盖独立显卡与统一内存平台。

Result: 在二比特权重设置下，框架将某八十亿参数模型在维基文本语言建模基准上的困惑度从九点四六降至八点四零，同时保留量化基线吞吐量的百分之九十五点四。另一模型采用二比特权重与键值缓存时，数学竞赛基准准确率由百分之十一升至百分之四十七点一，且显卡显存占用不变。在一百二十八千词元上下文下，三比特权重与二比特键值缓存取得了与四比特权重、八比特键值缓存方案相近的困惑度和速度，显存约减少一半。

Conclusion: 实验表明，显卡中常驻数据的精度并非模型准确性的上限，外部存储的数据传输延迟也不必决定解码速度。通过精度补充数据的选择性读取、计算重叠和跨步复用，可以在降低显存占用的同时显著改善量化模型的质量。

Abstract: Quantization reduces the memory and bandwidth demands of large language model (LLM) inference but can degrade accuracy; offloading higher-precision data can instead stall generation. We introduce StrataFlux, a framework that challenges this tradeoff by recovering accuracy lost to low-bit weight and key-value (KV) cache quantization while retaining quantization's memory and speed advantages. StrataFlux co-designs refinement representation, selection, and delivery to make host RAM and solid-state drives useful sources of additional precision. Its selective precision streaming reduces transfer volume, overlaps delivery with computation, and reuses fetched precision across decoding steps so generation need not wait for every transfer. We evaluate 2-4-bit weights and 2-3-bit KV-cache on dedicated GPUs and unified-memory platforms. For 2-bit Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct weights, StrataFlux reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 9.46 to 8.40 while maintaining 95.4% of the quantized base's throughput on RTX 5090. With 2-bit weights and KV, StrataFlux raises Qwen3-8B's AIME 2024/2025 accuracy from 11.0% to 47.1% at unchanged GPU memory use. At 128K context, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with 3-bit weights and 2-bit KV achieves comparable perplexity and speed to vLLM's 4-bit AWQ weights and 8-bit KV on RTX 5090, using approximately half the GPU memory. These results show that GPU-resident precision need not set the accuracy ceiling, nor offloading-tier latency dictate decoding speed.

</details>


### [284] [Muir: Linear Attention with Sensitivity-Allocated Gating and Matched-Filter Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08376)
*Tushaar Gangavarapu,Gil Tabak,Clemens JS Schaefer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出缪尔模型，通过按状态更新敏感度分配门控参数、匹配读出权重及稳定的并行训练算法，提升线性注意力模型的语言建模、长上下文检索与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门控增量网络二代模型使用通道级衰减及较高秩的擦除、写入门控，但两类更新的敏感度并不相同：键的激活与归一化会使能量集中于少数通道，而值写入则作用于所有通道，因此擦除门控不必具有与写入门控相同的秩。此外，衰减较慢的状态行范数更大，在未加权读出时容易主导结果，迫使模型加强写入时的擦除以抑制干扰。针对这些参数利用不均衡和状态读出失衡问题，论文寻求更有效的结构设计。

Method: 缪尔将各类门控融合为一个依据敏感度分配容量的低秩投影，以较低秩表示较不敏感的擦除操作；采用与状态匹配的读出门控，对不同状态行重新加权；并将节省的参数用于扩大前馈网络。针对大块长训练下标准分块算法数值不稳定的问题，作者设计了稳定且高效的并行状态更新算法。

Result: 在一亿六千六百万至十三亿四千万参数规模上，缪尔相较门控增量网络二代将困惑度降低百分之一点八至百分之二点零，相较变换器降低百分之二点五至百分之三点六；常识准确率最高提升一点九个百分点，并改善抽取式召回。在十三亿四千万参数规模下，缪尔在多跳及多针检索上优于两个基线；训练长度为四千词元时，可将上下文外推至一千万词元而困惑度无明显漂移，并在静态读出下对一百万词元内的通行码检索保持接近百分之百的成功率。其吞吐量与门控增量网络二代相当，达到一千亿词元验证损失所需时间快一点二倍；在三万二千词元训练设置下，训练速度为变换器的二点七倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，线性注意力模型的擦除、写入与读出机制可依据各自的敏感度和状态尺度进行差异化设计，而非采用均衡的门控容量与无差别读出。缪尔通过这种结构重分配，在保持高吞吐量的同时提升语言建模效果、长上下文检索能力及训练效率。

Abstract: Linear attention compresses context into a fixed-size key-value state for linear-time training and constant-memory inference. Gated delta-rule models decay this state and erase its prediction for an incoming key before writing a new value; Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) generalizes this with channel-wise decay and decoupled, full-rank erase and write gates. However, we show that state updates are far less sensitive to erasure than to writes: key activation and unit normalization concentrate key energy onto a sparse subset of channels, whereas value writes scale all channels, so the erase gate can be parameterized at lower rank. Furthermore, slow-decaying state rows accumulate much larger norms than fast rows, dominating unweighted readout and forcing stronger write-time erasure to suppress interference. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Muir, which fuses all gates into a sensitivity-allocated low-rank projection, re-weights state rows with a matched-filter readout gate, and reinvests the saved parameters in a wider feed-forward network. Because standard chunkwise algorithms become numerically unstable at the large chunk sizes needed to saturate wide matrix units, we design a parallel update for stable, efficient training. Across 166M-1.34B parameters, Muir lowers perplexity by 1.8-2.0% over GDN-2 and 2.5-3.6% over Transformer, raises commonsense accuracy by up to 1.9 points, and improves extractive recall over GDN-2. At 1.34B, Muir outperforms both baselines on multi-hop and multi-needle recall, extrapolates 2,560x from its 4K training length to 10M-token streams without perplexity drift, and maintains near-100% passkey retrieval to 1M tokens with static readout. Muir achieves these gains while matching GDN-2 throughput, reaching its 100B-token validation loss 1.2x faster, and training 2.7x faster than Transformer at 32K.

</details>


### [285] [Module-Aware Optimization for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08379)
*Guy Hadad,Haggai Roitman,Moshe Eliasof,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出图感知优化器“图穆恩”，利用图传播后层输入的协方差结构调整矩阵更新，在多个图学习任务上较常用自适应优化器更快达到目标验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同优化器为参数更新引入不同几何结构。现有矩阵感知优化方法虽能规范化权重矩阵更新，却没有考虑图神经网络中权重作用于图传播表示这一特点；因此，矩阵上看似适当的更新，仍可能在层输出中被图结构放大。

Method: 图穆恩根据图传播后的层输入协方差定义度量，并在该度量下对矩阵更新进行正交化；同时依据更新对层表示造成的影响来调整步长。理论分析指出，普通矩阵正交更新可能遭受图结构引起的更新放大，而该方法能够缓解这一问题，可视为适配图度量的极分解式更新。

Result: 在三个大规模图基准上，相较常用自适应权重衰减优化器，图穆恩达到目标验证损失的速度提升约一点三二至二点四二倍。在涵盖节点分类、归纳式图分类和图回归的十五种数据集与架构组合中，它有十三种达到目标所需时间更短，同时预测性能具有竞争力或更优。

Conclusion: 优化图神经网络时，仅考察权重矩阵本身的更新几何并不充分，还应纳入图传播表示的结构。图穆恩将这种图感知度量融入矩阵更新，在理论上缓解更新放大，并在多类图任务上展现出较好的训练效率与预测表现。

Abstract: Different optimizers impose different geometries on parameter updates. Adaptive methods such as Adam and AdamW use coordinate-wise updates, while matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon utilize weight-matrix structure through orthogonalized updates. For Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), however, the effective geometry of its weight matrices is shaped not only by its learned channel-mixing weights, but also by the graph-propagated representations it acts on. Thus, even if an update is well normalized as a matrix, its effect on the layer output can be amplified when applied to graph-propagated features. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMuon, a graph-aware optimizer that adapts Muon's matrix updates to the covariance structure of graph-propagated layer inputs. GraphMuon performs orthogonalization in a metric defined by these propagated representations and normalizes step sizes according to the update's induced effect on the layer representation. We show that theoretically, vanilla Muon can suffer from graph-induced update amplification, while our GraphMuon can mitigate this effect. As such, our GraphMuon yields a graph-metric analogue of Muon's polar update step. Empirically, GraphMuon achieves $1.32\times$-$2.42\times$ validation-loss time-to-target speedups over AdamW on three large-scale OGB benchmarks. Across 15 dataset-architecture configurations spanning node classification, inductive graph classification, and graph regression, it achieves lower time-to-target in 13 cases, while maintaining competitive or superior predictive performance. Code is available at https://github.com/guy-hadad/GraphMuon.

</details>


### [286] [Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394)
*Déborah Boyenval,Alina Malyutina,Jaana Oikkonen,Sammeli Liikkanen,Sampsa Hautaniemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套以生物学特征为依据、患者间数据严格隔离的评估框架，用于检验合成的配对核糖核酸表达与拷贝数变异数据是否保留关键结构，并以卵巢癌纵向队列进行示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对多组学癌症队列的采集成本高、共享困难，因此合成队列可作为补充资源。随着生成模型不断发展，研究重点不仅是提出新模型，更需要建立可靠标准，判断合成数据是否保留与生物学相关的结构。

Method: 研究构建了生物学特征驱动且患者互不重叠的评估流程，针对配对核糖核酸表达和拷贝数变异谱，分别评估各组学的保真度及两者之间的一致性，并以真实训练数据与留出数据之间的一致程度作为参照，同时加入衡量合成数据与训练数据接近程度的诊断指标。研究将三种基于变分自编码器的多组学整合架构改造用于生成配对数据，并在一项高级别浆液性卵巢癌纵向队列中开展案例分析。

Result: 在多项保真度和组学间一致性指标上表现领先的模型，同时也显示出最强的训练数据接近程度。这表明，仅凭部分生成质量指标不足以全面判断模型表现，训练数据接近程度等诊断指标也值得纳入评估。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了评估配对多组学合成数据的系统化流程和案例参考，可用于比较未来的多组学生成模型。其核心贡献是明确评估维度与参照方式，而非提出新的生成架构。

Abstract: Paired multi-omic cancer cohorts are costly to assemble and difficult to share, motivating synthetic cohorts as a complementary resource. As generative model development accelerates, we shift the focus from proposing yet another architecture to defining evaluation criteria that determine whether synthetic data preserve biologically relevant structure. We introduce a biology-informed and patient-disjoint framework for evaluating the generation of paired RNA expression and copy-number variation (CNV) profiles, and apply it to the longitudinal DECIDER high-grade serous ovarian cancer cohort. The framework assesses modality-specific fidelity and coherence between RNA and CNV pairs using agreement between real training and held-out data as a reference, alongside complementary training-data proximity diagnostics. We adapt three multi-omic integration architectures based on variational autoencoders to generate paired profiles. The model leading several fidelity and coherence measures also shows the strongest training-data proximity. The protocol and case study offer the community a reference for comparing future multi-omic generators.

</details>


### [287] [Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409)
*Joshua Stiller,Annika Schneider,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究时间依赖物理系统中保形预测的覆盖率失效机制，并提出适用于部分场景的加权方法以恢复有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测通常能提供不依赖分布假设的覆盖保证，但物理与工程系统中的数据随时间变化，可能导致预测区间系统性低估不确定性。理解这种失效的根源，并明确何时能够维持覆盖保证，对安全关键应用至关重要。

Method: 以偏微分方程描述的时间依赖物理系统代理模型为研究对象，分析函数空间中不同时刻概率分布的关系；进一步考察空间离散后的分布总变差距离如何随网格分辨率变化；最后在特定条件下采用具有原则依据的加权保形预测，建立随时间范围扩展的有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 研究证明，在函数空间中，即使两个时刻任意接近，其对应分布也可能互相奇异，因此无法实现精确覆盖保证。在离散情形下，分布间的总变差距离往往会随网格分辨率增加而指数级趋于饱和，因此必须限制空间离散尺度，才能保障覆盖。对于部分设定，加权保形预测能够在不断增长的时间范围内提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 时间依赖性与空间离散化会从根本上限制保形预测在物理系统中的覆盖能力。应用时需审慎选择网格分辨率；在满足相应条件的场景中，可利用加权保形预测获得随时间范围扩展的有限样本保证。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in scientific machine learning, where models inform safety-critical tasks such as flood forecasting, financial risk management, and thermal control in machines. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in time-dependent settings common to physics and engineering, these guarantees can break down, leading to systematic undercoverage. We study this problem in the context of surrogate models for time-dependent physical systems described by partial differential equations. We prove that in a function space setting, distributions at arbitrarily close times can be mutually singular, making exact coverage guarantees impossible. We further show that in discretized settings, the total variation distance between distributions often saturates exponentially fast with the grid resolution, mandating restrictions on the spatial discretization to ensure coverage. Finally, for certain settings, we show how to use principled weighted conformal prediction to obtain finite-sample coverage guarantees over growing time horizons.

</details>


### [288] [Spectral and Subspace Dynamics of LoRA in Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08414)
*Chenruo Liu,Zihan Wang,Yijun Dong,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示组均值基线扣除会使低秩适配更新更集中于少数奇异方向、加快子空间旋转，并据此为监督微调与强化学习设计不同的更新策略；实验中两种策略在多数评测设置下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩适配广泛用于监督微调和强化学习，但两阶段的学习动态如何反映在其优化的低秩增量中尚不清楚。理解这种差异有助于针对不同训练阶段设计更有效的适配方法。

Method: 研究者在数学推理任务上跟踪低秩更新的奇异值谱与奇异子空间，并逐项改变监督微调与强化学习之间的训练因素，以定位造成动态差异的关键因素。随后使用经低秩适配更新的二次多指标模型分析机制，并依据观察到的动态设计阶段专属策略：监督微调时展平完整更新的奇异值谱；强化学习时展平当前低秩增量张成空间之外的更新部分。

Result: 强化学习在各学习率下都会使奇异值谱趋于集中；监督微调则在中等学习率下使谱更为扩展。在较小学习率下，强化学习的奇异子空间旋转也明显快于监督微调。逐项对照表明，组均值基线扣除是这两类差异的来源。理论模型进一步指出，该机制改变了相干拟合与梯度噪声之间的权衡，使谱更集中、子空间旋转更快，同时令风险最优的学习率偏低。跨多个模型的数学推理实验显示，针对两阶段设计的策略在多数评测设置中优于所比较的方法。

Conclusion: 监督微调与强化学习的低秩适配更新具有不同的谱与子空间动态，组均值基线扣除是关键原因。因此，低秩适配不宜在两阶段沿用同一更新方式：监督微调应促进更新分散到更多奇异方向，强化学习则应促进低秩子空间旋转。实验结果支持这种按阶段调整的设计。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly used in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), often under similar optimizer settings. Yet, how the different dynamics of the two stages manifest in the low-rank increment that LoRA optimizes remains unclear. Tracking the singular spectrum and subspaces of LoRA updates on mathematical reasoning tasks, we find that RL concentrates the spectrum at every learning rate, while SFT expands it at moderate learning rates; moreover, RL rotates the singular subspaces much faster than SFT at small learning rates. Interpolating between SFT and RL one ingredient at a time, we pinpoint group-mean baseline subtraction as the source of both contrasts. A quadratic multi-index model updated via LoRA unveils the underlying mechanism: group-mean baseline subtraction tilts the balance between two competing forces, coherent fitting and gradient noise, toward spectral concentration and faster subspace rotation, while forcing the risk-favorable learning rate to be low. Guided by these dynamics, we tailor LoRA updates to each stage: in SFT, we flatten the spectrum of the full update to spread adaptation across singular directions, whereas in RL, we flatten the part of the update outside the current LoRA increment span to promote subspace rotation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple models show that the respective configurations for SFT and RL yield the best performance among the compared methods in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [289] [On the limitations of molecular representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420)
*Acer Blake,Fergus Imrie,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究系统比较了多类小分子表示，发现当前先进的分子嵌入并未在结构、三维形状或吸收分布代谢排泄及毒性相关相似性方面优于传统分子指纹或理化描述符；同时，传统表示也存在大量分子编码碰撞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子表示广泛用于聚类、数据集划分和虚拟筛选，但其评价通常局限于少数下游任务的模型表现，难以反映表示本身是否能有效捕捉分子的结构、形状及性质相似性。研究旨在更全面地评估现有表示的能力与局限。

Method: 作者对多种分子表示开展系统比较，涵盖先进的分子嵌入、传统分子指纹和理化描述符，并考察它们对分子图结构、三维形状及吸收、分布、代谢、排泄和毒性相关终点相似性的刻画能力。此外，还分析不同表示在一个含约一百零八亿个分子的大型可合成分子库中的编码唯一性。

Result: 没有一种当前先进的分子嵌入在捕捉图结构、三维形状或相关性质终点相似性方面表现得优于分子指纹或理化描述符。传统分子指纹也存在明显局限：根据所用表示不同，该大型分子库中约有二亿二千五百万至四十亿个分子无法获得唯一表示，可能影响虚拟筛选。

Conclusion: 当前分子表示尚不能全面、可靠地刻画小分子的多方面信息，且编码碰撞会削弱大规模虚拟筛选的区分能力。未来研究应超越狭窄的下游基准评价，开发能更好保留结构、三维形状和性质信息、同时提升编码唯一性的分子表示。

Abstract: Molecular representations such as fingerprints and learned embeddings play a central role in cheminformatics and small-molecule machine learning. They serve as the primary input for a diverse array of tasks, including clustering, dataset splitting, and virtual screening, yet their evaluation is often limited to downstream model performance on narrow benchmark tasks. We carried out a systematic investigation of the quality of molecular representations, finding that none of the state-of-the-art molecular embedding models capture graph structure, 3D shape, or ADMET endpoint similarity better than fingerprints or physicochemical descriptors. However, classical fingerprints also revealed their own material limitations. For example, we show that depending on the representation, between 225M and $\sim$4B molecules have non-unique representations in the 10.8B Enamine REAL database, with implications for virtual screening. Our findings provide an insight into the current limitations of molecular representations, outlining future research directions for the development of more robust encodings of small molecules.

</details>


### [290] [Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421)
*Ilker Gurcan,Kadir Ustun,Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从微分方程数据中发现连续内禀对称性的方法；在受控基准上，该方法恢复了完整的已表示对称生成元集合及其李括号结构，并能在训练域之外近似已知生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性蕴含微分方程的几何结构，可用于模型降阶与科学机器学习。传统方法通常依赖符号化的确定方程或控制方程的闭式表达，而许多数据驱动场景不具备这些条件。本文旨在直接从微分方程数据中识别对称性，同时区分真正的内禀对称方向与冗余、依赖特定解或几何上平凡的方向。

Method: 将内禀李点对称性的发现表述为作用于采样喷射空间数据上的算子问题。研究者构造采样确定算子，并结合对称延拓、逐步子空间限制，以及一个对可训练系数保持线性的固定非线性字典来识别候选生成元。另设秩稳定性预检，用于判断随着字典细化，所表示的内禀对称代数是否趋于稳定。

Result: 在一个具有解析已知结果的受控微分方程基准上，系统的已表示内禀对称代数为十一维。该方法恢复了完整的已表示生成元集合，所得李括号结构达到数值精度上的一致，并且能够在训练域之外近似一个已知生成元。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使缺少控制系统的闭式表达，也可以从精确或近似微分系统的数据中恢复显式的内禀连续对称生成元。该框架为基于数据的几何结构发现提供了方法基础；秩稳定性检验则有助于识别字典表示是否足以稳定刻画目标对称代数。

Abstract: Discovering continuous symmetries directly from differential-equation data can reveal geometric structure for model reduction and scientific machine learning without symbolic determining equations or a closed-form representation of the governing system. We formulate intrinsic Lie point-symmetry discovery as an operator-theoretic problem on sampled jet-space data. Admissible generators are identified from a sampled determining operator and separated from redundant, solution-dependent, and geometrically trivial directions. The framework combines prolongation, sequential subspace restrictions, and a fixed nonlinear dictionary linear in trainable coefficients. A rank-stabilization preflight test diagnoses systems whose represented intrinsic symmetry algebra does not stabilize under dictionary refinement. On a controlled differential-equation benchmark with an analytically known 11-dimensional represented intrinsic symmetry algebra, the method recovers the complete represented generator set, reproduces its Lie-bracket structure to numerical precision, and approximates a known generator beyond the training domain. Here we show that explicit intrinsic continuous-symmetry generators can be recovered from data obtained from exact or approximate differential systems.

</details>


### [291] [A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445)
*Angel Daruna,Michael Cogswell,Tixiao Shan,Yi Yao,Han-Pang Chiu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于隐马尔可夫模型的攻击行为预测方法，同时处理攻击者策略随时间切换，以及可执行动作受当前状态约束的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御者需要在攻击发生前预测攻击者的下一步行动，但攻击者可能在一个攻击过程中改变行为策略，而且动作参数的可行范围会随系统状态变化。忽略这些因素会降低预测与防御决策的可靠性。

Method: 将攻击者当前采用的行为策略建模为隐状态，并用隐马尔可夫模型描述其持续与切换。每种策略对应一个定义在可行性掩码动作树上的动作概率分布，动作由操作及其状态相关参数组成。观测动作前，利用当前策略信念预测下一动作；观测后，使用同一似然更新策略信念。对部分观测到的动作前缀，仅纳入已确定部分对应的局部因子。参数估计分解为凸逻辑回归拟合；策略状态未标注时，通过鲍姆—韦尔奇算法重复利用这些拟合。此外，作者推导了按到达概率加权的节点级库尔贝克—莱布勒信息表达式、针对策略切换路径的序列对数损失界，以及统一回退保证。

Result: 在网络防御基准测试中，过滤得到的隐状态使每步预测收益提高零点八纳特。攻击者控制器切换后，该方法的损失在三步内开始下降，而循环神经网络基线需要十步。

Conclusion: 将策略切换与状态相关的动作可行性纳入统一概率模型，有助于更及时地预测攻击者行动并适应策略变化。实验结果显示，该方法在策略切换后的适应速度优于所比较的循环神经网络基线。

Abstract: A defender anticipating an attacker needs a probability distribution over the attacker's next action from information available before that action occurs. Two structures complicate this: the attacker's behavioral regime persists and can switch within an episode, and each action combines an operation with state-dependent arguments whose feasible values change over time. We capture both with a hidden Markov model whose latent state is the active regime and whose regime-conditioned action law is defined on a feasibility-masked tree of executable actions. The same likelihood forms the next-action distribution and updates the regime belief once the action is seen, and a partially observed prefix contributes its resolved local factors. Estimation separates into convex logistic fits that Baum-Welch reuses when regimes are unlabeled. We derive a reach-weighted nodewise KL expression for regime information, a sequence-wise log-loss bound for switching regime paths, and a uniform fallback guarantee. On the CAGE 2 cyber defense benchmark the filtered latent state is worth 0.8 nats per step, and after a controller switch the loss begins to fall in three steps against ten for a recurrent baseline.

</details>


### [292] [Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457)
*Yi Feng,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究大常数步长下对抗训练的梯度下降动态，发现其隐式偏置、优化收敛和损失尖锐度演化均与标准训练存在重要差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对抗训练理论多研究小步长或趋于零的步长，此时梯度下降近似连续时间过程；但这类步长训练效率较低，也不常用于实践。因此，论文旨在理解大常数步长下对抗训练的实际动态。

Method: 论文先分析对抗线性逻辑回归中的确定性梯度下降，考察模型参数最终趋向何处（隐式偏置）以及损失下降速度（优化过程）；随后转向神经网络训练，研究对抗训练中损失尖锐度随训练的变化。

Result: 研究发现三点：一是对抗训练与标准训练之间的隐式偏置差异，即使在任意大的常数步长下仍然存在；二是对抗训练的优化误差不存在统一的收敛速率；三是对抗训练中的尖锐度可能呈现两阶段动态，不同于标准训练中观察到的边缘稳定性行为。

Conclusion: 论文拓展了对大步长对抗训练动态的理论认识，表明其参数趋向、优化收敛规律及尖锐度演化不能简单沿用标准训练的分析结论。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) is an important method for improving model robustness. We study the dynamics of deterministic Gradient Descent (GD) in AT. Most existing works in this direction focus on the small or vanishing step sizes regime, where GD resembles a continuous-time flow. However, such step sizes can make training inefficient and are rarely used in practice. In this paper, we study the AT dynamics of GD with large constant step sizes. We first study GD in adversarial linear logistic regression, focusing on two aspects: where the model parameters converge (implicit bias) and how quickly the losses decrease (optimization). We then turn to neural networks training and study how the sharpness of losses evolve during AT. Our results advance the understanding of the behaviors of GD with large step sizes in AT and highlight three key differences from its behavior in Standard Training (ST): (i) the implicit bias gap between AT and ST, previously established only for small step sizes, persists for arbitrarily large constant step sizes, (ii) different from ST, there is no uniform rate of convergence for the optimization error in AT, and (iii) sharpness can exhibit two-stage dynamics that differ from the Edge-of-Stability behavior observed in ST.

</details>


### [293] [User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487)
*Jonathan Kozaczuk,Zheng Dong,Noyan Evirgen,Keivan Majidi,Eddie K. H. Ng,Chris Porter,Qi Zhao,Jia Geng,Changmao Li,Brandon R. Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种事后、与模型无关的方法，将稠密用户嵌入分解为可解释的稀疏行为模式，并用自然语言标注这些模式，以支持用户群体检索与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户嵌入能够压缩丰富的行为信息，广泛用于推荐和个性化，但其表示通常稠密且难以解释。论文希望揭示嵌入中潜藏的可解释行为结构，并让这些结构可被自然语言理解和查询。

Method: 论文假设用户嵌入可视为多个可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加：线性嵌入中的这一结构可由用户行为生成模型直接推导，深度表示中则可由叠加假说近似解释。研究使用稀疏字典学习，特别是稀疏自编码器，从嵌入中恢复行为模式，再自动为模式生成自然语言名称并支持查询。该流程在嵌入生成后执行，不要求修改上游模型。

Result: 在合成数据上，稀疏自编码器恢复已知行为模式的效果优于经典稀疏基线方法，包括处理由变换器编码得到的嵌入时。在两个真实数据集上，研究将方法应用于经典模型和变换器模型生成的用户嵌入，并展示自然语言标签可用于零样本用户群体检索与特征描述，且相关嵌入从未经过文本训练。

Conclusion: 用户嵌入中的行为信息可通过稀疏分解转化为较易解释的模式，并进一步借助自然语言标签用于检索和刻画用户群体。该框架具有事后分析、模型无关的特点，为提升用户表示的可解释性与实用性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: User embeddings are central components of modern recommendation, personalization, and behavioral modeling systems. Though these embeddings can compactly encode rich behavioral information, they are inherently dense and opaque, limiting interpretability. To address this gap, we argue that user embeddings can be understood as sparse superpositions of interpretable behavioral modes. This structure is directly predicted by generative models of user behavior for linear embeddings and extends approximately to deep-learning representations by the superposition hypothesis. Superimposed modes can naturally be recovered by sparse dictionary learning methods, such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and recovered modes can be automatically named and queried in natural language. We validate mode recovery against known ground truth on synthetic data, where SAEs recover modes better than classical sparse baselines, including on transformer-encoded embeddings. We then apply the method to classical and transformer-based user embeddings from two real-world datasets. We also show how the corresponding natural-language labels enable zero-shot audience retrieval and characterization over embeddings never trained on text. The framework is post-hoc and model-agnostic, requiring no modification to the upstream embedding system.

</details>
